# Assistant and panel

← [README](../../README.md)

## Voice chat

The **Assistant** page needs the microphone, and browsers only allow it over https. The panel therefore also listens on `https://SPARK:31443` with a self-signed certificate; the browser warns once. The LLM connection is under Settings → Language model (default: qwen38 at `http://127.0.0.1:30001/v1`). Since V01.0.292 "Model" there offers a choice of the models the server at this address reports (`/v1/models`, admin only, kept 60 seconds, "Reload" asks at once); "Automatic" takes the first, "Type another …" opens the text field. If the list cannot be read, the text field stays with the reason above it. The installer takes the qwen38 API key from `~/.config/qwen38/api-key` of the user who runs `sudo ./install.sh`. Thinking is off for the chat so the answer starts right away. An animated assistant face shows whether it is listening, thinking or speaking; its mouth follows the voice. The same face sits in the bottom right corner of every tab, so you can talk from anywhere in the panel. Since V01.0.154 the admin picks under Settings → Defaults → "Gesicht des Assistenten" between *Robot* (as before, the default) and *Comic* (a caricature with spiky hair and a short mustache, since V01.0.187 only above the upper lip). Since V01.0.497 there is a third choice, *Comic (woman)*: a woman with long dark hair and a side parting, drawn in the same style, with lashes and lips that open when she speaks; she moves like the comic face and shows the expressions too. The iPhone app, the companions (Mac and Windows, as a still) and the Pebble draw her too. The comic face looks around, blinks with heavy lids, raises its brows, tilts its head, moves mouth and mustache with the voice and shows the state with a coloured ring: grey idle, blue listening, yellow thinking, green speaking, red error. The choice applies to everybody, the iPhone app included; more faces can be added in `static/js/face.js`. Since V01.0.521 every profile picks its own face under Me → Conversation → *Display* with pictures (*Default*, *Robot*, *Comic*, *Comic (woman)*); it applies on all of the profile's devices (panel, iPhone app, companions, Pebble), *Default* takes the Spark's face. In the iPhone app the same choice sits at the top of Settings → *Face* (with the next TestFlight build). The admin's pick has since been at the top of Settings → Defaults → *Face*, with pictures as well, and applies to guests, shared devices and everybody who picked nothing of their own; the presets never copy a face into a profile. The server only takes the fixed names (`profiles.FACES`), every device asks through `profiles.face_of`. Since V01.0.241 the small picture before each answer in the history shows the chosen face as well. Since V01.0.294 there is a switch "Gesicht zeigt, was es tut" next to it (off): the face then shows a small sign beside the head for what the answer is doing, taken only from the panel's events, never from the model's text: a magnifier for web, document or history searches, a calendar sheet for calendar and briefing, a letter for mail, a light bulb for Home Assistant, a note pad for memory and reminders, thought bubbles while thinking. After an action was done (device switched, something remembered, reminder set) it is pleased for a moment and winks, on an error it looks puzzled with a question mark, and after 5 minutes without conversation or use it falls asleep with "zzz"; a tap, a key or the mouse over the face wake it.

**Start page and password:** the panel opens with the assistant. Status, settings, voices and logs need the panel password (button "Settings"; the login ends after 7 days without use and renews itself while in use). "Assistant without password" under Settings → Security is off on new installs: then guests are locked out, profiles sign in on the start page with name and PIN (devices with a key keep working), and the admin signs in with "Admin". Switched on, the assistant is open to everyone on the network, also without a profile. **Guests only get questions and answers** (since V01.0.509, Dominik 2026-10-13): the language model answers from its own knowledge, without any tool, so no web search, timers, briefing, memory, history on the Spark or smart home. Above the history it then says "Without a profile: questions and answers only" with a *Sign in* button. The same holds for a voice a shared speaker does not recognize as its owner's, and for the admin in a browser without an own profile. The password can be changed under Settings → Security. Scripts can still use HTTP Basic.

**Release** (since V01.0.509, Dominik 2026-10-13): what the admin switches on under Features works at once for every profile. There is no own switch per profile for it any more. Only the admin blocks single profiles, under People and devices → Blocked functions or in the function's detail. A profile only sets up its part (enter a place, connect an account, pair a device). It can only switch off the few things the Spark does unasked, such as notes on its own, notifications to the phone, the morning sheet or night updates of its speakers; these are on from the start.

**Blocked, but own data (V01.0.531, Dominik 2026-10-14):** when the admin blocks a released function for one profile, its page under Me disappears; what the profile stored for it stays its own. While something is left, Me shows in place of the page only a short card: "The admin blocked "Weather" for you (People and devices → Blocked functions). Stored: 1 entry." and the button "Delete data" (with a question first). It deletes exactly what deleting on the function's own page removes, passwords and keys included (place, stop, calendars, mailboxes, address books, reminders, voice samples, Home Assistant connection, Telegram link, paired devices, MCP connections and services, the reMarkable with its documents, knowledge folders, rules of "Proactive" and of inbox tidying); then the card goes. Not included are all documents at once and chats, because other people belong to them. Technically every module registers its data in `features.OWN` (count and delete), `/api/whoami` names under `locked` only function, page and count, never contents; deleting goes through `DELETE /api/profile/owndata/{key}` (only the profile's own browser login, never a device key or guest, only while blocked, rate limit `owndata`). Journal: "profiles: own data deleted while blocked | weather | 1 entries …" (area memory).

**On the phone:** below 760 px the page opens with the big face (tap = talk) and two buttons below it: "Settings" opens the "Me" window ("Hey Spark" at the top), "History" the messenger view. That view fits the screen exactly: a slim top bar with an arrow back to the face, a small face, state, profile and menu, the history in the middle, and at the bottom a text field and round mic button (a stop button during the answer). The browser remembers which of the two views was open last. The menu (☰) holds new conversation, earlier conversations, conversation settings, language, light/dark and, for admins, every tab.

**Like the app (V01.0.296):** on a phone (below 760 px) the page looks like the iPhone app, the same on iPhone and Android. At the bottom are the tabs *Spark* (conversation), *Today* (setup and the same cards and sentences as Me → Overview), *Documents*, *Me* and *Manage*; you only see what your role allows (guests only Spark, Manage only with admin rights, Documents only when allowed). Spark shows the face at the top (smaller once there are bubbles), the history below, the big mic button and always the text field; top left "History" (earlier conversations as a sheet from below, swipe down closes) and "New". Me and Manage are lists like the iOS settings; sub-pages slide in from the right, "‹ Back" top left, the Android or Safari back gesture goes exactly one level back. Manage starts with the status, then Features, People and devices, Settings, Logs, Update, Backups and Appearance. Under Appearance (Manage or Me) "Show the computer layout" switches back to the old phone view (this browser only); the menu's "Show like the app" returns. It is only another presentation: no new endpoints, the server checks rights as before. Questions and answers use the width like the iPhone app: bubbles start about 16 px from the edge and run up to 40 px before the other edge; "Read again" and "Delete" show only after a tap on the message, below the bubble, and never make it narrower.

**Typing comes first (V01.0.701):** while something is typed into the text field, the face draws only about ten pictures a second instead of sixty (until 1.5 s after the last letter), and the page asks which face is on screen only four times a second. Typing stays smooth on slower phones, tablets and in the Android app. In the iPhone app the line to type in is its own view: a letter redraws only that line, not the face and the conversation.

**Typing comes first, part 2 (V01.0.749):** while text stands in the field, the field is marked "hastext" instead of "typing": the old name started an answer's waiting dots (`.typing::after`) invisibly at the field, and they laid out the whole page sixty times a second. The face's loading wheel turns only while it shows (thinking, searching). The conversation is closed off (`#chatlog{contain:strict}`): a letter no longer lays out every bubble of a long conversation again. Measured with the CPU slowed down six times and 120 bubbles: long tasks while typing from about 150 ms to 0, time to the next picture on average from 37 to about 25 ms.

**Quick start and tidier pages (V01.0.750):** an admin's page start on a slow phone (CPU throttled four times) took 11.5 s, now about 2 s; with only a profile 2.1 s, now about 1.8 s. The jump links asked for every hint whether a menu is visible, and the browser recalculated the whole page each time; that now happens once per frame. The cards under Status (Listening now, Services, NAS, Usage and others) ask the Spark only while Status is open, all together when it opens. The list of all guides is drawn under Features → Guides, the text behind „Guide“ at a switch at the first tap. The start page goes out gzipped (212 KB → about 40 KB), and every script and style file carries its fingerprint instead of the version number: after an update the browser loads only what changed and keeps the rest up to a year. On computers the face column is 320 instead of 420 px wide (buttons and switches one below the other), the conversation gets the room. Away from the assistant every page ends with room for the floating face: its last rows can always be scrolled out from under it, the width of the pages stays the same. Status has its menu like Settings: symbols and the blocks Watch (Summary, Live, Logs), Check (Checks, Improve, Hands-on test) and Technology (Usage, Models, Architecture); the footer stands at the bottom of the window. On phones „Manage“/„Verwalten“ fits whole into the back button. Since V01.0.758 Features and People and devices have their symbols too: every page in the menu beside them, and every group of Features carries the symbol of the same group under Me. Since V01.0.764 the list of profiles is tidy: a coloured circle with initials, one short line "last … · 2 devices · 30 facts", setup as a thin bar with "14/15", a chevron instead of "Details" (the whole line opens the person), search field and selects of one height, "+ New profile" beside the count; devices show their kind as a symbol (speaker, iPhone app, watch, terminal, Home Assistant, own key).

**As an app on your phone:** open `https://SPARK:31443` in the phone's browser, accept the certificate warning, then use "Add to Home screen" in the browser menu (iPhone: Safari → Share → "Add to Home Screen"). The assistant then starts full screen with its own icon. With the self-signed certificate Android creates a shortcut instead of an installed app; it works the same, it just does not show up in the app list.

**Better recognition:** a fixed default language (Settings → Speech recognition, e.g. German) instead of "auto" helps most with short sentences. The "Context" field takes names and terms that are often misheard; the model then prefers them. The 1.7B model is noticeably more accurate than 0.6B and needs about 2–3 GiB more memory.

**Second recognizer: Parakeet.** Under Settings → Speech recognition → "Recognizer" the admin switches between Qwen3-ASR (GPU) and Parakeet ([primeline/parakeet-primeline](https://huggingface.co/primeline/parakeet-primeline), a German fine-tune of NVIDIA Parakeet TDT 0.6B v3; the download is [flozen1981/parakeet-primeline-onnx](https://huggingface.co/flozen1981/parakeet-primeline-onnx), an int8 ONNX export of it, run via sherpa-onnx; idea from [winidi/dictate](https://github.com/winidi/dictate)). Parakeet runs on the CPU and needs about 1 GiB; the Qwen3-ASR engine is stopped meanwhile. It ignores the context field, and a stream returns the whole text in one piece. The first start downloads about 640 MB.

**Conversation comfort:** the assistant knows the date and time (the browser's time zone; can be turned off under Settings → Language model). Conversations are kept in the browser and can be reopened or deleted at the top of the conversation. With "live transcript" the text appears while you speak; in the pause the recognition is usually already done, so that step is skipped at the end.

**Guides:** every service that can be switched on has an openable guide (what it does, what you need, setup, example phrases, what leaves the house, switching off, troubleshooting): next to its switch under Features, at the top of its page in the "Me" window and collected under Features → Guides. Services are sorted into the same groups everywhere: Conversation, My day, Mail and documents, Home, On the go and devices, Security and access. The texts live in `app/panel/static/js/guides.js`; a self-test checks that no switch is left without a guide and group.

**Weather, contacts, parcels (V01.0.93):** three small services, each off by default; once the admin releases it under Features it works at once for every profile (each profile sets up the place for the weather and the address book in the "Me" window); never guests. *Weather* comes straight from Open-Meteo (no key, only the place's coordinates leave, own address configurable); the Spark builds fixed sentences from the numbers, also for the daily briefing and the proactive weather note. *Contacts* reads an address book over CardDAV (iCloud: https://contacts.icloud.com with an app-specific password, Nextcloud, others) once a day and keeps it encrypted: phone numbers, e-mail addresses, birthdays (in the briefing and as a notice), names instead of addresses in mail. Contact text counts as outside text. *Parcels* recognises shipping mails of DHL, Hermes, DPD, GLS, UPS and Amazon in the profile's own mailbox (14 days) and says what comes today, is on its way, waits for pick-up or was delivered; no carrier is asked, parcel lines count as mail text.

**Telegram (V01.0.96):** your own bot from @BotFather (guide in the panel). The admin switches it on under Features and enters the token (stored encrypted); the Spark asks Telegram for new messages itself, no open port needed. Each profile links its Telegram in the "Me" window with a one-time code (10 minutes) and writes or sends voice messages (speech recognition on the Spark). Strangers get one fixed sentence, groups nothing. Per profile, all off at first: answers also as voice messages, notifications over Telegram, personal data (calendar, mail, contacts, parcels, documents; also appointments and mail in notifications) and switching the smart home, only with the code word. Commands: /neu, /stimme, /trennen. When a message names a place in the panel ("Me → Telegram"), it gets an "Open in the panel" button if the admin entered the panel address for links (https) under Features → Telegram (since V01.0.448).

**Own speakers (ESP32, V01.0.99, without display since V01.0.104):** small ESP32-S3 boards (16 MB flash, PSRAM, INMP441 microphone, MAX98357A amplifier, e.g. the XiaoZhi breadboard boards) become a voice assistant in the room. Firmware: the open XiaoZhi firmware at a fixed version, German, wake word "Jarvis", without a built-in cloud address; a GitHub Action (`.github/workflows/firmware.yml`, settings in `firmware/xiaozhi.json`) builds it as release `fw-<version>`. The Spark fetches the newest one daily (or on a button), checks every file against `manifest.json` and keeps it with the previous one. Set up in the "Me" window → Speakers: over USB from Chrome or Edge (Web Serial, esptool-js 0.5.7 ships with the panel) the browser writes firmware and a settings block (Wi-Fi, Spark address, device key); the Wi-Fi password never reaches the Spark. Without a PC a board shows or says a 6-digit code. Each speaker is a device key of its profile (voice, memory, smart home with code word, room). Updates arrive over Wi-Fi when the board starts (per device "auto" can be switched off) or over USB. Admin switch `chat.esp32`, off at first, released for every profile; the reverse proxy must pass WebSockets. Board variants: no display, OLED 128×32, OLED 128×64, colour display, since V01.0.133 the Waveshare ESP32-S3-AUDIO-Board (round board with ES8311 audio chip and two microphones; display and camera are optional there). When a board is rewritten over USB with another variant, updates follow the board type it reports at start; the no-display variant never touches the display pins and, since firmware 2.5.1.3 (V01.0.132), no longer loads font and emoji either, which made it crash right after joining the Wi-Fi. Start check only by POST of at most 16 KB, at most 3 open codes per address, firmware only from github.com downloads with a size limit.

**Device updates (V01.0.381):** Features → "Device updates" (admin, off by default) makes the Spark look for new firmware for the speakers, the Android app and the Spark companion every 6 hours instead of once a day, as before only from GitHub's download addresses and with size and checksum from the manifest; *Look now* in Status → Device updates asks at once. The card shows per kind of device the version on the Spark, the last check and every device with its version. A profile with such a device gets one notification per new version, and so does the profile for admin notes; the version the Spark already has when this is switched on is not announced. The companion keeps showing its own updates. My status has one row per kind of device ("update comes tonight", "1 of 2 up to date"), the live view the target "Geräte-Updates" with counts. **Update speakers at night** (sub-switch; on from the start for every profile, can be switched off under Me → Conversation → Devices → "Update my speakers at night", plus "Updates by themselves" at the speaker): the boards fetch firmware only when they start. In the night window (default from 05:15 for 60 minutes, after the window of the nightly Spark update) the Spark first restarts one connected, unused speaker. When it comes back with the new version within 10 minutes, the others follow one by one, the next one 30 minutes later at the earliest. When it does not come back, the round stops for this version: owner and admin get a notification, and it goes on only after *Go on distributing* in Status. Never during a conversation, in room mode, during a test, a Notaus or own Notaus, and never during a Spark update. **Note on a new XiaoZhi version** (sub-switch): the Spark compares the newest release of github.com/78/xiaozhi-esp32 with the point our firmware builds on and sends the admin one note per new version. Nothing is built by itself; only the release's version number is used, never its text. Since V01.0.678 GitHub's answer may be up to 4 MB: it lists every file of the release (XiaoZhi: one package per board) and 256 KB was too tight ("XiaoZhi check failed: TooLarge" in the error log). **Firmware 2.5.2.8 (V01.0.711):** our speaker firmware now builds on XiaoZhi 2.5.2 (commit 49b4380). Little changes for our boards: the ESP32-S3 sets up microphone and speaker (I2S) the same way everywhere, and an unreadable serial number in the chip is ignored. All own patches (own server only, board without display, wait instead of dropping sound, Notaus button, quiet at once on the wake word, stop while it speaks) apply unchanged. Boards take only newer versions by update; going back to 2.5.1.7 works only over USB.

**Interrupting a speaker (V01.0.472):** while an own speaker talks, the board only listens for its wake word "Jarvis" (with echo cancelling where the board has a reference channel, such as the Waveshare board) or the button. Since firmware 2.5.1.7 it is then quiet at once: the firmware drops the rest of its play queue (`firmware/patch_unterbrechen.py`) instead of playing it for up to 1.2 s, and listens. Saying only "Stopp", "Halt", "Ruhe", "Hör auf" or "Danke, das reicht" then (fixed word list `esp32.STOP_WORDS`, the whole sentence) sends nothing to the language model: the speaker stays quiet and waits for its wake word again; Check shows "Stopp gehört". Any other question is answered as always. **Stop while it speaks** (admin: Features → Own speakers → Stop while it speaks, off at first, then for every profile): then it works without the wake word at all. At every connection the Spark asks the board through the MCP tool `self.spark.stop_word` (`firmware/patch_stoppwort.py`) to keep its microphone on while speaking; only boards with a reference channel do so, their echo cancelling takes out the own voice (breadboard boards keep it off). The Spark cuts what arrives while speaking into short pieces (at most `STOP_PIECE` = 3 s, one recognition at a time) and runs them through the speech recognition; only a whole sentence from `STOP_WORDS` counts, and not when it is in the last four sentences said (a "halt" in its own sentence is echo). It then ends the answer, sends `tts stop` with `spark_quiet`, the board drops its queue and the Spark listens. Nothing of it is stored; the log only says "stop word while speaking", the live overview counts "Lautsprecher hört auf „Stopp““ under connections. When the switch goes off, a board asked before is told "off" once at its next connection.

**Checking a speaker (V01.0.131):** under Me → Speakers every speaker has a *Check* button. It shows whether the admin switch is on and the profile is not blocked, whether there is firmware for the board variant, whether the board ever checked in, which address is in the board (with a warning for `https://<IP>` or port 31443, whose self-signed certificate the board refuses, for `localhost` and for a missing address) and what the speaker did lately: start check, connection, refused connection with its reason, wake word, microphone (length, loudest moment and mean in dB, whether there was speech), what was understood and how much sound went back. These lines stay in memory only (40 per speaker, gone when the panel restarts). *Check network* asks the address from the Spark the way the board does: `GET /api/esp32/ping` (answers with a marker of this Spark) and a WebSocket with `x-spark-probe: 1`; this shows whether the address leads to this Spark, whether the certificate is accepted, whether the reverse proxy redirects or blocks WebSockets. Allowed like Home Assistant (home network yes, the Spark's own service ports no), only these two paths, answers of at most 4 KB, six checks in ten minutes per profile. Behind a router without NAT loopback the check can fail although the board reaches the Spark. *Test: sound and microphone* plays the soft tone and a test sentence at the next "Jarvis" or button press (at once when connected) and then checks the microphone with the next sentence: the speaker says what it understood, or that it heard nothing. *Read the board's log (USB)* connects the board in Chrome or Edge, restarts it and reads for 45 seconds what the firmware reports; the page translates the known lines (Wi-Fi joined or not, certificate refused, Spark unreachable, status 403, pairing code, conversation connection, crash, brownout, sound error). The text stays in the browser, words like token or password are blacked out; *Copy* takes it along for a bug report. Since V01.0.136 the list under *Check* follows live (every 2 seconds while the page is visible); in room mode it only says that a sentence was heard and whether it speaks in a pause, never what was heard. Since V01.0.135 every speaker has a *Volume* slider under Me → Speakers (0 to 100; the Spark sends it at every connection through the firmware's MCP tool `self.audio_speaker.set_volume`, at once when connected) and a *Microphone* choice less sensitive / normal / more sensitive (from which level the Spark takes sound as speech: about −41, −47 or −53 dB). Since V01.0.134 a quiet level from about −47 dB (and three times above the background noise) counts as speech, quiet recordings are raised up to 20 times before speech recognition, and every question writes a line "esp32: microphone at …" with length and level to the log. The log now also shows the start checks ("esp32: start check from …") and the reason of every refused connection. Since V01.0.137 the Spark buffers the sound: it starts only when 0.6 seconds are ready, keeps at most 0.9 seconds in flight (since V01.0.138: 0.7) (the board holds 1.2 seconds) and after a stall collects 0.3 seconds again instead of sending single snippets. When speech output could not keep up, *Check* shows "Ton stockte N-mal" and the log "esp32: sound stalled"; if it stutters without that line, the board's Wi-Fi is the cause. Since V01.0.138 (firmware 2.5.1.5) the board no longer drops sound when its buffer is full but waits; the connection then slows the Spark down. Before, Wi-Fi delays added up in long answers, the buffer overflowed and the gaps grew towards the end. The Spark's lead is lowered to 0.7 seconds so old firmware has more room too. The board fetches the new firmware itself at its next start (unplug it briefly).

**Speaker page (V01.0.149):** Me → Speakers shows "My speakers" at the top (one line per speaker with its state: connected, room mode until, update available) and "Add a speaker" with two ways: *Set up a new board over USB* (Chrome/Edge on a PC) and *Board shows a code*. Tapping a speaker opens its page in sections: **Sound** (volume, microphone), **Room mode** ("Listen now" as a Start/Stop button, duration, how much it may say, *What it may offer* with the same seven kinds as in the browser, stored per speaker on the Spark, room in Home Assistant, detect more, and with "Ignore TV voices" also who it listens to and trial), **Voice** (teach "My voice through this speaker"), **Firmware** (version, update now, updates by themselves, rewrite the firmware over USB), **Check** (checks with a readable board name, check network, sound and microphone test, live recent events, board log over USB) and *Remove speaker* at the bottom. Admin: under Features → Own speakers the firmware state comes first, then the address with *Check address* (tried from the Spark like "Check network", admin only, at most 6 checks in 10 minutes). Under People and devices and Me → Security speaker keys are marked "Speaker, managed under Me → Speakers"; they cannot be moved to another profile.

**Lists for shopping and tasks (V01.0.96):** "Put milk on the shopping list", "What is on my to-do list?". Admin switch, off at first, then for every profile. Each of the two lists lives either on the Spark, where an iPhone shortcut with the device key fetches new entries (`POST /api/tasks/inbox?list=einkauf`, header `X-Speech-Device`; a GET only reads and changes nothing) into the Reminders app, or in a CalDAV task list of a calendar account (Nextcloud, mailbox.org, Radicale). Adding happens at once, ticking off and deleting by voice only after "Yes". After outside text the assistant adds nothing in the same answer.

**Page Lists (V01.0.854, plan `listen-teilen/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-20 "1a 2a"):** the lists have a page of their own in the menu (after E-mail, only with the function) and in the iPhone app under My things → Lists; Me → Overview and Today in the app show the shopping with its number and the first entries. Besides shopping and tasks you make up to 20 own lists (name, symbol), rename them and delete them with their entries; "Put screws on the DIY store list" reaches an own list, and the assistant never makes an unknown list itself. Every entry shows who put it on, when and from where (page, app, voice, Telegram, phone, watch, reMarkable, job, …). Ticking off on the page is one tap without a question; ticked off entries stay 14 days under "Recently done" with "Bring back". The shopping list sorts by departments (a fixed word list in the panel, never the model) and adds up amounts ("milk 2x" and "3 milk" become "milk 5×"). Endpoints: `GET /api/profile/tasks`, `POST /api/profile/listen`, `PUT`/`DELETE /api/profile/listen/{id}`, `POST /api/profile/tasks/{list}` (`/done`, `/undo`), `DELETE /api/profile/tasks/{list}/{entry}`; each with login, profile binding, CSRF, 8 KB and rate limit `listen`; looking and deleting also work while the admin blocked the profile. Log: lines `tasks:` with fixed words and numbers only (an own list is "own" there, never its name); Status → Live shows with the calendar only how many own lists and how many entries were added and ticked off today. Tests: `app/tests/test_listen.py`.

**Share lists (V01.0.865, stage 2 of the plan, Dominik 2026-10-20 "3a"):** its own admin switch under Functions → Lists for shopping and tasks, off at first; released, it holds for every profile, guests get nothing. Whoever owns a list shares it on the page Lists with "Share …" (or in the iPhone app) per profile: off, see or add to it. Add to it means adding and ticking off; deleting, renaming and sharing stay with the owner. Lists from a calendar account (CalDAV) cannot be shared. Shared lists stand under "Shared with me" with "from …", also in the app; by voice "Put butter on Sabine's shopping list" works. Whoever a list is not shared with does not even learn it exists (the same answer as for no list); whoever may only see gets a clear no when adding. What others add arrives at most every 10 minutes bundled as a note ("Anton put 3 entries on "Shopping"."), switched off with "Note on new entries" (each profile's own setting). Shared entries come from other people: the assistant reads them as outside data (`tasks_show` outside), never as a command. Endpoint: `PUT /api/profile/listen/{id}/share` with `{to, level}`, login, profile binding, CSRF, 8 KB, rate limit `listen`; a shared list is `u_….{list}` there. Log: `tasks: list share | ok see|add|off` and for others' entries `tasks: add einkauf shared | …` with `profiles.log_name`, never names or contents; Status → Live only counts how many lists are shared. Tests: `Teilen` in `app/tests/test_listen.py`.

**Lists in Reminders (V01.0.865, stage 3 of the plan, Dominik 2026-10-20 "5a"):** its own admin switch under Functions → Lists for shopping and tasks, off at first; it works with the calendar server for calendar apps (Functions → Calendar and daily briefing). A profile's own lists that live on the Spark (shopping, tasks, own lists; not a list in a calendar account, never another profile's list, also not a shared one) show as task lists (VTODO) under `/caldav/<profile>/liste-<list>/` and so in the iPhone's Reminders app (or Thunderbird, DAVx5 with tasks.org), through the same CalDAV account and app password as the Spark calendars. Both ways: putting on, ticking off, bringing back and deleting in Reminders change the list on the Spark, the page and the voice change what Reminders sees (ctag and etags). What comes in is data: exactly one VTODO with a title, of it only the title (cut) and whether it is done; else 415; a wrong If-Match 412; at most as many entries as a list holds. An entry from Reminders shows the source "iPhone Reminders" and keeps the app's object name and UID. The door is the calendar server's: login with the app password, only the own path, My emergency stop, rate limit `caldav`. Log: the `tasks:` lines with "from calendar app", never the entry; Status → Live counts the changes from Reminders with the calendar. On top there is a widget "Shopping" for the home and lock screen of the iPhone app: the open entries of the shopping list, reloaded as soon as a list changes in the app, else about every 30 minutes (with the iPhone's key, only with the lists released). Code: `app/panel/listendav.py`, `ios/SparkWidgets/SparkWidgets.swift`; tests: `app/tests/test_listendav.py`.

**Write link (V01.0.875, stage 4 of the plan, Dominik 2026-10-20 "4a"):** its own admin switch under Functions → Lists for shopping and tasks → "Write link", off at first; released, it holds for every profile, only the admin blocks single ones. On the page Lists at an own list "⋯" → "Share …" → "Link for people without a profile": expiry 7 days, 30 days or 1 year, optionally "may see the list", then "Make link". The link (`https://<panel>/#liste=<code>`) and its QR code show this one time only; the Spark keeps only the code's hash (`mitschreib-links.json` in the state folder), at most 20 open links per profile. Whoever opens the link sees a small page with the list's name: entry (several with commas), optionally a name, "Put on"; with "see" also the open entries, texts only. No ticking off, no deleting, no other list, no tool, no model. With you the entry shows the source "write link" and the typed name, and the note to the others of the list says "name (Link)". The assistant reads a list with such entries as outside text. "Revoke" in the same window works at once; an expired link, a deleted list, a list in a calendar account or the switch turned off end it as well. Protection: the code stands after the `#` and reaches neither the proxy nor the panel log, other sites (Origin) are refused, every request counts against its own rate limit, wrong codes lock like a wrong PIN, the owner's Mein Notaus holds every entry off. Log only "tasks: link made 30d | Profil …", "tasks: link refused | bad code"; State → Live counts open links, entries through them and refusals. On the iPhone the same under Lists → list → "Share …", with QR code and "Pass on the link".

**Everything open in the assistant (V01.0.867, plan `assistent-zentrale/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ok", stage 1):** What needs you comes as a **card with buttons into the day's conversation** (the same main conversation as "One conversation for all channels"): urgent finds from "For you" (bill, contract, reply, suspicion) and reminders that rang. On the card you deal with it at once ("Done", "Remind me", "Not important", "More of this") or open the place where you go on ("Add to calendar …", "Draft a reply …", "Draft a cancellation …": nothing is sent or saved before that place). "Later" puts the card away: this evening (6 pm, in the evening in three hours), tomorrow morning (8 am), next week (Monday 8 am) or no time; at that time it comes back **once** as "Wieder da: …" into the conversation and as a notification on one device. The **page Open** in the menu (after Lists, only with the function; Android through the panel view) shows everything in order: *Needs you*, *Later* (by time), *Done* (7 days, with "Bring back"), the list on the left, the card on the right; above the conversation stands "Still open: N", the menu entry carries the number, Me → Overview a card. In the iPhone app: cards in the conversation, My things → Open, a Today card. Done on one device is done on every one.
Technology: `vorgaenge.py` keeps `vorgaenge.json` per profile (one Vorgang per key `fuerdich:<id>` or `erinnerung:<id>`, state offen · gesehen · spaeter · erledigt · weg, counter `rev`). The conversation keeps only the number (`vg`) on an assistant message; every page draws the card from the store (`GET /api/profile/vorgaenge?seit=<rev>`; the stream `/api/profile/vorgaenge/stream` sends numbers only). The buttons are fixed names (`vorgaenge.AKTIONEN`); `POST /api/profile/vorgaenge/act` checks owner, kind, the source's rules (`fuerdich.acts`), the state (`rev`, else 409 with the new state) and the emergency stop, then the source's function runs. For you and the Vorgang are marked together. Only the panel makes a Vorgang, never the model; a sender from a mail stays text. Admin switch "Everything open in the assistant" under Timers and reminders, off at first, then for every profile (can be blocked), guests never. Open → Settings removes the done ones or all; mails and reminders stay. Important mails, messages, shared things and "what is open?" by voice follow in stages 3 and 4.
**Everything open, stage 2: proposals with a yes button (V01.0.876):** When the assistant proposes something that happens only after your yes (a new appointment, moving or deleting one, sending a mail by voice, tidying the mailbox, saving a draft), the card **"Waiting for your yes"** stands under its answer with exactly what would happen (what, when, where, calendar; for a mail to, subject and text). The first button says what it does ("Add", "Move", "Delete", "Send", "Save as draft", "Do it") and does the same as a typed "yes"; then the card shows the checked result ("Added to „Privat“"). "Change …" opens the Calendar page's form for a new appointment and the E-mail page's writing window for a new mail, both filled in; only there is it saved or sent. "Cancel" drops the proposal. The card also stands on the page Open under "Needs you" and in the iPhone app: so you confirm a proposal from a speaker or from Telegram on the phone. It lasts as long as the proposal (15 minutes, mail by voice 5 minutes) and has no "Later"; after an answer in the conversation, a newer proposal or the time it closes ("Answered in the conversation", "New proposal", "Expired"). At the press the panel checks owner, state, emergency stop and the module's switches again (such as "Send mails"); a mail's text stays only while it waits. The card comes with the switch "Everything open in the assistant", also without "Show steps and approvals"; without it the yes/no card of "Show steps and approvals" stays. Important mails as cards and buttons in the companion and in Telegram follow in stage 3.
**Meeting tasks under Open (V01.0.882):** When the Spark companion puts a meeting's tasks onto a list ("Ausgewählte auf die Liste"), every new task also becomes a card "From a meeting" under Open (not in the conversation), with the list, the meeting time and "due" as its deadline. "Done" ticks the list entry off, "Bring back" brings it back, "Not important" only hides the card. The other way round the card follows the list: ticked off (page, voice, app, iPhone Reminders) it is done, taken back it is open again, deleted it is gone. Overdue tasks stay open. Technology: kind `besprechung` in `vorgaenge.ARTEN`, key `besprechung:<list>:<entry>`, `vorgaenge.from_meeting`, `tasks.card_follows` and `tasks.tick_from_card`.
Technology: `profiles.pending_set` reports every waiting proposal to `vorgaenge.from_pending` (kind `vorschlag`, key `vorschlag:<name>:<time>`, words from the module's `card()`: `calendars`, `senden`, `tidy`). The yes button (`vs_ja`, stage `ja`) calls the same function as the typed "yes" (`calendars.carry`, `senden.carry`, `tidy.carry_out`) and answers `{text, gelungen}`; `vorgaenge.sweep` closes the cards after every answer and every minute. The approval card (`ablauf.waiting`) carries the number (`vg`), the panel and the iPhone then show the card. Live (calendar node) counts waiting proposals and those confirmed by button today.

**Bus and train (V01.0.102):** "When does the next bus leave?", "How do I get to Karlsruhe at 8?". Admin switch, off at first, then for every profile. Timetable from transport.rest (default https://v6.db.transport.rest, own address configurable); fixed sentences with delay, platform and cancellations. With a commute (destination, time, days) "Proactive" speaks up 45 to 5 minutes before when the connection is 5 or more minutes late or cancelled. Timetable text counts as outside text.

**Reddit and feeds (V01.0.739):** "What's new on Reddit?", "Did anybody reply to me on Reddit?", "What's new in my feeds?". Admin switch Features → Reddit and feeds, off at first, then for every profile, never for guests. Each profile connects its own Reddit inbox under Me → Reddit and feeds with the private RSS link (reddit.com → Settings → RSS feeds; since November 2025 Reddit hands out API apps only on request, the link needs none), adds subreddits (r/LocalLLaMA) and other RSS or Atom feeds (blogs, YouTube channels, Mastodon) and enters up to 20 keywords. The link carries Reddit's feed key: connecting asks for the code (level "Confirm"), it is kept sealed in the vault and never sent back. The panel fetches each source at most every 30 minutes through the guard (https only, public addresses, 2 MB, after a 429 only after two hours), reads XML only with defusedxml (no entities, no DTD) and decides with fixed rules: what the first fetch finds was already there; new in the inbox means "a reply to you" and comes at once through "Proactive" ("News from feeds", preference on), a keyword as a whole word in a title at most 3 times a day, everything else goes into the daily briefing (up to 3 replies and 5 posts of the last 24 hours, keyword hits first, then the feed's order). Titles, senders and links are outside text: the tool `feeds_news` hands them over as data, after it switching, memory and proposals are locked; the page shows links only with https and `noopener`. The Spark never posts, votes or sends anything to Reddit.

**Agent functions (V01.0.175):** the assistant works longer for you. Admin: Features → "Jobs" on and pick a level per profile: *off* (default), *read and report only* or *also act after "Yes"*. With the level the profile can start at once, Me → Jobs shows its jobs. Never guests, other voices or ESP32 speakers.
- *Background jobs:* "Research which heat pump fits the pool and let me know." The job runs in the background (search, read pages, with "my data" also appointments, reminders and documents), then a notification comes by browser, iPhone app and Telegram (as set under Me → Notifications). The full report is under Me → Jobs and in the log, on request as a document under "My documents" (switch "Also keep every report under "Documents"" or "As document" at the job). Limits: one job at a time on the Spark, it waits while someone talks to the assistant; 8 rounds, 4 searches, 5 pages, 12 tool calls, 15 minutes, 20,000 characters; 3 waiting and 20 jobs per day and profile. A job only has reading tools, its report counts as outside text and is never learned.
- *Scheduled jobs:* "Sum up my appointments of the week every Monday at 7." The assistant reads the plan back in fixed words, it is saved only after your "Yes". Once, daily, weekdays, weekly or monthly, at most 10, pause and delete under Me → Jobs. If the Spark was off for more than 3 hours at the planned time, that run is skipped.
- *Watching and own tasks (V01.0.508):* "Let me know when there is new firmware for my FRITZ!Box." or "Tell me as soon as the flight to Rome costs less than 100 euros." The panel recognizes the sentence itself, reads the watch out in fixed words (name, how often, price limit) and only makes it after your "Yes"; daily at 8 by default. Each run is a reading job; the model only names candidates (`FUND: value | address | quote`). A find only counts as new when the panel checks it: the page was read in this run or returned by the search, the quote stands there word for word, the value is in the quote, with a price limit the amount is below it, and the watch does not know the value yet. Only then a notification in fixed words (value and address) goes out; otherwise it stays quiet and only the history (14 days) records the run. After 5 errors in a row or 90 days without anything new the watch pauses itself and says why. At most 10 per profile. Change it by voice ("Watch that every Monday instead", after "Yes", can be undone) or under Me → Jobs → Own tasks: one list with search and filters for watches, scheduled jobs, repeating reminders, smart home rules of "Speaking up by itself" and important mail senders; every row has a switch (off = paused, stays saved), the detail has change, "Check now", "Forget what is known", history and delete. A paused reminder rings on no device. The iPhone app shows watches and scheduled jobs under "By itself" with a switch and delete. Admin: Features → Jobs → "Watching" (off), applies to every profile with agent functions, never to guests. Logs: `agent: watch run | result neu|still|bericht|fehler | new … | dropped …`.
- *Jobs with plan, questions and proposals (V01.0.744, ideas from Redde, package F):* A job gets three tools that only concern itself. `job_plan` writes its steps as a list (at most 6) and ticks them off; Me → Jobs shows it live. `job_ask` asks you one question with up to 4 answers to tap and waits up to 10 minutes, only in jobs you started yourself (scheduled jobs and watching never ask); the notification only says that a question waits, never the question. The panel refuses a question about passwords, PINs or codes. `job_propose` makes one proposal at most: a reminder, an appointment or an entry on your shopping or task list, never smart home, mail or anything outside your profile. The job carries out nothing: under the report the proposal stands in full in fixed words, with a warning when the job read outside text before, and "Yes" / "No". Only your yes carries it out, and only when it is unchanged (checksum), still open and at most 24 hours old; the emergency stop and the release of the function apply as always. With "Add" you add to a running job up to three times; the sentence reaches the model in its next step as your own words. All of it also in the iPhone app (open the job); Android shows the panel page. Admin: Features → Jobs → "Jobs with plan, questions and proposals" (off), applies to every profile with agent functions, never to guests. Logs only fixed words: `agent: job plan | steps … | done …`, `agent: job asks`, `agent: job question answered|unanswered|refused`, `agent: job proposal saved|done|declined|failed|refused`, `agent: job steer taken|refused`. Self-tests `test_auftragsplan`.
- *Routines (level "also act" only):* "Remember as routine Evening: terrace light on, pool to 36 degrees." After "Yes", later "Evening" is enough. Every step is a Home Assistant sentence and runs like a spoken command (same allowlist, same code word); at most 8 steps and 20 routines.
- *Services over MCP:* below "Agent functions" the switch "Services over MCP". The admin enters name, address (Streamable HTTP) and token (login plus a fresh code; token in the vault, never sent back). Every tool is off until the admin sets it to "read" or "act", and the profiles are ticked per service. Reading answers count as outside text; acting tools only propose and run after the "Yes". If a service changes a tool's description or inputs, it is off again after "Fetch tools again". The Spark never starts a program itself; home network addresses only with the permission under Security.
- *Own MCP services (V01.0.363):* below "Services over MCP" the switch "Own MCP services for profiles". Every profile connects up to five services of its own under Me → My MCP services (fresh code, key encrypted) and releases every tool itself ("Read" or "Act after yes"). Optionally in background jobs (read only) and with sign-in (OAuth). The admin sees all of them under Apps and interfaces and can block each at once. Details: [APIs and integration](apis-and-integration.md).

**Chats with other Sparks (V01.0.496):** write to people on another Spark by their address (plan `plaene/spark-verbund.md`, stage V1: text chats for two). Admin: Features → Chats → "Chats with other Sparks" (off), below it this Spark's address from outside (e.g. `speech.example.de`, the name only, reachable over https; without it the function stays off) and "Who may connect": only paired Sparks (default) or every Spark like e-mail. Pairing: "Make a pairing code" (works one day and once, `spark.example.de#…`), give the code to the other Spark's admin, who enters it under "Code of another Spark"; both compare the key's fingerprint in the list "Connected Sparks" (there Allow, Block, Remove, number of contacts, in/out, waiting). No switch of its own in the profile: when the function is on, for everyone with Chats, Chats → ＋ New chat → "🌐 Someone on another Spark" shows the own Spark address (`name@spark.example.de`, with QR code and Share), a field for a request to an address and the contacts. A request to me shows on top of the chat list as "Contact requests" with Accept, Decline, Block; only after Accept does a message get through, Decline tells the other Spark nothing (no new request from there for 30 days), at most 20 open requests per person. The chat is a chat for two with 🌐: ticks (✓✓ means the other Spark has the message), read receipts, replies, reactions, change, take back; photos, files, voice, private chats and groups across Sparks come later. Technically: every Spark has its own Ed25519 key (derived from the Spark's key, it only moves with a move backup) and shows the public part at `/.well-known/spark-verbund`. Every message is an HTTPS POST to the other Spark's `/api/verbund/in`, signed together with the receiving domain, with a time (±10 minutes) and a one-time number; a different key later is refused. Outbound only to public addresses (Wächter), nothing during the emergency stop. When the other Spark is away, this one tries again after 1 and 5 minutes, then every hour, at most 7 days, then ⚠ "not delivered". What comes from another Spark is someone else's text: it never goes to the language model or a tool, the own speaker can read it out. The other Spark only learns address, display name and message and gets the same answer to every request, whether the person exists or not. Live (tile Chats): "🌐 Verbund: Sparks, contacts, in/out today, waiting"; logs (area "Messages and chats"): `verbund: sent`, `verbund: refused signature`, `verbund: retry`, never a domain, name or text. The service watch checks "Reachable for other Sparks" (the own address from outside). The iPhone app shows such chats with 🌐; asking and accepting work in the browser.
**Photos and files to other Sparks (V01.0.568):** Admin: Features → Chats → Chats with other Sparks → "Photos and files to other Sparks" (off, needs "Photos, files and voice in chats"). Then photos, files and voice messages go in chats with other Sparks too, with the same buttons. The own Spark sends the file first, alone and signed (a head with time, one-time number, SHA-256 and size in a header), to `/api/verbund/blob`, then the message with the hash. The other Spark takes a file only from an allowed Spark with a known key (checked before a byte is read), exactly with the size and hash of the head, at most 500 MB per Spark and day and 2 GB waiting, keeps it sealed until the message comes (at most a day) and makes it anew as if uploaded there: a photo drawn again (without place), a file recognised by its content (else nothing arrives), a voice message encoded again and written down by its own speech recognition. None of it goes to the language model. When the other Spark takes no files, the message shows ⚠ instead of waiting a week. Live: tile Chats "files today n in / n out"; logs: "verbund: file in | kb 812", "verbund: refused file limit", "verbund: files dropped | n 1", numbers only.

**Groups with other Sparks (V01.0.568):** Admin: Features → Chats → Chats with other Sparks → "Groups with other Sparks" (off, needs "Group chats"). Then a group's admins also pick their accepted contacts on other Sparks (marked 🌐) when creating it or under "Add people". The group belongs to the Spark it was created on: only there its admins change name and members, and it passes every message on to the other Sparks (once per Spark, signed, through the same queue as chats for two). On the other Sparks the group reads "managed on another Spark"; leaving works there, and the home Spark hears it. Text only for now; photos, editing and taking back do not work in these groups yet. At most 10 other Sparks per group. None of it goes to the language model; logs with numbers only ("verbund: group …"), Status → Live shows the number of groups from here and from others.

**Messages in chats (V01.0.488):** the old "Messages to others" (since V01.0.192, once a page "Messages" of its own under Me) moved completely into chats. Admin: Features → "Chats" (off), below it "Message to everybody" and "Announcements on speakers" (both off). Once released they work for every profile. Me → Conversation → Chats now also holds "Who may write to me" (every profile with chats or only picked ones, plus Blocked and the own call name), "New messages on my speaker" (nothing, only who wrote, who wrote and read the text on request) and "Message to everybody" (on from the start, can be switched off). Guests can neither send nor receive. On its first start the Spark moves each profile's old messages once into the chat with their sender (voice messages as 🎤 with their text, "to everybody" with that prefix) and takes over the chats; the old recordings are deleted afterwards. Gone are the page "Messages" under Me, `/api/messages…`, "Switch on for every profile", the filter "messages on/off" under People and devices and the iPhone app's own notifications with a reply field.
- Sending: "Tell Anna dinner is ready", "Write sb that I'll be late" or "Message to Ben: …". The panel reads recipient and text itself (fixed rules), searches only among the profiles that take messages from you, reads the message back and writes it into the chat only after a plain "Yes" (marked 🎙). When chats are still off for you, the assistant asks whether to switch them on; your "Yes" switches on only your own profile. When sending is not possible it names the reason.
- Message to everybody: "Tell everybody we leave at eight" goes into the fixed group "Alle". Everybody who did not switch off "Message to everybody" is in it; nobody adds or removes members, there are no admins. "Stop taking part" under ⓘ switches "Message to everybody" off for you. At most 5 messages to everybody per hour.
- On the speaker: when no page of the profile is open, the own speaker says a new chat message at once ("Nachricht von Anna."), else at the next wake word within two hours (at most 3). Never in quiet hours, never from muted chats, from private chats only who wrote. Since V01.0.544 it never reads the text into the room unasked (it speaks before any voice was checked): with "read the text on request" it adds "Sag „Lies die Nachricht vor“, dann lese ich sie dir vor.", and the text is read only to the profile's recognized voice (speaker ID on, voice taught; otherwise it leaves the offer out).
- Announcement: "Announcement in the living room: dinner is ready" (or "everywhere") after "Yes", or Chats → ＋ → "📢 Announcement": pick speakers, type the text. Only on speakers whose owner allows announcements; when one is not connected, at its next wake word within ten minutes.
- Reading and replying: "Do I have new messages?" reads unread chat messages as outside text, never as an instruction; "Reply to her: coming" and "Yes". What a message says locks everything that changes something for the rest of the answer and the next turn, and never goes into the memory.
- iPhone: "Message with Spark" (Siri) writes into the chat, "everybody" into the group "Alle"; the Today card is called "Chats" and counts unread chat messages. Needs the next TestFlight build.
- Logs (area "Messages and chats") and Live (tile "Chats": plus "Alle: N people" and "N read aloud") show numbers only. The "Who may write to me" lists (`messages.json`) are now part of the backup.

**Chats (V01.0.473):** Writing like in a messenger, everything on the Spark. Admin: Features → "Chats", below it "Group chats" (both off). Once released they work for every profile. Under Me → Conversation → Chats each profile sets "Read receipts" (on) and "Text in the notification" (off). Then the menu has "Chats" (on a phone the "Chats" tab with the number of unread messages): the list on the left, the chat on the right; on a phone two pages with "‹" back. Ticks: ✓ sent, ✓✓ arrived on a device, blue ✓✓ read (only between profiles that both have read receipts on). Tap a message: reaction (👍 ❤️ 😂 😮 😢 🙏), reply, copy, change (15 minutes), take back for all (1 hour). ⓘ: mute (1 hour, 8 hours, always), "messages disappear" (24 hours, 7 or 90 days; in groups the admins set it), clear the chat for me, in groups name, members, admins and leave. Only who the other person takes may write (Me → Conversation → Chats → "Who may write to me": from whom, blocked); the sender only hears "does not take messages from you right now", never why. "Tell Anna dinner is ready" lands in the chat with Anna (marked 🎙), "Do I have new messages?" reads unread chat messages as foreign text, never as an instruction. Texts, group names and previews are stored encrypted with AES-256-GCM under `users/_chats/` (key derived from the Spark's key, each message bound to its chat and number) and are part of the backup. An open Chats page gets changes at once over a connection that carries numbers only; without an open page a notification goes to every set-up device (iPhone, Android, companion, browser), only "💬 name" and "New message", never through Telegram, not when muted or in quiet hours. Limits: 4000 characters per message, 240 messages per hour, 50 people per group. When a profile is deleted, its messages disappear from every chat. Logs (area "Messages and chats") and Live (tile "Chats": number of chats, messages today, open connections, notifications) show numbers only. Private chats with end-to-end encryption and calls follow.

**Photos, files and voice in chats (V01.0.473):** Admin: Features → Chats → "Photos, files and voice in chats" (off). Then the chat shows 📎 on the left (photo or file) and 🎤 on the right (voice message: tap, speak, ➤ sends, "Discard" throws it away; after 5 minutes it sends by itself). Photos: JPEG, PNG or WebP up to 15 MB; the Spark reads them and saves them anew (at most 2048 pixels, preview 480 pixels), which drops place and camera data. Files: PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, OpenDocument and text (txt, csv, md) up to 25 MB, recognised by content, never by name; the name is cleaned and gets the extension of what the file really is. Files are only for download (never opened in the panel). Voice messages are stored as AAC (plays everywhere, iPhone included), speech recognition writes what was said underneath. Everything is stored encrypted (AES-256-GCM, bound to chat, message and part) under `users/_chats/<chat>/`, at most 2 GB per person; taken back, disappeared and deleted messages take their files with them. The file name travels in the request header, search words in the body (POST), so none of it lands in the access log. "Do I have new messages?" names photos and files only as "📷 Photo" or "📄 File". Live (tile "Chats") shows count and size, never names.

**Invite into a chat (V01.0.473):** Admin: Features → Chats → "Invite into a chat" (off) and People and devices → New people "only by invitation". Then Chats → ＋ New chat shows "Invite someone": a name as a suggestion, "Make invitation", QR code and link (copy or share). The invited person picks a name and PIN and lands in the chat with you at once ("… joined with your invitation"); chats are already on for them, both take messages from each other (unless someone is blocked). Each invitation works 7 days and once, at most 3 open per person; the admin sees them under New people and can withdraw them. When the switch is off or the admin blocks the inviting person, the link stops working. Like every profile, the new profile gets what the admin released, never a role; the second sign-in step applies as for every invitation.

**Private chats, end-to-end (V01.0.479):** Admin: Features → Chats → "Private chats (end-to-end)" (off). Then Chats → ＋ New chat shows "🔒 Private" beside every person. The first time, the device gets its own key (ECDH P-256, made in the browser, not exportable, in IndexedDB); that counts like a new device and needs the second step's code. The device encrypts every message itself (AES-256-GCM, the message key wrapped for every added device of both people, HKDF over ECDH); photos are drawn anew first (no place or camera data), files and voice messages go with their own key. The Spark stores only the encrypted form and cannot read it, the assistant only says "private message", the search finds nothing, notifications say "🔒 New message". Under ⓘ are the safety number (12 × 5 digits, compare once face to face) and "Your devices" with Remove; when someone adds or removes a device, a line shows in the chat. History for a new device (V01.0.550): when the person already has a device, the new one waits a day for the history and shows a 6-character code in the private chat. An older device shows "🔒 New device" on top of Chats with the same code and "Transfer" or "Not me" (takes the new device out at once). "Transfer" wraps each message's key once more for the new device on the old one; the Spark stores only that extra wrap (marked with the device that made it) and never changes the encrypted text. The code comes from the new device's public key: had the Spark slipped in another key, another code would show. The Spark takes wraps only from an older own device of the same person, once per new device and only within that day. Without an older device a new device only reads what comes after it. Needs https. Browser, Android app and since V01.0.654 the iPhone app (its key lives in the iPhone's Secure Enclave, the same encryption with CryptoKit, also the safety number and history transfer; the app key reaches the private chat paths only while the iPhone app is on, other device keys never). Live: tile Chats "🔒 private, devices, today, history transfer: n waiting"; logs: "private key added", "sent | kind priv", "private history shared | n 120", "private history refused", numbers only.
**Calls, voice and video (V01.0.556):** Admin: Features → Chats → "Calls (voice and video)" (off). Then every chat of two on this Spark (ordinary or 🔒 private, not with other Sparks) shows 📞 and 🎥 at the top. Every open Chats page of the other person rings, their devices get "📞 Name is calling"; they answer with or without video or decline, and their other devices stop ringing. Sound and picture go encrypted over WebRTC (DTLS-SRTP) straight from device to device; the Spark only brings the two together (offer, answer and network candidates, checked for form and size only, never stored or logged) and passes each signal only to the one device that holds the call on the other side. Fixed rules: both have Chats and Calls, the other person takes messages from the caller, nothing rings in their quiet hours or with "Do not disturb" (missed), whoever is on a call is busy, 20 calls per hour, 45 seconds of ringing, at most 4 hours. Every call leaves one line in the chat ("📞 Call · 4:12", "Missed call", "Call declined"). When the admin switches calls off, running calls end within a minute. Browser and Android app (1.3.1 and later with camera); the iPhone app since V01.0.652 with Apple's call screen (see iphone-app.md, "Voice and video calls"). Its iPhones ring through a VoIP push from Apple that carries only the call's random number; the app asks the Spark who calls (`GET /api/anrufe/info`, only for the two people in the call) and gets no second "is calling" notification. Live counts "rang on the iPhone". Running and ringing calls also show as connections "Anruf im Chat" and "Anruf klingelt" (only the count) on Live, the summary and the HDMI Steuerzentrale (since V01.0.712). Since V01.0.733 each call also shows there under devices as one entry from ringing to hanging up; since V01.0.747 with both sides: the devices ("Browser ↔ iPhone-App"), voice or video and ringing or connected ("Videoanruf · verbunden"), and both people ("Anna ↔ Tom"), each name only with that profile's consent (as for requests, else "Person"), never the chat. Calls to other Sparks later.
**Calls by the assistant (V01.0.803, `rufen.py`):** Admin: Features → Chats → "Calls by the assistant" (off, needs "Calls (voice and video)"). Then the assistant understands "Ruf Sabine an", "Videoanruf mit Sabine", "Call Sabine", "Video call Sabine", "Can you call Ben?". The panel reads name and kind (voice, video, phone) from the person's own words by fixed rules; there is no tool for the model, the answer comes without tools and the model only says the sentence. The name works as for messages (name or call name, several matches get the question with buttons) and only for someone who takes calls from the caller (`anrufe.why_not`). Only the "Yes" on the same device in the same conversation calls (the approval card shows "Call"); "Yes, with video" or "voice only" changes the kind, anything else drops the proposal. After the yes the panel opens the chat of the two; in the browser, the Android app (page) and the iPhone app the usual call starts right on the device (event `anruf`). Asked at a speaker, on the phone, over Telegram (with personal data), at the watch, with Siri, CarPlay or the companion, the call waits three minutes on the Spark: every device of the profile gets "📞 Sabine anrufen" (at most 6 per hour, never over Telegram), and the page or app that opens next shows "Sabine anrufen? · Anrufen · Nicht jetzt" at the bottom (iPhone: a question when opened). A tap on "Anrufen" starts it, "Nicht jetzt" removes it on every device. "By phone" (3a): when the name fits no profile or the person says "übers Telefon"/"by phone", the iPhone app calls with the iPhone contacts as before (`iphone_action`); on Android the panel looks the name up in the own address book (Contacts → Settings, exactly one contact with a number, the mobile number first) and after the yes opens the phone app with the number (event `waehlen`, Android app 1.4.2 and later); only the tap on the green button dials. Never for guests, someone else's voice or under the Notaus. Spark diary: "Call started". Live, tile Chats: "per Assistent heute … gefragt, … gestartet, … aufs Handy, … übers Telefon, … abgelehnt" (numbers only). Journal: `chats: call assistant proposed|started here|sent to devices|phone app opened|declined|refused | why …`, never a name or a number.
**Calls when away:** Admin: Features → Chats → "Calls when away" (off) and the address from outside (empty: the address for other Sparks). Then the Spark runs its own relay (coturn, unit speech-spark-turn, only while the switch is on; the distribution's coturn service stays masked). In the router forward UDP 3478 and UDP 49160 to 49200 to the Spark. Each device gets an access per call that expires after an hour (TURN REST, secret derived from the Spark's key); the relay only passes packets to public addresses, never into the home network or to the Spark itself, without console, with limits per person and in total, without log. Status → Services shows "Relay for calls". Live: tile Chats "📞 Calls: running, ringing, today, relay on/off"; logs: "call started | video 1", "call answered | secs 4", "call ended | secs 252", "call not answered | why rejected", "call refused | why limit", "call relay started", numbers and fixed words only. The relay takes the address from outside only as a public IPv4 address (an extra IPv6 record of the name, often an fd… address for the home network only, no longer gets in the way, since V01.1.040). When the Spark cannot find it for a moment, a running relay keeps its last address ("call relay kept | why not found", Live "alte Adresse"); otherwise Live names the reason ("Adresse von außen zeigt ins Heimnetz", "Notaus: kein Internet", "Adresse von außen nicht gefunden") and the log says it once per reason, "call relay refused | why home address", no longer every minute; when it is back: "call relay back".

**No envelope in the assistant any more (V01.0.479):** the envelope beside the input and the "Message" button above the conversation are gone (Dominik: chats replace them). You write under Chats; "Tell Anna …" still goes into the chat. Since V01.0.488 the page "Messages" under Me is gone too (see above).

**Many profiles (V01.0.206):** under People and devices the admin searches by name, call name or device, filters (not used for 90 days, without a device, with/without second step), sorts (name, last used, newest) and pages through 25 at a time. "Details" shows each profile's devices, last use, call name, change PIN, reset second step and delete; deleting now needs the fresh code like changing the PIN. The device list shows only the devices of the profiles shown,. Since V01.0.257 the profile of an own device key (script, shortcut) and of a new device is picked from a list of all profiles instead of typing a name; the iPhone app, speakers, Pebble watch and Home Assistant keys are set up by each profile itself under Me, show "🔒 fixed" with where they are managed, and can only be deleted here (the server refuses moving them too). "Last used" counts the browser login (without the address) and device keys. **Call name:** optional per profile ("Tom", "Dad"), set by the admin in the details or by the profile under Me → Conversation → Chats; unique among all names and call names. **Same names:** when several profiles fit the spoken name (two "Thomas"), the assistant asks "Thomas Müller or Thomas Meier?" (at most 4); the answer ("Müller", "the first", call name) picks only among these, then comes the usual "Ja". When more than 4 fit it asks for the full name. An unknown name gets at most 3 similar ones, never the whole list. "To everybody" names at most 4 and "and N more".

**Home Assistant Assist over Wyoming (V01.0.100):** Home Assistant uses the Spark's speech recognition and voice (Voice PE, HA app, ESPHome speakers). Admin: Features → "Home Assistant Assist (Wyoming)" on and enter Home Assistant's address (Wyoming has no password; with no address listed the Spark answers nobody). Since V01.0.106 the field suggests the address from a profile's Home Assistant setup when switched on (home network addresses only; behind a reverse proxy enter the real HA address by hand) and shows addresses refused lately. In HA: integration "Wyoming Protocol", host = the Spark, port 31003; in the voice assistant pick "spark" for speech-to-text and text-to-speech. The service runs inside the panel, so it needs the full install. Since V01.0.334 Status → Live shows every transcription and every spoken answer for Home Assistant as a request of the device "Home Assistant" (way Hearing → text to Home Assistant, or Speaking → back), refused senders under Entrance; never the words. Since V01.0.603 the Spark answers Home Assistant's "describe" at once with the voice list from memory (read again in the background every 10 minutes): HA waits only 1 second for it, and a busy voice engine otherwise made it report "Unable to get info".

**Calling the Spark (phone, stage T1, V01.0.626, plan `plaene/telefon-anruf.md`):** Call the Spark from a phone in the house (DECT handset of the FRITZ!Box, IP phone) and talk to it like to an own speaker. The Spark signs in at the telephone system as an IP phone (`app/panel/telefon.py`, a small SIP part of its own in the panel, no foreign software): REGISTER with digest sign-in (MD5 or SHA-256), incoming calls with INVITE, ACK, BYE, CANCEL, OPTIONS and INFO; sound as RTP in G.722 (HD, 16 kHz) or G.711 (a-law, µ-law), own code in `app/panel/telefon_ton.py`; keys after RFC 4733. Admin: *Features → Phone* on; below, a wizard in four steps: 1. pick the system or "Search the home network" (asks `fritz.box`, `speedport.ip` and the gateway with SIP OPTIONS, home network only), 2. the clicks to add the Spark there as an IP phone, with a suggested user name and password to copy, 3. enter the access (address, port, user name, password in the vault; saving asks for the code, level "confirm") and "Check connection" with a plain answer instead of an error code ("User name or password wrong", "The system does not answer" …), 4. call the Spark's internal number from a handset (FRITZ!Box usually \*\*620): the calling number shows up and is assigned to a profile or as a "Shared phone". The systems' templates (FRITZ!Box, Telekom Speedport, Gigaset, Asterisk/FreePBX, 3CX, UniFi Talk, other SIP system) are in `app/telefonanlagen.json`; a new system is a new entry. A call: "Hallo Anna, hier ist der Spark.", question, answer (Weiche and chat as at a speaker, short answers without lists), listening again; after 20 seconds of silence "Noch etwas?", after another 10 seconds it hangs up; "Stopp" or the key # interrupts the answer; a goodbye ("Tschüss", "Danke, das war's", "Ich lege auf", also in the middle of an answer) and right after "Noch etwas?" a "Nein danke" end the call: the Spark says "Tschüss!" and hangs up itself (fixed word lists, never the model, V01.0.791). When the phone hangs up, its BYE ends the call at once; if none arrives (router, line), the Spark hangs up after 8 seconds without any sound packet from the phone, unless the phone put the call on hold (log `telefon: kein ton mehr vom telefon seit 8 s, aufgelegt`); at most 15 minutes. So the voice does not sound choppy (V01.0.824): the sound out goes from a thread of its own at a 20 ms beat, also while the panel is busy; speech starts only once 200 ms are ready or the sentence is complete, and after every gap it buffers more (at most 0.8 s); G.722 takes about half the computing time it took before (same bits). The log line `telefon: aufgelegt nach …` names gaps, late packets and `kodieren … ms je sekunde ton` at the end. Fixed rules decide who is calling: a handset that belongs to a profile uses that profile, but personal things only when the speaker recognition clearly knows the profile's voice (as at a shared speaker); at the shared phone the voice picks the profile for each question, else questions and answers as a guest; a handset nobody assigned only hears that it belongs to no profile yet (with *Phone: take guests* it gets questions and answers as a guest, never a tool); calls from outside are refused in this stage. One call at a time (up to 2), another caller hears "busy". Sound is never stored; questions and answers are in the profile's history ("Telefon DD.MM."). Status → Live shows the telephone system with signed in yes/no and today's numbers, Logs the area "telefon" (only internal numbers like \*\*611, never a number from outside, profiles through `profiles.log_name`), the Dienst-Wächter reports a lost sign-in. Self-test `test_telefon` with a made-up telephone system.

**Me → Phone (phone, stage T2, V01.0.627):** Each profile sets up under *Me → Phone* what belongs to it (`app/panel/telefon_ich.py`, file `users/<id>/telefon.json`, numbers and PIN in the vault). *My phone numbers*: add mobile and landline (at most 5; adding asks for the code, level "confirm"). A number counts only once it is confirmed: the page shows a code of 4 digits (valid 15 minutes), you call the Spark from that number and type it; after three wrong ones it is void. Only for this does the Spark take a call from outside in stage T2, otherwise it still refuses it. So nobody can put someone else's number on their profile, and a number belongs to at most one profile (`0170…` and `+49170…` count as the same). *Phone PIN*: 4 to 8 digits, not all the same and not in a row; changing only with a fresh code (level "change protection"). On the phone you type it and then #: on the own handset it stands for the voice for the rest of the call, at the shared phone it picks the profile it belongs to. Three wrong ones in 15 minutes lock the PIN at that phone for 30 minutes, the handset's profile gets a notification. The PIN is a salted PBKDF2 hash, never spoken, shown, given to the language model or written to the log; when the Spark does not clearly know the voice at the own handset, it says on personal questions that the PIN helps. *My handsets* shows the handsets the admin assigned, *My calls* time, length, kind and number of questions of each call (no number, no words), deletable one by one or all. The page is in `features.ME_PAGES` and so also in the iPhone and Android app. Live counts right and wrong PINs and confirmed numbers, Logs only fixed words (`telefon: pin richtig …`, `telefon: nummer bestaetigt …`, never a number, code or PIN).

**Calls from outside (phone, stage T3, V01.0.628):** With *Features → Phone → Calls from outside* (default off) the Spark takes calls the telephone system puts through to it from outside (in the FRITZ!Box choose the numbers the IP phone "Spark" rings for). Fixed rules in `telefon.Call.outside`: a number a profile confirmed under *Me → Phone* picks that profile; because a shown number can be forged, personal things only with a sure voice or after the phone PIN, and the greeting names nobody. Unknown or hidden numbers are refused; with *Unknown callers as guests* (the same switch as for handsets not assigned) they get questions and answers as a guest, never a tool, at most 20 a day; a PIN makes no profile out of them. If the number's profile is blocked, the call is refused. Logs write only "telefon: anruf extern bekannt als …", "… unbekannt als gast" or "… abgelehnt", never the number; Live counts the calls from outside and the guests, *My calls* shows them as "From outside". Fixed on the way: the phone function's switches were read from the wrong part of the settings, so the Spark never signed in at the system since V01.0.626.

**Calls in Live, Summary and the control centre (V01.0.731):** Every phone call stands as one request under the devices from picking up to hanging up, as "Telefon" (with the handset's name when the profile allows names in Live), reason "Anruf" or "Spark ruft an". Never a number. The questions in the call are requests of their own but since V01.0.931 stand on their call's card (one card per call, e.g. "db · Anruf · 2 questions"), their ways start there; before, each question got its own "Telefon" card, so one call looked like three phones. The list below still shows them one by one.

**Troubleshooting the phone (V01.0.712):** *A router between the Spark and the PBX:* if both are in different networks and a router translates the address in between (NAT, e.g. FRITZ!Box → UniFi Dream Machine → home network), the Spark sees it in the answer to its sign-in (Via "received"), signs in again with the router's address and names it for the sound too (log `telefon: nat zwischen spark und anlage`). The wizard under Features → Phone then says what the router needs: forward UDP 31060 and 31062-31069 to the computer with the panel, best only from the PBX's address. Without these forwards no call from outside reaches the Spark, and a test call rings but has no sound. Every call ends in the log with `telefon: aufgelegt nach … s, … fragen (reason), ton raus N pakete, davon M mit sprache, rein K pakete`. If M is zero, the speech output gave nothing (before it stands `telefon: sprachausgabe` with the kind of error or `leer`); if K is zero, the PBX sends no sound to the Spark. If the PBX refuses a call of the Spark, its answer code is logged (`telefon: anruf raus antwort 403`). If the PBX describes the sound only in the early answer (183) and the 200 OK comes without one, the Spark takes the one from the 183.
