**The Spark calls (phone, stage T4, V01.0.632):** With *Features → Phone → The Spark calls* (default off) the Spark places calls itself (`app/panel/telefon_anruf.py` decides with fixed rules, `telefon.Call.dial` dials: INVITE with digest sign-in, ringing up to 30 seconds, else CANCEL). *Wake-up call and reminder by call*, only ordered by the profile itself: "Call me tomorrow at 7" or "Remind me by call at 3 pm about the dentist" (tool `anruf_planen`, "Have the Spark call"; it only gets time, kind and text, never a number). The wake-up call says "Guten Morgen, hier ist der Spark. Es ist 7 Uhr.", then you can keep talking; a reminder is read only once the voice fits or the phone PIN was typed (if there is neither a voice nor a PIN to check, at once, it is the own phone), else it comes as a note. *Call back*: whoever calls from on the go with an unsure voice can say "Ruf mich zurück" instead of the PIN; the Spark hangs up and calls the stored, confirmed number that matches the call. A forged number thus never reaches the forger, and the call back counts like the PIN for that call. *Call on outages* (its own switch below): if a service of the service watch is gone 10 minutes or longer, the Spark calls the admin besides the note, at most once an hour, never between 10 pm and 7 am (the same time as "Only collect at night", always for calls), in the admin's quiet hours or during "Do not disturb"; if the service is still gone afterwards, the call comes then (log `telefon: alarm in ruhezeit, nur push` and `telefon: alarm nach ruhezeit angerufen`). *Whom*: the profile's assigned handset; with *Whom the Spark calls → also confirmed own numbers* else the confirmed number (needed for the call back). Never a number from the conversation, a mail or the language model. *How often*: at most 5 calls per profile and day, never during "Do not disturb" (ordered calls also ring in the own quiet hours, that time was asked for); not answered once more after 5 minutes, then a note. Every profile can switch off "The Spark may call me" under *Me → Phone → Calls from the Spark* and sees and deletes the planned calls there; *My calls* shows them as "The Spark called". In wizard step 4 "Spark calls" calls an internal number with a test sentence. Live counts calls out, answered ones, call backs, alarm calls and planned ones; logs name kind, profile and result, never a number from outside or a reminder text. A confirmation call to a number not yet confirmed does not come on purpose: the Spark would otherwise call any number typed in; confirming still works by a call from that number.

**Weather place:** postcode and place ("76307 Karlsbad") work; the name is searched in Germany and the postcode compared. When several places fit, the others can be picked; coordinates ("48.87, 8.50") work too.

**Web search:** switch on "Websuche" under Features and enter the address of your SearXNG instance right below it. If SearXNG's `settings.yml` allows `json` under `search: formats:`, the panel uses the JSON API, otherwise it reads the normal results page; "Test connection" shows whether it works. The LLM then searches by itself when a question needs current information (the LLM server needs tool calling; qwen38 has it on). The assistant says it is looking it up, reads the top pages and shows the sources under the answer. Since V01.0.130 the search is never silently missing: when it is off or has no address, the assistant says so, and the log has a line "chat: web search …" for every question (offered, off, no address, locked). Asking in so many words ("such im Internet", "google mal", "recherchier", "schau online nach", "search the web") always goes through the search. After reading a mail the search stays locked for the rest of that answer (a mail could otherwise carry its content to the web). Since V01.0.194 an answer from mail (also a mail note the assistant made by itself) stays in the next turn only when the new question is about it ("what else does she write?", "read the first one"); then the search stays locked and the assistant says so. Any other question ("what is going on in politics today?") leaves the mail answer out and searches.

**Wake word "Hey Spark":** with the checkbox below the chat the assistant keeps listening. Say "Hey Spark" and it listens; "Hey Spark, what's the weather?" in one go is taken as the question. The browser detects speech bursts, the speech recognition on the Spark checks for the phrase, nothing goes to the internet. The screen stays on; on a phone keep the app in the foreground.

**The "Me" window:** since V01.0.125 in the same groups as the Features page (conversation, my data, my day, mail and contacts, home, on the go and devices, access); on a computer it starts with an *Overview* (the state of each page, e.g. "2 calendars connected"), on a phone the state sits right below each entry of the list. Notifications and the daily briefing have their own page *Notifications*. the button with your name (or "Guest") at the top of the history and the settings icon below the face open the same window. On the left is a short list: Conversation and, logged in, Memory, Log and Security, plus, depending on the features switched on, Documents, Calendar, E-mail, Smart home, Voice, Von selbst (speaking up by itself) and Raum-Modus (room mode); as a guest it shows "Sign in". Conversation holds listening (stop on silence, live transcript, barge-in and its sensitivity), answer (voice, profiles only; speaking rate, answer length) and display. Guests always hear the default voice. Logged in, the settings live in the profile on the Spark and apply on every device, including speakers with a device key; as a guest only in the browser. "Hey Spark" is set per device because it keeps the microphone and screen awake. Defaults for guests and new profiles are under Settings → Defaults. The speaking rate keeps the pitch; it also works for other apps that send `speed` to the TTS API (vllm-omni backend).

**Own documents:** logged-in profiles upload their own files in the "Me" window (PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, OpenDocument, text, Markdown, HTML, CSV, e-mail as .eml; up to 20 MB each, at most 200 per profile). When asked about them, the assistant searches them and names the document with its page below the answer. Guests cannot upload anything, and no profile sees another's documents. Can be turned off under Features.

**Knowledge from uploads (V01.0.224, plan `plaene/wissen-aus-uploads.md`):** a profile's documents now live in one SQLite file `users/<profile>/wissen.db` (documents, text pieces, FTS5 full-text index over the same rough word stems as before, meaning vectors, pages still to be read). Older documents (`docs/<id>.json`) are taken over the first time they are opened, also after restoring an old backup. The backup copies the file with SQLite's backup function, so it is complete even while being written. Under Me → Documents "View" shows a document's stored text with its pages (and a kept original: pictures and PDFs in the browser, everything else only as a download), every document has a switch "the assistant searches it", "Try the search" shows the hits without the language model, and the sources below an answer can be clicked. Three switches below "Own documents", each off by default, only the admin under Features; once released they work for every profile (admin defaults never switch them on, never guests):
- *Read pictures and scans:* photos (JPEG, PNG, WebP) and PDF pages without text (the page's biggest picture) are cleaned like chat pictures (format check, at most 40 megapixels, upright, 1600 pixels, no location or camera data) and wait in the database. The language model reads one page after another and briefly describes what it shows, with no tools and only after a minute without anyone talking to the assistant (`wissen.quiet()`, where the speech priority rule hooks in); at most 30 pages per document, 200 waiting pages and 100 pages read per profile and day, two tries per page. Text in a picture stays information, never a command. In the assistant, "Picture to My documents" below the answer keeps an attached picture; in Telegram `/merken` as an answer to a photo or as its caption (also needs "Photos over Telegram").
- *Meaning search:* multilingual-e5-small (quantized ONNX, about 120 MB) runs on the CPU in its own low-priority process (`docembed.py`, `embed_worker.py`; since V01.0.801 on at most half of the cores and as `SCHED_IDLE` only in time nobody else needs, so speech and the panel come first while many pieces are caught up; since V01.0.816 a partial index finds the pieces without a vector instead of reading the whole table for each batch), starts only with at least 8.5 GB of free memory, takes about 300 MB and ends after 10 minutes without work. Work means only new pieces and questions to the document search; "Search locally first" uses the model only when it runs anyway and does not extend the 10 minutes (V01.0.444). Status → Summary shows a green "working" only while it computes, otherwise a green "standby" (loaded with its memory, or not loaded), plus what last needed it and in how many minutes it stops; the check also runs during a Notaus. Pieces get a vector in quiet moments; the search merges full-text and meaning ranks. A question waits at most 8 seconds for its vector, otherwise it searches full text only. The model comes once from Hugging Face; the files' checksums are then kept in `state/doc-embed.json` and other files are refused. Fixed packages: onnxruntime 1.22.1, tokenizers 0.22.1.
- *Keep originals:* the file stays under `users/<profile>/docs/files/` (pictures cleaned), up to the space per profile (`chat.doc_quota_mb`, 50 to 5000, default 500); when it is full only the text is kept. Opening works only as a download (`Content-Disposition: attachment`, HTML as `application/octet-stream`), never as a page of the panel.
Security: document text is outside text (`READS_OUTSIDE`): wrapped as data, actions and memory locked, never learned. New: after `document_search` the web search is locked for the rest of that answer too (a document could otherwise carry its content away). File names go to the model only quoted, shortened and without data markers. API: `GET /api/profile/wissen`, `PUT /api/profile/wissen/{id}` (`use`), `GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/file` (`?view=1` for pictures and PDFs), `GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/text` (also for the iPhone app under My things → Documents), `POST /api/profile/wissen/search`, `POST /api/profile/wissen/picture`; all need a profile login, changes only from the own browser, rate limit `doc`. Self-tests: `app/tests/test_wissen.py`.

**Document status (V01.0.228):** under Status → Summary the card “Own documents” appears once the admin switches on “Read pictures and scans” or “Meaning search” (`GET /api/admin/wissen`, admin login only). It shows the meaning model's state (off, starting, running with its memory in MiB, waiting for 8.5 GiB free memory, error with reason), how many pieces already have a meaning vector, how many pages still wait to be read, how many were read today and when last. These are sums over all profiles, never profile or document names or contents.

**Progress (V01.0.232):** under Me → Documents every document still being read shows "page 3 of 12 read" with a bar and why it is not moving right now: "reading page 4 now", "waits for a quiet minute" (someone talks to the assistant), "daily limit reached, continues tomorrow" or "Read pictures and scans is off". With meaning search it then shows "meaning 40 of 120" (and whether the model waits for free memory). While something runs and the list is open, it refreshes every 10 seconds. Everyone sees only their own documents. The iPhone app shows "page x of y read".

**Shared documents (V01.0.236):** with the admin switch *Features → Own documents → Shared documents* (off by default, released for every profile) every finished document gets "For everyone". A ticked document stays in its owner's database and is not read again; every other profile that may use the function finds it in its search (source "…, geteilt von name") and sees it under "Shared for everyone by others" with "View". Only the owner takes the sharing back, changes or deletes the document; the admin sees under Status → Summary what is shared and can take it back. Guests, Telegram without "personal" and voices a shared speaker does not recognize as its owner get no shared documents. Their text stays outside text like any document. Agents still search only their own documents.

**Sharing with profiles (V01.0.866, plan `teilen-profile/plan.md`, stage 1):** admin switch *Features → Sharing with profiles* (`chat.share`, off by default; once released for every profile, guests never). Every finished own document then has „Share“: a dialog with a person search, *Off* or *See* per profile, plus „All profiles“ (the former „For everyone“, with *Shared documents*). Saving needs a fresh code. The other profile finds the document under Me → Documents (source „From others“), in its search and on the new page **Me → Shared**; for its assistant it is outside text like any document. Me → Shared shows everything in one place in two tabs: *Shared with me* (documents, calendars and lists of others, „new“ since the last visit, Open) and *Shared by me* (per person „Take back“, for documents „Change“; taking back works at once). The data always stays with the owner, there is no copy; calendars and lists are still shared on their own page, they hang on `geteilt.py` as adapters. *Note on shared things* (Me → Shared, on by default): a note „Name shared something with you“ to the recipient's devices, at most one every 10 minutes, more are collected. Limits: 50 people per document, 500 singly shared documents per profile. Stored in `users/<profile>/geteilt.json`. Live (tile „Shared“) and logs (area messages, `geteilt: …`) show numbers only. Next: notes, Wissensbuch pages, pictures and music (stage 2), expiry and message (stage 3), sharing by voice and from the iPhone (stage 4).

**Complete scans and reading again (V01.0.238):** scanned PDF pages (less than 40 characters of text) are rendered as whole pages with pypdfium2 (fixed version 4.30.0, about 30 MB of memory for a moment per page) and handed to the language model. Up to V01.0.236 the Spark only took the biggest embedded picture; scanners storing a page as strips or JBIG2 lost pages that way. Up to 300 scanned pages per document are read (30 before), still at most 100 per profile and day; the rest follows on the next days, up to 600 pages may wait per profile. Pages from knowledge folders (NAS) and own pages (upload, app, Telegram, notebooks) count apart, up to 600 each, and own pages are read first (V01.0.799): a big knowledge folder no longer blocks an upload. If the own queue is full after all, the message says how many pages wait and what helps (upload again later or delete a document that is still being read). The document names any limit that applies ("only the first 300 of 412 pages without text", "2 pages without a readable picture skipped", "pages without text not read (Read pictures and scans is off)"). "Read again" under Me → Documents reads a document once more from its kept original (owner only, only with "Keep originals"); place, "For everyone" and "The assistant searches it" stay. A document attached to a question in the iPhone app still takes along only its first 20,000 characters; the answer then says it is only the beginning and that under "My documents" the whole text is searched.

**Read long documents at night (V01.0.240):** admin switch under *Features → Own documents → Read pictures and scans* (`chat.doc_night`, off) with a night window (`doc_night_from`/`doc_night_to`, default 01:00 to 06:00, may cross midnight). By day the Spark reads in quiet moments only documents with at most 10 waiting pages; longer ones only after 30 minutes without a question, or in the night window. At night pages are counted apart, up to 400 per profile and night on top of the daily limit of 100. Speech still comes first (vorrang.py). Me → Documents names the reason: "long document, read at night from 01:00" or "daily limit reached, continues at night from 01:00".

**Docling server (V01.0.499, plan `plaene/docling-anbinden.md`):** sub-switch "Docling server" under *Features → Own documents* (`chat.docling`, off by default; works only with an address). A [docling-serve](https://github.com/docling-project/docling-serve) on another machine in the home network reads uploaded PDFs, Office files and pictures a second time in quiet moments, with tables, columns and OCR; its Markdown replaces the text the Spark read itself (pieces, meaning vectors and the summary card are made again, the document says "mit Docling gelesen"). Scanned pages go to Docling first: pages where it found at least 40 characters are no longer read by the language model; the rest waits for the language model as before. Set up by the main admin only, with a fresh code: address `https://…` or `http://…` (http only to an address in the home network, with a warning in the panel; checked before every call, also after name resolution), API key (`DOCLING_SERVE_API_KEY`, stored sealed, never shown; a new address asks for it again), with its own certificate "Check certificate" as for the offsite backup (compare the fingerprint, accept exactly that one). "Read sample" sends a built-in sample page with a table and shows time and text, never a profile's file. The server gets only the file, named `dokument.<ext>`, never the profile, the file name or a question. Applies to all profiles once the admin switches it on; each profile can switch "Read with Docling" off for itself under Me → Documents (`doc_docling`, on by default, no admin preset), guests never. Until Docling has read it the file stays in `users/<profile>/docling-wait/` (also when originals are not kept); if the server does not answer the Spark asks again 10 minutes later, after 2 days it gives up and the language model reads. Switching off (admin or profile) lets waiting files go at once. Live shows "Docling-Server" with waiting and today's files (numbers only), the service watch asks `/health`, logs under Knowledge (`docling: …`), protection level "Data" for saving. Starting the server (other machine, Docker): `docker run -d --name docling -p 5001:5001 -e DOCLING_SERVE_API_KEY=<long random key> quay.io/docling-project/docling-serve-cpu:v1.37.0`; with a GPU the image `docling-serve-cu128`. For https put a Caddy or nginx in front.

**Size and space (V01.0.242):** PDFs may be up to 100 MB; the admin sets 20 to 300 MB under *Features → Own documents → Largest PDF file* (`chat.doc_max_mb`); all other kinds of files stay at 20 MB. Uploads that are too big are refused before they are read; the page says so before uploading. The "space per profile" (`chat.doc_quota_mb`, default 500 MB) now counts all uploads of a profile together (database with text, meaning vectors and waiting pages plus originals). When it is full, the Spark takes nothing new; originals are kept only while there is room. Me → Documents shows "Used: 120 MB of 500 MB" with a bar. Under Status → Summary the admin sees per profile how much is used and gives single profiles their own value (50 to 50,000 MB, `state/doc-quotas.json`, in the backup).

**Read on (V01.0.243):** "View" shows the stored text in parts of about 200,000 characters; "Read on" fetches the next part (`GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/text?start=`). Only the view is split, the search always uses the whole text (up to 2 million characters per document).

**Document card and tags (V01.0.245):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Document card and tags* (`chat.doc_brief`, off; released for every profile). In quiet minutes (speech priority applies) the language model reads the beginning of each finished document once (up to 3,000 characters, wrapped as outside text) and notes title, kind (contract, invoice, insurance, manual …), sender, date, deadline, number and 3 to 6 tags; at most 200 a day, existing documents bit by bit without being read again. The fields are checked (length, characters, date YYYY-MM[-DD], kind from a fixed list). Instead of file names the assistant sees one line per document (with many, the 30 that fit the question best, plus all tags) and can narrow `document_search` with `tags` or `art`; when no passage matches it gets the fitting cards. Under *Me → Documents* title, kind, deadline and tags show at the document; tags filter the list (also the ones others share), "Tags" sets own ones (empty = the automatic ones again; owner only). "View" shows "Belongs with": documents with the same number, the same sender or two tags in common. With the profile switch *Fristen anbieten* (`doc_due`, off) a deadline gets a "Remind" button (six weeks before at 9:00, else the day before); the assistant never sets a reminder from a deadline in a document by itself. Also `document_search` now always stays offered in a narrowed tool set, and more words (insurance, policy, warranty, manual, notice, cancellation period, tax, doctor's letter, findings, receipt, certificate …) count as a question about documents.

**Search check (V01.0.516, plan *suche-schaerfen*, stage 1):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Search check* (`chat.search_check`, off), below it *Test questions by the Spark* (`chat.search_check_auto`, off) and *Test questions per day and profile* (`chat.search_check_day`, 5 to 100, default 20); no profile switch, never for guests. Each profile keeps up to 300 test questions with a known right place (`users/<id>/suchpruefung.json`, in the backup): added by hand under *Me → Documents → Search check → Add a test question*, from an answer with "Name the right place" below its sources line, or by the language model in quiet minutes (`vorrang`, at most 3 per document). A question by the model is outside text and must keep fixed rules (one line, 8 to 160 characters, a question mark, no links, no command words, never four words in a row from the piece, at most 75 % of its words from the piece), else it is dropped. Once a night (2 to 5 o'clock, from 5 questions) or with "Check now" (at most every 10 minutes) every question goes through the same search the assistant uses (`documents.search`), once words only, once meaning only (only with meaning search) and once mixed; noted are the place of the right document among the first 30, whether the right page was among the first 5, and the time (median and slowest 10 %). The profile sees the shares among the first 5 and first, the history of the last runs and "Found badly" with "Try" (the first 5 hits now). The admin sees only sums over all profiles under *Status → Checks → Check the search*, the live overview the number of questions and the share; the journal fixed words and numbers only. The fixed test bank `app/tests/data/suchbank.json` (at first 15 invented papers, 45 questions; since V01.0.768 20 papers and 10 multi-document questions) keeps the word search at no less than 80 % among the first 5 in `test_suchpruefung` (measured 84 %).

**Context in the sections (V01.0.519, plan *suche-schaerfen*, stage 2, no switch):** documents are cut at their headings: Markdown headings (Docling, .md), "§ 3 …", "TOP 2 …", "Artikel 4", numbered lines like "2.1 Kosten" and short lines of their own over a paragraph (fixed rules in `documents.heading`, never a model). Inside a section it stays at about 900 characters with 150 overlap; small sections go with the next one; a Markdown table stays one piece up to 2,400 characters. Text without headings is cut exactly as before. Every piece gets a context line `chunks.head` from title (the card's, else the file name), kind and the headings above it (`chunks.part`). The word search looks at the context lines in a second index (`ftshead`) with weight 0.4 next to the piece's own text; the meaning search embeds context line and piece together. When the line changes (a new card), the piece's vector is computed again in quiet moments. Existing documents get their lines in quiet minutes (20 documents per step, no model, log line `wissen: kontext gebaut | N dokumente`, an event in the live overview); Me → Documents shows how many still wait. The model sees the section in the source line ("Vertrag.txt, Seite 2, Abschnitt § 4 Kaution"), cut to 60 characters and without brackets and quotes, because it is outside text. The test bank rose from 84 % to 89 % among the first 5; `test_suchpruefung` now asks for at least 85 %.

**Knowledge question instead of Spark help, only fitting passages (V01.1.131, Dominik 2026-10-26, Frauenkirche):** "Was kannst du mir zur Frauenkirche in Dresden erzählen?" (what can you tell me about …) went to the Spark help because of the words "was kannst du"; the answer came from the model's memory, with the list of example sentences below. Now "what can you tell me about/on X …" counts as a question about X (`hilfe.ABOUT`), unless X names the Spark, its panel or one of its functions; the example list only comes with "What can you do?" (`hilfe.can_ask`). With "look in the archive or Kiwix" the question goes to the own archive. `document_search` only passes on passages holding at least 60 % of the search words (the same rule as "Look locally first", `lokal.cover`; filler words like "information", "look", "archive", "Kiwix" do not count). If all drop out so, the model gets a fixed sentence: nothing fitting, say so, do not present other passages as an answer and look in the archive, Wikipedia or the web if offered (`chat_tools.NOTHING_FITS`); log line `wissen: dokumentsuche | N stellen ohne die suchwörter weggelassen`. Both sentences are in the Weiche's test bench; self-test `test_wissensfrage`.

**Tables in PDFs row by row (V01.0.972, Dominik 2026-10-23 "1 und 2", `pdftabellen.py`, no switch):** Asked "Which Sanjeevini card can I take for diarrhoea?" the Spark named a wrong card, because pypdf reads a table cell by cell, each cell on its own line, and which card belongs to which complaint got lost. Now the panel also looks at every PDF page with pdfium (only the drawn lines and boxes, about a tenth of pypdf's time): horizontal lines, cell frames or shaded rows give a table's rows, vertical lines or gaps in the text its columns, and each cell gets the text from its own box, whether it sits at the top, middle or bottom. Each row becomes one line `| Durchfall | SR 18 … | Hinweis |`. A table up to 2,400 characters stays one piece; a longer one is cut only between its rows, and every piece starts with its head row (`documents._rows`). A page without a drawn table stays as pypdf read it; after 20 seconds per document the remaining pages stay so too. PDFs already stored are looked at one by one in quiet minutes (`documents.due_tables`, column `docs.tablev`): if the original is kept (*Features → Own documents → Keep originals*), the PDF was read as text (not page by page by the language model, not by Docling, which keeps tables itself) and is at most 20 MB, it is read again when it has a table; log line `wissen: tabellen geprüft | neu gelesen`, `keine tabelle` or `original fehlt`, event "Tabellen neu lesen" in the live overview; Me → Documents shows how many still wait. Without the original only uploading it again helps. A PDF is outside text as before; the limits (at most 4,000 drawn parts and 600 lines per page, 20 seconds per document) keep a malicious PDF small. Self-test `test_pdftabellen`.

**Re-rank hits (V01.0.520, plan *suche-schaerfen*, stage 3):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Re-rank hits* (`chat.rerank`, off; no profile switch, never for guests), below it *Model* (`chat.rerank_model`: "klein" = multilingual MiniLM from mMARCO, about 120 MB, recommended; "gross" = bge-reranker-v2-m3, about 570 MB) and *Wait at most* (`chat.rerank_ms`, 300 to 2000, default 800). `wissen.search` then takes 30 candidates from the mix; a cross-encoder (`nachsortieren.py`, its own process `embed_worker.py rank klein|gross`, CPU only, low priority, starts from 4 GB or 6 GB of free memory, ends after 10 minutes without work) reads question and context line with passage together and orders them; passages below a fixed floor (score −4, about 2 %) are left out, and when nothing is left it honestly says "nothing found". A search never waits for the model to start (the start runs on its own, this search keeps the mix's order), never longer than the time budget (the late answer is dropped) and never for another re-ranking. "Look locally first" only re-ranks while the model runs anyway. The model files come once from Hugging Face; their SHA-256 are then kept in `state/doc-rank.json` (per size) and other files are refused. Status → Summary → Own documents shows the model's state (memory, last duration, budget), the live overview "Nachsortieren" as background work, Logs → Requests the step "Nachsortieren" with numbers, the journal only `wissen: nachsortieren | 30 -> 5 | 250 ms` or `wissen: nachsortieren langsam | 30 treffer | reihenfolge der mischung`. With the switch the search check measures a fourth line "Re-ranked", so the gain shows on the own documents.

**Multi-document questions (V01.0.768, plan *wissensbuch*, stage A):** a test question can name up to three more right documents (`mehr`) when its answer needs several documents at once ("What does the car cost a year for insurance and garage?"). It is measured apart: how many of its documents are among the first 5 passages the assistant gets from one search (*share*), and whether all of them are (*all found*); the other numbers count questions with one document only. Add one under *Me → Documents → Search check → Add a test question* with "More right documents" (a page then does not apply). With *Test questions by the Spark* every third attempt of the day tries a multi-document question first: the panel picks two documents with the same sender or a tag in common in their document card by a fixed rule (never the model), the language model writes a question from one passage of each that needs both; the same fixed checks apply against both passages, at most 60 such questions per profile and one per pair of documents. Shown under Me → Documents → Search check (rows "Multi-document questions", "Found badly" with "x of y among the first 5"), as sums under *Status → Checks → Check the search* and in the live overview as the share "all found"; journal `wissen: sammelfrage gemacht | 2 dokumente`, `… verworfen | <reason>`, a run with `N sammelfragen alle A teil T`. The test bank now has 20 documents and 10 multi-document questions; measured with words only 80 % share and 60 % all found, `test_suchpruefung` asks for at least 75 %. This is the yardstick the planned knowledge book has to beat.

**Knowledge book (V01.0.772, plan *wissensbuch* after Karpathy's LLM wiki, stage B):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book* (`chat.knowledge_book`, off by default; "Documents per day and profile" `chat.knowledge_book_day` 5 to 200, default 40). In quiet minutes (after the card step in `wissen.due_once`; with "Document card and tags" a document waits for its card) the language model reads one own document (at most 9000 characters) together with up to 5 fitting existing pages and proposes, as JSON, pages (topic, person, company, thing, place) with short sentences, each with the number of its passage. The panel decides by fixed rules what stays: a sentence needs a real passage of this document as its source, 8 to 400 characters and at least two words, no links or markup, no command words; every number and date must stand in the source (number probe). When a new sentence says the same with another value, both stay and are marked as a contradiction, never overwritten. At most 5 pages and 8 sentences per document, 60 sentences per page, 300 pages per profile. Under *Me → Documents → Knowledge book* the profile reads its pages by kind, jumps from each sentence to its source, removes a sentence with "Wrong" (it never comes back from the same document), has a page written again or deletes it. Questions: `document_search` brings the best page first (shared words, title counted three times) with its sources, then the passages; the system prompt names up to 40 fitting page titles. Pages are outside text (`chat.wrap_outside`, read lock as for documents), never go into the memory and hold only own documents, never "for everyone" ones of other profiles; guests never. The search check also measures multi-document questions in the mode "wissensbuch". Status → Live shows only pages, sentences, waiting documents and profiles; the journal only numbers (`wissen: wissensbuch eingearbeitet | …`). Tables `wb_pages`, `wb_sent`, `wb_docs`, `wb_block`, `wb_log` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`. Next: filing, Kiwix, Obsidian and NAS (stage D), apps (E).

**Checking the knowledge book (V01.0.779, plan *wissensbuch*, stage C):** once a night per profile (in the search check's night window, `suchpruefung.night`) the panel checks the book by fixed rules: a document whose passages changed since it was worked in (fingerprint `wb_docs.sig`, e.g. after "Read again" or Docling) loses its sentences and is worked in again. Sentences of documents that no longer exist go. `gedaechtnis.check` marks a sentence "out of date?" when a date in it passed after it was worked in (R1) or a time word like "currently" is too old (R2). Then the language model answers, for at most `chat.knowledge_book_sample` sentences a night (0 to 100, default 50; 0 = fixed rules only), only "does the passage say so?"; a no removes the sentence like "Wrong", an unclear answer leaves it. Deleted documents (Me → Documents) and left-out folders take their sentences at once (`documents.on_gone`, subscriber `wissensbuch.forget`). At the top of *Me → Documents → Knowledge book* are the contradictions (each value with its source; "Right" keeps that one, the others never come back from their documents) and the out-of-date sentences ("Still right" counts as said from now on, "Wrong" removes). Answers from the book carry "(maybe out of date)". History, Status → Live (number of contradictions and out-of-date sentences) and journal (`wissen: wissensbuch geprueft | …`, `… stichprobe bestaetigt|entfernt|unklar`, `… quelle weg | …`) only with numbers.

**Filing answers, Kiwix, Obsidian and NAS (V01.0.781, plan *wissensbuch*, stage D):** when an answer rests on at least two sources (own documents from `document_search` or Kiwix articles from `archive_search`/`article_more`), the chat shows "To the knowledge book" below it. The panel keeps the answer for one hour in memory only (at most 200, bound to the profile); only the owner's click files it, never the model. When filing, every sentence needs a passage from exactly these sources that holds all its numbers and shares at least 40 % of its words (`wissensbuch.match`), plus the same checks as when working in; sentences without a passage are dropped. The page has the kind "Answer", Kiwix articles stand as source "Kiwix: title" (no link, table `wb_kiwix`) and the nightly check treats them like documents. *Download as an Obsidian folder* (`GET /api/profile/wissensbuch/export`) returns a ZIP with one note per page, `[[links]]` between pages, sources as footnotes and `Wissensbuch.md` as index. When the profile has a filing folder (*Me → Storage*, "File here") and files what it makes automatically, a copy "Wissensbuch YYYY-MM-DD.zip" goes to `spark_ablage/Erstellt/` at night after the check, only when the book changed since the last copy. Journal: `wissen: wissensbuch abgelegt | …`, `… ablegen abgelehnt | …`, `… export | 1 datei`, `… nas abgelegt | 1 datei`.

**Knowledge map (V01.0.894, plan *wissensgraph* stage 1, Dominik 2026-10-21 "do everything as recommended"):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map* (`chat.knowledge_map`, off by default, child of `wissensbuch`; no profile switch, never guests). The knowledge book now has its own menu entry (*Knowledge book*, a full page with the tabs *Pages* and *Map*; the old box under Me → Documents is gone, the Me page `wbbox` stays the target of every way). After the knowledge book step the language model gets the same document again with at most 12 pages the panel picks (the document's pages, then pages whose whole title stands in the text) and proposes connections as JSON, each with a kind from the fixed list `wissenskarte.ARTEN` (belongs to, part of, owns, paid to, insured with, contract with, works at, lives in, located in, concerns, deadline for, replaces, depends on; causes: led to, causes, raises, lowers) and the number of its passage. The panel decides by fixed rules (`check_edge`): both ends offered, both named in the passage, the detail one line of up to 40 characters without link or command and with the number probe, a cause only with a word of reason in the passage (`SIGNALE`), at most 30 connections per document and 3000 per profile, "Wrong" (`wk_block`) for good. Existing documents are worked in bit by bit (fingerprint `wb_docs.sig`), deleted ones take their connections at once (`documents.on_gone`). Browser (own SVG code with `textContent`) and iPhone (SwiftUI) draw the map themselves from the JSON of `GET /api/profile/wissenskarte` (at most 150 points and 400 connections, around a page, of a document or the best connected ones); the model never delivers SVG, Mermaid or DOT. Export as Mermaid or Graphviz text from fixed code (names cleaned, no click, style or link lines), as SVG and PNG from the browser, in the Obsidian folder every connection as a `[[link]]` with its kind. Drag points, drag a document onto the map, "To the middle", zoom with the wheel and two fingers. Status → Live only connections, points, waiting documents and profiles; the journal only fixed words and numbers (`wissen: wissenskarte verbunden | n verbindungen | m verworfen | …`). Tables `wk_edges`, `wk_docs`, `wk_block` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`. Next: stage 2 (all documents, islands, filters, after a measuring test with 5000 documents), stage 3 (tools `wissen_weg` and `schaubild` after measuring), stage 4 (code files with a fixed reader).

**Knowledge store faster (V01.1.242, plan *wissensgraph* stage 2, step 1 of 4, Dominik 2026-10-29 "do as recommended"):** no new feature, no new switch. The vectors of the meaning search are kept in memory as float16 (half of the float32 before); `documents._similar` reckons in float32 block by block, 4096 passages at a time, so the ranking stays the same. The knowledge book finds its pages through an own search index (SQLite FTS5, table `wb_fts` with title and sentences of each page as search words); fixed SQL triggers note every changed, new or deleted page in `wb_ftsdirty`, and before each save the panel writes its row again. A book from before gets the index once on first opening (`wissen: wissensbuch suchindex aufgebaut | n seiten`). The count is as before (shared words, title three times); without FTS5 in SQLite the panel counts the words of all pages as it used to (`wissen: wissensbuch suchindex fehlt | wortzaehlung`). The knowledge map picks its visible part in SQLite: a fixed query counts the connections per page and document, around a page `WITH RECURSIVE` walks at most two steps over the connections, and UNION takes each page only once per step, so a ring never runs on; the limits of 150 points and 400 connections stay. Besides, two fixed queries with values only as parameters, never from the model's text: `wissenskarte.rund_um` (pages around a page with their distance) and `wissenskarte.weg` (shortest way between two pages, at most 4 steps, always the same one among equally short ways). For other parts of the panel there is the read function `wissensbuch.liste(uid, worte, k)`: read only, bound to the profile (empty when the knowledge book is not allowed for the profile, never for guests), it returns `[(page id, rank from 1)]` from the same FTS5 search, at most 50, never titles or sentences. Tables `wb_fts` and `wb_ftsdirty` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`.

**Knowledge map faster (V01.0.907, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1"):** the knowledge map has its own daily limit *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map → Map: documents per day and profile* (`chat.knowledge_map_day`, 5 to 500, default 200; before, `chat.knowledge_book_day` with 40 applied). A map step only names connections and is short, so the backlog of a full knowledge book (about 150 documents) is worked in within about a day. Still only in quiet minutes, only when the knowledge book has nothing to do, one document per step behind speech.

**Find in all data, stage A (V01.1.245, plan *such-index*, Dominik 2026-10-29 "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a"):** admin switch *Features → Find in all data* (`chat.fund`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, never guests or foreign voices). A menu entry *Find* of its own next to the knowledge book with the tabs *Search*, *Tags* and *Settings* (Me page `fundbox`). Per profile a file of its own `users/<id>/fund.db` (SQLite, FTS5): `fund_item` (source, reference, date, title, excerpt, fields), `fund_fts` (words of title, fields and tags, never a document's body), `fund_blind` (for the sealed sources from stage B), `fund_tag` (tags with an HMAC mark per profile, `vorrat.marke`, key by HKDF from the vault key and the profile id), `fund_hand` (own tags), `fund_stand`. Collecting contract `fund.QUELLEN` (name, feature, protection, kind, `neues_seit`, `noch_da`, `oeffnen`): in stage A documents, NAS knowledge folders, reMarkable and knowledge book, all as references; their text is searched through the own document search (`documents.search`, rank fusion RRF k = 60). Every five minutes per profile a round in the background (`vorrang.in_thread("Suchindex")`, at most 200 entries per source and round), only when the documents changed. **Fields and categories only by fixed rules:** amount after "Gesamt/Summe/Rechnungsbetrag", number after its fixed word, kind from fixed words or the document card, company only when the sender stands word for word in the text; categories only from the fixed list (`fund.KATEGORIEN` = kinds of the document card without "Sonstiges", now with "Bestellung" and "Buchung", plus 13 areas). Person, company and place pages of the knowledge book become tags (a stand-in until the knowledge map's nodes are there). **Own tags come first:** ✓ keeps, × removes one for good, + adds one; they survive "Rebuild", free tags of a document go to its document card. **In the assistant:** tool `fund_suche` (Weiche group "finden": a search word and something of one's own like an invoice, a contract, a mail or "my …", never with internet or archive), at most five hits of 400 characters each as outside text; the panel keeps filters (category, company, person, source, one year) only when the person's own words of the message hold them. Below the answer the card "Show in Find" (browser, apps through the panel view, companion). Ctrl K offers "Find in my data: …". Endpoints `POST /api/profile/fund/suche`, `GET /api/profile/fund/eintrag/{id}`, `PUT …/schlagworte`, `GET /api/profile/fund/stand`, `POST /api/profile/fund/neu`, `DELETE /api/profile/fund` (login, own profile, CSRF, size and rate limit), `GET /api/admin/fund` (numbers only). Log area *Find* (`fund: suche | 3 treffer | 41 ms | P1`, `fund: runde dokument | 12 neu | 0 weg`), live line *Find* with numbers only. Self-tests `tests/test_fund.py`.

**People, companies and deadlines (V01.1.253, plan *wissensgraph* stage 2, step 2 of 4, Dominik 2026-10-29 "Empfehlung umsetzen"):** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map → People, companies and deadlines* (`chat.knowledge_ents`, off by default, child of `wissenskarte`; no profile switch, never guests), own daily limit *Points: documents per day and profile* (`chat.knowledge_ents_day`, 5 to 500, default 200). After the map's connections the language model gets each document (the passages [S1] … and the pages [K1] … the panel offers) and proposes only JSON: `dinge` with kind, name and the number of the passage, plus `verbindungen` between the things (D1 …) and the pages. The panel decides by fixed rules (`wissenskarte.check_ent`): a kind from the fixed list `wissenskarte.ENT_ARTEN` (person, firma, ort, vertrag, konto, frist), the name stands in the passage (compared lower case, without accents, with spaces drawn together, `ent_norm`), 2 to 80 characters without link, markup or command word, a deadline only with a date in the passage, an account only as an IBAN or a number with at least five digits; at most 40 points per document, 200 passages per document and 5000 points per profile. Connections only from `ARTEN` with the same check as `check_edge`; one between two pages stays the map step's job. Stored in the profile's `wissen.db`: `wk_ent(id, art, name, norm, alias)` with a unique index on (art, norm), ids are never given out again; `wk_ment(ent, doc, chunk)` with the passages; the same name of the same kind (also an earlier name in `alias`) gets the same point. On the map the points are called `k<number>`, carry the colour of their kind, and buttons above the map show one kind with its neighbours; `view`, `rund_um` and `weg` take them along (the limits of 150 points and 400 connections stay). Other parts only read: `wissenskarte.finde_knoten(uid, name, art=None)` (exactly by name and other names) and `wissenskarte.knoten_liste(uid, art, anfang="", most=50)` (sorted by name), both through a read-only connection and only while the switch counts for the profile. Renaming (the old name becomes another name), merging (same kind only; passages and connections move to the target) and deleting (never comes back from these documents) happen only by the owner's click through `POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/knoten/{id}/name`, `…/zusammen` and `DELETE …/knoten/{id}`, with sign-in, profile binding, CSRF, size limit, rate limit and the code of "Confirm" (`stufen.LIST`); the model never decides about it. Every change calls the subscribers of `wissenskarte.GEAENDERT` as `fn(uid, alt_id, neu_id, was)` (`zusammen`, `weg` with `neu_id` None, `name` with `neu_id` equal to `alt_id`); an error there is logged and disturbs nothing. When a document goes (`documents.GONE`), its passages go with it at once, a point without a passage disappears and reports `weg`. Live shows only the number per kind, the log only fixed words and numbers (`wissen: wissenskarte knoten erkannt | n knoten | n neu | n verworfen | n verbindungen | n weg`). iPhone: the points on the map in the colour of their kind, "By kind" and "Delete"; renaming and merging in the panel. Self-test `test_wissensknoten`.
**Find, stage B: mail and attachments (V01.1.256, plan *such-index*, Dominik 2026-10-29 "2a 3a 4a"):** sub-switch *Features → Find in all data → Find mail and attachments* (`chat.fund_mail`, off by default, a child of "fund", needs Reading e-mail; never guests). `fundmail.py` reads **all** mail (2a), the newest first, with no upper limit: per mailbox the folders inbox, archive and sent (4a, role through `postfach.role`: the server's SPECIAL-USE flags first, else the usual names), never spam, the trash, drafts or Gmail's views; only reading (`tidy.Box`, EXAMINE, BODY.PEEK: mail stays unread) and only to the configured mailboxes (`mail.IMAP` = `netguard.IMAP4_SSL`). Per folder `fund_stand` keeps the UIDVALIDITY, the highest UID read and the lowest read backwards: each round first fetches the new mail (`UID n+1:*`), in quiet minutes (`wissen.quiet`: a minute without a question, nobody speaking) and at night (01–06 longer) the older ones backwards; another UIDVALIDITY reads the folder anew, once a day a reconciliation (`UID SEARCH ALL`) takes out what was deleted. Slices of at most 0.2 s reading and 20 mails, one transaction per slice, `vorrang.hold` in between (a pause while someone speaks); the round runs in a thread of low priority (`vorrang.in_thread("Suchindex")`), started apart from `fund.due_once` and never awaited: every five minutes for profiles that read their mail, else hourly, at once after a new mail (`mailidle.on_new`). **Protection only by space:** below a fixed free reserve of 2048 MB reading stops with a log line. **Sealed (3a):** subject, excerpt, fields (sender address, amount, number, folder role, attachment names), tag values and own tags only AES-GCM (`vorrat.seal`, bound to profile, source, entry and field), the words only as HMAC marks in `fund_blind` (per word stem, plus its first four letters and the sender's words); in plain text stand only source, entry key (mailbox id, a mark of the folder, UID) and date, and without a key nothing is written. **Attachments** (PDF, Word, OpenDocument, text …, at most 10 MB, text up to 30,000 characters) through the existing document reading (`documents.extract_pages`): text and fields (amount, invoice number, company by the fixed rules) go only into `fund`, never as a document into `wissen.db`; a scan without text keeps its file name. An attachment hit lifts its mail (one card with "Attachment: …"). "Open" shows the mail on the E-mail page in its folder (archive and sent, as there, only with "Order mail"). When a mailbox is removed, the search no longer finds its mail at once, the rows go with the next look. With mail released `fund_suche` counts as a mail reader (`offer()["mail"]`): the locks after mail (`LOCKED_AFTER_MAIL`) apply. Logs `fund: runde mail | 120 neu | 0 weg | 4800 offen | 12.3 s | P1`, `fund: mail platz knapp | … MB frei`, `fund: mail uidvalidity gewechselt | ordner neu`, `fund: mail konto entfernt | n weg`; live *Find*: "Mails: heute N eingelesen, M offen", "Platz knapp". Find → Settings shows "Mails not read yet". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_mail.py` (a fake mailbox). Not yet: "Take into documents" for an attachment (follows).

**Find, stage C: appointments, contacts, conversations, lists, reminders, pictures, songs and "from the surgery" (V01.1.257, plan *such-index*):** five more sub-switches under *Features → Find in all data*, all off by default, children of "fund", and each source also follows the function it comes from (`fundquellen.frei`); never guests. `fundquellen.py` holds the sources, `fund.runde` collects them in the same round as the documents (at most every five minutes, only when one of their files changed, `fundquellen.stempel`; a round that reached its limit of 200 entries per source goes on in the next one). *Find appointments* (`chat.fund_termine`, needs Calendar): the own calendars, only from the copy on the Spark (`kalenderspiegel`, a Spark calendar from `sparkkalender`), never a fetch of its own; calendars others share stay out. Title, place and description sealed like mail (AES-GCM through `fund.put_sealed`, words as HMAC marks), per object the ETag as its state (as a mark); a removed calendar leaves the search at once. *Find contacts* (`chat.fund_kontakte`, needs Contacts): name, company, position and town of every card sealed, never numbers, mail addresses or streets; name, company and town become tags (person, company, place). *Find conversations* (`chat.fund_chats`, needs Past conversations): per conversation only date, channel (Telegram, Siri, watch …) and number of questions; the words are searched by `recall.liste` (BM25 of the history, answers from mail stay out), the title is read from the conversation when shown, nothing of it is in `fund.db`. *Find lists and reminders* (`chat.fund_listen`, needs Lists or Timers and reminders): own lists on the Spark and lists of others only through `tasks.shared_to` (Share lists on for both, the share still there), plus every reminder; clear like their source. *Find pictures and songs* (`chat.fund_medien`, needs Painting pictures or Making music): wish, description and lyrics. A hit opens its page (the calendar on the appointment's day, the contact card, the list, the conversation in the chat, notifications, pictures, music). **Periods:** `frisch.spanne(text, now, zone)` is the one rule for "heute", "gestern", "vorgestern", "diese/letzte Woche", "diesen/letzten Monat", "dieses/letztes Jahr", "im März", "März 2024", "2023", "vor drei Wochen" (also in English); two different periods of one kind are none. `wissen.recent_window` and the filter of `fund_suche` take it. **"vom OP" (from the surgery):** `fund.bezug_aus` reads from the person's own words "vom/vor dem/nach dem/seit dem <word>" (sure) or "beim/zum/zur/von der <word>" (only when an appointment fits), never a time ("vom letzten Jahr", "vom Montag"). The panel looks in the own calendars for the newest past appointment (at most 3 years) with all word stems through its marks; entries 14 days before to 60 days after get a fixed rank bonus (1/60), nothing is dropped. Without an appointment a fixed sentence says "Einen Termin „OP“ habe ich im Kalender nicht gefunden." and the search runs without a window. The word itself is then no search word. On the page Find a line above the hits shows period and appointment. Logs `fund: runde termin | 3 neu | 0 weg | …`, `fund: bezug | termin gefunden`, `fund: bezug | kein termin` (never the word); live *Find*: "Termine N · Kontakte N …", "Bezug heute N gefunden, M ohne Termin". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_quellen.py` (a fixed time, never the clock).

**Find, stage D: tags by the model, points of the knowledge map, one ranking and the Sofort-Karte (V01.1.258, plan *such-index*, Dominik 2026-10-29 tag points 1–4):** sub-switch *Features → Find in all data → Tags by the model* (`chat.fund_schlagworte`, off by default, a child of "fund"; never guests), below it *Tags: entries per day and profile* (`chat.fund_tag_day`, 5 to 500, default 200). In quiet minutes (`wissen.due_once` after the knowledge map, only when `wissen.quiet()`: a minute without a question, nobody speaking) the language model reads one entry after the other through `llm.complete`, the newest first: title, excerpt and fixed fields, for documents the start of their text, at most 1500 characters, wrapped with `chat.wrap_outside`. Conversations and knowledge book pages have no text of their own in the index and stay out. The answer only as JSON `{"kategorien", "personen", "firmen", "orte", "frei"}`; it is data, never an order. `fund.pruefe` keeps a category only from `fund.KATEGORIEN` (at most 3), a person, company or place only when the name stands in the entry word for word (`wissenskarte.stands`, name rules `wissenskarte.clean_name`, at most 5 each), a free tag only through `documents.clean_tags` and never with a command word (at most 5); what the person removed (`fund_hand` minus) never comes back. A model tag never overwrites one of the rules, shows dashed with "by the model" and is never a filter of the tool; the full text finds everything anyway. Under *Find → Tags*, "New from the model" shows the proposals of the last 7 days: ✓ keeps one as your own, × removes it for good. At most two tries per entry; when an entry changes, the model reads it again. **Points of the knowledge map:** people, companies and places (from rules, contacts, the model and by hand) get the point of the same name (`fund_tag.ent` through `wissenskarte.finde_knoten`, read only; `fund` never makes a point), also later when the point comes after the entry (`fund.verknuepfe` in every round, at most 500). `fund.knoten_geaendert` listens to `wissenskarte.GEAENDERT`: merged → the tag follows the new point, deleted → it stays a name without a point. Knowledge book pages no longer stand in for people, companies and places. A point counts in the ranking like an own tag (+1/60). **Ranking:** every list of the same form `liste(uid, worte, k) -> [(key, rank)]` goes into the mix through `fund._fuse` with the same constant k = 60: `recall.liste` (conversations), `wissensbuch.liste` and, once it exists, `wissenskarte.liste` (pieces, their document is the hit); a broken list leaves the others standing. **Sofort-Karte:** when the person asks to find something (`fund.FIND_WORDS`) and one hit lies clearly ahead (the only one, or at least 1.5 times the score of the second, `fund.klar_vorn`), the panel answers itself with a fixed sentence (`fund.sofort_satz`: "Gefunden: Mail von Thomann vom 14. März 2024 mit dem Anhang „Rechnung 4711“, Betrag 129,90 €."), values without markup or link signs, at most 60 characters each; the model is not asked afterwards and rewords nothing (`chat_tools` hands `ctx["fest"]`, `chat_modell` says the sentence and ends). Panel chat and Android: card "Open the original" and "Show in Find"; Begleiter: "Show in Find"; Telegram: button "Original öffnen" to `<panel>/#fund=<no>` (only with the panel's address entered, only navigation); spoken (speaker, watch, Siri, CarPlay) the short form without the attachment. When the appointment of a "vom …" is missing, the fixed sentence says so first and the model answers. **Local first:** `lokal.SOURCES["fund"]` looks in the quick look into the clear sources without an index of their own there (knowledge book, lists, reminders, pictures, songs; never mail, appointments, contacts). Logs `fund: schlagworte modell | 3 ok | 1 verworfen | … | nicht_im_text=1`, `fund: schlagworte modell fehler | ValueError | …`, `fund: knoten zusammen | 2 schlagworte | …`, `fund: knoten verknuepft | 4 schlagworte | …`, `fund: sofort | 1 treffer klar vorn | …` (never a word); live *Find*: "Schlagworte heute N Einträge, M übernommen, K verworfen, L offen", "Sofort-Karten heute N". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_schlagworte.py` (an obeying model, a fixed time, browser test).

**Document list for many files (V01.0.413):** Me → Documents shows every document as one compact row (icon, title, kind · sender · source, date and size) instead of a card with all buttons; badges only when something matters ("due 20/12", "reading 3/12", "not readable", "for everyone", "search off"). The list scrolls with the page (before, a box of 28 % of the screen height), shows 50 rows and then "Show more", and is grouped into Today, Last 7 days, months and years when sorted by date. At the top a search field filters at once by title, file name, sender, kind, number and tags, Enter also searches the content (replaces "Try a search", hits open the document); source (uploaded, iPhone app, companion, reMarkable, jobs, from others), sort order (newest, name, deadline, size; source and sort order stay in the browser) and quick filters with counts (deadline open, being read, not readable, for everyone, search off) plus tags as a list. A click opens the details, beside the list on wide pages, as their own page with "Back to the list" on narrow ones: card, state with bar, tags edited in place, switches "The assistant searches it" and "For everyone", open original, read text (with "Belongs with"), remind, read again, delete. Documents others share are in the same list (source "From others"). "Select" ticks documents and acts on all ticked own ones: search on/off, for everyone on/off, add a tag, read again (at most 20), delete (with a question first), via `POST /api/profile/wissen/batch` (`{"do", "ids"}`, the profile's browser login only, CSRF, one step of the "doc" rate limit per request, at most 200 ids, 8 KB body, only documents of the own database, "for everyone" and tags only with their switches). The six switches and the note on file kinds sit folded under "Settings for documents". The layout "list with details" (CSS building blocks `ld-…` in app.css) is meant for more pages. The iPhone app is unchanged.

**Me pages tidied (V01.0.424):** memory, mail, reMarkable and the other Me pages follow one pattern: sub-tabs with counts instead of fold-out arrows (an orange count needs you), a status card with "⋯" for rare actions, compact rows with small buttons, no scroll boxes inside the page (the first 10, then "Show more"), "add" as a button, anything irreversible at the bottom in the danger zone. One tap answers a mail question, "Always for this sender" makes a rule; the preview is grouped by target folder. reMarkable: folder tree with ticks and its content beside it, saved with the bar at the bottom. Memory: search, filter (from you / automatic), change entries in place and delete several at once.

**Creating documents (V01.0.415):** On request the Spark writes letters, reports, minutes, notes, summaries, invitations, presentations and spreadsheets as a file: Word (.docx), PDF, PowerPoint (.pptx), Excel (.xlsx), Markdown, text or CSV. Switches: *Features → Own documents → Creating documents* (`chat.doc_make`, off) and below it *Own templates* (`chat.doc_make_templates`, off); once released both work for every profile; never guests or unknown voices, never over MCP. Flow: the Weiche group "erstellen" offers `document_plan`, `document_change`, `document_status` (and `document_send` for sending). The model only proposes an outline (kind, format, style, title, at most 30 parts, facts from this conversation, up to 3 own documents as sources); in the chat it shows as a card to change (title, parts, format, look). Only a plain "yes" in the same conversation or "Looks good, create" starts a background job (the outline waits 30 minutes). One model call per part with a fixed JSON schema and no tools; facts and document texts are wrapped as data (`<<<DATEN … DATEN>>>`), never as a command. Speech priority applies (the job waits while someone talks); one job runs, at most 3 wait, 10 per profile and day, 20 minutes per job; the emergency stop halts new jobs and sending. The Spark builds the file itself (`docrender.py`: python-docx, python-pptx, XlsxWriter, fpdf2, pinned with hashes in `requirements-panel.lock` and `requirements-docmake.lock`): no macros, no links to the outside, in spreadsheets only `SUM` formulas, CSV cells never start with `= + - @`, charts as bar, line or pie. Every content is checked before building (lengths, characters, parts, cells, pictures only from own documents). Result: under *Me → Documents → Made by the Spark* with preview, download in every fitting format, up to 20 versions ("Bring back"), parts "Edit" (by hand, no model), "Rewrite" with a wish, "Leave out", "Have a part added", switch format and look for good; also listed as a document with source "spark" (searched only after its own switch). By a sentence: "slide 4 shorter", "make a PDF of it", "send it to my PC" (understood without a document word for two hours after the last job). Sending only to own targets: PC (Spark companion, inbox), reMarkable (PDF), Telegram, mail draft with attachment (never sent). Sender for letters under *Me → Documents → Sender for letters*. Own templates (.docx/.dotx/.pptx/.potx, at most 10 MB, 10 of them) are checked on upload: no macros, ActiveX, embedded objects or external links, no zip trick. Storage: `users/<p>/erstellt/` (at most 100 documents). End of a job: push "document ready". Status → Live shows the target "Dokumente erstellen" with counts (running, waiting, done today), never contents. Logs: `docmake: Auftrag praesentation pptx 6 Teile (neu)`, `docmake: fertig … Version 1 in 84 s`. API (profile): `GET /api/make`, `POST|DELETE /api/make/plan/{id}`, `GET /api/make/job/{id}`, `GET /api/make/doc/{id}[/file]`, `POST /api/make/doc/{id}/edit|change|restore|send`, `/api/make/templates`. Plan: `plaene/dokumente-erstellen.md`. When the Spark asks back ("What should it be about?"), the answer stays with the file wish for 15 minutes in the same conversation (Weiche group "erstellen", V01.0.423). When the function is off, the model gets a fixed sentence on where to switch it on instead of the tools and does not write the content as text; Me → Documents then shows "Made by the Spark" with a "Switch on" button. Getting it everywhere (V01.0.429): once done, the card in the chat shows a small preview of the first slide, page or sheet (`peek` in `GET /api/make`), plus download, view and send. Where the browser has no login (the Android app hands downloads to the phone's browser, another device) there is a download link: `POST /api/make/doc/{id}/link` returns `/api/make/dl?t=…`, valid 10 minutes and at most 5 downloads, only for this file and version; the Spark keeps only the key's hash; at most 5 open links per profile; when the function is off no link works. "Copy link" in the preview. By mail two ways, both only with a mailbox that has "Allow reply drafts" on (E-mail → Settings → Inbox tidying), and never sent: "As mail draft" (to the address entered, empty: to the mailbox's own) and "File into the mailbox" (a mail from you to you with the file, unread in the folder "Spark/Dokumente", created when needed). By a sentence both go only to yourself (`document_send` with `mail` or `box`, no recipient). "Finished documents also" under Me → Documents → Made by the Spark (`doc_deliver`: PC, reMarkable, Telegram, mail draft or mailbox; only a target that is set up counts) delivers every finished document right after your yes; the push says where. iPhone app: Me → Made by the Spark (when the function is on) with preview, Apple's viewer for Word, PowerPoint, Excel and PDF and "Share" (save to Files, send on). Looks: without an own template the Spark builds presentations with a title slide, an overview slide (from 4 parts), numbered boxes, key-number cards (`stats`, up to 4), a quote box (`quote`), styled tables and charts; Word and PDF get a colored title block, numbered headings with a line, cards, quote box, zebra tables and page numbers; Excel a title band without gridlines, a colored head row, zebra rows, a set-off total row and a chart with values. New looks "Frisch" (fresh) and "Warm"; "Spark dark" prints light in Word, PDF and Excel. With an own template everything stays as before.

**Professional presentations (since V01.0.465):** For slides the language model picks one of ten types per slide and fills only its fields: section divider, key statement (one big sentence), icon grid (3 to 6 points with a symbol), big numbers, timeline or process, comparison in two columns, picture and text, chart with a message, conclusion and bullet points. Fixed rules in the Spark (`folien.py`) keep the variety: never the same type twice in a row, at most a third lists. A slide has at most 4 points of at most 8 words; the heading is a statement. When a part is too long the Spark asks the model once more, after that it shortens at a natural break and puts the full text into the speaker notes. Charts only with at least 3 different values that all have a source; the source stands below and the key value is highlighted, otherwise they become big numbers. The symbols (161 from Lucide, ISC license) are part of the repository. With the switch *Pictures from the Kiwix* (admin, off; then for every profile) the Spark fetches a picture for the title slide and "picture and text" slides from the Wikipedia in the own Kiwix, checks it and saves it anew (at most 3 MB), with source and license note on the slide. Before writing the Spark looks the title up in your Kiwix and your documents; every number in the text must be there, in your words or in the facts of the conversation, otherwise it is left out (small counts up to 10 without a unit excepted). The ready note says how much was left out. Word and PDF show icons, timeline and comparison as blocks of their own, Markdown and own templates as bullet points. Everything works without the internet. Since V01.0.482 there was a profile switch for it under Me → Documents → Settings for documents; since V01.0.509 the admin switch is enough.

**Picture recognition (V01.0.190, off by default):** a profile attaches a photo in the assistant (picture button next to the text field, paste or drop; up to three per question) and asks about it, typed or spoken. qwen38's language model (Qwen3.8 can see pictures) looks at it itself. Switches: the admin under Features → Picture recognition, where "Check picture recognition" sends a test picture to see whether the running lane accepts pictures; once released it works for every profile, for the iPhone app also "Let the model look at photos from the app", for Telegram "Photos over Telegram" (caption = question, reply to the photo = follow-up). Never guests, speakers or other device keys. The browser makes the picture smaller; the Spark reads it again (Pillow, at most 8 MB and 40 megapixels, JPEG/PNG/WebP only, with a time limit), turns it upright, scales it to 1280 pixels and writes it again as JPEG, so location (GPS) and camera data are gone. It is kept in memory only, bound to profile and device, 10 minutes after the last question (30 minutes at most, 3 per profile, 30 per hour), never on disk, in the backup or the log, unless the person keeps it with "Picture to My documents" (see knowledge from uploads). In an answer with a picture the model gets no tools; the answer counts as outside text, the next turn is locked like after a web page, and nothing is learned from it. Below the answer, "Store under My documents" keeps the answer text. API: `POST /api/chat/image` (raw picture) → `{"id"}`, then `POST /api/chat` with `"images": [id]`; `GET /api/chat/image` says whether the caller may.

**Painting pictures (V01.0.567, off by default, plan `plaene/bildgenerierung.md`):** The Spark paints pictures itself with FLUX.2 [klein], without the internet. The model only comes with the installation: `sudo ./install.sh --bild 4b-q8` (4B with Q8, Apache 2.0, recommended), `4b-q4` (less memory), `9b-q8` or `9b-q4` (FLUX Non-Commercial, private use only); the first install asks, the default is none. The engine is stable-diffusion.cpp (`sd-server`, pinned to one commit, built for the GB10 with CUDA, no Docker); the model files are pinned by revision and SHA-256 (`bildmodell.py`). The service `speech-spark-bild` (`bild_server.py`, only `127.0.0.1:31005`, API key) loads the engine at the first picture (`127.0.0.1:31015`, after checking the free memory) and stops it after *Unload after minutes without a picture* (default 10). Switch: admin under Features → Painting pictures (`chat.bilder`); once released it applies to every profile, never to guests, never over MCP. A profile paints under *Me → Pictures* (wish, format square, landscape, portrait or wide) or in the conversation ("Paint me a cat on a skateboard", since V01.0.591 also with the verb at the end, as in German "Kannst du mir ein Bild von … erstellen?", since V01.0.683 also caricature, comic and cartoon: "Create a funny caricature for me"): the router group "bild" offers `bild_malen`, the model writes an English prompt that is shown under the picture. The tool counts as a change (locked after outside text, emergency stop stage 2 holds it). The job runs in the background and waits for a pause in the conversation (speech goes first); one picture at a time and never beside a song, at most 10 pictures and songs waiting together (see Heavy jobs), 12 minutes per job. Fixed rules that cannot be switched off: a word list (nothing sexual, no violence, no hate symbols), no people the Spark knows (profile names and contacts), then an image check (ViT classifier, ONNX on the CPU; above 50 % the picture is discarded, without the check nothing is shown); every picture is marked as AI-made (PNG text "KI-erzeugt", IPTC `trainedAlgorithmicMedia`) and re-encoded. Storage: `users/<p>/bilder/` with `index.json`, *Pictures per profile and day* (default 30, rolling 24 hours), *Space per profile* (default 500 MB), at most 300 pictures; *Longest side* 512 to 1408 pixels (default 1024). In the chat a card shows the progress, then the picture with Download and "All pictures"; the answer keeps "Picture: <prompt>" and the job, so the chat shows the picture again after a reload, in every saved conversation and on every device. The card only asks once it stands in the answer (since V01.0.683; before, it sometimes stayed at "waiting …" although the picture was ready under *Me → Pictures*). When the assistant asks back after a picture wish (say "I do not paint real people, fictional ones instead?"), a short "yes" or "fine" is enough: up to 10 minutes after the own wish painting stays offered for it, without a web search. The iPhone app shows the picture right under the answer as well (touch and hold: save or share); for that its key may only read the job and the picture file of its own profile, the whole list, painting and deleting work under My things → Pictures (since V01.0.733 with the release of Painting pictures). *Me → Pictures* shows them as a gallery with wish, prompt, time, size, duration, download and delete, "Delete all pictures" on top. Finished through the iPhone app or Telegram: push "Bild". "Bild testen" below the switch paints a test picture and shows load time, paint time and memory (stage 0, measuring). State → Live: target "Painting pictures" with counts (running, waiting, done, failed and refused today), never wishes or prompts; service guard part "Painting pictures"; logs (area "Bild"): `bilder: auftrag 1024x1024 quadrat chat fuer …`, `bilder: gemalt …`, `bilder: nicht gemalt <reason> fuer …`, `bild: engine gestartet 38 s 11.2 gib`, `bild: engine beendet leerlauf`. API (profile): `GET|POST|DELETE /api/bilder`, `GET /api/bilder/job/{id}`, `GET /api/bilder/{id}/datei[?dl=1]`, `DELETE /api/bilder/{id}`; admin: `GET|POST /api/admin/bild-test`. Later: quick buttons, background removal and upscaling (stage 2b), Telegram photo and Open WebUI interface (2d).

**Changing pictures (V01.0.653, off by default, plan `plaene/bildbearbeitung.md`, stage 2a):** The Spark changes an own photo or a painted picture after the person's sentence, with the same FLUX.2 [klein] (reference picture to `bild_server.py`, at most 2 MP), without the internet and without a new model. Switch: admin under Features → Changing pictures (`chat.bilder_aendern`, below Painting pictures); once released it applies to every profile, never to guests, never over MCP, only in the own browser or the own app. Which picture is decided by the panel with a fixed rule before the answer (`bilder.from_turn`), never by the language model, and there is no tool for it (a turn with a photo has no tools anyway): (1) the first photo of this message when the text has a change word ("make … blue", "change", "remove … from the picture", "take the beard off" (German „mach den Bart weg“), "as a watercolour") and is no question about the picture ("What is in the picture?" stays with Recognising pictures); (2) without a photo: a short message (at most 200 characters) with a change or again word up to 10 minutes after a change takes its result ("brighter", "again"); (3) under *Me → Pictures* "Change" on a picture with an own sentence, plus "Upload an own photo" (JPEG, PNG, WebP, at most 8 MB, cleaned, without metadata, kept as PNG at most 2048 pixels). Pictures from mail, web, documents, other people's chats or Telegram groups never. Fixed rules: the word and name list from painting, plus for changes ID cards, signatures, stamps, number plates, bank notes, undressing, injuries and swapping faces; then the image check (fail closed). Every change is a new picture, the original stays ("Show the original"); the result is marked (PNG text "KI-bearbeitet", IPTC `compositeWithTrainedAlgorithmicMedia`). Day count, space and queue from painting apply; the model's answer only says that the picture is being changed, the card in the chat is called "Changing the picture". State → Live: the target "Painting pictures" also shows "changed" (count); logs (area "Bild"): `bilder: auftrag aendern 1024x768 …`, `bilder: geaendert 1024x768 21.4 s fuer …`, `bilder: nicht gestartet aendern aus|quelle fuer …`, `bilder: foto gespeichert|nicht gespeichert voll|kein bild fuer …`. Quick buttons (stage 2b, V01.0.657) under *Me → Pictures* on a picture, without a model, at once on the panel with Pillow: rotate left or right, mirror, brighter, darker, more contrast, black and white, crop to a square, half the size; again a new picture, a painted picture keeps its AI marks, an own photo gets none; does not count towards pictures per day; Live "schnell bearbeitet", log `bilder: schnell heller 1024x768 fuer …`. API (profile, also the app under My things → Pictures): `POST /api/bilder/{id}/aendern` (`{wunsch}`), `POST /api/bilder/{id}/schnell` (`{was}`), `POST /api/bilder/foto` (the picture as the body).

**Making music (V01.0.670 stage M0 measuring, V01.0.688 stage M1 making songs, plan `plaene/musik.md`):** Since stage M1 every released profile makes its own songs. Own admin switch Features → Making music (off, needs the installed model); once released it holds for every profile, the admin blocks single ones. Guests, MCP and outside text (after mail, web or a document, Notaus from stage 2) never get the tool. In the conversation: "Make me a song about our cat", "Compose a piece for piano"; the Weiche (group `musik`, tool `musik_machen`) keeps that apart from "Play music in the living room" (Home Assistant), a short "yes, with singing" within 10 minutes after the wish still counts. A follow-up on the last song (style, genre, "again", "can it be reggae style") gets the tool for 10 minutes after the wish, the start or the end of a song, and always right after an answer about a song; when the person only talks about the song, the model gets no tool and the rule never to promise one. If an answer promises a song anyway without `musik_machen` having run, the action check holds the sentence back (kind `musik`), since V01.0.700. Since V01.0.714 "Musik stück", "House Track", "Musiktrack" and "Beat" count as wishes too; after an answer with a web search (e.g. chords for the OP-XY) the person's own music wish still makes the song (`own` in `extras.offer`), and that answer stays along as data up to 7 messages back (`musik.BACK`) so key and chords are right. Mail and the Notaus still lock the tool. Under Me → Music (laid out like Documents and E-mail since V01.0.835, plan `musik-seite/plan.md`): on top the count, space and songs today with the "⋯" menu (Delete all songs), below it the card for a new song (wish, length as chips, switch "With singing" with language and lyrics). Below that a search (wish, description, lyrics) and quick filters All, With singing, Instrumental from six songs on, then the songs by day (Being made, Today, Yesterday, Last 7 days, Older): one row per song with ▶ in front, length and singing below, the time or day on the right. A click opens the details (beside the list on a computer, as a page of their own with "‹ All songs" on a phone): player with a slider, made, length, size, time and model, Download, "Make it again" (only fills the form with wish, length, singing and lyrics; nothing is sent before "Make the song") and Delete; the description by the model and the lyrics folded. One player for the rows and the details, so two songs never play at once; while a song plays whose details are not open, a bar at the bottom shows its title and time. Fixed rules (`musik.refuse_text`): without singing instrumental, without a length 2 minutes (10 s up to `music.max_s`, default 300 s); no word list (sexual, violence, hate), no "in the style of" an artist, no passwords, PINs or account numbers, names are fine (unlike pictures, since V01.0.694). Limits per profile: `music.day_limit` songs a day (10), `music.store_mb` MB of space (500), at most 200 songs; one song at a time per profile, at most 10 pictures and songs waiting together on the Spark (see Heavy jobs); a song waits for a pause in the conversation (`vorrang.py`) and may take 20 minutes. Picking the model: Features → Making music → Model ("As installed", 2B, 2B small, XL); models not installed are greyed out. Measured on tars on 2026-10-16: 2B 16.6 s for 30 s with a 13.4 GB peak, XL 17.6 s with 19.2 GB; another model ends the running engine first. Storage `users/<id>/musik/` (MP3 with the AI mark and `index.json`), Delete data of the release counts it.
