The panel's chat shows a song card with a player, the apps find the song under Me → Music and get a notice when it is done. A song waiting or being made shows in State → Live under "In the background" and on the target "Making music" (numbers only); the music service shows on the model computer's card (Status → Summary and the control centre), since V01.0.700. Logs with numbers and fixed words only: `musik: auftrag 120 s instrumental chat fuer …`, `musik: gemacht 120 s gesang 9.5 s fuer …`, `musik: nicht gestartet kuenstler fuer …`, never the wish or the lyrics. API: `GET|POST|DELETE /api/musik`, `GET /api/musik/job/{id}`, `GET /api/musik/{id}/datei`, `DELETE /api/musik/{id}`.

**Editing music (V01.0.863, off by default, plan `musik-experte/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-20 "alles nach Empfehlung umsetzten", stage E1 with today's model):** The Spark edits a song under *Me → Music*, without the internet, on a page of its own over the whole width (V01.0.885, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1", pictures `musik-bearbeiten/vorschlag-1.png`: "Edit" in the details opens it, the Me menu steps aside; on top ▶ with the time, below the big wave with the range, then the tools on the left, their fields in the middle, the versions on the right with ▶, a tap on a version edits that one; at the bottom a fixed bar with the button and the state of the running job; on a phone one column, the tools as a row to swipe, the versions behind a button; "‹ Songs" goes back; in the iPhone app the same order with a fixed button at the bottom), with the same ACE-Step (acestep.cpp: tasks `repaint`, `cover`, `cover-nofsq`; splitting into stems needs the base model and comes only after measuring, stage E2). Own admin switch Features → Editing music (`chat.musik_bearbeiten`, below Making music); once released it holds for every profile, never for guests, never over MCP. Kinds (`musik.ARTS`, `musik.plan_edit` checks every number with fixed limits): **Part anew** (a range on the wave, a sentence what should be different), **Extend** (at the front or the end, 5 to 120 s), **Genre** (ten buttons Pop, Rock, Reggae, Jazz, Classical, Electronic, Hip-hop, Schlager, Big band, Acoustic or own words, slider "free ↔ close to the original" in five steps, BPM), **Remix**, **Change lyrics** (the lyrics as parts verse, chorus, pre-chorus, bridge, intro, outro, instrumental: change the text, add, remove and move parts, in the panel by dragging the handle ⠿ with mouse or finger or with ↑/↓, in the iPhone app by holding and dragging; the song is then made anew close to its old sound or with "Make it completely anew" in its own length for added verses; Dominik 2026-10-20 "texte anpassen strophen hinzufügen und verschieben … immer auch mit drag and drop"), and without a model at once **Cut & fade** and **Tempo & key** (75 to 125 %, ±4 half tones; ffmpeg on the model computer, fixed filters only, one run at a time, 120 s limit, does not need the place of the heavy jobs) and **Detect tempo and key** (`/understand`). With the model up to three suggestions in one run; everything outside the chosen range keeps the original sound sample for sample (the engine does that itself). Every change is a new **version** at the original (`root`, `von`, `label`), at most 10 per song; the list shows each song once with "· N versions", the versions in the details with ▶ to compare, "As a song of its own", Download and Delete; quick filters Uploaded and Edited. AI versions carry the mark "AI-edited". **Upload an own file** ("⋯" menu): MP3, WAV, FLAC, M4A, OGG up to 30 MB and 10 minutes, 10 a day, only with the tick "This is my own recording or I own the rights to it" (`rechte=1`); the panel checks the first bytes, the model computer reads the file with ffmpeg from a pipe (only `file,pipe`) and returns its own MP3 without foreign tags, without the AI mark until it is edited. In the conversation the tool `musik_bearbeiten` (Weiche group `musik`, e.g. "Make my last song 30 seconds longer", "Turn my song into a reggae version"): the panel decides which song (always the newest own one), never the model; new lyrics only on the page. Songs per day, space, queue and Notaus of Making music apply. State → Live: target "Making music" also edited, uploaded and detected (counts). Logs (area "Musik"): `musik: bearbeitung richtung 2 vorschlaege seite fuer …`, `musik: bearbeitet richtung 2 fassungen 31.0 s fuer …`, `musik: bearbeitet tempo ohne modell fuer …`, `musik: hochgeladen mp3 182 s fuer …`, `musik: hochladen nicht gemacht rechte|gross|format|dienst fuer …`, `musik: erkannt 3 felder fuer …`, `musik: fassung eigenes lied fuer …`; never the wish, the text or the file name. API (profile, also the app): `POST /api/musik/hochladen?name=&rechte=1` (the file as the body), `POST /api/musik/{id}/bearbeiten` (`{art, …}`), `POST /api/musik/{id}/erkennen`, `POST /api/musik/{id}/eigenes`; on the model computer `POST /v1/musik/quelle|bearbeiten|ton|erkennen`.

**Writing lyrics for songs (V01.0.868, part of Making music, Dominik 2026-10-20 "musik ohne text sollte auch text generiert werden können aufgrund vom prompt"):** Under *Me → Music* with "With singing", the button **Write lyrics** stands next to "Lyrics"; with empty lyrics "Make the song" starts it as well. The language model writes verses and a chorus from the description with `[verse]`/`[chorus]`/`[bridge]`, in the chosen language and fitting the length (`musik.text_pairs`: about one verse-chorus pair per 45 s, 1 to 4). The lyrics appear in the field to change; the song is made only with the next click. The description goes to the language model as data in the block `<<<WUNSCH … WUNSCH>>>`. **Artist names** never reach the music model: the language model names the artists it found and describes their style in style words (e.g. "big band, swing, crooner vocals"); the panel cuts the clause "im Stil von …" with its names out of the description by a fixed rule (`musik.CLAUSE`), adds "Stil: …" and checks that neither the names the model gave nor the names behind "im Stil von" stay in the lyrics or the description, else it refuses. `musik.ARTIST` now also catches "im stiel …" with and without "von"; "so wie" no longer counts, and the artist rule holds for the wish and the description, not for own lyrics (there "wie von selbst" is just a line). In the conversation (`musik_machen`, request 18adb7): when `beschreibung_en` names an artist, the result tells the language model in fixed words to send the description again with style and sound words only (`musik.ARTIST_AGAIN`), and the person's own words lose the clause "im Stil von …" before they become the song's title. The panel checks the word list and secrets before the language model and on the lyrics afterwards. Works with the switch Making music, no switch of its own; Notaus (jobs) and the maintenance pause of the language model hold it off. Also in the iPhone app (Music: "With singing", lyrics, "Write lyrics"). State → Live: target "Making music" also "N lyrics written". Logs (area "Musik"): `musik: liedtext geschrieben 2 teile stil statt namen fuer …`, `musik: liedtext nicht geschrieben aus|notaus|wartung|leer|wortliste|geheim|kuenstler|sprachmodell <error kind> fuer …`; never the description or the lyrics. API (profile, also the app): `POST /api/musik/liedtext` (`{wunsch, sekunden, sprache}` → `{liedtext, wunsch, teile, namen, beschreibung}`, 16 KB, 10 per minute).

**Long description as a template (V01.0.926, Dominik 2026-10-22 "wir benötigen um einiges mehr an eingabe zeichen"):** The own description under *Me → Music* (and in the iPhone app) may now be up to **2000 characters** long instead of 300 (`musik.MAX_WISH`), about one page, e.g. a firm's text as the template for a song; as much as the document wish (`docmake.MAX_WISH`). The music model itself reads at most 600 characters (`musik.MAX_CAPTION`); so "Write lyrics" also returns a short English music description (`beschreibung`: style, mood, instruments, tempo, voice, no names; dropped with "in the style of"), which the page and the app send with "Make the song" as long as the description was not changed by hand. Without it the first 600 characters of the own words go to the music model. The panel checks the music description like the wish (word list, secrets, artists). The 16 KB body limit and the rate limits stay. API: `POST /api/musik` also takes `beschreibung` (at most 600 characters).

**AI Dungeon Master (V01.0.949, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-22 "ok und das ganze vom admin etwas anpassbar und erweiterbar", "soll auch gutes loot und leveling geben", "Die Spieler sollen Equipment traden untereinander können Charakter speichern und mitnehmen in nächstes Spiel"):** admin switch *Features → My data → AI Dungeon Master* (`chat.dungeon`, off by default; released for every profile the admin does not block; never for the assistant's guests). A role-playing game for up to five players on a page of its own in the menu **Dungeon Master** with the tabs *Rounds* and *Heroes*. Whoever starts a round picks title, world (fantasy, horror, science fiction, pirates, own), opening, tone (from 8, 12 or 16 with its own word list), turn time (1, 2 or 5 minutes), duration (6 hours, 1 day, 1 week) and a hero (name, people, class) and may change the Dungeon Master prompt; the panel's fixed rules always stand behind it. **Invite** makes a seat link with a QR code per player (`https://<panel>/#spiel=<code>`): whoever opens it plays as a guest without a profile or with their own profile. **Flow:** everyone writes what their character does (at most 300 characters); once all have acted, the time is up or the host taps "Go on now", the own language model answers in two steps in a fixed JSON schema: first which checks it needs, then the narration with consequences, loot, successes, NPC lines, scene and whom it asks. **The panel decides** (`dungeon.py`, rules and catalogue as data in `dungeon_regeln.json`, checked by `check_rules`): it rolls d20 plus value against a difficulty of 5 to 20 (20 always succeeds, 1 always fails), limits damage and healing to 1 to 6 (minus armour), takes only names of the group and kinds from fixed lists, refuses words of the tone and of the list "nie" and addresses a player who wrote nothing for two turns by name itself. A broken answer gets a second try, then a fixed sentence stands there and the turn stays open. **Loot and levels:** the model only reports the occasion (foe, chest, reward, boss) with a name and a sentence; rarity (d100 by occasion and group level, common to legendary, epic from level 2, legendary from level 5, pity after 6 finds), the item fitting the class, bonus, effect, receiver (who went longest without a find) and gold come from the panel. Experience by fixed numbers (a check passed, foes by rank, the scene's goal for everyone), levels 1 to 10, at most one level per game evening, credited only at the end of the round or chapter (since V01.1.008); every level brings life and a value point, levels 2, 4, 6, 8 and 10 an ability out of two offers. On the own sheet: equip, unequip, drink a potion, give to another player. **Trading:** "Offer a trade" picks a player, own things from the bag and gold for things from their bag and gold (up to 4 per side, at most 3 open offers per player); only when they tap "Accept" does the panel check again that both own everything and have room in the bag (12), and swap in one step; "Decline" or "Withdraw" takes it back, a traded thing drops out of every other offer. **Taking heroes along:** heroes of profiles stay stored with level, values, equipment and gold (`users/<profile>/dungeon-helden.json`, up to 8) and come along into the next round; a hero only ever plays in one running round. A guest taps "Get a hero code": shown once, valid 30 days, redeemable once, a newer code replaces the older one; with it they play on in a new round or take the hero into their profile. **Admin:** *Features → AI Dungeon Master*, button "Adjust rules", adds or changes worlds, tones, peoples, classes with abilities, items, effects, rarities, curves for experience and loot, limits and the default prompt as JSON (`dungeon-regeln.json` in the state folder): an entry with the same id replaces the shipped one, `"aus": true` takes it out, new ids are added; the panel refuses a rule set with errors with the list of errors and uses the shipped rules when the file is broken. **Protection:** the code stands after the `#`, the Spark keeps only its hash; other sites (Origin) are refused, every request counts against its own rate limit, wrong codes lock like a wrong PIN; actions and narration are data (in the block `<<< … >>>`, markers defused), unlock nothing and never go into memory; a guest only sees their round, never the assistant, a tool or a setting. Status → Live (target "Dungeon Master") only shows rounds, players, guests, turns, loot, levels, fallbacks and refusals; Logs (area "Dungeon Master") only fixed words and numbers (`dungeon: runde neu | fantasy | ab 12 | Profil …`, `dungeon: zug | ok | 2 proben | 3100 ms`, `dungeon: beute | boss | rar`, `dungeon: stufe | 4`, `dungeon: tausch | angenommen`). *Delete all game data* under Heroes also works while the admin blocked the game. API: `GET /api/profile/dungeon`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/runde`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/aktion|held|weiter|pause|platz`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/beitreten`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/held/{hid}`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/daten`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/platz|beitreten|lage|aktion|held|figur` (`{code, …}`); admin `GET|PUT|DELETE /api/admin/dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master stage 2 (V01.0.953, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "Stufe 2", Dominik 2026-10-23 "Alles umsetzten" = recommendations 1a to 5a):** **Campaigns:** the host taps "End chapter" in the round. The round ends, its narrations, the profiles' heroes and copies of the guests' heroes go into a campaign (`users/<profile>/dungeon-kampagnen.json`, up to 10). In the background the language model writes "The story so far" (fixed JSON schema, at most 800 characters, the earlier summary and the narrations only as game text in the block `<<< … >>>`); the panel checks it like every narration (no markup, no links, no words of the tone and of the list "nie"). If the answer does not fit or the model is paused, the last narrations stand there as a *draft*. In the tab *Rounds*, section *Campaigns*, the host can change the text (same check), have it written again, delete it or "Start chapter N": a new round with the same settings and their hero, the model gets "The story so far" as game text. Guests get new seat links and pick "Your character from the last chapter" when they join; each character can be taken only once. **Reading aloud:** a switch when starting and the host's "🔊 Read aloud/Reading off". After each turn the speech output speaks the narration in the narrator's voice and each NPC line in the NPC voice (the admin picks both at the top of "Adjust rules", `stimmen` in the rules); only one reading job of the game at a time, at most 1800 characters per turn. The sound is kept as WAV in the state folder (`dungeon-ton/`, the last 3 turns per round, it goes with the round) and is the same file for every seat: on the host's device it plays by itself, the others tap "🔊 Listen". **Speak instead of typing:** 🎤 at the action field records up to 30 seconds, the browser turns it into a 16 kHz WAV, the Spark's speech recognition writes the text into the own field; sending stays a tap. Guests may do this too (a transcript is not a tool), with their own rate limit `dungeonohr` and at most 1 MB per recording. **Apps:** iPhone *My things → Dungeon Master* shows rounds, heroes and campaigns with "The story so far", swiping deletes a hero or a campaign; "Play" gets a code for the own seat (shown once, only its hash stays, a newer one replaces it, no hero code for a profile's hero) and opens the table in the app, with microphone and reading aloud. New rounds and chapters are started in the browser. Android shows the panel page as before. Status → Live also counts campaigns, chapters ended, turns read aloud and spoken; Logs: `dungeon: kapitel ende | 2`, `dungeon: bisher | ok | 4200 ms`, `dungeon: vorlesen | ok | 3 teile | 9000 ms`, `dungeon: hoeren | ok | 48 zeichen | 900 ms`, `dungeon: sitzcode | app`. API for it: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/kapitel|vorlesen|hoeren|sitzcode`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/ton/{n}`, `PUT|DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/neu|weiter`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/ton|hoeren`. **More variety (Dominik 2026-10-23 "Können wir die Gegenstände varianz um einiges vergrößern. Zauber und Tränke ebenso"):** the catalogue now has 44 weapons (up to 11 per class), 18 armours, 17 pieces of jewellery, 18 potions, 22 spells, 12 other things and 28 effects. Potions heal and/or give a **blessing** (a value +1 to +4 for 1 to 5 turns, e.g. giant's draught strength +4 for one turn). **Spells** (new kind `zauber`, scrolls and spells, some only for certain classes) are cast with "Cast" from the bag: once, on yourself or the whole group, with healing and/or a blessing by the catalogue; the log shows "✦ … casts …", the model only learns who cast what and narrates it. A blessing counts in every check and wears off after narrated turns, at most 4 at once; the sheet shows "Strength +2 · 2 turns left". The admin adds own potions and spells under "Adjust rules" (`heilung` 0 to 30, `segen` {wert, bonus, zuege}, for spells `ziel` selbst or gruppe and `satz`); the panel checks them like everything else.

**AI Dungeon Master at the table, wave A (V01.1.056, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge." on the 18 ideas from tabletop projects, plan `/mnt/project-files/plaene/dungeon-tabletop-ideen.md`):** **Multiattack (point 12):** a foe can have `angriffe` in the rule data (2 to `ANGRIFFE_MAX` = 5, checked by `check_rules`); a boss always attacks at least twice (`dungeon_kampf.blows`). In the foes' turn the panel rolls every attack on its own against a standing hero; the foe window shows "2 × 1d8+2", the rule page the row "Attacks per turn", the strength of encounters (`BUDGET`) is tuned anew for it, the test bench stays at 70 to 90 % wins. **A foe's life as a word (point 11):** players no longer see a foe's life and maximum but its state (`dungeon_kampf.lage`): unhurt, bruised (above half), badly hurt (above a quarter), nearly beaten. Only the host sees the numbers; the view removes them for all other seats from the encounter card too. In the car the state says "The ogre is badly hurt". A state on a hero shows how many turns it still holds ("poisoned, 2 turns left"). **Names for listening (point 14):** whoever speaks at the table instead of typing has the recording sent to speech recognition with up to `HOER_WORTE` (60) names of the round (`dungeon.heard_words`): the heroes at the table, the people and places of the map, the foes of the fight and the things in one's own bag, never anything from the round's hidden truth. While a profile plays in the car, these names also come first when speaking in the car (`dungeon_auto.words`, `WORTE_S` 600 s). **Dice receipt (point 2):** every roll of the panel gets a number (W-1, W-2 …, `dungeon_erzaehler.receipts`), shown small next to the roll in the feed and sent to the model with all its numbers. If the narration names a die, a sum or a damage that is on no receipt of this turn, the panel repairs it by fixed rules (fault `beleg`, hint "The dice receipts hold: …"). **Read along (point 16):** while reading aloud, every device marks the sentence being spoken in its turn's narration; the reading state brings the words of every finished piece for it (`lesung.texte`), the device finds them in the narration by their words, also across highlights and quote marks. **Live:** Status → Live counts foes with multiattack and per day narrations repaired for numbers without a receipt and recordings heard with the round's names. Self-tests in `test_dungeon`: the boss test with multiattack, `test_a_foes_life_is_a_word_for_the_players`, `test_dice_receipts_hold_the_numbers`, the speaking test with the names, `test_reading_aloud_piece_by_piece_on_this_device` with the mark; `test_angriffe_zwei_bis_fuenf` in `test_dungeon_srd`.

**AI Dungeon Master: oracle (V01.1.062, tabletop point 8, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", yes/no with a chaos grade as common in solo play, own code; tables from Datasworn, CC-BY-4.0):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Oracle* (`chat.dungeon_orakel`, off from the start; `dungeon_orakel.py`). The host, alone at the table so in solo play, asks in the game master console under "🔮 Oracle" (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/orakel`, host only): a yes-or-no question with a likelihood (certain 90, likely 70, even 50, unlikely 30, hardly 10 %) or up to `TABELLEN_MAX` (3) tables of the oracle group (action, theme, place, descriptor, twist, complication, clue, peril, opportunity, trouble) or own tables of the admin. The round's chaos grade (1 to 9, start 5, a slider next to it) moves the chance by `CHAOS_SCHRITT` (5) percentage points per step, at most 5 to 95. The panel rolls a d100: in the lowest fifth of the yes "Yes, and …", in the highest fifth of the no "No, and …"; a double (11, 22 …) up to the chaos grade brings an event from action and theme. At most `ZUG_MAX` (6) questions a turn. Every answer stands in the feed with roll and chance; the model gets this turn's answers only as data (`orakel`) and the fixed rule to hold to them. **Own tables:** the Dungeon Master tab "Rules" has the part "Tables" with all tables of the catalog; "Own copy" and "Edit" add an own table (group `orakel` or `eigen`, 2 to 300 rows of `[German, English]` each, if wanted only for single worlds), `dungeon_tabellen.check_rules` checks it like all rules. **Live:** Status → Live counts per day questions asked and rounds with an own chaos grade. Self-test `test_the_oracle_answers_by_the_panels_dice` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: narrator bench (V01.1.065, tabletop point 4, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", agreed with the thread "Eigene Modell-Installation statt qwen38": against the running model in the panel, not in "modelle messen", fixed rules decide):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Narrator bench* (`chat.dungeon_erzaehlstand`, off from the start; `dungeon_erzaehlstand.py`). In the Dungeon Master tab "Rules" at "Narrator bench", "Check now" (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/erzaehlstand`, admin only, at most every `KNOPF_S` (120) seconds, one run at a time) plays eight fixed scenes through the same steps as a turn at the table (`dungeon.ask_checks` and `dungeon.tell`: system text, facts, schema, checks and rewrites are the same) on a round that lives only in memory: nothing is saved, no seat, no profile, no day counter. The scenes: a failed check (a fixed die) must not succeed, two heroes both appear, a fight with the panel's rolls names the foes by name and no number without a receipt, the children's tone without a word of its list, a person of the truth answers by name, an English round is told in English (at most `ENGLISCH_MAX` German words), a risky action asks for a check, a harmless one does not. Per scene 2 points when the first try passes, 1 after a rewrite, 0 when refused or only the panel's repair is left. With "Classification with probabilities" on, the model's own view stands beside it (`systemone` with the use `erzaehler`: how vivid, how closely it follows the actions and rolls), it gives no points. The result per model name (`llm.known_model`) is in `STATE/dungeon-erzaehlstand.json`, the last `MODELLE_MAX` (8) models side by side, only points, reasons and times, never narration text. When changing models: measure, switch, then "Check now". Since this version the turn at the table uses the same steps (`ask_checks`, `roll_facts`, `tell`), there is only one source. **Live:** Status → Live names the models measured and the best score. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_erzaehlstand`.

**AI Dungeon Master: narrator errors from a 👎 with a reason, taken over as bench scenes (V01.1.086, Dominik 2026-10-25 "können wir diese fehler für die zukunf immer abfangen und verbessern?", "1-3 und 1-7 umsetzten", points 2 and 3):** Part of the switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Narrator bench* (`chat.dungeon_erzaehlstand`, off by default), no switch of its own. While it is on, the table shows after a 👎 on one of the last three turns the question "What was wrong?" with fixed reasons (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.GRUENDE`: a failed check done anyway or something revealed, decided for another character, an item or spell missing, the fight does not match, gold or loot only told, wrong place, a person looks different or is elsewhere, repeats itself, something else; never free text). The reason goes with the 👎 (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/daumen` or `/api/dungeon/daumen` with `grund`), the panel keeps the turn as a case (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.case`): the actions, the panel's dice with their outcome and the narration of that turn, every hero with a name of the bench (Arvid, Bela, Cora, Dario, Ella, also in the genitive), without profile, seat or round id, only a hash of round and turn, so a second 👎 of the same turn replaces the case. The newest `FAELLE_MAX` (40) stand in `STATE/dungeon-erzaehlfehler.json`. In the Dungeon Master tab "Rules" at "Narrator errors" the admin sees every case (`GET /api/admin/dungeon/erzaehlfehler`), deletes it or takes it over with "Take over as a bench scene" (`POST` with `{fall, aktion: szene|loeschen}` or `{szene, aktion: loeschen}`, at most `EIGENE_MAX` (12) scenes, deleting works with the switch off too). Every run of the narrator bench plays the scenes taken over after the fixed ones: the same actions on a fresh round of the same world, tone and language, every check with the same outcome (`same_dice`: 20 for a passed one, 2 for a failed one, if need be the difficulty one above the total); the round's map, fight and people are not carried. Points as for the fixed scenes, the most grows with them. The result file stays without narration text. **Live:** Status → Live names the number of cases and of scenes taken over. Log lines "dungeon: erzaehlstand | fall | neu/ersetzt/leer/aus/uebernommen/geloescht/fehlt/voll/ungueltig". Self-tests `NarratorCases` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fix narrator errors, the narration learns from reports (V01.1.088, Dominik 2026-10-25 "können wir solche fehler in zukunkt direkt über das spiel melden und es wird im hintergrund automatisch korrigiert", "empfehlung" for 1 to 5):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Fix narrator errors* (`chat.dungeon_erzfehler`, off by default, counts for every profile the admin does not block; `dungeon_erzaehlstand.FIX`). With it (or the narrator bench) the table offers the fixed reasons after a 👎, new among them "Action misunderstood (question, direction)"; every reason has a fixed hint (`GRUENDE`, German and English). **1 Tell again:** a 👎 with a reason on the last narration has it told again once (`dungeon_erzaehler.retell_fixed`, at most once per turn, not while a turn, an X-card or another new telling runs, a stuck one frees after `NEU_S` (300) seconds). The model gets the reason's hint as its task, the old scene, the turn's actions and the outcome of every check only as data; the new narration must pass the word lists, the veils and the same fixed checks (`erz.judge` on the turn as it was, at most after the panel's rescue), else the old one stays. Dice, life, finds and place never change; the turn's pictures and sound files go only with a new text (`dungeon_grenzen.replace`, the same source as the X-card). The table shows "being told again …", then "told again" at the turn. **2 A world's hints:** every report counts with time, world and reason (numbers only, `meldungen`, at most `MELDUNGEN_MAX`); when a reason comes `WELT_SCHWELLE` (3) times within `WELT_TAGE` (30) days in a world, that world's narrator gets its fixed hint in its task for good (`world_task`, never words of a report). **3 Every night:** while the narrator bench is on and has scenes taken over, it plays all scenes once a night (2 to 5 o'clock, `due`), so every new model and version meets the reported errors. **4 Weeks:** the page "Narrator errors" shows the reports per week and reason of the last `WOCHEN` (6) weeks and the reasons that became a world's hint. **5 New rules:** clusters show on the page; a new fixed rule is built by the Dungeon thread, never by the system itself. **Live:** Status → Live names the scenes told again today and those being told again now. Log lines "dungeon: erzaehlfehler | neu erzaehlt | ok/bleibt/ausfall" and "| neu erzaehlen | aus/nein", "dungeon: erzaehlstand | nacht | start". Self-tests `NarratorCases` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: adventure import (V01.1.066, tabletop point 10, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge."; only the idea from open-tabletop-gm, own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adventure import* (`chat.dungeon_import`, off by default; `dungeon_import.py`). In the story planner (Dungeon Master tab "Rules" → "Stories" → "New story") "📄 Adventure from a file" stands at the top: a written adventure as PDF, Word, OpenDocument, Markdown, text or HTML (`TYPEN`, at most `MAX_BYTES` = 10 MB) becomes a draft (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/import`, a form with file, world, tone, acts, taboos and language; admin only, rate limit `dungeonplan` like the planner). The panel reads the text with the document pipeline (`documents.extract`; the file is not kept and does not go into the knowledge), cuts it at its headings by the same fixed rules as the document search (`documents.heading`) and gives the model a digest: every heading with the start of its section, at most `ABSCHNITTE_MAX` (60) sections and `DIGEST_MAX` (12000) characters, as data in the block `<<< … >>>` (markers defused, the file cannot close the block). The first heading counts as the title, the others as chapters; without a chosen number the story gets as many acts as chapters (3 to 5). The model joins the chapters in order into acts and keeps places and foe; it takes no game values, rules, loot or instructions from the file, and only the planner's fields come back. The panel checks the answer in the fixed schema (`SCHEMA_PLAN`) like a typed story (`check_plan`: lengths, number of acts, tone, "never", taboos), two tries; only "Check and save" keeps it. Fixed refusals with a log line: `aus`, `dateiart`, `zu gross`, `unlesbar` (such as a PDF without a text layer), `leer` (a digest of less than `TEXT_MIN` characters), `eingabe`, `sprachmodell pausiert`, `kein vorschlag`. Logs only fixed words and numbers (`dungeon: import | ok | 3 kapitel | 3 akte`, never the file's name or text). **Live:** Status → Live counts the adventures taken and refused today. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_import`.

**AI Dungeon Master: quest items (V01.1.069, point 15 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alles umsetzten" and 12:33 "echte Quest-Gegenstände", own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Quest items* (`chat.dungeon_quest`, off from the start; `dungeon_quest.py`). A quest item is a named thing the story needs (a key, a map, a seal): its own sign ✦, no weight, never for sale, passed from hero to hero. It comes three ways, each by fixed rules: an act of the story planner names its thing (field "Special thing", 2 to 40 characters, checked like the rest of the story with tone, "never" and taboos) and it lies in the scene while the act runs (`q_akt1` …); the map lays one at a place or in a vault (`dungeon.quest_legen(rd, ort_id, ding)`, the same id twice changes nothing); or the narration reports a find (`quest_fund`, at most one a turn): the panel takes it only when the narration names it, the name has 2 to 40 characters, passes the word lists, the round does not have it yet and counts fewer than `QUEST_MAX` (12) things; it goes to the finder, else it lies here. When a hero's action names a thing lying where the group stands, the hero takes it (before the narration, the model hears it as `quest_genommen`); what the group carries (`quest_gruppe`) and what lies here (`quest_hier`) the model hears only as data. In the hero sheet, tab "Bag", "✦ Special things" lists the own things with "Give …" and "Put down", and what the others carry; under "Lying here" one picks up what the narration has named (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/held` with `questgeben`, `questablegen`, `questaufheben`, for guests `/api/dungeon/held`). Doors and ways of the map open only when an action names the thing and the group carries it (`dungeon.quest_genannt`, `dungeon.quest_haben`); a used thing vanishes with a line in the feed (`dungeon.quest_verbrauchen`) and is never laid again. When a carrier dies or leaves the table, the thing lies where the group stands. Carried things go into the next chapter with their hero; things left lying stay behind. Players see a thing only once the narration has named it or someone carries it; the host sees all with their place (`quest_geheim`, host only; in the view filter `quest_versteckt` and `quest_anderswo`). Journal lines `dungeon: quest | …` with fixed words only; the live overview counts things, carried ones and today's taken, found, passed on and used ones.

**AI Dungeon Master: attacking several foes of one kind (V01.1.071, fault of 2026-10-25, Dominik: a spell rolled “geschafft” against two giant rats, both stayed at 7/7):** An attack happens only when a check in a fight has a target naming a foe. Until now `ziel` had to match the name exactly (“Riesenratte 1”); when the model named “Riesenratten” or no target at all, it stayed an ordinary check without damage. Now fixed rules decide (`dungeon_kampf._named`): first the whole name, then its start, then the kind (the name without its number, also as a plural or only its last part: “Riesenratten”, “die Ratten”, “giant rats”); it hits the first one still standing this turn. A check without a target still attacks when the player's action holds a word of attacking (`HIEB_WORTE`: greifen, schlagen, stechen, schießen, attack …) and names a foe that way; “I listen at the door” stays a check. When the action names magic (`MAGIE_WORTE`) but no spell of the list, and the hero's class can cast, the attack is a cantrip: 1d6 plus the better of Intelligence, Wisdom, Charisma, kind force, magical; otherwise the hero attacks with the weapon. The model only gives data for it; target and damage come from the panel.

**AI Dungeon Master: tables in full (V01.1.072, Dominik 2026-10-25 “ich sehe gar nicht die tabellen”):** In the Dungeon Master tab "Rules", part "Tables", the detail of a table now shows every row with its number, above it the die (d and the number of rows, every row as often; in a world only the rows for it) and, for a row that only counts in some worlds, those worlds. The rows sit in their own box that scrolls by itself. In the list every table has a sign (such as 👁️ Looks, 🔮 an oracle table without its own, ✎ an own one; `dungeon_regelseite.TAB_ZEICHEN`) instead of a dot. Only a view for the admin, the endpoints stay the same (`GET /api/admin/dungeon/katalog/tabellen`, every row with `tafel`).

**AI Dungeon Master: faster on the Pi (V01.1.073, point 14 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alles umsetzten", recommendation 14a, own code):** Every round is a file of its own (`dungeon_ablage.py`, state folder `dungeon-runden/<rid>.json`); `dungeon-runden.json` keeps only the shared values of all rounds (pictures of the day, thumbs per world). The old file with every round is split once on the first read. A cache keeps every file's text by its change stamp (time, size, file): only a changed file is read and brought up to date, every caller gets a fresh copy; only rounds whose content changed are written, an ended round's file is removed. Running rounds are not in the backup (they go hours after their end; heroes and campaigns live in the profile and are backed up). The table's poll every 3 seconds (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}?stand=…`, for guests `POST /api/dungeon/lage` with `stand`) gets `{"neu": false}` while the seat's stand holds: the round's change stamp, who is there, the reading aloud, pictures on their way and who types; while the Dungeon Master answers and at the latest every `STAND_S` (10) seconds the whole view comes. The poll checks the seat and a due turn on that one round only. The page loads only `dungeonlader.js`; the five game scripts (`DM_SKRIPTE`) come on the first opening of the Dungeon Master or of a seat link, with the running version as `?v=`. The live overview counts the "nothing new" answers. Splitting `dungeon.py` into modules (trade, pictures, voices) waits until no other thread builds on the file (guideline part 17).

**AI Dungeon Master: game master console as a window and a draggable column (V01.1.074, Dominik 2026-10-25 “die rechte spalte ist zu klein und unübersichtlich”, chosen “vorschlag 2 mit vorschlag 3 kombiniert”):** The table's right column now shows only the quest, the own hero with its buttons, for the host “▶ Go on now” (with “waiting for …”) and “🎲 Open the game master console”, below them the tabs Map, Threads and Date; the tab “Host” is gone. The game master console is a big window in the table's look (since V01.1.079 as cards, see below) with three columns: “This turn” (pause, market, behind “⋯” end chapter or round), “At the table” (read aloud, scene picture, invite with the link, last sentence in the mail) and “Narrator” (way of telling, narrator's voice, combat stage, game master dial); below them stand the story, random rules, oracle, truth of this chapter and the things. “Go on now” also stands on top of the console and closes it, so the host sees the narration; ✕, Esc or a tap beside it close it like every window. On the phone everything stands one below the other. The edge between the story and the right column can be dragged (or moved with the arrow keys), from 260 to 560 pixels, 320 instead of 290 from the start; the width is kept per device (`dm-breite`), a double click sets it back. Where the table has only one column there is no edge. Only the view changes: every button sends the same request as before, the panel checks it as before.

**AI Dungeon Master: end or delete every round (V01.1.075, Dominik 2026-10-25 “runden sollen immer löschbar bzw. beendbar sein. mit nachfrage wirklich.”):** In the list “Your rounds” every round has its buttons next to “Open”: the host can “End” a running round or “Delete” it at once, and “Delete” an ended one; a player hides an ended round. The first tap asks “Really end?”, “Really delete?” or “Really hide?”, only the second does it. “Delete” on a running round first ends it like every end (experience is paid, the heroes of profiles stay) and then deletes it with its sounds and pictures (`DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}?loeschen=1`, host only; a player gets 404). A player cannot end a running round of another host.

**AI Dungeon Master: a question stays a question, down stays down (V01.1.087, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich frage nach dem keller - es geht nach oben!?"):** "frage den kellner ob du in den keller gehen kannst" got a Dexterity check "Kellner ablenken und unauffällig zum Keller gehen", and the narration sent Morpheus up the stairs without asking the waiter. Three fixed rules: an action that only asks or talks (`dungeon_erzaehler.asks`: a word of `FRAGT`, not "frage mich", no word of `dungeon.RISIKO`, which now also knows "unauffällig" and "ablenk") gets no check or at most one on `FRAGE_WERT` (Charisma), never one with sneaking or distracting (`check_proben`, line "dungeon: probe | verworfen | absicht"; the model's task says so too). When an action names a way down (`UNTEN`: cellar, hinab, hinunter …) and the narration only one up (`OBEN`: hinauf, oben angekommen, attic …), or the other way round, that is the fault "richtung" (`wrong_way`); the rescue strikes the sentences with the wrong direction. When the narration of such a question tells neither question nor answer (no word of `ANTWORT`, no quote of at least `ZITAT_WOERTER` (3) words), that is the fault "frage" (`not_asked`); the rescue adds "… asks the question and waits for the answer.". Both with a fixed hint for the second try, day counters `regel_richtung`, `regel_frage` in the live line of narrations improved. Self-test `test_a_question_stays_a_question_and_the_way_down_stays_down`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the new place is in the narration (V01.1.092, with the map thread):** When `ort_id` leads the group to a neighbouring place of the map that the narration does not name (`dungeon_karte.ort_fehlt`), that is a fault "neuer_ort" of the fixed check (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`). The model writes once more with the hint "with ort_id the group goes to …, but your narration does not name the place" (the name comes from the map, as data). If it fails again, the group stays where it is (`ort_id` empty, log `dungeon: nachbessern | fest | neuer_ort`), so flag and narration agree. Live counts it with the repaired narrations.

**AI Dungeon Master: Dungeon judge (V01.1.093, Tabletop point 2 with the helper model):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Dungeon judge* (`chat.dungeon_richter`, off by default, needs *Classification with probabilities*). When the first narration of a turn passes every fixed check, `dungeon_erzaehler.richter` asks one fixed yes/no question over `systemone` (use "richter", with *Helper model → For the Dungeon Master* the small model first): does the narration contradict the actions or the outcome of the dice? Actions, checks (only succeeded or failed) and narration go along as data. The model only gives the probability; from 90 % (`RICHTER_MIN`) the model writes once more, with a fixed hint. If the new version fails a fixed check, the first one stays (`dungeon: richter | bleibt`). If the judge does not answer within 8 seconds or is unsure, the turn goes on without it. Log `dungeon: richter | ok | n %`, `| neu | n %`, `| ausfall | …`; Live: judge today ok, written again, without answer (numbers only).

**AI Dungeon Master: pause shown everywhere (V01.1.096, Dominik 2026-10-26 "Wenn der Dungeon Master ein Spiel pausiert, dann sollte auch angezeigt werden, dass das Spiel sich gerade in Pause befindet"):** When the host pauses a round (game master console, card "Turn", "⏸ Pause"), it now shows wherever the round is seen: in the round list in the browser and the Android app "⏸ paused" instead of "running", in the iPhone app in the round's row and in CarPlay after the turn. At the table a bar "⏸ Paused" stands above the narration for every seat and every guest (the clock stands still, actions can be written already); the host has "▶ Go on" right there. The other seats with a profile get one note each on pausing and on going on ("… is paused", "… goes on") over their ways (app, companion, watch), only when something changes and only who did not switch notes off at the table. In the car the Spark says "The round is paused right now" instead of "your turn". Live counts the paused rounds. The list carries `pause` for this (`GET /api/profile/dungeon`), log `dungeon: pause | an` or `| aus` and `dungeon: melden | pause` or `| weiter`.

**AI Dungeon Master: critical and fumble tables (V01.1.099, D&D idea 2, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Critical and fumble tables* (`chat.dungeon_krit`, off by default). When a hero rolls a natural 20 in a fight, the panel rolls a d12 on the critical table of the hit's kind of damage, on a natural 1 on the mild fumble table for melee, ranged or spell (`ideen.krit`, `ideen.patzer` in `dungeon_srd.json`). The line stands at the attack in the log (💥 or 🙃) and goes to the narrator as data (`kritisch`, `patzer`); the model never picks it. If it brings a state, the panel sets it for the line's turns: after a critical hit on the foe, after a fumble on the hero. On a foe it works by fixed rules: no turn (stunned, paralysed, unconscious …), disadvantage on its attacks (blinded, poisoned, prone …), advantage for the heroes against it, burning or bleeding costs it 1d4 life each turn. The fight bar shows its states; all end with their turns or with the fight. The catalog now also checks the content of the D&D ideas round 2 (Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir.") by fixed rules (`dungeon_ideen.check_rules`): the prices of a near miss (`knapp`, d20 without gaps, mild prices with `kinder`), props (`requisiten` with places and rooms of the map and an effect out of schaden_gruppe, umwerfen, deckung, vorteil, fluchtweg), heart's wishes (`wuensche`), companions (`gefaehrte_werte` level 1 to 10, `gefaehrten`, `gefaehrten_kinder`) and `licht` on tools (−1 always burns, like the Everburning torch, else 1 to 20 rooms). Live: critical and fumble tables rolled today. Log `dungeon: kampf | krit tabelle | w`, `| patzer tabelle | w`, `dungeon: kampf | gegner setzt aus`, `dungeon: kampf | gegner zustand`. Self-tests `Fight.test_critical_and_fumble_tables` and `Fight.test_the_round_two_tables_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: honours of the evening (V01.1.100, D&D idea 1, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Honours of the evening* (`chat.dungeon_ehrungen`, off by default; `dungeon_ehre.py`). While the round runs, the panel counts per seat from its own dice: hits, critical hits, damage, 20s and 1s on attacks and checks, checks passed, Dexterity checks passed, potions for friends and the life they gave (`rd["ehre"]`, never from the model). When the round ends (also with the end of a chapter), it gives fixed titles to the seat with the most: Blade of the evening (damage), Rescuer of the evening (help), Lucky one (20s), Quietest shadow (from 2 Dexterity checks) and Hard-luck hero (from 3 ones); on a tie the seat taken first. At the children's table they are Bravest heart, Best friend, Lucky one and Quietest fox; there is no hard-luck hero and no count of ones there. The log shows "🏅 Honours of the evening" with the titles and one certificate per hero with their numbers (log art `ehrung`, the music thread plays its fanfare with it); the round list in the browser and the iPhone app show the titles at the ended round (`ehrungen` in `GET /api/profile/dungeon`). Then the next evening counts anew. Live: honours given today. Log `dungeon: ehrung | n titel | m urkunden` or `| keine`. The line of a critical or fumble table at the attack now starts with "Critical:" or "Fumble:". Self-test `Fight.test_honours_of_the_evening`.

**AI Dungeon Master: near miss (V01.1.101, D&D ideas round 2, idea 1, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Near miss* (`chat.dungeon_knapp`, off by default; `dungeon_knapp.py`; the idea "Pay the Price" of Ironsworn and Starforged by Shawn Tomkin, via datasworn, CC BY 4.0, own texts). When a check misses its difficulty by 1 or 2 (never on a natural 1, never on attacks), the Spark asks the seat once a turn at the table: "Near miss! Give up, or make it after all at a rolled price?" with "Give up" and "Pay". The others see who is deciding. Who has not chosen after 20 seconds (`WARTE_S`) gives up. Who pays passes the check; the panel rolls the price with a d20 on the table `ideen.knapp` in `dungeon_srd.json` (at the children's table only the mild rows with `kinder`), the dice receipt says "knapp" and `preis` (the fixed id), the narration gets the line as data (`knapp_mit_preis`). The price works by fixed rules: noise on the map (`dungeon_karte.laerm`, once the map thread has it), disadvantage on the checks of the next turn, 1d4 damage (never below 1 life), a tool of the bag breaks (as on a 1, the smith mends it), up to 10 gold (never more than the hero has); time and trust (`zeit`, `nsc`) stay in the story. The Spark does not ask in the car. Doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/knapp` `{zug, wahl}` and for guests `POST /api/dungeon/knapp` `{code, zug, wahl}` with login or guest code, profile binding (only the own seat, only the turn asked, only once, only "zahlen" or "aufgeben"), CSRF, a size limit of 256 bytes and an own rate limit `dungeonknapp`. Live: asked, with a price, given up, without an answer, refused. Log `dungeon: knapp | frage | n plaetze`, `| gezahlt | art`, `| aufgegeben`, `| ohne antwort`, `| preis | art`, `| gewaehlt | wahl`, `| abgelehnt | …`. Self-test `Fight.test_a_near_miss_gives_up_or_pays_a_price`.

**AI Dungeon Master: helping and group checks (V01.1.102, D&D ideas round 2, idea 4, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Helping and group checks* (`chat.dungeon_helfen`, off by default; `dungeon_helfen.py`; after "Working Together" and "Group Checks" of the SRD 5.1, CC BY 4.0). **Helping:** When a seat's action has a word of help (`WORTE`: helfe, hilf, unterstütze, help, assist …) and another hero's name (a part of the name from 3 letters as a whole word), the panel rolls that hero's first check of the turn with advantage; a disadvantage cancels it. Never for oneself, never on attacks, only who can act, one helper per hero. The roll shows "🤝 with help from …", the narrator gets `geholfen_von`. **Group check:** When two or more seats roll the same value against the same difficulty in one turn, it is a group check: if at least half pass, all count as passed (`gruppe` on the roll, "👥 group check", for the narrator `gruppenprobe`). Both are fixed rules on the panel's rolls (`dungeon.roll`); the model only tells. No new endpoint. Live: helped today, group checks passed and not. Log `dungeon: helfen | vorteil` and `dungeon: helfen | gruppe | n von m | geschafft` or `| nicht`. Self-test `Fight.test_helping_and_group_checks`.

**AI Dungeon Master: writings (V01.1.103, D&D idea 3, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Writings* (`chat.dungeon_schrift`, off by default; `dungeon_schrift.py`; handouts for all, some or nobody as in NeonBrasil/DM-Dungeon-Music, BigJk/snd and Hinnful/Evermist, all MIT, own code). When the heroes find something written, the narration reports it in the field `schriftstueck` (at most one a turn): title, the text as it stands, the kind (letter, wanted poster, verse, diary, note, inscription) and the sender. The panel checks it by fixed rules (plain text, at most 600 characters, at least 20, the round's limits and the list "never") and keeps it in the round (`rd["schrift"]`, at most 40). At the table it appears at its turn as parchment with a seal sign, a handwriting font and the sender and stays in the tab "Writings" of the right column. The host sees "All · Nobody · names" below and decides with a tap who sees it (`POST …/erzaehler` `{schrift: {id, sicht}}`); a secret writing shows "only for you" at its receiver. Other seats never get a secret writing (the sight filter, secret `schrift_geheim`, with a leak test); the narrator knows the last three in its facts so the story keeps to them, but never decides who sees them. The app shows them at the game table. Live: found, dropped, for one seat only today. Log `dungeon: schrift | gefunden | art`, `| verworfen`, `| sicht | alle|niemand|ein platz`, `| sicht | abgelehnt`. Self-test `Fight.test_writings_as_parchment_for_whom_the_host_chooses`.

**AI Dungeon Master: monster album (V01.1.104, D&D idea 12, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Monster album* (`chat.dungeon_album`, off by default; `dungeon_album.py`, own code; the foes' sayings `sage` stand in the catalog `dungeon_srd.json`). When the panel's fight defeats a foe, every hero that was there when the fight began and still sits at the table counts one win over this kind of foe (`h["album"]`, kept with the hero like its bag, at most 400 kinds). The tab "Album" of the right column shows the own entries with stars per stage: after the first win name, picture, description and saying, after the third the values (level, life, armour, attack, damage), after the fifth weaknesses, resistances, immunities and the states its blows cause. Only the panel counts, from its own dice; the model gives nothing. When the hero is loaded only the catalog's foes with whole numbers stay (`album.fix`). The app shows the album at the game table. Live: new entries and stages reached today. Log `dungeon: album | stufe N`. Self-test `Fight.test_the_monster_album_knows_more_after_each_stage`.

**AI Dungeon Master: scars (V01.1.105, D&D idea 8, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Scars* (`chat.dungeon_narben`, off by default), below it *Scars only* (`chat.dungeon_narben_nur`, off by default); `dungeon_narben.py`, wounds as in giffyglyph/darker-dungeons (CC BY-NC-ND, idea only), own code; the tables `ideen.narben`, `narben_kinder` and `narben_sprueche` stand in `dungeon_srd.json`. When a hero stands up again after lying down (Gnade too) or a foe hits it critically, the panel rolls a d20 on the wound table: mostly only a scar, on a few rows a mild consequence, −1 on a value (in `dungeon.total`), until a healer in a settlement takes it. At most one wound per hero and turn, at most 8 per hero (`h["narben"]`, checked when loaded). *Scars only* never gives a consequence, the children's table only plasters. The healer is in the settlement's window (`dungeon_gold`, 8 gold per level of the group) while the switch is on or the hero still has a consequence; it takes every consequence, the scars stay. In a settlement a fixed line of `narben_sprueche` asks about a scar. The hero sheet shows the scars, a consequence in red; the log one line per wound; the narrator reads the last three per hero in its facts and never rolls itself. The app shows it all at the game table. Live: wounds, with a consequence, plasters, at the healer today. Log `dungeon: narbe | narbe|folge|pflaster | auf|gnade|krit`, `dungeon: dienst | heiler | N gold`, `| heiler | nein`. Self-test `Fight.test_scars_and_lasting_wounds_with_a_healer`.

**AI Dungeon Master: content of the D&D ideas round 2 (V01.1.112, ideas 1, 3, 6, 7 and 9, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** `dungeon_srd.json` holds the fixed tables under "ideen", checked by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`. **Near miss** (`knapp`): 20 prices on a d20, each with a fixed id (it goes into the dice receipt as "preis") and an effect out of noise, time, disadvantage, damage, tool, gold and NPC. At the children's table only noise and time apply (`kinder`). The rows follow the idea of "Pay the Price" from Ironsworn and Starforged (Shawn Tomkin, via datasworn, CC BY 4.0, credited in "lizenz") and are rewritten for the fixed effects. **Props** (`requisiten`): 58 with a sign, kinds of places and rooms and exactly one effect (schaden_gruppe, umwerfen, deckung, vorteil, fluchtweg). Every kind of place and room of the map has at least 3. **Light:** the new things torch (`holzfackel`, 1 gold) and glow stick (sci-fi), each with `licht` 4 rooms. **Heart's wishes** (`wuensche`): 12 for all worlds and 8 each for fantasy, horror, sci-fi and pirates, at most 160 characters each. **Companions:** `gefaehrte_werte` for levels 1 to 10 (life about 80 % of a hero, armour, attack, damage and pay per chapter), 4 templates per world in `gefaehrten` and 3 animal friends for the children's table in `gefaehrten_kinder`. All texts are our own, the sources of the ideas count only as ideas. Self-test `test_runde2_fuer_jede_szene_und_welt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: campfire and rest (V01.1.108, D&D idea 4, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Campfire and rest* (`chat.dungeon_rast`, off by default; `dungeon_rast.py`, short and long rest after the SRD 5.1, CC BY 4.0, own code; the tables `ideen.landschaften`, `nachtwachen` and `fragekarten` stand in `dungeon_srd.json`). Outside a fight the host calls "Short rest" or "Long rest" in the game master console (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/rast` `{art}`, only the host, rate limit `dungeon`). Short: every hero standing gets a quarter of its life back (at least 1), at most twice between two long rests. Long: the panel rolls three night watches with the d20 on the table of the landscape (the map place's kind, a vault is "Underground", else "On the road"): quiet, noise, visit or raid. Without a raid full life and every spell, with a raid only half the life and no spells; at the children's table a raid is only a noise. At most one long rest in 8 turns. Every seat gets a question at the campfire. While the rest waits for its narration `rd["rast"]` stands (the music plays "camp"); the narrator gets kind, landscape, night watches and questions in its facts and only tells, the next narrated turn ends the rest. Above the story a box shows the night watches and the own question; the app shows it at the game table. Live: short and long rests, disturbed nights, refused today. Log `dungeon: rast | kurz`, `| lang | 3 wachen | ruhig|gestoert`, `| abgelehnt | Error`. Self-test `Fight.test_rest_at_the_campfire_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: travel stages (V01.1.111, D&D idea 9, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Travel stages* (`chat.dungeon_reise`, off by default; `dungeon_reise.py`, own code; the tables `ideen.reise_rollen` and `reise_ereignisse` stand in `dungeon_srd.json`, the stages of a way come from `dungeon_karte.etappen`). When the map leads the group along a way of at least 2 stages (not in a vault), the panel rolls a journey: every seat with a standing hero takes a role (scout, hunter, watch, pathfinder; own names in the science fiction world), the roles change with the turn. Every role is a d20 on its value against 10 plus the stages (a 20 always passes, a 1 always fails). The first failed role decides the consequence: caught off guard (every hero rolls at a disadvantage next turn), hungry (1 life less, never below 1) or lost (a day more on the road); if all pass, the journey is "rested" with a 20 (2 life), else quiet. An event of the landscape's table with this outcome comes with it. `rd["reise_tage"]` counts the days on the road (for the world's calendar, D&D idea 6). The next narrator gets stages, landscape, roles with outcome and event in its facts and only tells; the log shows a card "🧭 Journey" with every roll. Live: journeys, roles passed and failed today. Log `dungeon: reise | 3 etappen | 1 von 2 | hungrig`. Self-test `Equipment.test_a_long_way_is_a_journey`.

**AI Dungeon Master: tongues of the peoples (V01.1.114, D&D idea 7, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Tongues of the peoples* (`chat.dungeon_sprachen`, off by default; `dungeon_sprachen.py`, the idea of brisvag/gibberify, GPL-3, only as an idea, own code; the sounds come from the name syllables `namensilben` in `dungeon_srd.json`). The narration may let one person of the scene say a short greeting or cry in its people's tongue (`fremd` `{name, volk, sagt}`, sagt is the meaning). The panel takes it by fixed rules: only a tongue with name syllables (Elvish, Dwarvish, Halfling, Orcish, Gnomish, Draconic, Infernal), at most 60 characters and 8 words, no refused word, never when the meaning already stands in the narration's quotes. The sounds come from the meaning by a fixed rule: every word picks the people's syllables by its hash, the same sentence always sounds the same. Who understands is fixed: a hero of that people (a half-elf understands Elvish), a bard or a wizard every tongue. Everyone sees and hears the sounds (read aloud in the person's voice); the meaning stands only in `rd["fremd"]` and goes through the view only to seats that understand and to the host, never to the car and never to the model (visibility filter `fremd_bedeutung` with a leak test). The others see "(not understood, Elvish)". Live: foreign lines, how often understood, refused today. Log `dungeon: sprache | elf | 1 verstehen`, `| verworfen | sprache|name|laenge|wort|im text`. Self-test `Equipment.test_a_peoples_tongue_only_for_who_knows_it`.

**AI Dungeon Master: calendar of the world (V01.1.115, D&D idea 6, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Calendar of the world* (`chat.dungeon_kalender`, off by default; `dungeon_kalender.py`, the idea of fantasycalendar/Fantasy-Calendar, no licence, only as an idea, own code and own lists). Every world has twelve months of 30 days, one or two moons and festivals (own lists per world, the children's table takes the general ones). A round's first day follows fixed from its start time and title (`rd["kalender"]` `{start, halbe}`). Time runs only by fixed rules: every stage of a way on the map half a day (`dungeon_karte.etappen`), a journey that goes astray a day more, a long rest or a night at the inn a whole day, at most 20 days at once, nothing inside a vault. On top of the table stand the date with the moon phases, a festival today or within the next 7 days and, in a settlement, the market day every 7th day; on a festival a settlement's services cost 20 percent more (`dungeon_gold.price`). The narrator gets date, festival and market day only as data and may not change them. Live: half days of the world passed and festivals reached today. Log `dungeon: kalender | 2 halbe tage | weg|reise|rast|gasthaus`, with `| fest` when a festival is reached. Self-test `Equipment.test_the_worlds_calendar_runs_only_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: chronicle book (V01.1.116, D&D idea 11, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Chronicle book* (`chat.dungeon_chronik`, off by default; `dungeon_chronik.py`, ideas of rpgtex/DND-5e-LaTeX-Template, MIT, and canismarko/dungeon-sheets, GPL-3, only as ideas, own code and own layout). When the host closes a chapter, the panel writes a page into the campaign's book (`buch`, at most 30 pages) only from the round's data: number and title, turns, cover (the last scene picture), the heroes with people, class, level and scars, the honours of the evening, the writings for everyone (one for a single seat stays out) and the places of the map; "The story so far" comes onto it once written. The model writes nothing for it. Only the campaign's owner sees the book (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/chronik`); it can be turned, made as a PDF on the Spark (`…/chronik.pdf`, fpdf2 with the panel's fonts, rate limit `dungeonpdf`) and deleted page by page (`POST …/chronik/loeschen` `{kapitel}`). Every chapter's end stands in the log as `kapitel_ende` `{kapitel}`, also without the switch (the bard's hero song waits for it). Live: pages, PDF, deleted, refused today. Log `dungeon: chronik | seite | 2 helden | 1 schriften`, `| ansehen | 3 seiten`, `| pdf | 40 kb`, `| geloescht`, `| abgelehnt | Error`. Self-test `Campaign.test_a_chronicle_page_for_every_chapter`.

**AI Dungeon Master: hero book to the reMarkable (V01.1.143, Dominik 2026-10-27 "Nach Empfehlung umsetzen, aber auch gerne in Farbe"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Hero book to the reMarkable* (`chat.dungeon_buch`, off by default, needs "Sending to the reMarkable"; `dungeon_buch.py`, own code and own layout "Old grimoire"). Every hero of a profile gets a book as a PDF in the page format of the profile's own reMarkable (`rmpapier.MODELS`): contents with "New since the last edition", hero sheet (values, life, armour, gold, load, the six scores, what it wears, abilities, bag, protection, scars), chronicle of encounters (per chapter evenings, fights, people met, levels; fellow heroes only with name, people and class), people (only what the meetings unlocked, never wish, secret, role or goal, "x of y"), bestiary (by the stages of the monster album, "x of y kinds") and items. Tabs at the edge (a bar at the bottom on the Move) and bookmarks jump to the parts. Three layouts: reMarkable 2 in two columns in greyscale, Paper Pro in two columns in strong colours, Paper Pro Move in one column with relatively larger type, tables broken into lines, the hero sheet over several pages; every text is measured, nothing reaches over the page. Pictures only the panel's own (portrait, faces, pictures of beasts and items), opened again and brought to the device's pixels: colour on Pro and Move, else 16 grey steps with error diffusion; without a picture a drawn symbol. Colour is never the only sign (rarity is written, stages are dots). The choice "Colour" under *Me → reMarkable* (`rm_color`, "In colour" or "In greyscale", shown only for Paper Pro and Move) applies to everything the Spark lays out for the reMarkable; a change applies from the next sending. The book comes by itself half an hour after the end of a round or a chapter (several chapters in one evening make one sending, at most once a day per hero, an unchanged book does not go again) and on "Send now" under *Dungeon Master → Heroes* (there also "Preview (PDF)") or with "Send my hero book to the reMarkable". As long as nobody wrote into it, the new edition replaces the old one; with handwriting a new edition comes and the old one goes to the archive. Once "Notes – NAME" comes along, lined with the same tabs, which is never replaced. The hero's chronicle (`chronik`, at most 300 entries) holds only fixed kinds with catalog ids, whole numbers and the fellow heroes' names, peoples and classes, never a text of the model; it is written only for profiles with the switch and checked whenever it is read. Guests, figure codes and guest accounts get no book; the balance bench writes nothing. The sendings count in the reMarkable's daily limits. `GET /api/profile/dungeon/buch`, `GET …/buch/{hid}.pdf` (rate limit `dungeonpdf`), `POST …/buch/{hid}/senden`. State `users/<id>/dungeon-buch.json`. Book and notes lie in their own folder "Dungeon-Master" (Dungeon Master) below "Spark" (or the chosen folder), made when needed, also without "Folders by kind" (`rmpapier.OWN_FOLDER`, Dominik 2026-10-27); a book still loose in "Spark" moves there with its next edition (the same document, without handwriting), one the person moved stays where it is, and notes already sent stay put because they may carry handwriting. "Send now" answers at once and makes and sends the book in the background (since V01.1.157); the page shows per hero "being made and sent …", then "done" or "failed" with step and reason (took too long, no connection, cloud refused, too large, daily limit) and asks again while it runs; a second press starts nothing new. A book stays small: pictures are made only once per book, in colour as JPEG, in greyscale as PNG with 16 steps, fonts only as subsets; with 60 pictures it stays below 2 MB (before up to several MB, which ended in `ReadTimeout` on a slow line). When uploading, the wait grows with the file's size (`remarkable.upload_timeout`: connect briefly, read 60 s plus the time for a slow line, at most 15 min), and a timeout is tried once more after 5 s; this applies to everything the Spark puts on the reMarkable. Live: sent today, unchanged, not sent, in progress right now. Log `dungeon: buch | gestartet | knopf`, `| gebaut | seiten 9 | bilder 7 | kb 610`, `| gesendet | seiten 9 | bilder 7 | auto`, `| fehler | hochladen | ReadTimeout` (step `bauen`, `bild`, `liste`, `hochladen` or `wurzel`, then the reason in fixed words), `| uebersprungen | unveraendert`, `| uebersprungen | laeuft`, `| chronik | kapitel | 2 helden`, `| notizen | gesendet`, `| abgelehnt | aus`; before it possibly `remarkable: cloud ReadTimeout, trying once more`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_buch`.

**AI Dungeon Master: foe announcements and stance (V01.1.117, D&D ideas round 2, idea 2, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Foe announcements and stance* (`chat.dungeon_haltung`, off by default; `dungeon_haltung.py`, own code). After every fought turn each foe still up announces its next blow at a standing hero (the panel's dice, `rd["kampf"]["ansage"]`, log card `ansage`): "takes aim" +2 to hit, "winds up" −2 to hit and double damage dice on a hit, "waits for an opening" with advantage. In the foes' next turn its first blow goes at that hero; if the hero fell meanwhile, the foe attacks as before. The stance stands in the action, with fixed words, also by voice and in the car (the buttons 🛡 and 💨 put it in): "Defend" gives +2 armour against the foes, the own attack is then at a disadvantage; "Dodge" gives advantage on saves against states. The narrator gets the announcements only as data (`ansagen`) and the receipts `wie_angesagt` and `haltung` in the foes' turns. Live: announcements, defend, dodge today. Log `dungeon: ansage | 2 gegner`, `dungeon: haltung | abwehr|ausweichen | 1`. Self-test `Fight.test_foes_announce_and_heroes_defend_or_dodge`.

**AI Dungeon Master: use the surroundings (V01.1.118, D&D ideas round 2, idea 3, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Use the surroundings* (`chat.dungeon_umgebung`, off by default; `dungeon_umgebung.py`, own code). Where a fight goes on, 2 to 3 things of `ideen.requisiten` lie in the room or place (`dungeon_karte.requisiten`, rolled once per place, `rd["kampf"]["requisiten"]`), at the table as buttons under the input. When a hero's action names one (its name or the last word of it, also by voice and in the car), that is its deed this turn: one check of the panel instead of the checks the model wanted for it, d20 plus the value of its kind (dexterity, strength or wisdom) against 12, at the children's table 10, with the seat's own die at the real table; each thing once a turn. On a success it works at once by a fixed rule: `schaden_gruppe` 1d6 bludgeoning to every foe (as hits with the thing, resistances count), `umwerfen` a foe lies on the ground (the one named, else the one with the most life), `deckung` +2 armour against the foes, `vorteil` the group's attacks with advantage, `fluchtweg` the foes cannot reach the hero. Then the thing is used up (`dungeon_karte.requisit_benutzt`); if the check fails, it stays. The narrator gets the things only as data (`umgebung`) and the check as a receipt. Live: used, worked, missed today. Log `dungeon: umgebung | probe | 2`, `dungeon: umgebung | schaden_gruppe | ok|daneben`. Self-test `Fight.test_props_of_the_fight_work_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: whisper to a person (V01.1.119, D&D ideas round 2, idea 5, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Whisper to a person* (`chat.dungeon_fluestern`, off by default; `dungeon_fluestern.py`, own code). Once a turn a seat whispers something to a person of the scene (the people of the NPC memory in the scene and whoever spoke in the last two turns), at most 200 characters, never from the car. New doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/fluestern` (login, own seat, CSRF, 2 KB, rate limit `dungeonfluester`) and `POST /api/dungeon/fluestern` (seat link, same door as `/api/dungeon/platz`). The model answers as that person (at most 400 characters) with its card, its attitude towards the hero, the place and the last narration; the whisper stands only as game text between <<< and >>>. The panel checks the answer (plain text, no word of the word list) and keeps it only for that seat (`rd["fluester"]`, view filter `gefluestert` in the leak test); the host only sees that and with whom someone whispered. Nothing is read aloud at the table, 🔊 reads only on the own device. Fixed rules of the panel: the attitude at most one step (NPC memory), a gift of at most 10 gold only from a warm person and once a round, a d20 up to 3 minus the attitude means betrayal: the table shows "… passes on what … whispered", and the next narration gets the whisper as game text. Live: whispered, betrayed, gifts, without an answer, refused today. Log `dungeon: fluestern | gefluestert | w14`, `… | verraten`, `… | geschenk`, `… | stumm`, `… | abgelehnt | ValueError`. Self-test `Turns.test_whisper_to_a_person_only_for_the_own_seat`.

**AI Dungeon Master: heart's wish (V01.1.120, D&D ideas round 2, idea 7, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Heart's wish* (`chat.dungeon_wunsch`, off by default; `dungeon_wunsch.py`, own code, the list of wishes per world `ideen.wuensche` from the DnDAppFiles thread). Every hero may carry a heart's wish (`held["wunsch"]`): one of its world's list or own words of at most 160 characters. New doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wunsch` (login, own seat, CSRF, 1 KB, rate limit `dungeonwunsch`) and `POST /api/dungeon/wunsch` (seat link, same door as `/api/dungeon/platz`); both empty takes the wish away. Only the own seat, the host as game master and the model see the wish (view filter `herzenswunsch` in the leak test). Per act of the story (without a story every 8 turns) the panel gives the narrator at most one wish, the heroes with an open wish in turn by their seats: in the facts only the name (`herzenswunsch_von`), the wish itself only as game text between <<< and >>>. When the narrator reports the hero in `wunsch_erfuellt`, it counts only by fixed rules: this act's hero, it acted this turn, the narration names it, the wish still open. Then there is the experience of an act (`ep.akt`), and the table shows "💖 …'s heart's wish has come true.", never the wish. Live: set, fulfilled, refused reports, refused today. Log `dungeon: wunsch | gesetzt | liste|eigen`, `… | erfuellt`, `… | verworfen`, `… | weg`, `… | abgelehnt | ValueError`. Self-test `Turns.test_hearts_wish_secret_one_per_act_in_turn`.

**AI Dungeon Master: hire companions (V01.1.121, D&D ideas round 2, idea 9, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir."):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Hire companions* (`chat.dungeon_gefaehrte`, off by default; `dungeon_gefaehrte.py`, own code, the companions per world, the children's table's animal friends and the values per level `ideen.gefaehrten`, `ideen.gefaehrten_kinder`, `ideen.gefaehrte_werte` from the DnDAppFiles thread). In a settlement "🤝 Hire a companion" is one more service of "Gold with a purpose" (`dungeon_gold.serve`, no new door, the hero's move through `/held` with `{was: "dienst", id: "gefaehrte", i}`): only while fewer than four heroes play, as many as fill the group up to four, at most two. Life, armour, attack, damage and pay come from the fixed table by the group's level; the pay is due each chapter from the hero's gold, the first on hiring. In a fight the panel rolls a blow for every companion (d20 + attack against the armour of the foe with the least life), the foes can strike it like a hero (without states). At half its life a courage check once a fight (d20 ≥ 10, else it flees this fight), at 0 it goes down; after the fight it stands again (never lost for good). A new chapter without pay: a courage check, else it leaves. No loot, no experience. In the encounter budget every companion counts as half a hero (`dungeon_kampf.budget`), so the balance holds. The model gets the companions only as data and their blows as receipts, it never decides. A campaign keeps them. Live: hired, blows, down, fled, left, pay today. Log `dungeon: gefaehrte | angeheuert`, `… | voll`, `… | unbekannt`, `… | sold | N gold`, `… | ohne sold | bleibt`, `… | gegangen`, `… | am boden`, `… | moral | geflohen|bleibt`. Self-test `Turns.test_companions_hired_fight_by_the_panels_dice`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fight turn check (V01.1.122, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-5", after a turn in which "kämpfe gegen die wache" became the Dexterity check "Wache mit Dornenranken treffen" against 10, without damage, without a charge and with +110 XP in the middle of the fight):** fixed rules of the panel, no switch. 1. Whoever fights a foe in a fight attacks it: the attack words also know "kämpfen", "bekämpfen", "hauen", "prügeln", "fight" (`dungeon_kampf.HIEB_WORTE`); when the model's check names a foe to hit, it becomes an attack against the armour with a damage roll too; when the model asks no check at all, the panel adds the attack (`dungeon: probe | kampf | angriff ergaenzt`). 2. Only the player chooses a spell: naming it in the action chooses it and costs its charge; when only the model names a spell in the check's label, the player's action stands there (`dungeon.spell_named`, `dungeon: probe | zauber nicht gewaehlt`). 3. When the narration reports a goal reached or an act's end while a fight runs, the panel waits until the fight is over (`ziel_wartet`, `akt_wartet`, `dungeon: ep | ziel | wartet kampf`, `dungeon: akt | wartet kampf`); the fight's experience comes from its foes. 4. Every XP entry says in brackets what for (check, new place, fight, goal, heart's wish, act); when in a fight a check names a foe and still stays a check, the log shows `dungeon: probe | kampf | kein angriff`. 5. Self-tests `Fight.test_a_fight_turn_keeps_to_the_panels_rules` and `Powers.test_known_spell_uses_a_charge_when_told`, plus the scene "Fighting becomes an attack against the armour" in the narrator bench.

**AI Dungeon Master: people catalog (V01.1.128, NPC catalog points 1, 3, 4 and 5 with balancing, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-5" and "das ganze muss natürlich im balancing berücksichtigt werden"):** Admin switch *Features → My data → "People catalog"* (off; builds on "NPC memory and chronicle", applies to the round's host). 1. At the end of a round every person the table met goes into the catalog (`dungeon_personen.collect`): its card (looks, manner, occupation, wish, secret), its traits, role and goal from the world's truth, its voice, its portrait and its attitude towards each hero by the hero's name; each world has a name only once, at most 300 people (the oldest from rounds go first, the admin's own stay). 2. The admin sees, changes, deletes and adds people under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "People (NPC)"* in the same list with detail as the other parts; fixed rules check every input (name 2 to 40 characters, one of the worlds, only the card's fields, traits from −3 to 3, no word of "nie", no name twice in a world). Wish and secret are seen only by the admin there and by the host in the round. 3. Per act (without a story per chapter) the panel offers the narrator up to three known people of the world, only as data (`bekannte_personen`, rolled with round, chapter and act), never one with a word of the table's lists; when the narration brings one of them with exactly its name, it comes with its stored card, traits, voice and face and knows the heroes at the table again (chronicle "X recognises Y."); any other new name is a new person as before. After the round its entry follows: attitudes, number of meetings, face. 4.
