When a person fights, it takes the fixed values of a bestiary template by its occupation (`dungeon_personen.VORLAGE`: knight → knight, veteran, guard; mercenary → veteran, thug, guard; guard → guard; scout → scout; thief → bandit; spy → spy; priest → acolyte, cultist; noble → noble; else guard), the strongest that plays in the world and fits the budget; the model never names values. Up to two people of the scene stand with their name in the foes' offer (`p:<name>`), never one friendly to a hero here (attitude 2 or more), never one beaten or fled before; a person comes only once. Beaten or fled goes into the chronicle afterwards. 5. **Balancing:** a person counts in the budget and the experience with its template's experience times a measured weight (`GEWICHT`, e.g. thug 1.65, noble 0.66), because the templates are of uneven strength. For this the bench plays encounters of people only: on average the group wins 70 to 90 % at "medium", every cell lies between 50 and 98 % (`SPANNE`; with few templates single cells swing more than with the whole bestiary). 6. Log `dungeon: personen | gesammelt | N neu`, `| wieder | N helden`, `| kampf | besiegt` or `| geflohen`, `| setzen`, `| zurueck`, `| abgelehnt | …`; Live: people in the catalog and today new, met again, recognised, fought, changed, deleted. State `dungeon-personen.json`. 7. Self-tests `Fight.test_people_catalog_keeps_the_people_met_and_brings_them_back`, `Fight.test_a_person_who_fights_takes_a_fixed_template`, `Bench.test_people_who_fight_keep_the_aim` and `Page.test_the_people_of_the_catalog`.

**AI Dungeon Master: album page "People" (V01.1.135, NPC catalog, Dominik 2026-10-26 "auch in das begegnungs / monster album buch der spieler mit aufnehemen"):** No new switch: the page is there when "Monster album" and "People catalog" are on for the host. At the round's end, when the catalog collects the people met (`dungeon_personen.collect`), each of them counts one meeting for every living hero at the table (`h["personen"] = {catalog id: meetings}`, `dungeon_album.met`). The tab "Album" then has the pages "Monsters" and "People". After the first meeting the page knows name, face, looks and world, after the second occupation and manner, after the fourth the traits and how the person stands towards exactly this hero (`PERSONEN_STUFEN = (1, 2, 4)`). Wish, secret, role and goal never show there, they stay with the admin and the host; a person with a word of the lists of the round's tone is left out. Only a seat of a round in which a hero met the person loads the face (`dungeon_gesicht.may_see` with `album.knows_face`). The hero's file keeps only ids in the catalog's form and whole numbers (`album.fix`, at most 300). Log `dungeon: album | person stufe N`, Live "N people stages".

**AI Dungeon Master: own stories (V01.1.136, NPC catalog plan, Dominik 2026-10-26 "nach empfehlung umsetzten" and "und 2": points 1, 2 and 3b):** Own admin switch *Features → My data → "Own stories"* (`dungeongeschichten`, off by default), new module `dungeon_geschichten.py`. 1. **⋯ on every story card:** when you start a round every card has a "⋯" with Edit, Copy and Delete. Edit opens the story planner's form (title, idea, tone, goal, foe, places, taboos, acts with "↻ Write again") in a window of its own. A story of the admin or one that comes with the Spark becomes the host's own copy on saving, the Spark hides the original only for that host. Delete hides such stories only for this host ("Show N hidden again" brings all of them back), the own stories it deletes for good. Copy makes an own copy "(copy)". 2. **"＋ New story" first:** the card comes before all others. Pick a tone, keywords if you like, "Create": the language model writes title, idea, goal, foe, places and acts in the chosen world (`dungeon.propose_plan`), the panel checks the draft like a typed one (`dungeon.check_plan`: lengths, tone, "nie") and keeps it as an own story. "Write it yourself" opens the empty form. 3b. **Every host its own:** up to 20 own stories per host (`EIGENE_MAX`), no other host sees, changes or plays them (404). The admin sees all of them under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "Stories" with the host's name, changes them (they stay the host's) and counts only the own for the admin's limit. A round plays only a story the host may play (`dungeon_geschichten.find`), in the car the Spark offers the own ones first. The file `dungeon-geschichten.json` keeps the own ones with `"von"`, `dungeon-geschichten-aus.json` the hidden ids per host. New endpoints under `/api/profile/dungeon/geschichten/…` (view, save, copy, delete, show, create, act) with login, profile binding, CSRF, size and rate limit (`dungeongeschichte`, the planner `dungeonplan` per host). Switch off: no ⋯ and no ＋, own stories are missing from the choice, hidden ones are back, nothing is deleted. Logs `dungeon: geschichten | gespeichert / kopiert / geloescht / ausgeblendet / wieder gezeigt / erstellt / abgelehnt`, Live "N own stories, today …".

**AI Dungeon Master: people of the catalog in stories (V01.1.137, NPC catalog point 2, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-5"):** No new switch: the people catalog counts (`dungeonpersonen`, with "NPC memory and chronicle"). In the story planner (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "Stories", for a host in the window of the own story) below the taboos there is "Known people from the catalog": the people of the chosen world with name and occupation to tick, up to three (`dungeon_personen.PLAN_MAX`); wish and secret never show there. When the world changes, the form shows its people. The panel keeps only ids of the catalog from the story's world, each once (`dungeon_personen.plan_ids` in `dungeon.check_plan`). "Create a proposal" and "＋ New story" give the language model up to 12 people of the world only as data (name and occupation, `bekannte_personen`); whoever the draft names with exactly their name comes ticked (`dungeon_personen.pick`). A round of that story puts the people with their role and goal from the catalog first into the chapter's truth, never twice (`dungeon_personen.into_truth`), and offers them to the narrator first in every act; so they come with their fixed card, voice and attitude. Catalog off: no list, nothing in the truth. Log `dungeon: personen | geschichte | N in der wahrheit`.

**AI Dungeon Master: choose a person's voice (V01.1.150, Dominik 2026-10-27 "stimmen verändern, die genutzt werden wenn diese etwas sagen"):** Under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "People (NPC)"* every person shows its voice, its own or "(by the name)" when it has none yet (`dungeon_stimmen.of_name`). Below "Voice when the person speaks" the admin picks one each of age, gender, sound, manner and pace from the fixed lists of `dungeon_stimmen.json` ("npc", words `dungeon_stimmen.WORTE`). "▶ Sample" (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/personen/probe`, admin only, only with the people catalog on, rate limit "dungeonprobe") speaks the fixed sample sentence in the description the panel builds from fixed English words (`dungeon_stimmen.describe`), without VoiceDesign in the rules' fixed NPC voice. "Save voice" (`was` "stimme" at the catalog endpoint) sets only the voice, the person otherwise stays as it was, also "from a round"; "By the name" takes the own voice away again. `dungeon_stimmen.clean` refuses a wrong voice and nothing is written; the JSON of "Edit" gets the same check. When a round meets the person, it speaks with that voice (`adopt` puts it into `npc_stimmen`). The person's portrait is painted by the pictures thread (`dungeon_dingbild`). Log "dungeon: personen | stimme" and "dungeon: personen | probe", Live counts "Stimmen gesetzt".

**AI Dungeon Master: ask back on unclear actions (V01.1.134, plan `plaene/dungeon-rueckfrage.md`, Dominik 2026-10-26 "wie empfehlung umsetzen": stages 1 and 3, the model as stage 2 only later):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Ask back on unclear actions* (`chat.dungeon_rueckfrage`, off by default), released for every profile; guests at the table get it the same way, it is part of the game. When an action is sent the panel checks it by fixed rules (`dungeon_rueckfrage.unclear`): too short (fewer than 2 words or 8 letters, "ok", "hm"), gibberish (one character four times, half the words without a vowel), only a pronoun as the last target while several fit ("I attack him" with two foes, "take it"), or no doing (no word of `TUN_WORTE` and the fight's word lists, no subject with a verb like "Ich durchsuche …" or "Wir rasten", no name of the scene, no quoted speech). Then the action stays as written but marked: below the own action stands "❓ What exactly does Morpheus do?" with the reason, up to three suggestions and "Keep it". The panel builds the suggestions from fixed patterns and the scene's data (foes still up, things in a fight, the own attack spell, people here, ways of the map), never from the model's text; every suggestion is clear itself. Tapping one puts it into the input, where it can still be changed. While the question is open the seat does not count as "has acted" (`dungeon.due`), but the turn waits at most `WARTE_S` (30) seconds after the action, then it counts as written (review 27.10. package 6). The panel asks only once a turn for the same text, "Keep it" (`so_lassen`) takes the mark off. In the car (`dungeon_auto`) the action goes in at once as said and the question is read with "First …, second …"; the CarPlay list "What exactly?" shows the suggestions and "Keep it" as buttons, "the first" or "keep it" answers by voice, only the panel's suggestions count. The model never sees the question. Log `dungeon: rueckfrage | regel | kurz` (`salat`, `fuerwort`, `kein tun`), `| vorschlag genommen`, `| so lassen`, `| klar`; Live counts questions, suggestions taken and "kept". Self-tests `Rueckfrage.test_the_table_of_clear_and_unclear_actions`, `test_asked_at_the_table_never_blocking`, `test_asked_once_for_the_same_text`, `test_never_from_the_model_and_only_the_own_seat`, `InTheCar.test_an_unclear_action_is_asked_back`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the hero's gender (V01.1.132, Dominik 2026-10-26 "männlich weiblich divers sollte auswählbar sein"):** No new switch. When making a hero (host, seat link and guest) one picks "male", "female" or "non-binary" below the name; on the hero sheet (tab "Spend points") it can be changed any time. Heroes made before stay unset until one is picked. The panel takes only these three values (`dungeon.GESCHLECHT`, `dungeon.gender`) and refuses anything else (fault "geschlecht"). The model gets each hero's field `geschlecht` with the pronoun ("female, call her she"; non-binary "they or the name") and the fixed rule in `KANN_REGEL`. A fixed check of the narrator (`dungeon_erzaehler.wrong_pronoun`, fault "fuerwort", day counter `regel_fuerwort`) finds a wrong possessive right after a hero's name (at most three words between, no comma, no article, no other person) before a part of the body or a thing of its own ("Lia raises his blade"): first a second try with a fixed hint, then the panel sets the right word (his ↔ her, sein ↔ ihr, for non-binary the article or "their"; without a sure word the sentence stays). Log `dungeon: nachbessern | fest | fuerwort` or `| bleibt | fuerwort`. The voice proposed for a hero comes from the voices of its gender (`STIMMEN_W` for female, the others for male, non-binary all; with none free, any). The portrait names "a man", "a woman" or "an androgynous person"; after a change it may be painted anew at the same level (`bild_neu`), the old one stays until then. Log `dungeon: held | geschlecht | geaendert`. Self-test `Voice.test_a_hero_has_a_gender_for_words_voice_and_portrait`.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit (V01.1.127, report `dungeon-kampf-audit/bericht.md`, Dominik 2026-10-26 "Empfehlung umsetzen"; rule 6 after Dominik's find "the hand on the hilt of his dagger … but my inventory says nothing of a dagger"):** No new switch, these are corrections of the fixed combat rules. 1. **Attack from the action:** in a fight the panel decides by fixed word lists (`dungeon_kampf.intent`) what an action does, never the model's check. `ANGRIFF_WORTE` (greife … an, schlage, steche, haue, kämpfe, töte, ramme, attack, fight, stab …, also with prefixes such as er-, be-, zu-) always attack, with no foe named the first still standing; `BEZUG_WORTE` (ziele, werfe, feuere, stürme … los, schneide, aim, throw, charge …) only with a foe named; `NICHT_ANGRIFF` (greife nach, treffe mich mit, stoße die Tür auf, betrete, campfire …) never counts. Foe names with a hyphen (Meervolk-Späher) are recognised. The model's check for an attacker is dropped (log `dungeon: kampf | angriff | aus aktion` or `| modell ohne probe`), advantage only comes from fixed sources (states, surroundings, stance, a foe on the ground), never from the model. 2. **Target fallen:** when the foe named already fell this turn, the attack takes the next one still standing (`| ziel neu`); with none standing it lapses without a check and without experience (`| verfaellt`). 3. **Damage spells:** a spell with a damage kind always attacks and costs exactly one charge, only when its attack came; a spell on foes without damage (`KONTROLLE`: sleep, hold person, fear …) costs no charge in a fight until the foes' saving throws are built (plan `plaene/dungeon-rettungswuerfe.md` in the project files). The seat then reads "… does not work on foes in a fight yet, the charge stays." or "… had no target left, the charge stays." (log `dungeon: kraft | keine ladung | kontrolle` or `| kein angriff`). 4. **No healing by the model in a fight:** the panel drops `heilung` in the consequences while a fight runs (`dungeon: folgen | heilung | kampf verworfen`); healing only comes from potions, spells and abilities. 5. **Peace only with a check:** when an action asks for peace (`FRIEDEN_WORTE`: ergebt euch, verhandle, lasst uns reden, surrender, parley …) and does not attack, the panel rolls Charisma against the higher of 12 and 10 + the strongest foe's level (`FRIEDEN_SG`); only a success ends the fight with "frieden" and half the experience. A `kampf_ende` "frieden" of the model without this check does not count (`kampf | frieden | ohne probe`). 6. **Rule 6, weapons by the hero sheet:** the model gets each hero's list `waffen` (equipment and bag) and the fixed rule to name only these. When the narration gives a hero a weapon it does not carry with a possessive (sein, ihre, his …), that is the fault "dabei" (`dungeon_erzaehler.not_carried`): first a second try with a fixed hint, then the panel strikes the sentence. A short sword counts as a sword, "blade" and "weapon" always fit, another person's weapon never counts. Day counter `regel_dabei`. 7. **Balance measured anew:** because no attack is lost any more, groups won up to 96 % at "medium". `BUDGET` for levels 1, 2, 5, 6, 9 and 10 and `GRUPPE` (0.7 for one hero, 0.4 for each further one) are set anew with the bench, every cell lies at 70 to 90 % again. 8. Self-tests in `Fight`: `test_kampf_angriffswoerter_tabelle`, `test_kampf_angriff_aus_aktion`, `test_kampf_ziel_gefallen_neues_ziel`, `test_kampf_kein_vorteil_vom_modell`, `test_kampf_schadenszauber_immer_angriff_und_keine_ladung_ohne_wirkung`, `test_kampf_keine_modellheilung`, `test_kampf_frieden_nur_mit_panelprobe`, `test_narration_gives_no_weapon_the_hero_does_not_carry` and the simulation `test_kampf_invarianten_simulation` (300 fights with a fixed seed through the real steps of a turn: hits only by the rule, life within its bounds, the fallen do not act, one end of the fight, experience only then, a charge only with a spell, death only after three failed saves and never at the children's table, every attacker gets its attack).

**AI Dungeon Master: one sorting for every typed action (V01.1.241, Dominik 2026-10-28 "es kommen zu viele fehler aktuell … mache mir vorschläge", proposal 7, "ok"):** No new switch, a rebuild with fixes. 1. **One module:** every fixed word list that sorts a typed action (attack, attack on a named foe, flight, peace, everyday, question, selling, buying, rest, going, and the gentle words, magic, throwing and the table of the values of a check) now stands in `dungeon_einordnung.py` instead of being spread over the fight, the panel, the narrator and the guide. 2. **Exactly one kind:** `einordnung.kind` gives every action exactly one kind by a fixed order: attack, attack on a named foe, flight, peace, everyday, question, selling, buying, rest, going. Fight, checks, the narrator's check, the map, the witness rule, the follow-up question and the protocol checker all ask the same module, so no list tells a sentence something another does not. Asking for peace with a word of talking ("Lasst uns verhandeln") counts as talking too. 3. **Contradictions found and fixed:** "Ich frage den Wirt nach dem Weg. Dann gehe ich los." was an attack on a named foe (now a question; "gehe … los" attacks only with "auf"), "Wir schlagen hier unser Lager auf und rasten." an attack and a rest at once (now a rest), "We rest by the fire." an attack (now a rest), "Ich laufe davon" flight and plain going without a check at once (now only flight), "Ich sage Hallo" everyday and a question (now everyday, no check), "Ich schlage vor, wir reden" is talking. 4. **Bench:** `test_dungeon_saetze` checks the one kind of each of the about 300 sentences and that every helper (`fight`, `asks`, `typed`, `everyday`) says the same; the table of the values of a check (`einordnung.values`) serves the checks and the guide's hints.

**AI Dungeon Master: anchor table, page tables and protocol checker (V01.1.240, Dominik 2026-10-28 "es kommen zu viele fehler aktuell … mache mir vorschläge", proposals 3 to 6, "ok"):** No new switch: fixed rules, a measurement and self-tests. 1. **Anchor table:** `tests/dungeon_anker.py` names for every field of every JSON answer of the model (narration, checks, story, truth, summaries, whisper, X card, scribe; about 110 fields) its kind (words only, fixed list, offered by the panel, a name of the group, named by the narration, the panel decides) and the fixed check that holds it. `test_dungeon_anker` compares it with the schemas: a new field without an anchor or a check that is gone turns it red. Two gaps are closed: a thing for the memory (`dinge`) and a writing count only when the narration shows them (`erz.check_things` with `foe_named`, `schrift.check` with `schrift.WORTE` or the title). 2. **Page tables:** `test_dungeon_tabellen` compares the tables of the game page (a check's level, rarity, a power's kind, weapon properties, resistance, night watch, a companion's state, experience reason, bench mark, time of day, the settlement's houses and services, damage kinds, the map's room kinds) with the panel's lists, and every reason the panel refuses with needs its sentence on the page (`DMGRUND` or another game script; general reasons with their why in `ALLGEMEIN`). 3. **Protocol checker** (`dungeon_protokoll.py`): after every told turn all fixed checks run once more over the narration that stays, the repaired one too (`rest`); what is still found the journal names ("dungeon: protokoll | rest | …", else "dungeon: protokoll | ok"). It also checks the round's names (`runde`): a word for no name as a foe, a person card or a person of the truth, and a person card that is a foe too. The Live overview counts checked narrations, those with faults left and wrong names. A round copied from the game page it reads back into its turns (`read`) and finds by fixed rules what it shows wrong (`text_faults`: two levels for one number, a placeholder as a foe, a missed spell with its effect sentence, a merchant the narration does not show, a sentence about the panel, a question as "going needs none", a check instead of "cannot do that yet"). `cd app && python3 -m tests.test_dungeon_protokoll copy.txt --fall name` keeps it in `tests/dungeon_protokolle/` with its findings as header, `test_dungeon_protokoll` checks every case against its header. The first case is the round in the Old Library with 16 findings. 4. **Round run:** the narrator bench has the scene "15 turns without faults left" (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.RUNDLAUF`): a round of 15 fixed turns (entering an inn, asking Oskar, a spell not known, opening a book, raising the staff, buying without a merchant, setting up camp …) runs through the real model, each narration the next turn's history. Every kept narration goes through the protocol checker and no speaker may be called "keine"; 2 points when every turn passes at the first try, 1 when none keeps a fault. The bench counts nothing into the day's numbers (`ask_checks` with `count=False` too).

**AI Dungeon Master: sentence bench (V01.1.243, Dominik 2026-10-28 "es kommen zu viele fehler aktuell … mache mir vorschläge", proposal 1 and part of 2, "ok"):** No new switch, these are corrections and a self-test. 1. **Fixed sentences:** `tests/dungeon_saetze.py` holds about 300 player sentences in German and English with their kind (attack, attack on a named foe, peace, flight, going, question, buying, selling, rest, everyday, nothing of these). `test_dungeon_saetze` runs every word list (`kampf.intent`, `dungeon.typed`, `erz.asks`, `dungeon.everyday`) over every sentence; the old table of attack words is part of it. A sentence a list sorts wrong at the table comes in with its fix. 2. **Misses fixed:** a staff with an article ("meinen Stab"), Stichwort, Schlagzeile, opening a book, a map or setting up a camp ("aufschlagen"), unpacking, ramming a stake into the ground, beating one's own fear, fighting through undergrowth, setting sail, killing time, "ans Feuer", battle map, kick off, swing open are no attack any more; "Wir ergeben uns" asks for peace; "Ich trete ein" is going. 3. **Abilities not named like spells:** the abilities Feuerball, Unsichtbarkeit, Licht, Gedanken lesen and Kampfschrei are now called Flammenkunde (Flame lore), Verblassen (Fade), Leuchtende Hand (Glowing hand), Lügen spüren (Sense lies) and Anfeuern (Rally), ids unchanged, because a hero with the ability "Fireball" only got its bonus for the spell instead of "cannot do that yet". `check_rules` now refuses any name that stands for two kinds (spell, ability, thing, foe; a role word like "guard" only as a foe); so the ability Zäh is called Hardy instead of Tough in English (a foe has that name). 4. **Spell names without a verb:** a spell's own name counts without "cast" too ("Ich werfe einen Feuerball", "Fireball!"). Names that are also everyday words carry `"alltagswort": true` in the rules (spells Light, Haste, Sleep, Darkness …; abilities Camouflage, Evasion, Blessing, Steadfast, Cleanse): such a spell counts only with a verb of casting ("wirke", "zaubere", "beschwöre", cast), and such an ability the hero lacks gives no "cannot do that yet" ("Ich nutze die Tarnung der Büsche").

**AI Dungeon Master: faults of the round in the Old Library (V01.1.236, Dominik 2026-10-28 "passt das alles?" with the log of a round, "1-9"):** No new switch, these are corrections of the fixed rules. 1. **Difficulty:** the dice bar names the panel's word (`sg`, 13 is "medium") like the card; only a number without a level goes by the SRD table, also for own dice. 2. **Damage spells:** the line "casts Fire bolt" stands without its effect sentence, whether it hits is told by the attack line alone. 3. **Fireball:** the wizard's ability is named like the spell; the renaming and a fixed check follow with the DnDAppFiles thread. 4. **Panel in the story:** a sentence in which the panel speaks ("Das Panel würfelt …") leaves the story (`dungeon.panel_says`, in English only with a word of rolling or checking, a door panel stays). 5. **Reason without a check:** who only asks hears "who only asks or talks …" instead of "walking needs none". 6. **Entering:** "betrete", "trete in … ein" and "enter" are walking without a check, whatever the model adds, and never an attack (`kampf.intent` with `NICHT_ANGRIFF` for typed actions too). 7. **Goal reached:** a group at its goal reads "Goal reached: name", without inflection. 8. **Merchants:** a merchant of the model opens only when the story shows one (its name or a word of `dungeon.MARKT_WORT`), else `dungeon: markt | nicht erzaehlt`. Self-test in `test_dungeon` (Bibliothek).

**AI Dungeon Master: "keine" is no name (V01.1.235, Dominik 2026-10-28 "ist das ein fehler", a person card "keine", town guard with a spear):** No new switch, it is a correction of the fixed rules for people. The model had written the word "keine" (none) as the name of a guard without a name. 1. **Never a person:** a word for "no name" (`dungeon_nsc.LEERWORTE`: keine, niemand, unbekannt, namenlos, none, nobody, unknown …) or a name without a letter never becomes a person, not in the fixed truth, not learnt from the narration, not through the scribe check (reason `leer` in the log, never the name); a line of the narration with such a speaker is dropped. 2. **Old cards:** such a card or person of a running round goes away at the next turn with its place on the map (`dungeon: nsc | leer | N weg`). Self-test in `test_dungeon` (ReviewPersonen).

**AI Dungeon Master: foe names known when bent (V01.1.211, narrator bench of 28.10. with 18 of 20 points, "English round in English" 0 points with the reason "gegner"):** No new switch, it is a correction of the fixed check. 1. **Bent and in another order:** a foe of the encounter counts as named when its whole name is there as before, or every word of its name is in one sentence by its stem, in any order (`dungeon_erzaehler.foe_named`): "giant, red-eyed rats" for "Giant rat", "warrior kobolds" for "Kobold warrior", "jungen roten Drachen" for "Junger Roter Drache", and fixed plural forms (f becomes v as in wolf, wolves; man becomes men; the last vowel with its umlaut as in Wolf, Wölfe). Filler words such as "of", "the", "der" do not count; the words in two sentences or another foe never count. 2. **The bench shows the foe:** when a scene fails on "gegner", the reason is followed by the catalog foes that were missing (catalog names only, never the model's text). Self-test in `test_dungeon_erzaehlstand`.

**AI Dungeon Master: settlement reordered (V01.1.209, Dominik 2026-10-28 "can we improve the settlement view.. it is very cluttered", proposal with pictures, "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a 6a"):** No new switch (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Gold with a purpose*). The window "🏘 Settlement" replaces the up to ten equally big cards of V01.1.055: 1. **Houses (1a):** the services belong to a house (`dungeon_gold.HAEUSER`, one source): inn (stay the night, rumour, hire a companion), smithy (mend, improve, drill a socket, take runes out, fuse runes), healer, teacher; the view gives `haeuser` with the services there are here. 2. **List and house (2a):** the houses on the left with a small picture and what works there right now ("2 offers", "nothing right now", "🌙 shut until morning"), the chosen house on the right; on the phone first the list, a tap opens the house with "‹ Houses". 3. **Rows (3a):** one row per offer with its name, a short sentence and a button with verb and price; too dear, the button is grey with "N gold short"; the price calculation stands only once, small, at the bottom of the house. 4. **Empty (4a):** what does not work right now is a thin dashed row with the reason (slept here tonight, nothing broken, no rumour left, shut at night); a house without an offer stands pale at the bottom. 5. **Head (5a):** one slim line with place, time of day, the own gold big and the group's small. 6. **Pictures (6a):** one picture per house and world is the picture thread's (`haeuser[].bild`); until then the house shows the first picture of its services, else the place's, else its sign. Self-tests in `test_dungeon` (the view's houses, the window on computer and phone).

**AI Dungeon Master: game master gives things (V01.1.207, Dominik 2026-10-28 "the admin should be able to give the characters items (things gold runes etc.)", "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a"):** New admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Game master gives things* (`chat.dungeon_geben`, off by default, 5a). 1. **Where (1a):** in the game master console under "Only for you" the tab "🎁 Give", only for the host and only in the admin view; every request checks the admin again (`dungeon_geben.admin`): the panel's admin login or the host's own login as an admin profile (role admin, not manager), never a guest, another seat or the language model. 2. **What (2a):** an item of the catalog of the round's world with a rarity up to "epic" (a fixed rarity stays, the thing is known at once), stackable things like potions and spells up to 5 pieces; gold up to 1000 at once; runes up to 5 of a kind, only with *Runes and sockets*; experience at most up to the next level. Never a legend, never a fixed legendary thing, never a quest item. The list comes once on opening (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/geben`), with a search. 3. **Limits (3a):** as in the game: a full or too heavy bag (`dungeon.room`), gold never above 100000 (`GOLD_MAX`), rune bag 40, one level a game evening (`dungeon.gain`), after which a waiting runeword wakes. 4. **At the table (4a):** a fixed line "🎁 The game master gives Arvid: Longsword." (log kind `geben`); the next narration gets it as data (`spielleiter_gibt`) with the task to tell it as a find or a gift, inventing nothing more. Live: things, gold, runes and experience from the game master today, refused (numbers only). Log `dungeon: geben | ding|gold|rune|ep | ok|nein`, `dungeon: geben | ding | voll`, `dungeon: geben | kein admin`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_geben`.

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and sockets, stage 2 and 3 (V01.1.205, plan "Runes and sockets", Dominik 2026-10-28 "go on with the runes to the end"):** Under the switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Runes and sockets* (`chat.dungeon_runen`, off), no new switch. 1. **Runewords:** when the runes of a runeword sit in its order in a plain, known thing with exactly as many sockets at its place (weapon, shield, head or body; `dungeon_runen.match`), it wakes as soon as the hero has its level: right at the insert or later on a new level (`wake`). The panel sets `it["rw"]`, the extra effect comes through the same rules as every rune (effect, value, to hit and damage only in attacks, armour class, healing potions) and has its own cap (9a): at most like "legendary" (weapon 10, body 8, the rest 4), two kinds of resistance a thing, to hit still at most +3. The worth is base price + rune prices + base price × 8. The preview says beforehand "Then the runeword Stormsong wakes: …" or "wakes only from level 5"; the table shows "Runeword Stormsong wakes in Arvid's Longsword: …", the thing "Runeword Stormsong: …". All seven runewords stand in the rune bag under "Runewords" as goals: runes in order (the ones there highlighted), place, sockets, from level, extra and the own things it fits now. When the panel reads a hero, a runeword stays only where its runes sit. 2. **Smith** (with *Gold with a purpose*, in a settlement or at a merchant, shut at night, never in a fight; `dungeon_gold.serve` with `dungeon_runen.DIENSTE`): *Drill a socket* 25 × group level × new socket count, never fails, at most one drilled socket a thing, never above the base's sockets and never into a runeword (7a); *Take runes out* broken for 5 × group level or saved for half the rune prices back into the bag, a runeword always goes out (6a); *Fuse runes* three of the same into one of the next rank for a quarter of its price, up to rank 9 (8a). Each service has a card in the settlement window with its price and what is missing. 3. **Giving:** in the rune bag every rune goes to another hero at the table with "Give …", never in a fight, never into a full bag. 4. **Drag and drop (12a):** a rune can be dragged onto an own thing with a free socket, fitting things light up; then the same preview as from the list follows, only *Set* puts it in. 5. **Car (12a):** "Which runes do I have?", "What is missing for Stormsong?" are answered at once, "Put Uruz into the longsword" says the preview back with "Is that right?", only "yes" sets it, a refusal comes with its reason. 6. **Bench:** the balance bench first sets into every weapon and body armour the strongest runeword whose runes are all in the bag and that thing and level take (`with_runes`), then the strongest runes as before; the self-test still keeps it at most +8 points of wins. 7. **Rules page:** for runes and runewords "Fixed rules" now names waking, the runeword cap and the smith's prices; every runeword carries its strength against the cap as a mark weak, balanced or strong (`dungeon_runen.word_mark`). The picture of a finished runeword comes from the picture thread (`dungeon_dingbild.runenwort`) once it is there. Live: also runewords woken, sockets drilled, taken out, fused and given today (numbers only). Log `dungeon: rune | runenwort | ok|deckel`, `dungeon: dienst | sockel|loesen|verschmelzen | …`, `dungeon: rune | schenken | ok|nein`, `dungeon: auto | rune_setzen | …`. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_runen` (`Runewords`, `Smith`, `test_runes_in_the_car`), among them every runeword of the catalog rune by rune under its caps.

**AI Dungeon Master: rules page complete (V01.1.203, Dominik 2026-10-28 "do I see the runes under the rules like everything else?", "is anything else missing under the rules?"):** No new switch. The Dungeon Master tab "Rules" (admin only) has new parts, each with list, detail and values in fixed words: 1. **Runes** (rank, meaning, price, what they do in a weapon, armour and shield, from which group level they fall, whether the trader has them, what merchants pay) and **runewords** (runes in their order, place, sockets, from level, addition; at the table only with stage 2 of the runes). On top, foldable, "Fixed rules around it": the switch *Runes and sockets* with on or off and a jump link (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Runes and sockets), sockets per base, finds, trader, rune bag, the caps (weapon 6.5, body 6, the rest 3, to hit +3, armour class +2 per thing and +3 per hero, life +8 per hero, one resistance per thing), setting and the word per world, all from the numbers in `dungeon_runen`. 2. **Effects** (named by the start of their sentence, with effect, damage kind, raised value and allowed kinds). 3. **Place kinds of the map, rooms of the vaults, traps** (saving throw with value and DC, damage, worlds), **riddles** (question, hint, answer) and **encounters on the way** (kind, weight, worlds). 4. **Stall events** with the switch *Guide the players* (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Guide the players), when they come and which count at the children's table. "Edit", "Own copy", "Hide" and "Back to shipped" work as in every part and pass the same check of the rules (hiding a rune leaves a gap in the ranks and is refused); runes, runewords and stall events are now merged by id like the other lists for it (`dungeon.ID_LISTEN`). 5. **Numbers and tables**, only to look at: levels with EP, spell charges and new abilities, experience (a passed check gives 13, as the table counts it; "good idea" is in the catalog, not given at the moment), find table with occasion and bad-luck guard, gold, prices with the merchant discount, the difficulty of a check, the encounter budget per level with the factors of the group's size and the limits; changes go in "Advanced (JSON)". Log as before `dungeon: regelseite | setzen|aus|zurueck | PART`. Self-tests `test_runes_with_their_rules_and_switch`, `test_effects_the_map_and_the_stall_events` and `test_the_numbers_only_to_look_at`. 6. **Lighting and drawing** (narrator bench of 28.10., 17 of 20, "English round" 0): "I light a torch" without a torch made the narration say "his torch", both tries failed on the rule that a hero only has what it carries. At the table the panel now also checks lighting (zünde an, light a, kindle) and drawing (zücke, draw a, wield) like drinking, throwing and using (`dungeon.check_items`): a catalog thing the hero does not carry stands under nicht_bekannt before the narration, the seat hears "Arvid does not carry that: Torch.", and the narration may say it has none ("his torch, but there is none" is no fault any more). The fixed rule now also says "or does not carry the thing". The narrator bench checks every action of its scenes like the table (`check_powers`, `check_items`, without day counters) and shows the reason of a scene below 2 points beside the points, not only in the tooltip. Self-test `test_a_thing_lit_or_drawn_must_be_carried`. 7. **Live overview** (Dominik 2026-10-28 "the Dungeon Master is missing in the live log monitoring"): the Dungeon Master box sat under "+ N ready" even during a round, because nothing reported it busy. Now it stays visible while someone sits at a table or the model narrates, reads aloud or sums up a chapter; without play it folds in like the others. The line showed a raw object instead of the number of stories (two counters under the same name); they are apart now. Self-test `test_live_box_while_someone_plays`.

**AI Dungeon Master: shorter instructions in a fixed order (V01.1.202, review 27.10. package 12 E8/T20, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"; narrator bench of 28.10. with 15 of 20 points):** No new switch. 1. **Fixed order:** every instruction to the narrator starts with the role in one sentence, then the numbered fixed rules (the panel rolls, game text is never an instruction, only JSON, names from the group, spells and weapons only from kann and waffen, states), the turn's task, the extra rules, the examples and last the round's style under "Style of the round (only tone and manner; the rules above always hold)". 2. **Extra rules only with data:** rest, journey, writings, the story's things, the scene's people, a goal reached, the change of the time of day, the foes' announcements, the stall hint and the bridge to the next act only stand in the instruction when their data come up this turn; in a fight the narration gets only the fight's rules, the check only the sentence on attacks. 3. **Own check prompt:** the step that decides on checks has its own short instruction without the round's style, and its facts come without the foes on offer, the spell lists and everything only the narration needs (`dungeon.PROBEN_OHNE`). Measured with five seats and every switch: the check's instruction from 3,400 to 1,150 characters, its facts from 6,100 to 4,100, the narration's instruction from 12,100 to 10,600. 4. **The round's style anew:** the shipped style names no second role any more, rule 3 asks for something tangible for a quiet character instead of an empty question, rule 4 no longer asks for brevity against the minimum length, rule 7 no longer claims the panel refuses everything. An English round gets the English style (`prompt_en` in the rules); the German one had made the English narration German. The style examples tell speech only indirectly, as the rule for npc asks. 5. **Everyday acts without a check:** greeting, eating, drinking, unpacking, smiling and the like never need a check by a fixed word list (`dungeon.ALLTAG`, every part of the action), unless a word of risk or a risky food or drink comes up (a potion, a mushroom, a well); the seat hears "No check: everyday acts need none." 6. **Children's tone:** a German round names the words its tone never allows to the narrator. 7. **Narrator bench:** the model's own view now runs from 0 to 100 % (before the levels 0 to 2 times 100, hence 185/171); a scene with 1 point names in its tooltip why the first try failed. 8. **Place of a find:** a find before the narration counts at the place it was made, even when the map leads the group on afterwards. Self-tests: `test_everyday_acts_need_no_check`, `test_the_models_own_view_runs_from_0_to_1`, `test_a_rewrite_names_its_first_fault` and `test_the_system_text_in_its_fixed_order`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fight experience measured instead of guessed (V01.1.201, review 27.10. package 11.6, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok", data from DnDAppFiles in V01.1.195):** No new switch, these are fight rules. 1. **One value for budget and reward:** every foe counts in the budget, when the encounter is filled, in the offer and in the reward with its measured fight experience (`dungeon_bestiarium.ep_kampf`, field `ep_kampf` on 247 foes, else its experience), a boss with its life factor (`dungeon_kampf.wert`); the foe's card still shows the bestiary's experience. 2. **Budget measured anew:** `BUDGET` for levels 1 to 10 is now 119, 169, 279, 341, 492, 605, 694, 1093, 1263, 1455 and `GRUPPE` 0.44, 0.91, 1.36, 1.72, 2.05; every one of the bench's 50 cells lies at 70 to 90 % wins, 82 % on average; level 1 has 119 so a lone hero at level 1 still gets a medium encounter from one measured foe. 3. **People:** a person counts with its template's fight experience times `dungeon_personen.GEWICHT` (measured anew, since people come with their guards). The occupations' rows now reach higher: knights and mercenaries up to the gladiator, mercenaries and thieves with the bandit captain, spies up to the assassin; so a strong group still meets foes that fit half its budget. The people bench measures 47 cells, 83 % on average, each between 64 and 96 %. Against a vault's master and a lone boss every group size wins at least 64 % on average. 4. **Self-test steady:** `test_a_fight_turn_keeps_to_the_panels_rules` rolls fixed misses in the foes' turn, so no hero goes down by chance and the turn closes before the other's action. 5. **Vaults:** when no room fits the band, a guardian's room also takes a count just above it when that lies nearer (two bandits instead of one, `dungeon_karte._room_foes`). Self-tests: the people in `test_people_who_fight_keep_the_aim`, budget and boss in `test_the_aim_at_medium_for_every_level_and_group` and `test_a_fight_shuts_the_market_and_a_boss_counts_with_its_life`, the vault in `test_vault_rooms_within_the_budget`, a hero's reward in `test_a_heroes_reward_does_not_hang_on_the_group`.

**AI Dungeon Master: why the group moves on (V01.1.200, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 12 E14, the map's part):** No new switch. When the group goes to another place or into another room by the majority rule, the table reads a fixed line like "On to Silberhafen, because Arvid wanted to go.", with both names for two, "because the majority wanted to go" for more. Going into or out of a vault has its own lines already. Log `dungeon: karte | weiter | <anzahl>`.

**AI Dungeon Master: guardian and master rooms within the fight's budget (V01.1.181, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 4 B1, Dominik "ok", the map's part):** No new switch. Until now the rooms of a vault had no budget: a guardian was one foe near L/2 (two from 3 heroes on), the master one near L+1 with helpers. Alone or three strong the master could hardly be beaten (0 to 48 % wins), at level 9 and 10 not even the guardian. Now the panel fits both to `dungeon_kampf.budget`: a guardian's room lies at 0.8 to 1.0, a master's room at 1.1 to 1.25 times the budget (`dungeon_karte.WAECHTER`, `HERR`), counted with `dungeon_kampf.adjusted` and a boss with its life factor (`dungeon_kampf.wert`). The panel picks the kinds by challenge as before, the nearest first, at most two kinds, up to 4 of a kind and 6 in all; it fills the count by experience (`raum_gegner.anzahl`). A master alone above 1.5 budgets (`HERR_MAX`) gives way to the next weaker one. When none of the nearest kinds fits, the panel takes the strongest kind from half the challenge on that does. The self-test checks every world, level 1 to 10 and 1 to 5 heroes.

**AI Dungeon Master: the fight holds the group, going only with a majority (V01.1.163, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 packages 3 and 5, Dominik "ok", the map's part):** No new switch. While a fight stands the group goes nowhere, even when the model sets an `ort_id`, and nothing happens on the way; whoever wanted to go reads "You are caught in the fight: win, make peace or flee first, then you move on.", the narrator gets `karte.festgehalten`. Only a flight that worked (`kampf.entkommen`) frees the way. The panel now sees going only in a sentence without a verb of asking (`dungeon_karte.geht`): "I ask the innkeeper the way", "I draw my sword", "I break the door open", "I clean my sword" and "I read on" are no going any more; "ziehen", "brechen", "weiter", "kommen" and "rein" count only with a direction, a neighbouring place or a word like "weiter", "auf", "los", "nach" right after them ("Wir brechen auf", "Wir ziehen weiter"), "Ab nach Süden!" and "Zur alten Mühle!" still go. Whoever takes someone along ("komm mit uns") or writes "Ich komme mit" goes along. The group moves only when more than half of the seats acting this turn want to go and nobody writes "I stay" (`dungeon_karte.gruppe`); else it stays, the narrator gets `karte.bleibt` with the names of those not going, and the way to a neighbouring place (`asked_ways`) is not forced. Logs `dungeon: karte | verworfen | kampf`, `verworfen | ungefragt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the table says why (V01.1.199, review 27.10. package 12, points E14, T17 and T18, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch, fixed words only. 1. **Dice card:** next to the number stands the level the panel set: "vs difficulty 16 · hard" (`dungeon.PROBE_SG`). 2. **No check:** when the panel drops a check of the model, a line shows only at that seat (`dungeon.OHNE_PROBE`): "No check: walking needs none.", "No check: a hero who is down rolls nothing." or "No check: who only asks or talks rolls Charisma at most.", once a seat and turn (log `dungeon: probe | ohne | gehen`, `zustand` or `absicht`); buying, selling and resting keep their own line. 3. **Loot:** with more than one hero the loot line says why this hero gets it: "(found it)" or "(longest without a find)" (field `grund` in the round's log). 4. **No healing of 0:** a hero already at full life shows no healing (log `dungeon: folgen | heilung | voll`). 5. **True journal words:** an action of a hero who is down is refused with `dungeon: abgelehnt | zustand` instead of "figur-code", a settlement's service in a fight writes `dungeon: dienst | … | kampf` instead of "keine siedlung". The "no attack" line exists since package 2. Self-tests in `ReviewAnzeige` (`test_dungeon`).

**AI Dungeon Master: master and boss in the bench (V01.1.198, review 27.10. points B1 and B3, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch, part of the balance bench. Besides "mittel" the bench plays two tables against bosses (`dungeon_pruefstand.bosses`): **Master** is a vault's master's room exactly as the map builds it (`dungeon_karte._room_foes`, 1.1 to 1.25 times the budget), **Lone boss** one boss of the world alone, worth 0.8 to 1.2 times the budget with its life factor (`BOSS_BAND`); the worlds come in turn. Aim: every group size wins at least 60 % on average over the levels (`BOSS_ZIEL`). Measured: against the master 1 hero 80 %, 2 heroes 70 %, 3 heroes 74 %, 4 heroes 92 %, 5 heroes 86 %; a lone boss in the band exists only from level 5 to 8, the groups win 75 to 100 % there. The bench's table shows the columns "Master" and "Lone boss" per level and below it the group's mean; the live overview names how many group sizes lie below the aim (log `dungeon: pruefstand | boss | N gruppen unter dem ziel`). Self-test `test_the_master_and_a_lone_boss_are_won_often_enough` (`test_dungeon_pruefstand`).

**AI Dungeon Master: reward per hero (V01.1.197, review 27.10. package 11.8, points B9 and B10, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch, these are fight and find rules. 1. **Experience (B9):** the experience of a won fight is no longer divided among the heroes. Every hero the fight began with gets its level's budget (`dungeon_kampf.BUDGET`) times the encounter's adjusted experience divided by the group's reference budget (`reference`: budget per hero times the group factor with companions, without the life share, the dial, the fight stage and the children's table), times the share won (the fled and peace half, a lone foe `allein` half). So a harder fight gives more, and a hero alone needs about as many fights for the next level as one of five. A fight begun before this rule is divided as before. 2. **Finds (B10):** there is one find for every two heroes (`dungeon.funde`): after a fight an odd hero brings one more half the time (1, 3, 5 heroes: 0 or 1, 1 or 2, 2 or 3), at a chest, a reward and a boss it is rounded up (at least one). Without a find only the coins come, the fight's line shows "+N gold each" (log `dungeon: beute | gegner | nur gold`); every further find goes to who went longest without one. A find the model names always comes. 3. **Gold (B10):** the coins are no longer divided, every hero gets them whole (`dungeon.coins`, the catalog's range times the level); the ranges in `dungeon_regeln.json` are smaller for it: foes 1 to 3, chest 2 to 9, reward 3 to 11, boss 8 to 26. Measured anew (`dungeon_pruefstand.economy` for 1, 3, 4 and 5 heroes): a hero earns 1.26 to 1.79 times what play costs at every level, the aim is 1.2 to 2. Self-tests `test_a_heroes_reward_does_not_hang_on_the_group` (a hero alone and one of five need at most 25 % more or fewer fights up to the last level, a fight gives about the level's budget) and `test_the_economy_keeps_its_aim` for 1, 3 and 5 heroes (`test_dungeon_pruefstand`).

**AI Dungeon Master: foe count by the panel, people with guards (V01.1.196, review 27.10. package 11.7, points B8, B14 and B16, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch, these are fight rules. 1. **How many come (B8):** the model names at most two kinds of the offer in `begegnung`, by `id` only (`dungeon_kampf.ARTEN`; there is no `anzahl` any more). The panel sets the count by a fixed rule (`fill`): every kind once, then always one more of the kind that brings the adjusted experience nearest to the budget while it stays at most 1.1 times it, until it is at least 0.9 times (`BAND`), 6 foes at most and 4 of a kind; still above 1.1 times, the strongest goes, a boss and a person last. The budget is the one of the host's fight stage. 2. **Alone:** when the scene wants a single foe (a duel, a lone guard), the model sets `allein: true` at its only kind; then exactly one comes, for half the reward (`ALLEIN`, log `dungeon: begegnung | allein`). 3. **People (B14):** a person fights with the template of its occupation's row whose experience is nearest to half the budget (`dungeon_personen.nearest`, `HALB`), no longer the strongest, and brings its guards: the weakest template of its row as plain foes (`guards`), counted by `fill`. A person always comes exactly once. 4. **Bench as at the table:** the bench counts „mittel“ by the same rule `fill`, "schwer" only up to 1.5 times the budget as at the table (B16); the people bench brings people as the table does (one or two of a drawn occupation's row with their guards, `people`) and measures only cells where people reach "mittel" (big groups high up do not: the rows end at knight and veteran). Measured anew: `BUDGET` level 2 = 152 (was 145) and `GRUPPE` for five heroes = 2.08 (was 2.02); every cell lies at 74 to 90 % wins, 83 % on average, the people in 40 cells at 54 to 98 %, 83 % on average. Self-tests `Fight.test_the_panel_counts_the_foes_into_the_band` (every choice of the offer ends in the band or has no room for one more, the model's number never counts, alone half the reward) and the people in `test_a_person_who_fights_takes_a_fixed_template` in `test_dungeon`, "mittel" and "schwer" as at the table in `test_a_group_an_encounter_and_a_fight_by_the_real_rules` (`test_dungeon_pruefstand`).

**AI Dungeon Master: runes in the bench (V01.1.193, review 27.10. package 11.5, report "Abstimmung mit den Runen" 4, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch. While *Runes and sockets* is on, the balance bench plays every cell a second time with the same groups and their runes (`dungeon_pruefstand.with_runes`, own dice): sockets on weapon and body armour as with a find (`dungeon_runen.sockets`), the runes of every level's finds up to its own (`occasions`: every fight the level takes, a chest every third, a reward, a boss every other level) by the real find rule and that level's rank (`dungeon_runen.draw`, the same rule as at the table), and every rune of the trader (ranks 1 to 4, the gold assumed). Each hero fills every socket with the rune that makes the thing strongest within the caps (`dungeon_runen.fits`), a new rune before a second of the same, the weapon first; that is more than a player gets, so it is the upper bound. Measured (200 fights a cell): with runes +2.1 points of wins on average, the single cells between −5 and +13. The self-test `test_runes_win_at_most_eight_points_more` keeps the mean at +8 at most (`RUNEN_PLUS`) and every cell with runes at 98 % at most (aim + 8), and the economy sells every rune found for half its price and stays within its aim (1.2 to 2). The budget table stays the one of V01.1.188. The bench's page shows the column *with runes* and the mean, the file `dungeon-pruefstand.json` has `runen` for it. Live: "mit Runen +N Punkte". Log `dungeon: pruefstand | runen | N punkte`. The rules of finds, sockets and setting stand only once in `dungeon_runen` (`sockets`, `draw`, `fits`); table and bench call the same.

**AI Dungeon Master: invented rune (V01.1.192, review 27.10. package 7, report "Abstimmung mit den Runen" 7, Dominik 2026-10-27 "ok"):** No new switch. The panel rolls runes only after the narration (`put_find`), so the narration never hands one out. The check "find" (`found_untold`) therefore also catches a rune in a sentence with a word of `FINDET` and none of `NEIN`, with or without a hero's name ("You find a Tiwaz rune." becomes "Group: Tiwaz"): the name of a catalog rune (four letters or more, never the name of a person of the round), "runeword" and the world's word for a rune in the singular as a whole word (rune, chip, pearl, seal; "pearls" on the beach and "old runes" on the wall do not count, `_rune_terms`). A rune the named hero has already (in the rune bag or in one of its things) does not count. The runewords' names (Ember, Daybreak …) are everyday words and never count, nor does "socket". Hint, day counter `regel_fund` and struck sentence as with every invented find. Self-test `Table.test_a_rune_told_as_found_is_struck` in `test_dungeon_runen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and sockets, stage 1 (V01.1.190, plan "runes and sockets", Dominik 2026-10-27 "1a 2b 3a 4a 5a 6a 7a 8a 10a 11a", caps 9a, switch 13a, review 27.10. "Abstimmung mit den Runen"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Runes and sockets* (`chat.dungeon_runen`, off by default), for every round with a profile as host. 1. **Sockets:** weapons and armour from common to "rare" come from a find with sockets 30 times in 100 (`it["so"]`), their number fixed by the base: a weapon of d8 or more 3, else 2; body 3; shield and head 2; hands and feet 1 (`dungeon_runen.sockel_max`). 2. **Runes** (the catalog's 13, ranks 1 to 13): the find after a fight brings a rune 5 times in 100, a chest 15, a reward 20, a boss always; after 8 fight finds without a rune the next one comes surely (`PECH`). The highest rank is the group's level + 2, the low ones come more often. The rune goes to the hero with the fewest runes of this round, into its rune bag (`h["runen"]`, beside the bag, takes no place, at most 40). The trader sells ranks 1 to 4 (1 to 3 of each), every merchant buys runes for half their price. 3. **Setting:** in the hero sheet under *Bag* you pick, at a rune of the rune bag, an own thing with a free socket where it works. The panel's preview says what it does there and how strong the thing then is ("Kenaz into socket 1 of Longsword: Hits burn: +1d6 fire damage. Strength 0 → 3.5 (at most 6.5)."), or why not; only *Set* puts it in, then it stays for good (`POST …/held` with `{was: "rune", tun: "pruefen"|"einsetzen", rune, i}`, the same doors as every move of the hero sheet). Never in a fight. 4. **Effect** through the rules that are there (`item_effects`, `worn_bonuses`, `life`, `resists`, `rabatt`, `dungeon_kampf.rk` and the attack): elements as the weapon's effect, a value +1 like jewellery (per value only the strongest counts), life, armour class, protection (twice on the body), resistance, merchant discount, healing potions +2, gold per defeated foe. A weapon rune's to-hit counts only for its attacks, never for checks. The line *Sockets 3: Kenaz (…) · free · free* stands at every thing with sockets. 5. **Caps (9a):** a thing with runes is at most as strong as the same base in "epic" (weapon 6.5, body 6, the others 3; a weapon's own effect counts like +1d6, so a "rare" weapon takes no element rune), to hit from rarity and runes at most +3, armour class from runes at most +2 a thing and +3 a hero, life from runes at most +8 a hero, one kind of resistance a thing, each element once a thing. 6. **Worlds (10a):** the same rules, only the word changes: rune, in science fiction chip, with pirates pearl, in horror seal. 7. **Switch off (13a):** runes and sockets stay with the heroes but do nothing and fall no more; on again, everything counts as before. The smith never betters a thing with runes. Runewords, drilling sockets, removing, fusing and trading runes between heroes come in stages 2 and 3. Live: runes found, set, refused, bought, sold and finds with sockets today (numbers only). Log `dungeon: rune | fund | rang N`, `dungeon: rune | pruefen | …`, `dungeon: rune | einsetzen | ok|deckel|voll|sockel|kampf|aus`, `dungeon: rune | kauf|verkauf | rang N`, `dungeon: rune | sockel | N`, `dungeon: rune | gold | N`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_runen`, among them every chain of up to three runes on every base and every rarity with sockets: under every cap or refused.

**AI Dungeon Master: abilities when used, only the strongest bonus, bench like at the table (V01.1.188, review 27.10. package 11, points B6, B7, B11 and B13, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“, on B13 „je Wert nur das stärkste“):** No new switch. 1. An ability's bonus counts only when it always works (`immer`) or is used in this turn's action ("I use Sweeping blow", the *Use* button; `h["einsatz"]` from `check_powers`, empty again after the turn); its tile says "when used". 2. Per value only the strongest bonus of jewel, worn gear and ability counts, never the sum, like the blessing (`dungeon.worn_bonus`, `ability_bonus`, `total`); a worn piece that does not count for it says in the hero sheet "Does not count for Strength: something else gives more there, only the strongest counts." (`outdone`, `worn_view`). 3. New fixed effects (`KRAFT`): *Weapon master* 1 more damage on every weapon hit, *Hardy* 3 more life per level (at once when picked, `relife`), *Laying on hands* once a scene 1d6+2 life for the friend named or oneself, *Revive* once a scene a friend who is down back with 3 life (`use_abilities`, never on the dead, a fixed line without a target). 4. The sentences of the other abilities of the panel's own five classes promise nothing the panel does not compute (`dungeon.VERSPRECHEN`), e.g. "A wide blow that makes room." instead of "Hits every foe right next to you.". 5. Peaceful creatures of the bestiary (`dungeon_bestiarium.friedlich`: villager, unicorn …) are never offered as foes, neither in a fight nor in quests. 6. **Find table (B11):** "rare" only from level 3, "epic" from level 5 (rows 1 to 4 new, from level 5 as before; for every occasion, the merchant too). 7. **Bench like at the table (B6):** the balance bench takes every class of the world in turn from a drawn start (alone too), raises every hero level by level like a player (`dungeon_pruefstand.raise_to`: the first of the two abilities offered, every level's point on the first value of the class's order) and gives it gear of the rarity of the median find from foes up to its level (`gear_rarity`, no assumption of its own); as at the table it never draws a peaceful foe (`encounter`). Measured anew with it: `dungeon_kampf.BUDGET` levels 1 to 10 = 99, 145, 242, 314, 440, 547, 635, 1003, 1138, 1350 (now growing with the level) and the group factor `GRUPPE` per size 1 to 5 = 0.42, 0.92, 1.36, 1.69, 2.02 (`group_factor`, beyond +0.33 per hero): a hero alone gets clearly easier fights. Every cell of the bench lies at 74 to 86 % wins. Self-tests `test_an_ability_counts_when_used_and_only_the_strongest`, `test_abilities_the_panel_computes`, `test_an_ability_promises_only_what_the_panel_computes`, `test_peaceful_foes_are_never_offered` and `test_the_find_table_keeps_its_promises` in `test_dungeon`, `test_heroes_like_at_the_table` in `test_dungeon_pruefstand`.

**AI Dungeon Master: honest sentences for the abilities of the SRD classes (V01.1.191, data, review 27.10. Paket 11 B7, Dominik "ok", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** The 7 classes from the SRD (barbarian, bard, druid, monk, paladin, sorcerer, warlock in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json`) no longer promise, in an ability without a fixed effect, anything the panel does not compute (`dungeon.VERSPRECHEN`: numbers, damage, life, healing, targets, hits, dodging, checks). 12 sentences are new, such as Rage "When rage grips you, you put all your strength into the fight." instead of "harder blows, less pain", Dodge "Stays calm and is hard to catch." instead of "Dodges every blow", Twin spell "Shapes spells with skill." instead of "hits two targets"; the bonus on their value stays, it counts when used. The paladin's Lay on hands now really heals like the healer's (`wirkung` `heilen`: once a scene a friend or yourself by 1d6+2). The self-test `test_an_ability_promises_only_what_the_panel_computes` now checks all 12 classes.

**AI Dungeon Master: measured combat XP per foe (V01.1.195, data and check rule, review 27.10. Paket 11.6, B4 option a, Dominik "ok", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** 247 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` now carry `ep_kampf`, the XP they are really worth in a fight. The main thread measured it with the bench as at the table (a group of four against 1 to 4 of them, 60 fights each, `ep_kampf` = `ep` ÷ the ratio with 80 % wins, at most a quarter to four times). Examples: the shambling mound 6300 instead of 1800, the archmage 2800 instead of 8400, the orc warrior stays at 100. `ep` stays for the display and the D&D reference; 24 foes without a measurement (peaceful, very small and very big ones) still count with their `ep`. `dungeon_bestiarium.ep_kampf(g)` reads the value (else `ep`), `dungeon_bestiarium.check_rules` requires a whole number within a quarter to four times `ep`. The main thread switches budget and reward over in a version of its own. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_kampf_ep`).

**AI Dungeon Master: English foe names as in the SRD (V01.1.212, data, asked by the main thread, thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** An English narrating model wrote "stirges" for the "Giant mosquito" (id `stirge`) and the foe check fired. Four English names in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` are now as in the SRD: Stirge (instead of Giant mosquito), Animated armor (instead of the British spelling Animated armour) and Swarm of piranhas (instead of Swarm of quippers, fitting the id `piranhaschwarm`) and Warg (instead of Worg, as in the new Monster Manual and fitting the id `warg`, so card and narration show the same name). The German names stay. All other names of the SRD foes are the names of SRD 5.1 or 5.2.1. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_englische_namen_wie_im_srd`).

**AI Dungeon Master: unique spell names, everyday words marked (V01.1.238, data, Dominik 2026-10-28 "check the rest too", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** "Ich setze Feuerball ein" gave no "cannot do that yet", because an ability had the same name as the spell: the check took the sentence for the own ability. So in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` no spell is named like an ability or another item any more. The spell `schildzauber` is "Shield spell" (Schildzauber), no longer "Shield" like the armour, so "I use my shield" is no longer refused. The druid ability `tiersprache_d` is "Animal whisper" (Tierflüstern), the bard ability `spottlied` "Mocking song" in English; the ids stay, old heroes keep everything. 26 spells and 4 abilities named by an everyday word (such as Darkness, Silence, Fear, Sleep, Command, Knock, Fly, Daylight, Guidance, Darkvision, Inspiration, Nature lore) carry `"alltagswort": true`. The main thread reads the field: so "I use the darkness to sneak in" gives no wrong "cannot do that yet". Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_zaubernamen_eindeutig_und_alltagswort`, `test_satz_pruefstand_zauber` with typed sentences both ways).

**AI Dungeon Master: choose the gender, voice and pronoun follow (V01.1.187, review 27.10. package 10, point Z12, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch. 1. Whoever makes a hero (a new round, a seat link) must pick male, female or non-binary (`dungeon.made_hero`, else the error "Please pick male, female or non-binary."); the panel's heroes (bench, test table) need none, old heroes stay unset. 2. In the hero sheet the voice list has two groups, "Female voices" and "Male voices", the hero's own first (`stimmen_w`, `dmVoiceGroups`). 3. When the gender changes and the voice belongs to the other (`dungeon.voice_fits`) and was not described by the player, the hero takes a fitting new one (`voice_for`, log `dungeon: held | stimme | nach geschlecht`). 4. People with a card (`dungeon_nsc`) get the same pronoun check as heroes (`dungeon_erzaehler._figures`): "Grimma hebt seine Hand" becomes "ihre", "Er" after a sentence only about Grimma becomes her name. Self-test `test_gender_is_chosen_and_the_voice_follows` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: one age and fixed looks per person (V01.1.186, review 27.10. package 10, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch (under "NPC memory and chronicle"). 1. **Age:** the panel writes one age per person onto the card once (`rd["nsc"][k]["alter"]`: kind, jung, mittel, alt; `dungeon_nsc.age`): from the words of its looks ("is quite old", `ALTER_AUSSEHEN`), else by the name; with an adult occupation (any but `KIND_BERUF`) never a child. Voice (`dungeon_stimmen._of`), portrait and facts (`nsc_szene[].alter`) read this field. 2. **Gender:** the chapter's truth no longer has a field `geschlecht`; the gender comes from a title or role, else from the name. Age and gender the model reports for a person with a card or of the truth are dropped (log `dungeon: nsc | stimme | verworfen`). 3. **Fixed looks:** the panel rolls hair, eyes, beard and build from the tables `nsc_haar`, `nsc_augen`, `nsc_bart`, `nsc_statur` onto the card (`dungeon_nsc.looks`, `MERKMALE`): grey or white hair and such a beard only when old, a beard only for a man from young on. The narration check compares them (`dungeon_erzaehler.looks_off`, "blond" is a colour too), the table sees them among the people, the catalog keeps them. 4. **Fight:** a child never fights (`dungeon_personen.fighters`). 5. **Role and occupation:** when the truth's role names another occupation of the table, the panel sets it (log `dungeon: nsc | beruf | nach rolle`), never for a person from the catalog. 6. **Catalog:** `collect` keeps gender, age and the voice with both, `adopt` brings them, a voice chosen there sets both; the rules page "People (NPC)" shows gender, age and looks. Live: occupations by the role, age or gender from the model dropped. Self-test `test_one_age_and_fixed_looks_per_person` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: story progress (V01.1.185, review 27.10. E12, decision 4, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** The stall counter, the wink in three stages and the clues to the turning point (steps 4 and 5 of "Guide the players") are the story's core and have their own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Story progress*, on from the start (`features.DEFAULT_ON` "chat.dungeon_fortschritt", no extra model call). `dungeon_anleiten.kern` counts for `progress`, `seen`, `clue_facts`, `facts` and the tasks `WINK_TASK` and `SPUR_TASK`; while "Guide the players" is off the level Normal counts (wink after two turns without progress). The narration's form, the buttons "What you can do here", questions to quiet players, the check "vage" and the way-out button stay under "Guide the players" (off from the start). Live: its own part "Fortschritt der Geschichte" with stall winks per stage and clues shown (`KERN_ZAEHLER`). Self-test `test_story_progress_is_core_and_on_from_the_start` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the panel decides (V01.1.184, review 27.10. package 9, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch. 1. **Checks:** the model names only a level (`leicht`, `mittel`, `schwer`, `sehr_schwer`), the panel sets the number (`PROBE_SG`: 10, 13, 16, 19); an old answer with a number gets the level it falls in. The value must fit the fixed table of activities of the player's words (`dungeon_anleiten.WURF_WORTE`: climbing is Dexterity), else the panel takes the table's (`fitting_value`, log `dungeon: probe | wert | tabelle`). The experience of a passed check no longer depends on the level (`PROBE_EP_SG`). 2. **Loot:** when a check that searches or opens passes (`FUND_TAT`, not for traps or tracks, `FUND_NICHT`), the panel draws the find before the narration (`first_find`) and gives it to the narrator as the fact `fund` (who, what); it goes to the finder. A place gives one find only (`fundorte`), also the loot the model reports (log `dungeon: beute | ort schon`), and that goes to the hero who alone acted. A name of the model does not stay when it names the head word of another thing („Silberner Dolch“ for a torch, `name_fits`); proper names like „Glockenbrecher“ stay. 3. **Healing and damage:** the panel always drops healing in `folgen` (log `dungeon: folgen | heilung | verworfen`); rest, potions and spells heal. Damage outside a fight is rolled by the panel (1d4, after a hard check 1d6, `SCHADEN_W`), never the model's number. 4. **Act end and goal:** `akt_fertig` counts only when the narration names `WENDE_WORTE` (2) content words of the turning point (`dungeon_anleiten.named`, log `dungeon: akt | modell | ohne wende`); when the panel moves on by itself after the time, the next turn first tells the turning point as `bruecke`. A goal gives experience only in the last act and with words of the goal in the text (`goal_told`). Live: check value by the table, act end without a turning point, goal without a reason, finds before the narration, loot refused at the same place. Self-tests `ReviewPanel` and `Stories.test_round_follows_the_act_model_decides_panel_limits` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: people are people (V01.1.180, review 27.10. package 8, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, under *NPC memory and chronicle*. 1. **One fixed check** `dungeon_nsc.ist_person` says whether a name may be a person and otherwise why not: a hero, a companion, a foe of the running fight, a name of the bestiary (also with a number or in the plural, „Goblins“), a group of the truth or a mere role word (`ROLLENWORTE`, „der Fremde“, „ship AI“, or a word ending in `ROLLEN_ENDE`, „Schattenwächter“). It holds for the chapter's truth, for learning from the narration, for the merchant and in the people catalog; the log line `dungeon: wahrheit | person | abgelehnt | <reason> | <where>` never names the name. 2. **Merchants:** the kind comes from fixed words of the name or sentence (`HAENDLER_WORT`: „Alchemistin Mira“ sells potions), else it is drawn; a name that is no person gives the default name, in German in both forms (`HAENDLER_IN`). A merchant with a name of its own gets a card with the kind as occupation (`dungeon_nsc.merchant`, log `dungeon: markt | person | neu`), so portrait, voice and catalog work. A treasure is bought, never offered (`NIE_ANGEBOT`). 3. **A person in a fight:** an occupation without a fight row such as farmer or ship AI never fights as a „guard“ (`dungeon_personen.NICHT_KAEMPFER`); a person in a fight shows its portrait (`dungeon_kampf._picture`) and counts for no monster in the album. Live: „today N names refused as a person, M merchants with a card“. Self-tests `ReviewPersonen` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: going not first (V01.1.179, review 27.10. package 5, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch. The group moves only by majority and without „I stay“ (`dungeon_karte.gruppe`). When others have acted this turn already and all of them stay, the suggestions of the ask-back and of *guide the players* (`dungeon_rueckfrage.points`) put „I look around carefully.“ before the way of the map, so a button does not lead the group away. When one goes, the old order stays. Self-test `Rueckfrage.test_going_is_not_offered_first_when_the_others_stay` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pronoun, place, secret, track, action (V01.1.178, review 27.10. package 7 part 3, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, more fixed checks of the narration. 1. **Pronoun** („fuerwort“): a sentence starting with „Er“, „He“ or „She“ right after a sentence naming one person only, a hero of another gender (any pronoun for divers), is wrong (`ER`); the repair puts the name in. German „Sie“ is never judged (many or polite). 2. **Invented place** („ort_erfunden“, `place_untold`): a word of `ANKUNFT` (reaches, enters …) with a settlement word and a name („die Burg Drachenfels“, `ORTSWORT`) or a name ending in `ORT_ENDE` („Eichenburg“) that no place of the map and no person has, with no `ort_id` and not in a vault; the sentence is struck, the group stays where the map has it. 3. **Secret** („geheim“, `secret_told`, rule 7): a sentence with `GEHEIM_ANTEIL` (60 %) of the longer words of a secret of the fixed truth, with no check of this turn succeeded, is struck. 4. **Track after a failed search:** a sentence with the check's thing and a word of `SPUR` (leads, towards, north …) or a place of the map counts as „probe“. 5. **Action passed over** („ignoriert“, `ignored`): when the narration names the hero but none of at least `TAT_WORTE` (2) content words of the action, the next try gets a hint when another fault asks for a rewrite anyway; alone it never fails a try (`NUR_HINWEIS`), the panel adds nothing. Day counters `regel_ort_erfunden`, `regel_geheim`, `regel_ignoriert`. Still open from the package: the state in the thing memory (open/locked …), since an action may change a state. Self-test `ReviewPruefung.test_pronouns_places_secrets_tracks_and_ignored_actions` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: nothing invented (V01.1.177, review 27.10. package 7 part 2, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, new fixed checks of the narration (`dungeon_erzaehler`), each with a hint for the next try and, when that fails, a struck sentence. 1. **Weapon without a possessive** („dabei“): the hero's name, then a word of `ZIEHT` (draws, grabs, drinks …), then the head noun of a catalog weapon, armour, shield or potion (`SCHUTZ_KOPF`) the hero does not carry, nobody else named between; when the hero's own action names the thing it does not count. Gear never goes into the thing memory (`check_things`). 2. **Find** („fund“, `found_untold`): a sentence with the hero, a word of `FINDET` and the head noun of a catalog thing (`FUND_ARTEN`) that is neither loot nor a quest find nor in the bag. 3. **Gold in words:** „twenty gold pieces“, „a handful of coins“ (`GOLD_WORT`) count like figures. 4. **Foes without an encounter** („gegner_neu“, `foe_untold`): a bestiary name with a word of `ERSCHEINT` (bursts in, appears, attacks …) that neither this turn's encounter nor the fight nor a hero's action has; peaceful beings, humans (`wesen` „mensch“), people of the round and words such as „Roch“ or „Schatten“ (`GEGNER_MEHRDEUTIG`) never count, names only as a whole word („Wolfshafen“ is no wolf). 5. **Time jump** („zeitsprung“, `ZEITSPRUNG`): „three days later“, „the next morning“, with no rest running and no travel (`ort_id`). Head nouns that are common words too (`MEHRDEUTIG`: fire, poison, luck …) never count. Day counters `regel_fund`, `regel_gegner_neu`, `regel_zeitsprung` (in the live overview's narration rules). Self-test `ReviewPruefung.test_invented_gear_finds_foes_and_time_jumps_are_caught` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the check catches everything (V01.1.176, review 27.10. package 7 part 1, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, these are corrections of the fixed check. 1. **Every fault:** `dungeon_erzaehler.faults` returns every fault of a try, not only the first (`judge` still gives the first); the hint for the next try names all of them (`hints`), and the mildest try is kept (`milder`: the worst fault first, then fewer faults). 2. **Repair checked again:** when every try fails, `repair_all` works through all faults, the strikes first, then the fixed sentences; then it checks again: a hero who acted and is missing gets a fixed sentence from their roll („Silas tries: open the lock, it fails.“) or „… waits“; an `ort_id` leading to a place the text does not name keeps the group where it is (log `dungeon: nachbessern | nachgeprueft | namen` or `| neuer_ort`). 3. **Failed check:** a sentence telling the failed check as done is now caught also when it only carries the hero's name and a word of success, and struck instead of adding a contradiction; then the turn's loot is lost (`| beute verfallen`). 4. **Answer in the NPC field:** when a hero asks, a line of a person in `npc` with at least `ZITAT_WOERTER` words counts as an answer. A repair that can do nothing writes `| bleibt | <fault>`. 5. **Retelling after 👎:** when the new text has a hard fault (`HART`, such as a failed check told as done), the old one stays; lighter faults are repaired by `repair_all`. The narrator test bench no longer counts the rules' day counters (`faults(…, count=False)`). Self-tests `ReviewPruefung` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: life only with a reason (V01.1.175, Dominik 2026-10-27 „wiso verlor ich ein leben? erklärung“):** No new switch. After a natural 1 on Charisma („Silas zur Kooperation überreden“) a hero lost 1 life without the narration saying so: the narrating model had set the consequence „damage“, and outside a fight the panel used to take it without a reason. Now `dungeon.grounded` checks by a fixed rule: damage outside a fight counts only for a hero whose check of this turn on a value of `KOERPER_WERTE` (strength, dexterity, constitution) failed, such as a jump, a climb, a lock that snaps; the line then names „check „…“ failed“. Any other damage is dropped (log `dungeon: folgen | schaden | ohne grund`, day counter `schaden_ohne_grund` in the live overview). Healing and hits by foes stay. The narrator's task says so too: damage only after such a check and with its reason in the narration. Self-test `LifeLossReason` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the game table stays calm (V01.1.174, Dominik 2026-10-27 „die seite im spiel läd zum teil immer neu, bilder flakern, texte formatieren sich um und springen“):** No new switch. When a poll brought something new (who is typing, who is here, a sentence being read aloud …), the table used to build its whole HTML anew: every picture was set and loaded again, texts wrapped anew, columns jumped, and the feed went to its end every time. Now `dmMorph` (static/js/dungeon.js) puts the new HTML into the nodes there: a node stays when it is the same thing (same tag, id, key or start of its text, looked for up to `DMMORPH_VOR` (6) places ahead), only what changed is set, a picture keeps the address it already has. Input field, focus, open sections and scroll stay. The feed goes to its end only when you were there already or a new entry comes. Browser self-test `Windows.test_the_table_stays_put_across_polls` in `test_dungeon`: four polls with something new, no node built anew, nothing moved, the scroll stays.
