**AI Dungeon Master: voices from a description visible (V01.1.172, Dominik 2026-10-27 „stimmprobe nahm bei jedem charakter das standard model“, „stimme klang bei alles gleich“):** No new switch. An own voice per person (age, gender, sound, manner, pace) needs VoiceDesign on the Spark (Einstellungen → Sprachausgabe → „VoiceDesign zusätzlich bereitstellen“). While it does not run, every person speaks in the rules' fixed voice (women in the female one), the sample too. Until now the panel asked the speech output only from a round's view and at most every 10 minutes, so after a restart the catalog of people and its sample always took the fixed voice. Now catalog and sample ask themselves while VoiceDesign counts as off, at most every `DESIGN_NEU_S` (30) seconds (`dungeon.design_up`). When it does not run, the catalog's voice and the game master console (with reading aloud on) show „Voices from a description are not running right now: every person speaks in the fixed voice“ with the jump link to the setting. When VoiceDesign fails in the middle of a game, the log names the reason („dungeon: stimmen | voicedesign … | feste stimme“). Self-test in `test_dungeon_regelseite` (a person's voice).

**AI Dungeon Master: understanding the input (V01.1.171, review 27.10. package 6, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, these are fixes. **Ask back:** „No doing“ asks only when the sentence has no verb: a subject (ich, wir, du, ihr, er, sie, the name of a hero at the table) with a word after it that ends like a verb (-e, -n, -t; in English any word) counts as doing (`dungeon_rueckfrage.subject_verb`), never a word of `KEIN_VERB` („Ich bin müde“, „Ich habe Hunger“). `TUN_WORTE` also knows durchsuchen, verbinden, rasten, bestellen, befragen, verfolgen, plündern, entschärfen, steigen, zünden, ducken, bewachen and kaufen. A question holds the turn at most `WARTE_S` (30) seconds after the action, never the turn's whole time any more. Before, the game asked back for 32 of 45 clear sentences („Ich durchsuche die Truhe“). **Checks:** Plain moving (a word of going, none of sneaking or climbing, none of searching, opening, taking or attacking), buying, selling and resting by the player's own words need no check, whatever the model writes (`dungeon.typed`; log „dungeon: probe | verworfen | kauf“). **Typed game mechanics:** Buying and selling work only over „⚖ Merchant“, a rest is called by the host in the game master console. When a seat types „Ich kaufe …“ or „Wir rasten“, only that seat gets a fixed line with the button that opens the window („⚖ Merchant ›“ while a market is open, „🎲 Game master console ›“ for the host), and the narrator hears `nicht_erfolgt`: it tells it at most as an intention, without gold, goods or healing. „Ich gebe Grimma 10 Gold“ with a person of the scene really takes the gold (line „⚖ Arvid gives Grimma · 10 gold“, the narrator hears `gegeben`); more gold than the hero has stays with a fixed line. Things go on as before over an errand „bringen“. Before, such sentences came to nothing: „Ich kaufe bei Grimma einen Heiltrank“ left gold and bag unchanged. **Small things:** „Heiltrank“, „Heiligtum“ and „heilig“ no longer count as help for a person's attitude (`dungeon_zeugen.TATEN`), and a verb checks only things of its kind: „Ich lese das alte Schild“ looks for scrolls, no shield (`dungeon.DING_ART`). **Live:** Status → Live counts typed hints and gold given. Log lines „dungeon: getippt | kauf | hinweis“ (verkauf, rast, gold), „dungeon: getippt | geben | gold“. The one shared classification of every word list comes only in the big tidy action (guideline part 17). Self-tests `ReviewEingabe` in `test_dungeon` with the 45 and 50 sentences of the report (E2, E3, E13, E16, T15, T16).

**AI Dungeon Master: recognising attacks and healing (V01.1.170, review 27.10. package 2, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch. **Attack:** In a fight an action is an attack when it names a foe standing and carries no word of healing, protecting, talking, looking, hiding, giving or waiting and neither flees nor asks for peace (`dungeon_kampf.SANFT_WORTE`). New are „greife … an“ up to the sentence's end when a foe is named, „stürze mich auf …“, „lasse … fliegen“, „gehe … los“ with up to 8 words, „verpasse … eins“ and „packe …“. „Ork“ names the Ork-Krieger (names are also split at hyphens, an equal word from 3 letters counts). Before, „Ich greife den Goblin mit dem Schwert an“ was no attack: the check passed, the goblin lost nothing. When a model's check still stays a check although it names a foe, it pays no check experience and the seat gets the line „No attack recognised. Write for example: "I attack Goblin."“. **Healing:** „Ich heile Bela“ (heal, tend, bandage, cure) with the name of a hero at the table or the own one casts a healing spell of the class with a charge, by a fixed order: for another one for allies or the group, the lowest grade first, then the one with the most healing (`dungeon.heal_named`). Without a charge a fixed line comes. The spell's line at the table now names the healing („· Bela +7 life“). Log lines „dungeon: kann | heilen | zauber“, „| leer“, „| kein zauber“, „dungeon: probe | kampf | kein angriff | hinweis“. Self-test `Fight.test_review_attacks_are_recognised_and_healers_heal` with every sentence of the report (E1, T4, T5).

**AI Dungeon Master: fights hold and are less deadly (V01.1.169, review 27.10. packages 3 and 4, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“ with decisions 1 and 2):** No new switch, these are rules of the fight. **The fight holds:** While a fight runs no merchant comes (`open_market`), the host cannot open the market and nobody buys, sells or has a thing named (hint „First the fight …“, reason `markt kampf`); a scroll of knowing still works. The flight rolls now count before the map's step, so a successful flight can still lead to a neighbouring place in the same turn; the map thread blocks the change of place in a fight. **Boss:** A boss counts in the budget with its life factor (`dungeon_kampf.wert`, `faktor`, the same number as its life, also for the map's rooms), at most one boss an encounter. A room of the map brings its foes in the number the map sets (`raum_gegner.anzahl`, at most `ANZAHL_MAX`, 1 without the field). **All down:** When in a fight every hero at the table is down or unconscious and one of them lay already, the fight ends in mercy for all: each wakes with 1 life, loses half its gold and one thing, there is no experience (`dungeon.down`, log „dungeon: zustand | gnade | gruppe | n“). When one lies while others still fight, death stays possible as before. **Stable:** Three successful rescue rolls now mean „stable but unconscious“ instead of „stands up“: no more rolls, healing gets it up, with no fight the hero comes to with 1 life. A 20 gets it up at once as before. **Breather:** After every won fight each hero up gets a quarter of its life back (`dungeon.breather`, `VERSCHNAUFEN`), without a switch, as part of the fight's rules like the rescue rolls. **Follow-up fights:** An encounter's budget grows with the group's share of its life (sum of life over sum of maximum life, at least `ANTEIL_MIN` 0.4), the danger display uses the same value. **Live:** Status → Live counts stable heroes and breathers. Self-tests `Permadeath.test_the_whole_group_down_is_spared_and_a_won_fight_gives_breath`, `test_rescue_rolls_get_a_hero_up`, `Fight.test_a_fight_shuts_the_market_and_a_boss_counts_with_its_life` and the flight before the map's step in `test_kampf_flucht_nur_mit_mehrheit_und_panelprobe`.

**AI Dungeon Master: nothing breaks any more (V01.1.168, review 27.10. package 1, Dominik 2026-10-27 „ok“):** No new switch, these are fixes. **Prompt:** The style examples of the world and the tone now stand at the very end of the task, after every rule, in their own block between „[Examples]“ and „[End of the examples]“ (`dungeon_erzaehler.STIL_BLOCK`). Before, the rules for voices, weapons, conditions and the map ran into the last example line without a paragraph and so counted as „only an example“. **Word lists:** A fixed list of harmless words that hold a word of the lists („knackt“ holds „nackt“, „Blutbuche“, „Blutegel“, „Schlachtross“, „tötet keine Fliege“ …) is taken out before the check (`dungeon.HARMLOS`, one helper `dungeon.word_hit` for the round, the hero's name and the people catalog). Every other part of a word still counts („Massenmord“, „splitternackt“), so no real hit is lost. Before, the whole narration failed when the model wrote „Ein Ast knackt“. **Outage brake:** When no usable narration comes, a fixed sentence stands in the log as a system line, never as a narration the model reads as the story so far in the next turn (`dungeon.outage`). The next try comes after `DM_S` (75) seconds at the earliest; after `AUSFALL_HALT` (2) outages in the same turn the turn waits with the line „The Dungeon Master cannot get through right now …“ until the host presses "Go on now". Before, the panel asked again on every request and the log filled up. **Children's table:** When a seat writes a word the round's word list refuses and a fixed table can soften (töten, ermorden, enthaupten, abschlachten, umbringen), the panel takes the action as „Ich besiege den Goblin.“ and asks the seat once with „Here foes are defeated, not killed. Your action reads:“ (`dungeon_rueckfrage.milder`, also without the Rückfrage's switch, never blocking). What cannot be softened stays refused. „Besiegen“ counts as an attack in a fight (`dungeon_kampf.ANGRIFF_WORTE`). **Live:** Status → Live counts turns waiting for "Go on now" and softened actions. Log lines „dungeon: ausfall | neu | 1“, „| halt | 2“, „dungeon: aktion | wortliste | milder“, „dungeon: rueckfrage | milder“. Self-tests `ReviewSofort` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: one gender per person (V01.1.167, Dominik 2026-10-27, screenshot „Leute“: „silas - männliches bild - mystikerin“, „schattenwächter händler? stimmt das?“):** No new switch, under *NPC memory and chronicle*. Until now gender, occupation and pronouns came from three sources: the portrait from the voice traits, the occupation as a random row with a fixed ending, the pronoun from the model. Now the gender is written once when the card is made (`dungeon_stimmen.fix`, on the card `rd["nsc"][…]["geschlecht"]` with the heroes' words weiblich, maennlich, divers): from the chapter's truth (new field `geschlecht`, the task asks for a proper name instead of a title), else from a title or role („Schattenwächterin“ female), else from the name. Portrait (`dungeon_gesicht.who`), voice, occupation word (form `m` or `w` of the table `nsc_beruf`, `dungeon_nsc.beruf_wort`) and the narrator's pronouns (`geschlecht` in `nsc_szene`) follow it. When the name or role names an occupation („Schattenwächter“ → guard), the card takes it instead of a random one (`dungeon_nsc.beruf_of`). Cards made before keep the gender their portrait was painted in; their occupation word adapts. The tables themselves (both forms per occupation, „looks“ only outward things) are kept by the DnDAppFiles thread.

**AI Dungeon Master: persuade to come along (V01.1.166, LlamaTale idea 4, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 „1-7“, idea only, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Persuade to come along* (off; builds on „NPC memory and chronicle“ and needs a round with a map). Without the switch a word like „come along“ or „join us“ takes a person of the map along at once. With it the panel decides who comes (`dungeon_mitkommen.py`, the question is recognised by the map thread's `dungeon_karte.fragen`): a person not at the group's place, without a card or at most neutral towards the hero (attitude below +1) stays without a check; „devoted“ (+3) comes without a check; in between the panel rolls a Charisma check instead of the model (friendly 13, warm 11). The answer is in the narration's facts (`mitkommen`), the model only writes the person's sentence and decides nothing. After the narration the map takes the person along (`dungeon_karte.mitnehmen`), the feed shows „Grimma comes along with you.“ or „Grimma stays here.“, the chronicle notes the joining. Whoever comes along travels and speaks with the group, but is no hired companion and does not fight by the panel's dice. Log „dungeon: mitkommen | probe/ja/nein/abgelehnt/treu/weg“, Live counts questions, those who came and those who stayed.

**AI Dungeon Master: going to a neighbouring place (V01.1.159, Dominik 2026-10-27, screenshot: „go to the old library“ got a Wisdom check for tracking, the group stayed in the alley):** No new switch. When an action names a word of going and exactly one neighbouring place by name or direction („go to the old library“, „I go north-east“), it is a walk (`dungeon_karte.asked_ways`). Fixed rules: the model's check for that seat goes, unless the action has a risky word (sneak, climb …); the facts name the walk (`karte.gewollt`) with the task to tell the way and the arrival; when the narration leads elsewhere, the fixed check „gehen“ sends it back once, and when the second try fails too the panel takes the way itself and adds „You set off, your destination: …“. A fight, or foes coming up this turn, holds the group. Also under *Spieler anleiten*: „vague“ now wants at least one point from the panel's data (a person here, a thing near, a foe, a way by its target or direction), the narration's own things alone no longer count („a presence“), and „What you can do here“ shows up to three ways. The dice card no longer shows the receipt number („W-2“), only as a tooltip with real dice; the fixed repair names the rolls instead of the numbers. The story fades out softly at the top instead of looking cut off under the header lines.

**AI Dungeon Master: wandering people and small gestures (V01.1.156, LlamaTale ideas 6 and 7, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7", ideas only, own code):** Two admin switches *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Wandering people* and *→ Small gestures* (off; build on "NPC memory and chronicle", apply to the round's host). Wandering people: after a long rest (`dungeon_rast.rest`) `dungeon_wandern.rest` calls the map's rule `dungeon_karte.wandern(…, "lange_rast")`: every person not travelling with the group moves one way on with `WANDERN` percent towards the place `dungeon_nsc.ziel_ort` names (a place of the map its open goals name, else a place of the kinds in `BERUF_ORT` for its occupation, its own when it fits). Every move stands as a rumour in the log, the next narration gets them as data (`gewandert`) with the task to tell only a meeting again; then `rd["gewandert"]` goes. Not between chapters, because the next chapter rolls a new map. Small gestures: `dungeon_nsc.geste(rd, rng)` picks a person of the scene with a trait of ±2 or more and its fixed line from `GESTEN` ({nsc, art, text}), without the model and without state; when one is due and that it never comes in the car is decided by the round's waiting time (`dungeon_warten`), which writes it into the log with the art "geste". Logs `dungeon: wandern | 1 personen`, `dungeon: karte | wandern | lange_rast | 1`, `dungeon: nsc | geste`. Live overview: long rests on the map, people moved, gestures.

**AI Dungeon Master: when a small gesture comes (V01.1.183, LlamaTale idea 7, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7"):** The gestures of `dungeon_nsc.geste` now show at the table. On every request of the round the panel checks by a fixed rule (`dungeon_warten.geste_faellig`, from `dungeon.maybe_close`): the turn is open, nothing has happened at the table for 2 minutes (`GESTE_S`; no action, no table talk, no gesture, counted from the turn's start), no fight, no pause, the narrator is not answering, no question for real dice waits, and at most 2 gestures a turn (`GESTE_ZUG`). Then `dungeon_warten.geste` writes the line of kind "geste" into the feed (🤲, fields `nsc`, `geste` for the gesture's kind, `text` de and en); nothing else in the round changes. If nobody fits, the time still counts and the next try comes only after another 2 minutes. While a seat plays from the car (`dungeon_auto.im_auto`) no gesture comes. An error here never breaks the request, it becomes a journal line. Live: gestures, nobody fitting, left out for the car (numbers only); log `dungeon: geste | gezeigt`, `| keine person`, `| auto`, `| fehler | kind`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_gesten`.

**AI Dungeon Master: errands from people (V01.1.155, LlamaTale idea 2, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7", idea only, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Errands from people* (off; builds on "NPC memory and chronicle", applies to the round's host). Before the narration `dungeon_auftraege.seen` reads every open action with fixed word lists (`ARBEIT`, `GEBEN`, `ZURUECK`) and the person of the scene it names (`dungeon_nsc.scene`, `dungeon_nsc.named`). A new errand (person not hostile, without an open errand, at most `OFFEN_MAX` = 3 open) comes from `roll`: kind from `ARTEN` (bring a thing of the market, talk with another person, defeat a foe of the world up to the group's level), reward `LOHN` times the group's level, experience `EP` for everyone at the table, two clues from fixed data (where the person was last by the map and its occupation, that merchants sell the thing and its text, the foe's lore and text), the reason is the person's wish from its card. Done only by receipts: bring, when the action gives the person the thing and the hero carries it (the thing goes); talk, when the other person is in the scene and named in an action; defeat, when a hit of this turn fells such a foe. Giving it back makes the person one step cooler. The narration gets the open errands with clues (`auftraege`) and the turn's decisions (`auftrag_jetzt`) as data and only tells the reason and the thanks; only the panel gives and closes. After the narration `auftraege.apply` carries it out: gold to the hero, experience to everyone (`ep_tisch`, reason "auftrag"), the giver one step warmer, lines in the log and the chronicle. Every seat sees the errands at the side of the table with reward and clues to open, in the apps too through the table view. Logs `dungeon: auftraege | neu | bringen`, `| erfuellt | reden`, `| zurueck`, `| kein neuer`. Live overview: given, done, given back.

**AI Dungeon Master: read thoughts (V01.1.154, LlamaTale idea 5, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7", idea only, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Read thoughts* (off; builds on "NPC memory and chronicle", applies to the round's host). Before the narration `dungeon_gedanken.checks` looks at every open action: when it casts the spell `gedanken_lesen` this turn (`kraft` from `check_powers`) or the hero still has the blessing of the spell or of the potion `gedankentrank` (a blessing with `q` from `QUELLEN`, `dungeon.bless` keeps the catalog id for it) and a word of `LESEN`, and it names a person of the scene (`dungeon_nsc.scene`, `dungeon_nsc.named`), the panel rolls an Intelligence check in place of the model's checks for that seat; how hard comes from the person's attitude towards the hero (`SG`: hostile 15, neutral 13, fond 11). The narration gets the check as a receipt and, only in this turn, the task to tell only the listening, never the content: the model writes no line for it, so the secret cannot reach the table through the narration. After the narration `gedanken.apply` writes a fixed line from the person's card (wish, secret, attitude towards the hero) into `rd["gedanken"][pid]`, failed "… shuts their thoughts away from you.". Only this seat sees the line (`gedanken.view`), the host only who read whose thoughts; new secret `gedanken` in `dungeon_sicht.GEHEIM`. The car never reads the line aloud, because others may listen there. Logs `dungeon: gedanken | probe`, `| gelesen`, `| verschlossen`, `| kein ziel`. Live overview: how often thoughts were read, read, shut away.

**AI Dungeon Master: witness rule (V01.1.152, LlamaTale idea 3, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7", idea only, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Witness rule* (off; builds on "NPC memory and chronicle", applies to the round's host). Before the narration `dungeon_zeugen.seen` reads every open action: an attack by `dungeon_kampf.intent`, theft, a gift or help by the fixed word lists `TATEN`, plus a person of the scene (`dungeon_nsc.scene`) by its whole name or the last word of its name. The fixed table `FOLGE` gives the steps: attack and theft target −2, everyone else present −1; gift and help target +1. A theft whose Dexterity check passes this turn stays unseen; an attack on a foe of the running fight is no deed. The narration gets the deeds and the new attitude as data (`zeugen`, plus a task only in this turn) and only tells the reaction. After the narration `zeugen.apply` sets the attitude (once per person and hero a turn, within −3 to +3), the log shows every change, attack and theft go into the chronicle "in front of witnesses"; the model's own report for the same pair is dropped this turn (`zeugen.drop`). Logs `dungeon: zeugen | diebstahl | 1 zeugen`, `dungeon: zeugen | diebstahl | unbemerkt`. Live overview: deeds in front of witnesses, witnesses with a new attitude, thefts unseen.

**AI Dungeon Master: guide the players, steps 3 to 5 (V01.1.151, plan `dungeon-anleitung/plan.md` ideas 4, 12, 6 and 11):** Under the same switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Guide the players*. New fixed check "vage" in `erz.judge` (`dungeon_anleiten.vague`): if the narration names fewer than two points of the scene (people here, things nearby and of the narration, foes and props in a fight, speaking people, targets of the ways; as many as the scene has when there are fewer) or ends with an empty question of `HOHL` ("Was tut ihr?", "Was macht ihr nun?", "What do you do?"), the narrator writes it once again with a hint naming up to three names to take up; the second try stays as it is (`erz.repair`, "nachbessern | bleibt | vage"). A 👎 can carry the fixed reason "Did not know what we could do" (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.GRUENDE` "vage", only while the switch is on). The narrator bench has the scene "Sackgasse" (a hall with Grimma, ways of the map and a chest; the narration must name two of them and end with a concrete choice; the check always counts there). Logs `dungeon: anleitung | vage | zu wenig`, `dungeon: anleitung | vage | hohl`. Live overview: told again as vague. Step 4, stall hint (`anleiten.progress`): progress by fixed rules is a new place, a new or closed thread, a new thing, an act done, a fight that begins or ends. Without progress after `WINK` told turns (Beginner 1, Normal 2, Experienced never) the next narration gets stage 1 as data (`wink` 1: show something tangible clearly that leads on), one turn later stage 2 (an event of the fixed table `stillstand` of the rules, checked in `check_rules`, at the children's table only mild ones, rolled by the panel), one more turn later the panel adds a line "Tipp: Ihr könnt mit Grimma sprechen oder nach Norden gehen." from the scene's data; then the stages start again. Way out after a failed check: a failed check to find something out stays failed, but the seat's buttons of the next turn start with another way to the same goal ("I ask Grimma about it.", else a way of the map). Logs `dungeon: anleitung | wink | 2`, `dungeon: anleitung | ausweg | 1`. Live overview: hints per stage, ways out. Step 5, clues (the three-clue rule): every act of a story carries up to three clues to the turning point (`spuren`, 5 to 100 characters each, checked in `check_plan` with the words of the tone); the planner and the import ask for them, each of the 50 shipped stories has two per act in German and English, the planner form has a field "Clues to the turning point". The plan's facts name the next clue not shown yet (`anleiten.clue_facts`, with "deutlich" at stall stage 1). When a narration names most words of a clue, it counts as shown (`anleiten.seen`, `rd["spuren"]`) and as progress; a new act starts again. A clue not shown yet is secret: a narrator's way that names it is dropped. The host sees ✓ (shown) or 🔎 per clue in the plan. Log `dungeon: anleitung | spur | 1/2`. Live overview: clues shown.

**AI Dungeon Master: guide the players, steps 1 and 2 (V01.1.148, plan `dungeon-anleitung/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-5"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Guide the players* (`chat.dungeon_anleiten`, off). Step 1 (ideas 3, 7 and 10): the narration gets a fixed form (`dungeon_anleiten.py`): first what the deeds cause, then what is to be seen now, and in every narration at least two things to take up, with names (a person, a thing, a way), only from the facts or its own narration; at the end a concrete choice for one figure, never an empty "What do you do?". Every world has a model narration that ends this way (`welten[].anleiten` in the rules, one pair German and English, checked like the style examples). Whoever wrote nothing for `STILL` turns gets a question the panel builds from the scene's data, never from the model's words: in a fight the foes standing (the weakest first), else people here and ways of the map, such as "Bela, do you talk to Grimma, or do you go north?"; with only one point "do you look around here more closely" is the second, with none the old line stays. Logs `dungeon: anleitung | frage | 2`, `dungeon: anleitung | frage | zu wenig`. Live overview: questions to quiet players from the scene, too few points. Step 2 (ideas 1, 2, 8 and 9): below every narration every seat that has not acted yet gets "What you can do here" with buttons from data only (`anleiten.buttons`): the narrator's ways of this turn that passed the fixed check, the scene's points of `dungeon_rueckfrage.points` (one source with the Rückfrage: foes, a prop, people here, ways of the map, looking around), a healing spell or potion when someone is below half their life, a thing of the scene and an open thread. A tap puts the text into the action field; an action that is one of the buttons counts as taken. The narrator may suggest two to three ways in `wege` every turn; the panel takes at most `MODELL_WEGE` = 2 that are short, have a doing word, name something of the scene or the narration, have no word of the refused lists and no word only a later act, the turning point or a secret of the truth knows (`anleiten.check_ways`). The host chooses the level per round in the game master console under Narrator (`erzaehler` with `anleitung`): Beginner four buttons with the value that likely counts (fixed table `WURF`, never the difficulty), read aloud in the car after every narration; Normal three buttons (default); Experienced no buttons, only the form of the narration. In the car "What can I do?" says the buttons, the CarPlay list "What you can do here" shows them, "the first" or a button sends the action. Additional logs `dungeon: anleitung | zeile | 2`, `| knopf genommen`, `| weg | 2`, `| weg verworfen | geheim 1`, `| stufe | einsteiger`. Live overview additionally: lines, buttons taken, ways taken and refused.

**AI Dungeon Master: time of day by the panel (V01.1.145, LlamaTale idea 1, plan `plaene/dungeon-llamatale-ideen.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7"; only the idea of LlamaTale, LGPL-3.0, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Time of day by the panel* (`chat.dungeon_tageszeit`, off). The panel sets the time of day by fixed rules, never the model (`dungeon_tageszeit.py`): an even half day of the world's calendar is morning and day, an odd one evening and night, `ZUEGE` = 6 told turns each. After twelve turns of a half day the calendar runs half a day on by itself (`kalender.weiter` "zeit"), also while the calendar of the world is off (it then counts silently). Ways and journeys run as before, a long rest and the inn always end at the next morning. Fixed effects: 1. At night smith, lesson, improving gear, healer and the merchant are shut (`tageszeit.LADEN_ZU`, refusal "nacht", in the settlement window "🌙 Shut at night"); inn, rumour and companions stay open. 2. At night outdoors (not in a vault, where `dungeon_alarm` counts the torches, and not in a settlement) a hero without darkvision rolls checks at a disadvantage when nobody in the group has a light (light spell, a thing with `licht`, a torch in a bag). 3. At night the panel offers foes of the night (bestiary `nacht: true`) when at least as many fit as it offers. The narrator gets the time of day as data and on the first turn after a change `tageszeit_neu`, then tells the change in one or two sentences; its field `szene.tageszeit` does not count while the switch is on. On top of the table stands "🌇 Evening · 4 turns left". Logs `dungeon: tageszeit | nacht | panel`, `dungeon: tageszeit | zu | lehre`, `dungeon: tageszeit | dunkel`. Live overview: changes, checks in the dark of the night, shops shut. Without the switch the narrator reports the time of day as before (only forward).

**AI Dungeon Master: night foes in the SRD catalog (V01.1.146, data, LlamaTale 1 "time of day from the panel", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** 76 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carry `"nacht": true`: creatures that per the SRD are mostly about at night (undead such as skeleton, zombie, ghoul, specter, shadow, wight, vampire spawn and wraith; wererat, werewolf, weretiger and werebear; wolf, worg, bats, rats, spiders and owlbear; peoples with sunlight sensitivity such as kobolds and drow; nightmare and night hag), plus bandits, highwaymen, thugs and spies lying in wait in the dark, and own night creatures of horror, sci-fi and pirates (living scarecrow, shadow child, ghost pirate, xeno crawler, sentry drone). There are some on every level from 0 to 15. In every world at least 5 night foes fit the budget of one hero of level 1 to 3; self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_nacht_gegner_je_welt_und_stufe`).

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and runewords in the catalog (V01.1.153, data and check rules, plan `dungeon-runen/plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1a 2b 3a 4a 5a 6a 7a 8a 10a 11a", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** No switch and no effect in the game yet; the main thread builds the core with the switch "Runes and sockets". `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carries 13 runes (`runen`, rank 1 to 13, historical rune names, public domain: Uruz, Ehwaz, Ansuz, Fehu, Kenaz, Isa, Thurisaz, Nauthiz, Jera, Berkano, Sowilo, Algiz, Tiwaz) with meaning (strength, team …), price (20 to 1500 gold) and effect per place `waffe`, `ruestung` and `schild` (`null` = not possible there, e.g. Algiz in a weapon), plus 7 runewords (`runenwoerter`: Ember, Frostward, Stormsong, Wanderer's luck, Rootstrength, Daybreak, Victor's oath) with the runes in a fixed order, place (`waffe`, `koerper`, `nebenhand`, `kopf`), number of sockets, minimum level and extra effect. An effect names fixed keys only: `treffer` and `schaden` (1 to 2, weapon only), `effekt` (an id from `effekte`, only where its "nur" fits), `werte` with `bonus` 1, `leben`, `widerstand` (a damage type), `rabatt`, `gold`, `heiltrank`, `rk` and `schutz`. The new module `app/panel/dungeon_runen.py` checks both as part of `dungeon.check_rules` (own ids, ranks without a gap, prices up to "epic", only these keys, a runeword's runes exist and are possible at its place, sockets = number of runes, no sequence twice). Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_runen_und_runenwoerter`).

**AI Dungeon Master: occupations in both forms, looks only outward (V01.1.161, data and check rule, asked by the main thread, thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** Every row of the NPC table "Occupation" (`nsc_beruf`, 61 occupations) names besides `t` the male and the female German form (`m`, `w`: Heiler/Heilerin, Bergmann/Bergfrau, Gelehrter/Gelehrte, Hirte/Hirtin, Steuermann/Steuerfrau, Schiffsjunge/Schiffsmädchen, Wache/Wache), so the occupation fits the person's fixed gender; "Bord-KI" (ship AI) is no longer a person's occupation (review 27.10., Z2/Z8). "Looks" (`nsc_aussehen`) describes only the outside now: 12 rows about manner and behaviour moved to "Manner" (`nsc_art`) as a short trait (absent-minded, speaks with an accent, never travels alone, winning, full of energy, calm and composed, nervous, eccentric, swaggering, uncanny, shifty-eyed, speaks in a whisper). `dungeon_tabellen.check_rules` requires `m` and `w` on every occupation (`FORMEN`, nowhere else allowed) and refuses in "Looks" any word of the fixed list `AUSSEHEN_NICHT`. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_berufe_mit_formen_und_aussehen_nur_aeusseres`).

**AI Dungeon Master: what a foe is, companions with gender and age, fixed looks (V01.1.164, data and check rules, Dungeon review 27.10. Z7, Z9, Z11 and T24a, Dominik "ok", thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** Each of the 268 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carries the field `wesen`, one of `mensch` (an ordinary human), `humanoid` (another folk of human shape: elf, dwarf, goblin, orc, lizard and sea folk, lycanthropes), `riese` (giants, ogres, trolls), `tier` (animal), `untot` (undead), `konstrukt` (anything built: golem, animated object, robot, drone, AI), `drache` (dragon), `unhold` (devils and demons), `elementar`, `fee` (fey and hags), `himmlisch` (celestial), `pflanze` (plant), `schleim` (ooze), `aberration` (alien beings, the xeno creatures too) and `monstrum` (every other creature of myth). This is the one source instead of the hand-made lists in the picture module; the gaps of the review (pirate, commoner, scout, goblin warrior, werebear …) are closed. The new module `app/panel/dungeon_bestiarium.py` holds the list `WESEN`, the reader `wesen(g)` and the check as part of `dungeon.check_rules`; an admin's own foe may leave the field out. The companions (`ideen.gefaehrten` and `gefaehrten_kinder`) carry `geschlecht` (as for heroes: weiblich, maennlich, divers), `alter` (jung, mittel, alt; never a child, companions fight) and `art` (mensch, tier, maschine): Grete is a middle-aged sellsword, Brunhild an old archer, K-7 a machine, Wuschel an animal. `dungeon_ideen.check_rules` requires the three fields. New NPC tables for fixed looks the panel rolls onto a person's card (Z9, the main thread builds the roll): `nsc_haar` (hair, 15 rows such as "curly red hair", "a bald head", in science fiction "bright blue hair"), `nsc_augen` (eyes, 7), `nsc_bart` (beard, 10, with "no beard", only for male people from young on) and `nsc_statur` (build, 12). A row of hair, eyes and beard names its colour as a fixed word (`farbe`: rot, braun, schwarz, grau, weiss, blau, gruen), and the text shows exactly that colour, so the narrator's look check recognises it; `dungeon_tabellen.check_rules` allows `farbe` only in these tables (`FARBE`). Five foes carry `"friedlich": true` (commoner, unicorn, couatl, young silver dragon, giant seahorse; review T24a): the encounter offer never draws them as foes (`dungeon_bestiarium.friedlich`). Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_wesen_und_gefaehrten`).

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit wave 2, the foes' saving throws (V01.1.142, report `dungeon-kampf-audit/bericht.md` points H4 and N6, data from V01.1.140):** No new switch. 1. **H4 spells with a saving throw:** when a spell of the catalog has a saving throw (`rettung`, 44 spells, such as Fireball, Sacred Flame, Sleep), in a fight the hero no longer rolls against the armour class; the panel rolls for the foe: d20 + the bonus of its value from the bestiary against 10 + the proficiency bonus + the caster's value (as for a check) + the spell's bonus above the blessing on it (`kampf.ZAUBER_SG`, `kampf.spell_save`; 8 in D&D, but a save knows no critical hit and no advantage, with 10 the balance bench keeps its aim); a 20 always passes, a 1 never. Failed: the full damage and the spell's condition (`zustand`, such as prone, restrained, unconscious) for its turns; passed: with `halb` half the damage, else nothing. Logs `dungeon: kampf | rettung gegner | geschafft / nicht geschafft`; the story shows a row of its own "saving throw Wisdom = 3 vs DC 15 · failed" with damage and condition. 2. **Banishing spells:** a spell without damage with a saving throw and a condition (`kampf.bann`: Sleep, Entangle, Hold Person, Command, Fear, Hypnotic Pattern, Stinking Cloud, Banishment and others, 13 spells) now works on a foe in a fight and costs a charge when it is cast at one; the caster gets no blessing of their own from it. Only the other control spells without a saving throw (Counterspell, Bane, Slow, Polymorph, Confusion) still do not work in a fight and cost nothing. 3. **Healing a friend:** a spell with the target "verbuendeter" (Healing Hands, Healing Word, Cure Wounds and others, 10 spells) works on the hero whose name is in the action (`dungeon.friend`, only the exact name), else on the caster; never on a dead or unconscious hero. The rules page shows the new rows "Foe's saving throw" and "Condition on the foe". 4. **N6 the heroes' saving throw:** when a foe's hit causes a condition, the hero rolls against the bestiary's DC (`sg`, 10 to 19), only without one against 10 + a quarter of the foe's level.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit wave 2, scrolls, order and targets (V01.1.139, report `dungeon-kampf-audit/bericht.md` points M4, M7, N1, N2 and N4):** No new switch. 1. **M4 attack scrolls:** whoever casts a scroll with a damage spell from the bag (Fireball, Ice Lance, Chain Lightning) makes the next attack in this or the next turn that spell, with its damage kind and die, once (`h["rolle"]`, logs `dungeon: rolle | bereit` and `dungeon: kampf | rolle | angriff`); unused it lapses. 2. **M7 order:** it is fixed: first all heroes act, then all foes. So a spell's healing and blessing (`cast_powers`) now come before the turn's hits (`consequences`); a healer with 1 life who heals 8 and takes 9 damage is down afterwards. There is no initiative. 3. **N2 targets:** when a target names several foes, the whole name wins, then the kind (the end of the name: "Ratte" is the giant rat, never the rat swarm), then the start; among several of the same kind the one with the least life. 4. **N4 experience:** a fight's experience is divided rounded down (25 for 2 heroes: 12 each). The share of a hero who dies in the fight goes to the living; the share of one who left the table lapses. 5. **N1 milder than 5e on purpose:** there is no "massive damage" (dead at once), and damage while down does not count as a failed death save; foes do not attack heroes on the ground anyway.

**AI Dungeon Master: saving throws, conditions and DCs in the SRD catalog (V01.1.140, data, report `dungeon-kampf-audit/bericht.md` points H4, N6 and N7, thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** Data only in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json`, following SRD 5.2.1 (CC-BY-4.0). 1. **Saving throw and condition:** 18 spells without damage that act on foes carry `rettung: {"wert": …}` (one of the six scores), 13 of them also a `zustand` from the list (Sleep unconscious, Hold person paralysed, Entangle and Telekinesis restrained, Fear frightened, Command prone, Colour spray blinded, Stinking cloud poisoned, Banishment incapacitated, Animal friendship, Suggestion, Hypnotic pattern and Dominate person charmed); Bane, Slow, Confusion, Polymorph and Faerie fire have no condition in the SRD and carry only the saving throw. 2. **Damage with a saving throw:** 22 damage spells carry `rettung`, 17 of them `"halb": true` (success = half damage, e.g. Burning hands, Thunderwave, Lightning bolt, Cone of cold, Cloudkill); without `halb` (Sacred flame, Acid splash, Vicious mockery, Ice knife) a success means no damage; Black tentacles: failure damage and restrained, success nothing. Spells with an attack roll (Fire bolt, Magic missile …) and smites (Searing smite, Thunderous smite) keep no saving throw. 3. **Target "verbuendeter":** Revivify, Raise dead, Cure wounds, Healing word, Lesser and Greater restoration, Death ward, Remove curse and Heroism act on a creature touched or seen in the SRD and carry `ziel: verbuendeter`. 4. **DC per foe:** Each of the 268 foes carries `sg`, the DC of its conditions: for 89 foes from the SRD 5.2.1 stat block (the save that causes one of its conditions, else its highest DC), for the other 179 by the SRD formula 8 + proficiency bonus of its level + best modifier (states of the mind with Intelligence, Wisdom or Charisma, the rest with Strength, Dexterity or Constitution). 5. **Guard:** a spear, so `schaden_art: stich`. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_rettung_zustand_halb_und_sg`). `dungeon.ZIELE` knows "verbuendeter"; until the rules for it are in the panel (main thread), it acts like "selbst".

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit wave 2, blessings, armour and flight (V01.1.138, report `dungeon-kampf-audit/bericht.md` points M3, M5 and M6):** No new switch. 1. **M3 blessings do not stack:** on a value only the highest blessing counts (`dungeon.blessing`), three times Thorn Vines gives +3 instead of +9, the same blessing again only renews its turns. **Concentration:** when a hero casts a new spell with a blessing, the hero's last blessing ends on every hero (`dungeon._cast`, log `dungeon: segen | konzentration | N beendet`); blessings of other heroes, of potions and altars stay. In a spell attack the spell's bonus counts only as far as it is above the blessing already on the value. 2. **M5 armour class and damage:** a new helper `dungeon.body` gives the value as the body has it: the score's bonus and the blessing, less a wound, if asked with the items worn for the value and their effects (never a weapon: its bonus belongs to the weapon's hit and damage, never twice); without abilities and lessons (those still count only for checks, `dungeon.total`). The armour class takes Dexterity with the items (with the armour's cap), a blessing on Dexterity (Shield, Haste, cat's potion) counts as magic past that cap. Damage takes the score's bonus and the blessing without item values (with them the group won too often at level 8 on the balance bench), so Berserker Rage (+4 Strength) also hits harder. 3. **M6 flight only by a check:** fixed words (`kampf.FLUCHT_WORTE`: fliehen, flüchten, Rückzug, wegrennen, abhauen, sich zurückziehen, flee, run away, retreat, escape) make an action a flight, an attack word wins. When more than half of the heroes who can act flee, each of them rolls the panel's Dexterity check in place of the model's checks, against the higher of 10 and 8 + the strongest foe's level (`FLUCHT_SG`, `kampf.escape`). If at least half pass, the fight ends with "flucht", else it goes on (`kampf | flucht | entkommen / gestellt`). Fewer fleeing: the words stay words (`kampf | flucht | keine mehrheit`). A `kampf_ende` "flucht" of the model alone no longer ends anything (`kampf | flucht | ohne probe`). The flight check gives no check experience.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat word boundaries (V01.1.133, check `dungeon-kampf-audit/nachpruefung-v130.md`):** No new switch. 1. **Word boundaries:** every word of `NICHT_ANGRIFF` ends at a word boundary. "Ich greife Nachtmahr an" and "Ich schlage Sicherheitsdroide" are attacks on exactly that foe again; before, "greife nach" or "schlage sich" swallowed the start of the name and turned it into a model check with check experience in the middle of a fight. A self-test attacks every foe of the bestiary without an article (German and English, eight and five verbs) and demands an attack with the whole name. 2. **Word table:** attacks now also include "schlage dich / euch nieder", "trete ein paar Mal nach" and "Gebt Feuer auf die Wache" (with a foe named). No attack: "Ich haue ab", "schlage Alarm", "trete die Tür ein", "werfe der Wache Gold zu", "I hit the road" and "I strike a deal"; "Ich trete ein" stays no attack. 3. **A hero down rolls nothing:** the panel drops a model check for a hero at 0 life, unconscious or dead (`dungeon: probe | verworfen | zustand`), so no more check experience for it.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit, follow-up check (V01.1.130, check `dungeon-kampf-audit/nachpruefung-welle1.md`):** Three gaps of wave 1 closed, no new switch. 1. **No more false alarms:** `NICHT_ANGRIFF` now also knows "trete den Rückzug an", "trete zur Seite / zurück / beiseite / ein / vor / näher", "schlage vor", "schlage mich in die Büsche", "werfe der Wache einen Blick zu", "gebe der Wache Feuer", "step back", "retreat", "fall back", "cast a glance", "suggest" and "propose"; these actions never attack. "Ich trete die Wache" and "Ich schlage auf den Ork ein" stay attacks. 2. **The model's target alone never attacks:** whether an action attacks is decided only by its words (an attack word, a damage spell, or a reference word with a named foe). The `ziel` from the model's check only picks which foe an attack already recognised hits. 3. **Peace once a fight, no check EP:** the Charisma check for peace happens once a fight (`kampf.frieden`); a second try in the same fight rolls nothing (`dungeon: kampf | frieden | schon versucht`). The check itself gives no check EP, only a success gives half the fight EP. 4. **Control spells without a model check:** when a hero casts a spell from `KONTROLLE` in a fight, the panel drops the model's check for that hero (`dungeon: probe | kampf | kontrolle verworfen`) until the saving throws are built; before, it gave 13 EP. 5. **No attack when down:** a hero on the ground, unconscious or dead never attacks, not even with an earlier action (`attacks` checks `zustand`). 6. **Bench measured anew:** the bench now attacks with an attack action and starts every turn without the actions of the turn before; before, casters also attacked in the turn of their healing potion, so large groups won too often. `GRUPPE` is now 0.7 and 0.33 for each further hero, every cell lies at 72 to 88 %. For the people (`dungeon_personen.GEWICHT`) veteran and knight now count 1.15 instead of 1.21, the mean lies at 75 %, every cell at 57 to 96 %. Self-tests `Fight.test_kampf_angriffswoerter_tabelle` (new rows), `test_kampf_modellziel_allein_kein_angriff`, `test_kampf_frieden_einmal_je_kampf_ohne_proben_ep`.

**AI Dungeon Master: telling richly (V01.1.109, Dominik 2026-10-26 "ausführliche erzählweise ist immer noch kanpp"):** The round's way of telling only raised the upper limit so far. Now the narrator's task also names a minimum length (`dungeon.WEISE_MIN`: brief none, normal 500, rich 1000 characters, always 300 below the upper limit, `erz_min`) and a fixed style sentence (`WEISE_STIL`): for "rich" several paragraphs with senses, mood and a moment of its own for every hero who acted, for "brief" a few clear sentences. For "rich" the panel checks the minimum by a fixed rule (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`, fault `knapp`): too short is told anew once with a fixed hint, then the try stays. Log `dungeon: nachbessern | knapp`, `| bleibt | knapp`; counted with the narrator rules. Self-test `Fight.test_longer_at_the_moments_and_the_rounds_way_of_telling`.

**AI Dungeon Master: heroes comment (V01.1.110, Dominik 2026-10-26 "ebenso fehlt ein bisschen das die helden mehr eigende komentare abgeben", chosen "2a"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Heroes comment* (`chat.dungeon_kommentar`, off by default; `dungeon_kommentar.py`). In the field `helden_sagen` the narrator gives every hero who acted this turn a short line in its own words about its own deed or the outcome. The panel checks every line by fixed rules: only heroes whose seat acted (not one that got an ooc line, not one lying down), one line per hero, plain text up to 120 characters, no number, no word of the word list, no first-person deed ("ich gehe", "we take" …) and no deciding word. The lines stand in the narration's log entry (`helden`) and at the table as a speech bubble "💬 name" under the narration; read aloud, the hero's voice speaks them after the narration, the players' own words in quotes still come first. What a hero does only its player writes. The app shows them at the game table. Live: said, dropped today. Log `dungeon: kommentar | N gesagt | M verworfen`. Self-test `Turns.test_heroes_comment_on_their_own_deeds`.

**AI Dungeon Master: more fixed checks of the narration, each with a rescue (V01.1.084, Dominik 2026-10-25 “können wir diese fehler für die zukunf immer abfangen und verbessern?”, chosen “1-3 und 1-7 umsetzten”):** After every turn the panel checks the narration with fixed word lists, never with the model, in addition to names, checks, foes, dice receipts, veils, places, things and repetition: a fallen foe acts no more and a standing one does not die without a hit receipt (`gefallen`); what the character does not have or cannot do does not work, a thing the hero does not carry too (the action gets the grey line “… does not carry that”, `fehlt`); the narration names no amount of gold or loot, the panel sets it (`gold`); a hero who did not act this turn decides nothing (`fremd`); a person's hair, eye and beard colour follow their NPC card (`aussehen`); the time of day only goes forward, a new morning only after the night (else “tageszeit | verworfen”). On a fault the narrator writes again with a hint; when the last try fails too, the panel rescues it itself: it strikes the sentences with the fault as long as enough narration stays, takes the speech from people at another place, or adds a fixed sentence (a missing hero “waits to see”). Every rescue writes a line “nachbessern | fest” or “| bleibt”, the live overview counts the rescues, things not carried and discarded times of day.

**AI Dungeon Master: game master console as cards (V01.1.083, Dominik 2026-10-25 “muss alles besser angeordnet und geaphsh aufgewertet werden”, chosen “empfehlung umsetzten für das spielleiter pult”, proposal 1 “cards”):** On top of the console stands the big “▶ Go on now”, beside it one chip per hero showing who has acted (✓) and who is still missing. Below it four cards of equal height, two side by side: “Turn” with pause and market as switches and behind “⋯” end chapter or round (asking again), “At the table” with the tiles read aloud, scene picture and invite in one row and the switch “Last sentence in the mail”, “Narrator” with the way of telling as a band of buttons brief, normal, rich and below it the voice with sample and change, “Difficulty” with the combat stage as a band of buttons easy to deadly and the game master dial as a switch “hold” with a bar of the danger. What only the host sees stands at the bottom in one strip “Only for you” with tabs story, truth, things, random rules and oracle instead of loose boxes. Small caps only for the cards' headings, no cut-off choice list any more. On the phone the cards stand one below the other. Only the view changes: every button sends the same request as before, the panel checks it as before. With it the catalog checks the tables of the D&D ideas (Dominik 2026-10-25 “1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du”) by fixed rules before a round sees them (`dungeon_ideen.check_rules`): under “ideen” in `dungeon_srd.json` critical hit tables per damage type and fumbles for melee, ranged and spells (d12, states only from the list, 1 to 3 turns), landscapes with night watches (d20 without gaps) and travel events, question cards, travel roles, scars (d20, at most −1 on one value) with plasters and sayings, and the saying on every foe (`sage`). They are played only in the next versions.

**AI Dungeon Master: content of the D&D ideas 2, 4, 8, 9 and 12 (V01.1.090, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du wie du alles umsetzten kannst."):** `dungeon_srd.json` holds the fixed tables under "ideen", checked by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`. All texts are our own, because the templates from the repos (darker-dungeons CC BY-NC-ND, VoloBot and rpg-card without a licence) count only as ideas. **Critical tables:** 12 rows per damage kind for a d12. Rows 1 to 4 are narration only, the others set an existing condition for 1 to 3 turns, incapacitated, paralysed, stunned and unconscious always for 1 turn only. **Fumbles:** melee, ranged and spell with 12 mild rows each; a condition only hits the one who fumbles, never a friend. **Landscapes:** 13, among them "weg" as the fallback and "huegel" and "gras" from the map's terrain, each with the kinds of places it stands for, with night watches on a d20 (quiet, noise, visitor, ambush) and 8 travel events (quiet, rested, hungry, lost, surprised). **Campfire:** 12 questions for all worlds and 12 each for fantasy, horror, sci-fi and pirates. **Travel roles:** scout, hunter, watch and pathfinder with a value, names per world and a consequence on failure. **Scars:** d20, rows 1 to 4 with −1 on a value until a healer, otherwise only a scar; at the children's table only plasters; plus 8 lines from the tavern. **Legend:** each of the 268 foes has the field `sage` (at most 200 characters per language) for the monster album. Self-tests `test_jeder_gegner_hat_eine_sage` and `test_ideen_tabellen_und_sagen` (no line carries a word of the children's tone).

**AI Dungeon Master: the table on the phone (V01.1.078, points 1 to 6 of the table review of 2026-10-24, Dominik 2026-10-25 “alles 1-5 umsetzten”):** On the phone the table's head is one row: title, turn, 🔊 and “⋯”. Under “⋯” are “← Rounds”, “How to play”, light or dark, “Full screen”, music and sounds and, for the host, the game master console; the tabs Rounds, Heroes and Rules stay away at the table, which uses the whole width. The fight stands in one box with a row per foe that no longer sticks over the narration; the encounter in the story is just a line of foes, a tap opens their window with every value (on a computer too). The field to act stands above the bottom bar in every pane (Story, Group, Hero, Map), above it “● Your turn” and how many have acted; the narration ends above the field. The group is a list with picture, name, life bar and state, a tap on a hero shows its values and equipment (on a computer too). The “Hero” pane shows the armour class, a row “Quick” (drink a healing potion, abilities and spells with a charge, the weapon in hand; ability, spell and weapon write their sentence into the field, the panel checks it as if typed) and the bag, a tap on a thing opens its window. The first time the guide only opens as a small card “How it works” with three sentences and “More”; the pages start with “What you write” and “When it goes on”, “Map and vaults” comes later. When the iPhone app opens the table there is no “Full screen”, and a guest without a profile no longer sees a Spark row at the table. A failed check by which a character wants to find something out (examine, search, read, recognise …) no longer gives anything away (Dominik 2026-10-25, the letter in the Schattengasse): when the narration or the grey line still names a direction, a trail or a find, the narrator writes again, and if need be the panel strikes those sentences. Plain going or travelling no longer gets a check: the panel drops a check whose purpose names only a word for going and none for a risk (quietly, climb, flee …). When the narrator repeats the last scene even on the last try, the panel strikes the sentences told before, as long as enough new ones stay. Security: display only, every action goes through the panel's checks as before; “&app=1” in the link only changes the view; the narration check follows fixed word lists, never the model.

**AI Dungeon Master: factions with clocks (V01.1.064, tabletop point 7, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", clocks with fields as common in tabletop play, own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Factions with clocks* (`chat.dungeon_fraktionen`, off from the start; `dungeon_fraktionen.py`). Every group of the chapter's fixed truth (`wahrheit.gruppen`, name and what it wants) becomes a faction with a clock of 4, 6 or 8 fields the panel rolls; at most `FRAKTIONEN_MAX` (6). After every told turn the panel rolls a d6 for each running clock, from `UHR_WURF` (5) it moves a field. The narration reports in `fraktionen` at most 2 groups whose goal an action of the heroes brings closer (`hilft`) or hinders (`stoert`); the panel then turns the clock a field, but only when the narration names the group (also without its article), and only once per group and turn. A full clock means: the group has got what it wanted. That stands in the feed (and in the chronicle while "NPC memory and chronicle" is on), the next narration gets it once as data (`fraktion_voll`) with the fixed rule to tell how the world changes, and the group holds the group's place on the map (`dungeon_fraktionen.occupied`: the hook for the map thread). The model gets every faction with goal and clock only as data. **Visibility:** at the table "⏳ Factions" opens a window with the clocks of the groups the narration has named, never their goal; the host sees every group with its goal there (`fraktionen_geheim`, `dungeon_sicht.NUR_GASTGEBER`, in the visibility self-test as the secret "fraktion_ziel"), adds own ones, changes goal and fields, turns a clock by hand (−/+) or removes a group (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/fraktion` with `name`, `will`, `felder`, `schritt` or `weg`, host only, fixed values only). The factions go on into the next chapter. **Live:** Status → Live counts factions, full clocks and fields turned per day. Self-test `test_factions_with_clocks_turned_by_fixed_rules` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat stages easy to deadly (V01.1.063, tabletop point 13, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", own code; the table from the SRD 5.2.1, CC-BY-4.0):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Combat stages* (`chat.dungeon_stufen`, off from the start; `dungeon_kampf.STUFEN`). The host chooses in the game master console at "⚔ Combat stage" Easy, Medium, Hard or Deadly (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` with `kampfstufe`, host only, only a value of the list). Medium stays the measured budget (`BUDGET`, 70 to 90 wins in 100). For Easy and Hard the panel takes the ratio of the SRD 5.2.1 budget table (`budget` in the catalog: low, moderate, high, XP per hero for levels 1 to 10) to Medium, averaged over the heroes' levels; Deadly is Hard times `TOEDLICH` (1.5), since the table has no deadly. Without the table fixed ratios hold (`STUFE_OHNE`: 0.67 and 1.4). The stage works from the next encounter on together with the children's table and the game master dial; the danger the players see still measures against Medium. `dungeon_kampf.check_rules` checks the table (10 numbers each, low < moderate < high). **Live:** Status → Live counts rounds with an own combat stage. Self-test `test_combat_stages_by_the_srd_table` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: foes, spells and combat budget from the SRD 5.2.1 (V01.1.063, Dominik 2026-10-25, tabletop point 13, data from the thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** The catalog grows by 44 foes and 30 spells from the System Reference Document 5.2.1 (CC-BY-4.0). Among the foes are goblin warrior, pegasus, sprite, werebear, young brass, silver and white dragons, stone, frost and fire giant, roc, pirate, freebooter captain, merfolk, reef shark, vampire familiar and everyday people like scout, guard captain and tough; among the spells cure wounds, guiding bolt, ice knife, shatter, moonbeam, wall of fire and greater restoration. All foes carry damage type, resistance, immunity, boss and multiattack like the others. The experience (`ep`) of 21 new foes that fight harder with the game's rules than their challenge rating says (such as guard captain, archelon, fire giant, young brass dragon, acolyte, axe beak) is calibrated one to three steps higher, until the balance bench shows 70 to 90 % wins at "medium" for every level and group size again (`test_the_aim_at_medium_for_every_level_and_group`); so they come only for fitting groups and give more experience. The table "combat budget" (`budget`) comes from the SRD 5.2.1. **Children's tone:** no name and no text of a foe, item, condition, people, class or background holds a word of the children's tone word list, else the narrator could never name it at the children's table; old entries are renamed for it (for example "giant gnat", "dragon slayer", "heliotrope", the potion "Dragon's ember", the condition "wounded"). Self-tests `test_budget_aus_dem_srd_521` and `test_kinderton_im_katalog` in `test_dungeon_srd`. Attribution: *This work includes material from the System Reference Document 5.2.1 ("SRD 5.2.1") by Wizards of the Coast LLC, available at https://www.dndbeyond.com/srd. The SRD 5.2.1 is licensed under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License, available at https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode.*

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC goals (V01.1.061, tabletop point 6, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", idea from NarrativeEngine-P, MIT, own code):** Part of *Features → AI Dungeon Master → NPC memory and chronicle* (`chat.dungeon_nsc`, `dungeon_nsc.py`). With its card every person gets six traits (`WESEN`: brave/fearful, honest/sly, kind/gruff, generous/stingy, calm/hot-headed, loyal/fickle), each 2d4−5, so −3 to +3, the middle most often, and a goal on three levels (`EBENEN`): today (2 steps), soon (4, the person's goal from the hidden truth, else from the wish table) and in life (6), all different. After every told turn the panel rolls a d6 per person for its next open goal: from `ZIEL_WURF` (5) one step on, for a brave person (mut 2 or more) from `ZIEL_MUTIG` (4). A reached goal stands in the chronicle only as "Oskar has reached a goal."; what it was goes only as data into the next narration (`nsc_ziel_erreicht`), and a goal for today is followed by a new one. For the people of the scene the model gets the traits as words ("very brave", "somewhat stingy") and the open goals with their state; only the host sees both in the window "👥 People and chronicle" with progress bars (`dungeon_sicht`: `nsc_ziele` host and model only). **Live:** Status → Live counts per day steps towards goals and goals reached. Self-test `test_npc_traits_and_goals_move_between_turns` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC memory, chronicle and reputation (V01.1.060, tabletop point 5 with stage 5c, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", ideas from loreweaver and NarrativeEngine-P, MIT, own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → NPC memory and chronicle* (`chat.dungeon_nsc`, off from the start; `dungeon_nsc.py`, `dungeon_tabellen.py`). Every person of the hidden truth gets a card the panel rolls from the world's fixed tables: looks, manner, occupation, wish and secret. It also keeps its attitude to each hero (−3 hostile to +3 devoted) and up to `KANTEN_MAX` (4) relations: owes, fears, likes, knows about, each to a hero or another person. The model only reports changes (`nsc`, at most `AENDERUNGEN_MAX` = 3 per narration); the panel takes an attitude only when person and hero stand by name in the narration, one step per hero and turn, and a relation only to a hero or a known person. A new attitude stands in the feed ("🤝 Oskar is now friendly towards Arvid."), relations only the host sees. **Only the scene to the model:** with the switch only the people of the scene go to the model with their card, relations and attitudes (`nsc_szene`: at the group's place on the map, travelling with it, or named in the last `SZENE_ZUEGE` (2) turns), all others only by name (`nsc_fern`); the truth's list of people is left out then. The model also gets `NAMEN_VORSCHLAG` (3) free names from the syllables of the world or a people (`freie_namen`), so new people are not always called the same. **Chronicle and reputation (stage 5c):** by fixed rules the panel writes into the chronicle what mattered: meetings, fights won, heroes fallen, new levels, threads closed, acts, a chapter's end, an attitude at ±3; up to `CHRONIK_MAX` (40) lines, the last `CHRONIK_FAKTEN` (6) as data for the model. A hero's reputation is the mean attitude of all who know them (notorious, distrusted, unknown, respected, celebrated). People, chronicle and reputation go on into the next chapter. At the table "👥 People and chronicle" next to the hero sheet opens a window with the people met (looks, manner, occupation, attitude to each hero), the reputation and the chronicle per chapter; the host also sees wish, secret and relations of each person and whom the group has not met yet (`nsc_geheim`, `dungeon_sicht`: host and model only). **Live:** Status → Live counts people with a card, lines of the chronicle and per day attitudes and relations changed. Self-test `test_npc_memory_cards_attitudes_and_chronicle` in `test_dungeon`, the secrets in the leak self-test `test_every_secret_stays_with_its_viewers`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the same face for NPCs (V01.1.068, tabletop point 15, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", plan https://claude.ai/artifact/4QwoVfd2irhoQEeLmj5ngK with the recommendations 1a to 5a, idea from Project_Infinity, MIT, own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Faces for NPCs* (`chat.dungeon_gesicht`, off from the start; `dungeon_gesicht.py`); it works only together with "NPC memory and chronicle" and the host's "Painting pictures". Every person with a card the table has met gets a portrait once. The minute loop (`dungeon_dingbild.due`) paints it in the background like a hero's portrait: it counts to the game's daily picture limit, the voice comes first. The prompt is made only of fixed parts: the world's style (the picture book in the youngest tone), age and gender from the person's fixed voice traits (`dungeon_stimmen`, the same as for reading aloud), looks, manner and occupation from the card (the tables' English column), light, angle and mood from the fixed lists; never the model's text and never the person's name, checked like every picture wish (`dungeon.picture_text`). A person who has not spoken yet waits `WARTE_ZUEGE` (2) turns so their voice is settled. **Reference for the scene picture:** a later scene picture (`dungeon.queue_scene`) takes up to `VORLAGEN` (2) portraits along as reference pictures (`bilder.paint` with `refs`, the picture model takes at most two): first the people of the last narration (who speaks, then who is named), then a hero who acted in that turn and has a portrait; each with a fixed phrase ("the old woman from reference image 1 with the same face"). So a face stays the same from picture to picture; a scene picture with a reference takes a little longer on tars. **At the table:** "👥 People and chronicle" shows each person's face round next to their card, a tap makes it large; next to a person's line in the feed it stands small. The host taps "↻ Paint the face again" (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/gesicht` with `name`, host only, rate limit `dungeonbild`, daily limit); the old picture stays until the new one is there, and also stays when the new one does not come. The portrait goes on with the card into the next chapter (`rd["nsc"][name]["gesicht"]`); a picture no round and no campaign names any more goes with the hourly sweep. Loaded like an item's picture (`nsc-<hash>`), but only for a seat of a round that names it (`dungeon_gesicht.may_see`). **Apps:** iPhone and Android show the table as the panel's view, so the faces too. **Live:** Status → Live counts faces, those painted and not come per day and scenes with a reference. Logs only fixed words: `dungeon: gesicht | ok | 41000 ms`, `dungeon: gesicht | abgelehnt | tag`, `dungeon: gesicht | neu malen`, `dungeon: gesicht | aufgeraeumt | 2 bilder`. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_gesicht`.

**AI Dungeon Master: arms for group and factions (V01.1.079, D&D idea 10, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 reihenfolge bestimmst du wie du alles umsetzten kannst.", plan `plaene/dungeon-dnd-ideen.md`):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Arms for group and factions* (`chat.dungeon_wappen`, off by default; `dungeon_wappen.py`, `dungeonwappen.js`). The panel draws every coat of arms itself as SVG, without a picture model, always the same from a start value: shield shape (5), division (10, such as per pale, quarterly, per chevron, chief, bordure), two tinctures by the old rule (metal next to colour: gold or silver next to red, blue, green, purple or black) and a charge of 16 own drawings (star, sun, moon, cross, lozenge, ring, heart, sword, tower, crown, key, tree, mountain, lightning, chalice, fish); on a divided field the charge swaps the tinctures ("counterchanged"), otherwise it stands in a tincture that stands out. The group's arms hang on the campaign (otherwise on the round) and so stay the same from chapter to chapter; the host chooses colour and charge in the "Factions" window (with "Factions with clocks") through "Choose colour and charge" ("?" = chance), the rest stays. The choice is kept in `users/<id>/dungeon-wappen.json` (at most 50, the hourly sweep removes those of rounds and campaigns that are gone). Every faction (`dungeon_fraktionen`) carries its own arms from its name, never the group's colour together with its charge. **Security:** A code names an arms only with words of the fixed lists; `svg(code)` builds the picture and checks it (`check`, with `defusedxml`): only the elements and attributes of the list `ERLAUBT`, no scripts, no links, no `style`, references only to parts of the same picture. The page shows it only as a picture (`img` or `image` with a `data:` address), where nothing of an SVG ever runs. **Visibility:** Every seat gets in the view `wappen` the group's arms and those of the factions it already sees (their clock, their banner on the map); only the host also every faction (`fraktionen_geheim.alle[].wappen`, sight filter) and the choice. Other parts of the game take `gruppe(rd)`, `fraktion(rd, name)` and `svg(code)` (mails, chapter cover, chronicle) or in the page `dmWappen`, `dmWappenFr`, `dmWappenGruppe` and `dmWappenHref` (map). Endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wappen` (login, `browser_profile` with CSRF check, size limit `dungeon.BODY`, rate limit `dungeon`, only the host of a running round). Log lines `dungeon: wappen | gewaehlt | eigen/zufall`, `| abgelehnt | aus/wert`, `| aufgeraeumt | N wahlen`; Live overview: rounds with arms, chosen and refused today. **Licence:** Building an arms from shield, division, tinctures and charge follows the project Azgaar/Armoria (MIT licence, https://github.com/Azgaar/Armoria); code and drawings are our own, no parts of WappenWiki (CC BY-NC) and no foreign charges. Self-tests `test_dungeon_wappen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC tables, name syllables and oracle in the catalog (V01.1.060, Dominik 2026-10-25, tabletop points 5 and 8, data):** 15 fixed tables (`tabellen`) give randomness by fixed rules: for side characters looks, manner, wish, secret and occupation, for the oracle action, theme, place, descriptor, plot twist, complication, clue, peril, opportunity and settlement trouble. Each row can apply to single worlds (eye patch for pirates, implants for sci-fi), each table has at least 6 rows per world. Name syllables (`namensilben`, not `silben`: those are the map's place names) per people and for sci-fi and pirates build names from start, middle and end (method like Syllabore, own syllables). The oracle rows and many NPC rows are translated from Datasworn, only from tables under CC-BY-4.0, reworded to be family-friendly. Attribution: *This work is based on Ironsworn, Ironsworn: Delve and Ironsworn: Starforged (found at www.ironswornrpg.com), created by Shawn Tomkin, and licensed for our use under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International license.* Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: at least 6 rows per world, syllables for every people, attribution in file and docs.

**AI Dungeon Master: the host's random rules (V01.1.059, tabletop point 9, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", idea from AnyWorld, MIT, own code):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Random rules* (`chat.dungeon_zufall`, off from the start; `dungeon_zufall.py`). In the game master console under "🎲 Random rules" the host gives the round up to `ZUFALL_MAX` (5) rules of their own (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/zufall`, host only): a name, a chance in percent, a trigger (every told turn, a fight starts, a new place), an effect (damage or healing 1 to 6, gold won or lost 1 to 50, a hint for the narration) and whether one roll counts for all or one per hero. The round's word list checks name and hint. When the trigger comes the panel rolls a d100 (a hit at or below the chance), applies the effect by its own rules (damage and healing like every consequence, gold never below 0 and never above the most) and writes every roll into the feed. The model only hears the hint of a hit as data (`zufall`) and never the rules; only the host sees them (`dungeon_sicht`: `zufall` host only, `zufall_hinweis` model only). **Live:** Status → Live counts random rules and per day those that hit and missed. Self-test `test_the_hosts_random_rules` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: lines, veils and the X-card (V01.1.058, tabletop point 18, Dominik 2026-10-25 "ich hätte gerne alle punkte umgesetzt - bestimme selbst die reihenfolge.", plan `/mnt/project-files/plaene/dungeon-tabletop-ideen.md`; before the first game with an uncensored model):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Lines and X-card* (`chat.dungeon_grenzen`, `dungeon_grenzen.py`), on from the start as protection (`features.DEFAULT_ON`). **Lines and veils:** under "🛡 Lines" next to the hero sheet every seat sets what never appears in the game (lines) and what is only hinted at (veils), up to `GRENZEN_MAX` (8) short words each, separated by commas (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/grenzen`, guests `POST /api/dungeon/grenzen`). The table sees all the words together, never whose they are. A line counts like a word of the word list (`dungeon.bad_words`): the panel refuses an action with it, a narration, an NPC line or a name with it is thrown away and told again. A veil may stand only once in a narration; more often is the fault "schleier" (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`), the model tells it again with a fixed hint, on the last try the panel strikes the further sentences with the veil (`dungeon_grenzen.cut`). The model gets both lists only as data (`grenzen` with `nie` and `nur_andeuten`) plus the fixed rule `GRENZEN_REGEL`, never the words as an instruction; the hint when telling again does not name them either. **X-card:** "✖ X-card" says with one tap, without a reason and without a name: that was too much. The feed only says "Someone laid the X-card."; the reading of this turn stops on every device, the model tells the last scene again more calmly (`dungeon_grenzen.retell`, the old scene only as data), checked by the same word lists, else a fixed sentence fades out; its scene pictures and sound pieces go, and the next `X_ZUEGE` (3) narrations hear that they should lead away from it. At most one X-card a minute per round, not while a narration is being made. **Live:** Status → Live counts seats with lines or veils, retellings running and per day lines set, X-cards laid and narrations repaired for a veil. "How to play" has a page "Lines and X-card". Self-test `test_lines_veils_and_the_x_card` in `test_dungeon`. **View filter (point 3, idea from loreweaver, MIT, own code):** every secret of a round stands once in `dungeon_sicht.GEHEIM` with its viewers: the host, another seat (guest, TV and app show a seat's view), the car and the language model. The hidden truth, the story's plan and the things' features are seen only by the host and the model, a foe's life as a number too, another seat's open action only by itself and the model, a question outside the game only by its own seat, the table talk only by the seats and never the model, a seat's key and device by nobody. `sicht(doc, betrachter)` is the last step of every view of the table (`dungeon.view`). The self-test `test_every_secret_stays_with_its_viewers` plants a marker in each secret and looks for it in what each viewer gets (`dungeon_sicht.docs`): it must stand exactly where `GEHEIM` allows it. A new secret joins in the same commit. **Scribe check (point 1, idea from loreweaver, MIT, own code):** own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Scribe check* (`chat.dungeon_schreiber`, off from the start: one more model call each turn; `dungeon_schreiber.py`). After every told turn a small second call reads the narration (only as data) and proposes up to `VORSCHLAG_MAX` (6) changes the panel has not counted yet. The panel takes by fixed limits at most `NEHMEN_MAX` (4) a turn, once a turn: gold only when the number and a gold word stand in the narration, at most `GOLD_JE_STUFE` (10) times the level and not when the panel gave this hero gold already; a thing only from the ground, when it stands in the narration and fits into the bag; life never in a fight (there the dice count), at most `LEBEN_MAX` (3) and never down to 0; the place only without a map (else the map moves); a person only new and with the round's truth. The hero must stand by name in the narration. Every proposal writes a log line ("angenommen" or "verworfen" with its reason), every one taken a line "📜 Noted" in the feed. Status → Live counts those taken and dropped per day. Self-test `test_the_scribe_proposes_and_the_panel_takes_by_fixed_limits`.

**AI Dungeon Master: a settlement with life (V01.1.055, Dominik 2026-10-25 "so umsetzten" on the draft, 1a 2a 3a 4a 5b):** The "🏘 Settlement" window shows the place's picture on top (by day or at night, from the picture thread) with its name, the time of day and gold: one's own large, the group's only to look at, since everyone pays from their own gold. Every service is a card with its picture on top (its sign until it is painted), a line of life from fixed lists per world (never the model's), the calculation small above (for example "10 × level 2 × 4 heroes = 80 gold") and a button with a verb: stay the night, mend, listen, improve. What one's own gold does not cover is grey and says "N gold short"; a service that does not work right now (already slept, nothing broken) is pale. The lesson shows the six values as a 3 × 2 grid with before → after (for example STR +3 → +4), the price once below. The settlement's view (`dungeon_gold.view`) brings `tageszeit`, `gold_gruppe`, `stufe`, `helden` and per service `je` (gold per level) for it. Self-tests `test_gold_with_a_purpose_in_a_settlement` and `test_hero_sheet_merchant_and_item_windows` (window at three widths, grey without gold). Without "Gold with a purpose" everything stays off.

**AI Dungeon Master: economy part 3 (V01.1.054, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12" on the review of the economy, points 6, 7, 9, 11 and 12):** **Economy on the bench:** the balance bench (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Balance bench) runs 200 runs of a group of four per level with the real finds, prices and services (`dungeon_pruefstand.economy`): fights up to the next level from the budget, a chest every three fights, a reward, a boss in every second run, a merchant every four fights with its purse. It shows a hero's income (median and worst tenth), spending (inn, healing potions, rumours, smith), the ratio and the dearest single find. The aim is a ratio of 1.2 to 2 (`WIRTSCHAFT`); when a level lies outside, the log writes "stufen wirtschaft ausserhalb". **Gold keeps its worth:** the inn costs 10 gold times the level per hero (before fixed).
