In a settlement the smith improves a weapon or armour without a fixed rarity by one step up to "epic" (`dungeon_gold.better`), never a legend; the price is the full worth of the piece afterwards (`better_price`), from "rare" on it gets an effect. **Oils:** a flask with `oel` (a damage kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning) goes from the bag with "On the weapon" onto the weapon in the main hand: the next 3 hits (`OEL_TREFFER`) do +1d6 of that kind, then the blade is clean again; without a weapon nothing is used up, an oil is never drunk. The item shows "Oil: … N hits left". **New effects:** thundering (thunder), corroding (acid) and withering (necrotic) burn like fire, each with its own kind. Self-tests `test_the_economy_keeps_its_aim`, `test_gold_with_a_purpose_in_a_settlement`, `test_an_oil_on_the_weapon_for_three_hits`, `test_weapon_effects_for_thunder_acid_and_withering`, `test_every_common_kind_of_attack_has_its_protection` (in every world each kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning that at least five foes attack with has a protection thing there).

**AI Dungeon Master: oils in the SRD catalogue (V01.1.057, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12", point 9):** five flasks with `oel` in `dungeon_srd.json`: thunder oil (thunder), lightning oil (lightning), fire oil (fire), frost oil (cold) and blessed oil (radiant), 25 gold each, common and in every world. So in every world a hero has a weapon of that kind or an oil against a weakness to thunder, lightning, fire or cold. Self-tests `test_schutz_nur_aus_der_liste` (an oil is a potion with a kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning) and `test_jede_welt_trifft_donner_blitz_feuer_kaelte`.

**AI Dungeon Master: economy part 2 (V01.1.053, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12" on the review of the economy, points 3 and 5):** **Coins:** a foe gives the group 5 to 15 gold times the level (before 2 to 10), so coins become the main income. **Merchants with a kind:** every merchant is a smith (weapons, armour), an alchemist (potions, spells) or a trader (jewellery, tools, scrolls, treasures) (`HAENDLER`) and sells only its kind. It pays half the worth for its kind, a quarter for anything else and for unknown things (`sell_price`). Its purse holds 50 gold times the group's level per visit (`KASSE`); what heroes buy from it fills it again; when it is empty it refuses with its own sentence. The merchant's window names its kind, what it takes for half the worth and the purse. **Find table:** the rarity of a find comes from a fixed table per level (`fundtabelle` in the rules, `find_row`, `NIE` = never) instead of thresholds plus twice the level. From a foe no rarity from "uncommon" to "epic" gets rarer with the level (before, "rare" fell from 19 to 12 per cent when "epic" came in); legendary things come from level 5 on only from bosses, at most 10 in 100 (before 27), no longer from chests and rewards. Under Rules each rarity shows from which level it occurs. The balance bench equips its groups by a fixed assumption per level (`GEAR_SELTEN`), so a new find table does not move the aim by itself. Self-tests `test_the_find_table_keeps_its_promises`, `test_a_merchant_of_a_kind_with_a_purse`.

**AI Dungeon Master: economy part 1 (V01.1.052, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12" on the review of the economy, points 1, 2 and 4):** **One price rule** (`dungeon.price`): the art's base price (or the thing's own) times the rarity's factor; a thing with a fixed rarity and its own price already has its final price and is not multiplied again (before, the vorpal sword cost 125 000). Every price lies within the band of its rarity (`PREISBAND`: common up to 50, uncommon up to 250, rare up to 1000, epic up to 4000); legendary things have no price and cannot be sold. What cost more without a fixed rarity (plate armour, breastplate, dragon armour, elven bow, greatsword and others) now costs 50. Since good armour is affordable earlier with it, the "medium" encounters at level 1 and 2 are a little bigger (`kampf.BUDGET` 95 and 150); the balance bench still holds 70 to 90 per cent. **Treasures by level:** a treasure has a class instead of a price (`klasse`, `SCHATZ`: small 10, medium 25, large 60 gold) times the group's level when found (`it["st"]`); large treasures only from chests and bosses (`SCHATZ_GROSS`). **Potions and scrolls by strength:** a healing potion costs 2 gold per life healed (`HEIL_GOLD`), a spell scroll 25 × (grade + 1) (`ROLLE_GOLD`); a hero starts with 25 gold. Under Rules → Items a fixed rarity shows its final price or "not for sale". Self-test `test_every_price_lies_in_its_band`.

**AI Dungeon Master: prices, treasures, weapons and protection in the SRD catalog (V01.1.052, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12", data):** Every own price lies within the band of its rarity (common up to 50, uncommon up to 250, rare up to 1000, epic up to 4000). Legendary things like the vorpal sword or dwarven plate have no price any more and cannot be sold. Gear without a fixed rarity costs at most 50 (musket, laser weapons, combat suit, hacking module), so it gets no bonus. Each of the 38 treasures has a class instead of a price (`klasse`: small, medium, large); the panel computes its value from the class and the group's level. 24 SRD spells carry their damage type (fire bolt fire, thunderwave thunder, vampiric touch necrotic). 12 new weapons close the gaps: thunder hammer and storm spear (fantasy), bell mace (horror), shock baton, arc thrower and sonic cannon (sci-fi), blunderbuss, eel harpoon and storm sabre (pirates), flame sword, frost dagger and pitch torch. 18 protection things (`widerstand`) halve one type, never more: 7 rings (fire, cold, lightning, poison, necrotic, psychic, acid), 6 potions for 10 turns, shield belt against force (sci-fi), amulet of the living light against necrotic (horror, pirates), salamander cloak, heat suit and coral helm; the old potion of resistance now really protects against fire, cold and poison. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: prices within the band, treasures with a class, protection only from the list of damage types.

**AI Dungeon Master: damage kinds (V01.1.049, Dominik 2026-10-25 "Müssen wir Gegenstände und Waffen etc. noch erweitern gegen was und Zaubersprüche, gegen was sie Stärke bzw. Schwäche haben?", "ja so umsetzten" = points 1, 2, 4, 6 and 8, plan `/mnt/project-files/plaene/dungeon-schadensarten/`):** **Spells with their own kind:** 31 damage spells have `schaden_art` (Fire bolt, Fireball, Burning hands … 🔥 fire, Ray of frost, Ice lance, Cone of cold ❄️ cold, Shocking grasp, Lightning bolt, Chain lightning ⚡ lightning, Thunderwave, Thunderous smite 💥 thunder, Sacred flame, Divine favour ☀️ radiant, Poison spray, Stinking cloud, Cloudkill 🐍 poison, Life drain, Vampiric touch 🌑 necrotic, Eldritch blast, Magic missile, Spiritual weapon ✨ force). Before, every attack spell hit as force, also against fire-immune foes. A spell without damage (Shield, Bless, Sleep …) no longer attacks, it stays a check. The item card shows the kind. **Magic against resistance:** a weapon with a bonus of +1 or more and every spell go through a resistance to slashing, piercing and bludgeoning (`kampf.against`, `KOERPER`); not through an immunity or other kinds. **Protection for heroes:** armour, jewellery and potions can have `widerstand` (`WIDERSTAND_ARTEN`). Worn or drunk it halves a foe's hit of that kind (`resists`, `consequences`), a potion for `SCHUTZ_ZUEGE` = 10 told turns (`h["schutz"]`). New in the catalog: Ring of frost ward, Ring of fire ward, Ring of grounding (lightning and thunder), Amulet of vitality (necrotic and poison), Potion of fire resistance, Potion of cold resistance, Antitoxin (poison and acid). The hero sheet shows 🛡 with the kinds, the feed "halved". **Found out first:** at the table a foe's card shows only what the group found out (`kampf.known`, `rd["kennt"]`): a hit with "double", "halved" or "no effect" reveals that kind, a passed Intelligence check in a fight (`KENNER`) everything of the foes in the fight; the rest shows as "❔ still unknown". The bestiary under Rules still shows everything. **Coverage:** the self-test `test_every_common_weakness_has_its_weapons` checks per world: against every weakness at least three foes there have, there are at least two weapons, spells or a weapon effect of that kind. **Time of day:** the narrator reports `szene.tageszeit` (morgen, tag, abend, nacht); the panel keeps it in `rd["tageszeit"]` and gives it back in the facts, the stage picks the day or night picture by it.  **Balance:** because spells now hit weak spots, a group wins more easily; the "medium" encounters are a little bigger at level 4, 5, 7 and 8 for it (`kampf.BUDGET` 380, 450, 950, 1000), the balance bench holds 70 to 90 per cent; its casters take only spells with a damage kind. **Nothing jumps** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "das auswählen angriff oder heilen lässt das bild springen hoch und runter", "die rechte seite springt immer wider hoch z.b. beim erzähler aussuchen"): on a big screen the hero sheet has a fixed height (at most 900 pixels), the filters under "What I can do" change only its content; the group and the right column keep their scroll place when the table repaints (`dmColKeep`). Self-test in `test_hero_sheet_merchant_and_item_windows`. Status → Live counts foes found out and hits halved by resistance. "How to play" explains it under "Damage kinds". Self-tests `test_damage_kinds_of_spells_weapons_and_heroes`, `test_every_common_weakness_has_its_weapons`, `test_the_time_of_day_by_the_narrator`.

**AI Dungeon Master: game mails in the Dungeon style (V01.1.048, Dominik 2026-10-25 "die E-Mails, die du verschickst, sind die auch so ein bisschen im Layout vom Dungeon Master gehalten?", "1-3 und 5 umsetzten", draft `/mnt/project-files/plaene/dungeon-mail/`):** The mails to guests with a guest account (`dungeon_gast.message`) have, next to the text, an HTML part like a page of the game: a frame, a heading with the world's sign, a book-like font, only styles inside the mail, no picture and nothing loaded from outside (no tracking pixel), every value escaped. **Colours per world** (`FARBEN`): fantasy parchment, horror dark red, sci-fi steel blue, pirates sea green, any other world as parchment. **Last sentence:** "Your turn" shows under "Last at the table" the narrator's last sentence (`teaser`: at most `VORSCHAU` = 160 characters, without links and addresses). The host switches it off at the table under "✉ Last sentence in the mail" (`rd["mail_vorschau"]`, on by default; the line shows only when a guest with an account sits at the table). **Play on:** the button is a one-time link `https://<panel>/#weiter=<t>` (`WEITER_S` = 12 hours, valid once, only its hash lies in `dungeon-gaeste.json`), never the seat's key. `/api/dungeon/weiter` gives the device a fresh key for exactly that seat instead (`play_on`, `p["hx"]`, at most `WEITER_KEYS` = 3), while the seat still belongs to the guest account; a new seat link of the host takes all of them back. The panel's address comes from the host's seat link (`rd["base"]`). Self-test `test_turn_mail_looks_like_the_game_with_a_one_time_link`. **Heroes tab** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "unter dem Reiter Helden … was hat er denn für Gegenstände, Gold und so weiter"): every hero has under "🎒 Equipment and bag", folded, what it wears (per place), its bag with where each thing was found, its gold and its spells; changes still only at the table. Self-test `test_the_heroes_tab_shows_what_a_hero_has`.

**AI Dungeon Master: joining without cheating (V01.1.047, Dominik 2026-10-25 "Held am Boden → neuen Charakter erstellen, beitreten, heilen, wieder raus", "ok" to 1+2+3+6+7, plan `/mnt/project-files/plaene/dungeon-beitritt-schutz.md`):** **Waiting at the edge:** whoever takes a seat during a fight sits at the table but waits at the edge (`p["wartet"]`): no action, no moves of their own with bag and potions, not in the fight's group, the turn does not wait for them. The feed says "… arrives and waits at the edge until the fight is over.", the group shows them with ⏳, the host can take the seat back. Once the fight is over (or the round ends), they come to the table (`dungeon.arrive`). **First turn:** in their first turn at the table (`p["neu_zug"]`, also after waiting) a hero gives nobody anything: no potion for a friend, no gift, no trade, no spell on the group; the model hears "just arrived", and a healing it tells for a friend does not count when only new heroes acted that turn. **Fleeing:** a fight's group is fixed at its start (`kampf["helden"]`). The fight's experience is shared by it; whoever leaves the table meanwhile has fled (a line in the feed, their share stays out, they lose the table's experience as before), whoever came later gets none. **One device, one hero:** a device that takes a seat gets the cookie `dmgeraet` (random, HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict); the seat keeps only its hash. A second seat of the same round on that device is refused, also next to the host's seat. **Live:** Status → Live counts "waiting at the edge" and per day joins during a fight, heroes who left a fight, help blocked in a first turn and refused second heroes. "How to play" explains it under "When it goes on". Self-tests in `test_dungeon` (Fight): `test_join_during_a_fight_waits_at_the_edge`, `test_the_first_turn_gives_nothing_to_others`, `test_leaving_a_fight_is_fleeing_and_the_share_stays_out`, `test_one_device_one_hero`. **Death saves as in D&D** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "was kann ich in diesem Fall im Singleplayer machen? aktuell geht nichts mehr", "1 und 2"): when a hero is down, from the next told turn the panel rolls a d20 for them each turn (`dungeon.save_roll`, `h["rettung"]` = successes, failures): `RETTUNG` (10) or more succeeds, a 1 counts as two failures, a 20 gets them up at once with 1 life, as do three successes; after three failures they fall as before (unconscious at the children's table). Every roll is a line with 🎲 in the feed, the hero sheet shows "saves ✓ … · … failures left". Older states count their turns down as failures. **Mercy:** if they are the only living hero at the table (`dungeon.alone`), they do not fall (`dungeon.spare`): a stranger brings them to safety, they wake with 1 life, a running fight ends without experience ("The fight is lost, but nobody dies."), they lose half their gold (rounded up) and one thing from the bag; the next narration hears of it (`GNADE_FAKT`). Foes attack only heroes who stand anyway. Status → Live counts death saves and rescues by mercy. Self-tests `test_rescue_rolls_get_a_hero_up`, `test_the_only_hero_is_spared`, plus `test_down_three_turns_then_dead_and_things_lie_here` with failed rolls.

**AI Dungeon Master: bestiary with 62 new foes (V01.1.039, bestiary points 3 and 9 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Ja, alles nach Empfehlung umsetzen"):** The catalog `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` has 62 more own foes, now 224 (142 from the SRD, 82 own): 19 for sci-fi (such as repair bot, cloaked hunter, station core AI), 10 for pirates (deck rat, sea witch, deep kraken), 8 for horror (dollmaker, plague doctor, the Pale Countess), 8 for fantasy (rune guardian, frost giantess, ancient fire dragon), 2 for sci-fi and pirates (star buccaneer, comet raider) and 15 for every world (highwayman, storm keeper, iron colossus, star whale). Their values follow the balance formula per level (`dungeon_balance`), texts in the tone of the children's table, damage kind, weakness, resistance and immunity from the fixed list, 17 bosses of levels 10 to 15. So every world has at least three foes on each level 1 to 12. A world no foe names for itself (the own world, a new one of the admin) meets the fantasy foes and the ones for every world (`dungeon_kampf.plays_in`, the same rule for the encounter and the list). In the tab "Regeln", part "Foes (bestiary)", each row shows the damage kind as a symbol (🗡️ slashing, 🏹 piercing, 🔨 bludgeoning, 🔥 fire, ❄️ cold, ⚡ lightning, 🧪 acid, 🐍 poison, 🌑 necrotic, ☀️ radiant, 🧠 psychic, 💥 thunder, ✨ force), then ▲ weakness (double damage), ▼ resistance (half damage) and ✕ immunity, the mark "👑 Boss" and "new" (a foe with `seit` in the last 30 versions). The detail adds what a boss does more (more life per hero, phase 2 at half life, two attacks, a better find), the EP per hero for 2 to 5 heroes and the worlds. Filters: world, level 0-4, 5-10, 11+, bosses, new. The new foes have no picture yet; "Alle Bilder malen" paints them. Self-test `test_dungeon_bestiarium`. **A failure stays a failure** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "wiso ging die türe auf?"): the narration model gets for every check what it was for and the result in words ("failed: it does not happen this turn"). If it still tells a failed check as done (a sentence names the check's thing with a word like "gives way", "bursts open", "off its hinges" and without "not", "holds"), it writes again. For this check and for foes not named by the card's name there is a third try; if all fail, the panel tells the truth in a fixed sentence after it ("But the check "break the door" failed …", "Before you: 2 × Dunkelmantel."). An encounter does not depend on a check's outcome: the panel offers fitting foes (budget by group and level), the model decides in the narration whether a fight comes and picks only from the offer. Self-tests `test_a_failed_check_stays_failed`, `test_foes_not_named_three_times_the_panel_names_them`. **Feed** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "gandalf undef ?"): when a hero went down or got up again, the feed showed "suffers: undefined"; now it says "is down" and "is back on their feet" again, and a potion at full life shows "already at full life" instead of "+0 life". **Portrait and hero sheet** (Dominik 2026-10-25 "bei gästen passiert nichts wenn man auf portait malen klickt"): the table's answer (such as a refused portrait with its reason) now also stands at the top of the hero sheet and at the merchant, which lie over the table; at "Life" the sheet shows "down".

**AI Dungeon Master: faster over the proxy (V01.1.036, Dominik 2026-10-25 "es ist alles langsamer geworden", "ok - alles umsetzten"):** A HAR recording over the Synology proxy showed only about 50 to 70 KB per second; the page itself computes as fast as before (a round's view 12 to 13 ms). Hence: **Reading aloud as MP3:** every piece read aloud is kept as MP3 next to the WAV (48 kbit/s, about a tenth; ffmpeg on the panel computer), the browser fetches the MP3 (`?mp3=1`, a guest `"mp3": true`), the iPhone app still the WAV; the next piece loads while the current one plays instead of only after it. **Reading counts as speech:** while a turn is read aloud, the scene picture, the music and all other background work wait (`vorrang`), even when the speech output runs on the model computer and the panel does not see its mark. **Small pictures:** lists (rules, bag, seats) fetch a small copy (96 pixels at most, made once and kept in `dungeon-bild-klein`, deleted with the picture); only the merchant and the detail window show the large picture. Every picture address carries the picture's version, so the browser keeps it until it is painted again (before, the rules page loaded all 391 pictures again on every visit). **Compressed:** the admin's catalog and rules come gzipped (from 4 KB); a round's view never (invite links and the players' text, protection against BREACH).

**AI Dungeon Master: legendary pieces (V01.1.035, item point 4 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Ja, alles umsetzen"):** The shipped file `app/panel/dungeon_legenden.json` holds 20 named pieces per world (fantasy, horror, sci-fi, pirates; the own world takes the fantasy ones): a wearable base item of the catalog, an own name and sentence in both languages and two effects from the list of effects (at most one with damage, only ones that fit the kind). The catalog's items of fixed rarity "legendary" (from the SRD, such as the Vorpal Sword) count as such pieces too. A find that comes out legendary becomes one of them by fixed rules of the panel, never by the model: only from a boss, as a reward or from a chest; a merchant never sells one (at most epic there); each only once a campaign (the round remembers the ones found, a new chapter takes over the list, and none comes that a hero at the table already carries); first one for the receiver's class; with none free the find is epic. A piece comes unknown like any legendary find; once known it shows name, sentence, both effects and "✦ One of a kind", and both effects count for checks, damage, armour class and the ▲/▼ comparison. A selftest checks the file against the rules (`dungeon_legenden.check`). Status → Live shows the number of pieces found that day.

**AI Dungeon Master: items part 2 (V01.1.034, item points 6, 7, 8 and 10 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Ja, alles umsetzen"):** **Tool quality:** a tool helps by its quality (`WERKZEUG`): common "simple" +1, uncommon "good" +2, from rare "masterwork" +3; the tool's line names quality and bonus, the roll carries `werkzeug_plus` and the check bar shows exactly that part (before a fixed +2). **Prices per kind:** potions, scrolls, spells and tools (`GUENSTIG`) cost half the rarity's factor, weapons, armour and jewellery the full one. **Jewellery:** common jewellery is a treasure without a value; magic jewellery (from uncommon) always raises a value by at least +1. **▲/▼:** in the bag, at the merchant and on the ground every wearable item shows "▲ better than yours", "▼ weaker than yours" or "= like yours" against what the hero wears there (the weakest of its places; weapon: mean damage and bonus, armour: protection, twice on the body, and bonus, jewellery: bonus, effects counted, unknown only the base), and "✗ not for your class". The panel computes the numbers (`dungeon.strength`, `compare`, view `vergleich`).

**AI Dungeon Master: items with fixed effects (V01.1.033, item points 3 and 5 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Ja, alles umsetzen"):** **Rarity bonus:** common +0, uncommon +1, rare +2, epic and legendary +3, never more (`BONUS_MAX`, `check_rules` refuses more). **Effects:** from "rare" on (`EFFEKT_AB`) a wearable item gets an effect from `effekte`, and each is a fixed rule of the panel (`dungeon_kampf.WIRKUNG`): `schaden` (weapons only) +1d6 damage of its kind on every hit, twice on a critical, through the foe's resistance, immunity and weakness like any damage (fire, cold, lightning, poison, radiant); `lebensraub` (weapons) the hero heals 1d4 on every hit; `glueck` a natural 1 on a check or attack is rolled again once ("🍀 Luck" at the roll); `wachsam` (armour and jewellery) armour class +1; otherwise a value bonus (+2 on one of the six values). Effects the panel could not compute ("once per scene invisible", "breathes under water" …) are gone; such an old item counts only by its base. An unknown item works only once identified. `check_rules` asks every own effect for a `wirkung` (with `art` for `schaden`) or a bonus and allows `nur` only from the wearable kinds. **Rarity per level:** the find roll (d100 + occasion + 2 per level, `FUND_STUFE`) counts for the rarest kind without the level bonus; so legendary is rarer than epic at every level (before, from level 5 it was more frequent). A self-test checks both.

**AI Dungeon Master: gold with a purpose (V01.1.032, idea 9 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alle umsetzen"):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Gold with a purpose* (`chat.dungeon_gold`, off by default; `dungeon_gold.py`). In a settlement (a place of the map of the kinds village, town, tavern, station, bazaar or harbour, `SIEDLUNG`, not inside a vault) or where a merchant stands, the hero card shows "🏘 Settlement" with four fixed offers of the panel, never of the model, and never in a fight. Prices grow with the group's level (`PREIS` per level): **Inn** (3 gold per level and hero, one pays for all): full life and all spell charges for every hero not unconscious or fallen, a hero down stands up; once a game evening per round. **Smith** (5 per level): a tool that broke on a natural 1 comes back into the bag; the last 5 are kept, only while the switch is on. **Lesson** (20 per level): +1 on one value until the round or chapter ends regularly, one at a time. **Rumour** (3 per level): the panel names the first unvisited place on the shortest way to the map's goal (else an unvisited neighbour) with its direction, once per place. The hero pays with an own move (`POST …/held` with `{was: "dienst", id, i, wert}`, for guests `/api/dungeon/held`). Security: no new endpoint, only fixed rules and prices of the panel; logs `dungeon: dienst | <offer> | N gold` and a fixed word per refusal; the live overview only counts the offers paid today.

**AI Dungeon Master: heroic courage (V01.1.031, idea 8 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alle umsetzen"):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Heroic courage* (`chat.dungeon_heldenmut`, off by default; `dungeon_mut.py`). Once a game evening (round and day) every hero may call on heroic courage with an action: tap "⭐ Heroic courage" before "Act", the action carries `mut: true`, the panel notes it only while a charge is free. If a check fails, the panel rolls it once more; if an attack hits without being critical, it becomes critical (double damage dice); if it misses, the panel rolls it once more. If the first roll succeeds, the courage stays for later; per action it works on one roll at most. The roll shows "⭐ Heroic courage". **From the table:** the other players' actions of the last three turns carry a 👍 (`POST …/daumen` with `{zug, aktion}`, for guests `/api/dungeon/daumen`); when at least half of the other players (rounded up) give an action a 👍, its hero gets a second charge for the evening (`LOB_MAX` = 1). Never for oneself, not alone at the table. The state lives in the round (`mut`, `mut_lob`); the next evening brings a new one. Security: no new endpoint (the existing thumbs endpoint with login, profile binding, CSRF, size and rate limit takes `aktion`), only fixed rules of the panel, never the model; logs `dungeon: heldenmut | bereit|keiner frei|genutzt|lob|geschenkt|lob nein|lob alt|aus`; the live overview only counts how often courage was used today.

**AI Dungeon Master: experience for more than fighting (V01.1.030, idea 7 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alle umsetzen"):** The panel gives every hero at the table fixed experience, never the model: for a **new place of the map** the group enters for the first time `ep.ort` (10), for an **act of the story done** (`dungeon.step_act`, whether the model reports the turning point or the panel moves on) `ep.akt` (100), and for a **fight ended in peace** (`kampf_ende` "frieden") the experience of the foes still standing then, as for defeated ones; that counts only when a check passed in the same turn (persuading, say), otherwise not. A solved riddle of the map gives half of `ep.ziel` (50) as before. The numbers are in the rules under `ep` and can be set (`ort` 0 to 200, `akt` 0 to 1000, checked by `check_rules`). The experience gathers like any other as "+X EP still at the table" and comes at the end of the round. Logs `dungeon: ep | ort | N`, `dungeon: ep | akt`; the fight line in the log counts `frieden`. No new endpoint. Self-test `Equipment.test_experience_for_more_than_fighting` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: game master dial (V01.1.028, idea 6 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alle umsetzen"):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Game master dial* (`chat.dungeon_regler`, off by default; `dungeon_kampf.turn_dial`). After every fight, once its last damage is with the heroes, the Spark sets the round's next encounters by fixed numbers: won with less than a quarter of the group's life lost 10 % harder, a hero down, a lost fight or a flight 15 % easier, else it stays; at most 25 % either way, at the children's table never above 0. The dial changes the experience a medium encounter may have (`dungeon_kampf.budget`), so the offer of foes and the limit of the encounter; the danger ("easy", "medium", "hard") is measured against the dialled value, and the players see only that. The host sees in the game master console the line *🎚 Game master dial* with the setting ("danger +10 %") and *hold*, which keeps it where it is. The setting belongs to the round; switch off means the measured strength without the dial. The model decides none of it. Live counts the day's steps (harder, easier); logs `dungeon: regler | hoch | N`, `runter | N`, `gleich | N`, `fest`, `festgehalten`, `frei`. API (host): `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` with `{regler_fest: true|false}` (sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit as before). Self-test `Fight.test_the_game_master_dial_by_fixed_numbers` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: balance bench (V01.1.027, idea 5 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alle umsetzen"):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Balance bench* (`chat.dungeon_pruefstand`, off by default; `dungeon_pruefstand.py`). The Spark plays fights by itself, with the real rules and dice of `dungeon_kampf` (attacks, the foes' turn, damage, states, flight) and the catalogue including own rules, without the language model and without a round: per level 1 to 10 and group size 1 to 5, 200 fights of danger "medium" each, once a night between 2 and 5 or at once with *Play it through now* (at most once a minute, never two runs at a time). The group is fixed: the classes in turn, the first people, made like a player's hero and raised to the level, the class's first weapon and the best body armour up to the level's price, both of the rarity of a medium find of that level. Each hero drinks its healing potion below a third of its life, otherwise attacks the foe with the least life (a caster with its attack spell of grade 0). The encounter has one or two kinds of foes from the whole bestiary, exactly of the danger asked. Under *Adjust rules* the part *Balance bench* shows the wins per level as bars with the aim of 70 to 90 % as a green band (red outside), plus life lost in won fights and turns, for 1 to 5 heroes. **Measured in:** the first run showed that a group of four almost always won at "medium", a lone hero only about every second fight. The experience of a medium encounter per hero and level (`dungeon_kampf.BUDGET`, levels 1 to 10) is now measured with the bench, and it grows with the group (`GRUPPE`: 0.7 for one hero, 0.33 more for each further one; measured anew with the combat audit V01.1.127, because no attack is lost any more when its foe already fell in the same turn, and with the follow-up check V01.1.130, because heroes drinking a potion or lying on the ground no longer attack with the action of the turn before). A self-test keeps every cell (level and group size) in the aim; whoever changes foes or weapons sees it there. In the real game abilities and healing spells help too, which the bench does not use. No fight of the bench counts into the day's numbers, no round is touched. The result is in `dungeon-pruefstand.json` in the state folder. Live: cells, below and above the aim, whether it is playing. Logs `dungeon: pruefstand | start | nacht` (or `knopf`), `fertig | N zellen | M unter dem ziel | S s`, `knopf | ok` (or `aus`, `zu oft`, `laeuft`). API (admin): `GET` and `POST /api/admin/dungeon/pruefstand` (sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit `dungeonpruef`). Self-tests in `app/tests/test_dungeon_pruefstand.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fight part 2 (V01.1.026, bestiary points 4 to 6 and item point 2, Dominik 2026-10-24 "alles nach empfehlung umsetzten"):** **Weapon properties:** every weapon carries a damage kind (`schaden_art`: slashing, piercing, bludgeoning, for wands force, radiant or necrotic) and properties (`eigenschaften`), each a fixed rule of the panel (`dungeon_kampf.EIGENSCHAFT_REGEL`): light is critical from a 19, finesse hits with the better of Strength and Dexterity, a throw (the action says "throw", fixed words `WURF_WORTE`) too, heavy attacks at a disadvantage below Strength 13, versatile rolls a die two sides bigger with the off hand free, reach keeps the foe it hit at a distance (its attack on that hero in the same turn at a disadvantage). The item shows kind and rules as a line of its own. **Damage kinds:** every hit has a kind (weapon, spell, fists = bludgeoning). A foe with `resistenz` takes half the damage, with `immun` none, with `schwaeche` double; the log shows the kind and "halved", "no effect" or "double!", the foe's window shows what it is weak against or protected from. **States from hits:** when a foe with `zustaende` (poisoned, grappled, frightened, burning …) hits, the hero rolls a save with Constitution (Wisdom against charmed and frightened) against 10 + a quarter of the foe's level. Failed, the state lasts its turns from the catalogue and works by fixed rules: disadvantage on own attacks or checks, advantage for the foes, no attack (paralysed, stunned …), burning or bleeding costs 1d4 life each turn. All states end with the fight. The seat shows them with ☠, the log every save, the model gets them in the facts. **Bosses:** a foe with `boss` has more life per hero (+50 % for each further one), a second phase from half life (+2 to hit, a line in the log), attacks twice a turn and leaves a better find (occasion "boss"). The SRD's foe data (damage kinds, resistances, bosses) is filled by the DnDAppFiles thread; `check_rules` checks all new fields. Self-test `Fight.test_weapon_properties_damage_kinds_states_and_bosses`.

**AI Dungeon Master: place in the story so far, characters from elsewhere, parts 3 and 4 (V01.1.024, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1-5 umsetzten" on "back at the old place after moving on, its characters there again"):** Parts 1, 2 and 5 are the map's (the way back only when an action names it; `dungeon_karte.leute`: who is here, who elsewhere and at which place, who travels along; the note "place not changed"). **Story so far with places:** every narration carries its place when it goes into the log (`where`: `ort`, `ort_name`; a vault's rooms count as the place above it). After a change the model gets under `bisher` only the new place's narrations and the last one as a bridge, each with "[place]" in front (`told_here`); turns from before without a place stay as they were. The facts carry `personen` from `dungeon_karte.leute`, and a fixed rule in the task says: nobody is at another place than given there, whoever stands under "anderswo" does not speak here unless travelling along (`ORTE_REGEL`). **Check:** if a character speaks who is elsewhere by the map and does not travel along, the model writes the narration once more (`dungeon_erzaehler.elsewhere`, hint "anderswo", log line `dungeon: nachbessern | anderswo`); when the group is just walking to that place, it is allowed. Self-test `BetterStories.test_a_narration_keeps_its_place_and_others_stay_elsewhere`.

**AI Dungeon Master: story planner (V01.0.955, Dominik 2026-10-23 "Können wir auch für den Admin so einen Geschichtengenerator machen", "natürlich mit LLM-Unterstützung", "Alles wie empfohlen, nur beim dritten Punkt nehmen wir B. Das Modell soll entscheiden, wann es weitergeht."):** under *Features → AI Dungeon Master*, button "Adjust rules", section **Stories**, the admin keeps up to 30 stories: title, world, tone, idea, the heroes' goal, foe, up to 6 places, up to 10 taboos and 3 to 5 acts with a title, content, a turning point at the end and planned turns (2 to 30). **Create a proposal** lets the own language model draft the whole story from a few keywords (at most 300 characters), **↻ Write again** a single act with a free hint; the keywords stand only as data in the block `<<< … >>>` (markers defused), the answer comes in a fixed JSON schema, and the panel checks it by the same rules as a typed story (`check_plan`: lengths, number of acts, turns, words of the tone, "nie" and the taboos); only "Check and save" keeps it (`dungeon-geschichten.json` in the state folder). Whoever starts a round picks the story as a card under *Dungeon Master → Rounds* (since V01.0.961 together with the shipped ones, see below); world and tone then come from the story, the round gets a copy, and a chapter of a campaign plays on in the same act. **Flow:** the model gets only goal, foe, places and the current act (data block) and reports with `akt_fertig` when the turning point is told; the panel takes that only after 3 turns of the act, always moves one act on, never back, and moves on by itself when an act runs one and a half times as long as planned (`step_act`). After the last act "The story is told." stands there. The taboos count in every narration of the round like the list "nie". **Spoilers:** the players only see "Act 2/4" and "Act 3 begins.", only the admin and the host see the plan (to unfold at the table). Status → Live shows stories, acts moved on today and proposals (numbers only); Logs only fixed words (`dungeon: vorschlag | geschichte | ok | 4100 ms`, `dungeon: akt | modell | 2`, `dungeon: geschichte gespeichert | 4 akte`). API (admin): `PUT|DELETE /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/{gid}` (`neu` for a new one), `POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/vorschlag` (`{welt, ton, akte, stichworte, tabus}`), `POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/akt` (`{plan, n, hinweis}`), own rate limit `dungeonplan` (10 a minute).

**AI Dungeon Master: ideas from a DM prompt (V01.0.956, Dominik 2026-10-23 "haben wir das in diesem stiel umgesetzt oder können wir ideen daraus ziehen?", "alles ja", Gitea dropped):** **Advantage and disadvantage:** the model may report `vorteil` or `nachteil` per check (the situation helps or hinders); the panel then rolls two d20 and takes the higher or the lower, the story shows both rolls ("Advantage 14/7"); any other value counts as a plain roll. **OOC line:** when an action is unclear or no move at all (a meta comment), the model does not narrate it but writes that character a short line in `ooc` (at most 160 characters, only names of the group, checked like the narration) that only this player sees as "(OOC: …)". **Length:** a narration has at most 900 characters, the opening of a round, a chapter or an act up to 1500 (`erz_max`). **Prompt:** the default prompt also asks for senses (smells, light, sounds, feelings) and stays with world, tone and what happened so far; the panel's fixed rules still stand behind it.

**AI Dungeon Master: table theme (V01.0.957, Dominik 2026-10-23 "mir gefällt dieses layout ganz gut … können wir das verbessern?", "ok" = recommendations 1a to 5):** the table has a look of its own (since V01.0.961 the whole game page, light or dark, see below): dark ground, a serif font of the system (nothing is loaded), the narration as a parchment card with an initial and "Turn n", actions as speech bubbles with the avatar in the seat's colour, rolls as a slim line, act lines in gold. On the left hero cards of all players with life bar, the six values (with the bonus for rolls) and protection as a grid, equipped items as small tags and the state (acted, thinking, away), the own one framed in gold; the head shows the title in small caps and the current place; under the narration "Waiting for: …", the input, the microphone and **Act**. On a phone story, group and own sheet come one below the other. **Colours per world** (`welten[].farben` with `grund`, `karte`, `pergament`, `tinte`, `gold`, only `#rrggbb`, checked by `check_rules`): fantasy parchment and gold, horror blood red, science fiction blue, pirates turquoise, own world violet; the admin changes them under "Adjust rules". **Full screen** (Dominik "can we pop it out into full screen?"): "⛶ Full screen" in the header, also for guests, shows only the table across the whole screen (the browser's full screen; where there is none, as in the iPhone browser, the table fills the window); the button, Esc or leaving the browser's full screen end it. **How to play** (Dominik "do we also have a guide for the players?"): "❔ How to play" opens a short sheet above the table for players and guests (what you write, when it goes on, how the panel rolls, life, loot and trading, listening and speaking, the hero code); the first time you take a seat on a device it opens once by itself (remembered only in the browser).

**AI Dungeon Master: six values as in D&D (V01.0.958, Dominik 2026-10-23 "die werte der helden können wir diese erweitern wie bei dungeon and dragons", "ok" = recommendations 2a and 3a):** instead of strength, dexterity and wits every hero has **Strength, Dexterity, Constitution, Intelligence, Wisdom and Charisma** (`werte`, exactly six, `konstitution` among them). Each value is a score as in D&D, the **bonus** for rolls follows from it: (score − 10) / 2 rounded down, so 8 gives −1, 10 gives 0, 16 gives +3 (`mod`). The panel rolls d20 plus that bonus plus equipment, abilities and blessings. **When creating** a hero the player lays out the fixed row 15, 14, 13, 12, 10 and 8 (`verteilung`) on the six values: drag numbers, or tap two to swap them, preset by the class's `reihe` (warrior strength first, mage intelligence, rogue dexterity, healer wisdom, ranger dexterity); the panel takes only exactly that row, otherwise the class's order counts. The **people** add +1 or +2 (human +1 everywhere, dwarf constitution +2 and wisdom +1, elf dexterity +2 and intelligence +1, halfling dexterity +2 and charisma +1, orc strength +2 and constitution +1, each 0 to 2). **Constitution** gives life: the class's start plus the bonus, per level `leben_plus` plus the bonus (at least 1); when a value point raises the bonus, life grows for every level. Every level brings a value point (+1 on a value, at most 20, `WERT_MAX`). **Older heroes** are carried over when read (`six`): values anew by the class's order plus the people, every point comes back to spend again, life fitting the level, items and blessings on wits count for intelligence; log "dungeon: held umgestellt | sechs werte". **The admin's own rules** from before keep working (`_alt`): "verstand" becomes "intelligenz", a class's old `werte` become its `reihe`. Abilities, effects and items are spread over the six values (battle cry on charisma, iron skin on constitution, the healer on wisdom). The hero card at the table and the own sheet show score and bonus. **Hero voices** (Dominik "Die einzelnen Spieler, können die auch sich eine Stimme generieren und dann quasi mit der Stimme antworten?", "ja, nach Empfehlung umsetzen" = 1a and 2a): on the own sheet every player, a guest too, picks a voice of the speech output under "Your hero's voice" (list from `/v1/voices`, fetched in the background at most every 10 minutes, `STIMMEN_S`); "▶ Sample" speaks a fixed sentence in that voice (made once per voice and kept, own rate limit `dungeonprobe`). When reading aloud is on, the voice speaks only what the hero says **in quotes** ("…", „…“, »…«, at most 200 characters, `says`), before the narration and in the same sound track of the turn, one after another rather than at once; a line with a word the tone, "nie" or the story's taboos refuse is not spoken. Only a voice from the list can be picked; without a voice only the narrator reads as before. **Voice from a description** (V01.0.959, Dominik "dann 1b gucken, ob das funktioniert und dann auch 1b umsetzen"): when Settings → Speech output "Also serve VoiceDesign" is on (a second engine on the model computer with the same memory again; `/v1/voices` reports `voicedesign`), a field "or describe it" stands under the voice choice, for example "deep, rough dwarf voice". Only letters, digits, spaces, comma, full stop and hyphen are allowed, 3 to 80 characters (`KLANG`), no word the tone, "nie" or taboos refuse; the description goes to the speech output as `task_type: VoiceDesign` with `instructions` and counts before the picked voice. The voice is made anew for every line, so it sounds alike, not always exactly the same. With VoiceDesign off the picked voice or the narrator speaks again.

**AI Dungeon Master: D&D look, ready-made stories, light or dark (V01.0.961, Dominik 2026-10-23 "diese seite sieht auch ziemlich trist aus das muss angepasst werden das es direkt nach d&d aussieht", "es soll auch ein auswahl menü geben mit 10 vorgefertigen geschichten je bereich", "könenn wir auch noch einen hell dunkel modus einbauen?"):** the whole *Dungeon Master* page (rounds, campaigns, new round, heroes) and a guest's invitation window now have the table's look in the colours of the world. **New round** in four steps: 1 World, 2 Story, 3 The round (title, opening, tone, time per turn, duration), 4 Your hero. World, tone, people and class are chosen as **cards with pictures** (`zeichen` in the rules, only a picture without letters, at most 8 characters, checked by `check_rules`; without one ◆); the people's card names the bonus (for example "DEX +2 · INT +1"), the class card its two main values and life. Tapping a world shows the page in its colours right away. **Ready-made stories:** 10 stories per world come with the Spark (`dungeon_geschichten.json`, German and English, 3 acts each with a turning point, goal, foe and places, different tones). They stand as cards with title, summary, tone and number of acts under step 2, together with the admin's stories ("by the admin") and "Tell freely". The panel checks every shipped story when reading it by the same rules as an admin's story (`check_plan`) and leaves out one the rules refuse right now (for example when the admin takes a world out). A chosen story sets its tone (the other tone cards are locked) and fills in its title as long as you do not type your own. A guest sees no stories. **Light or dark:** the button "☀ Light" or "☾ Dark" at the top of the page, at the table and in the guest's window switches between the dark vault and light parchment; both take the colours of the world. At first the game follows the panel's or the device's setting, the choice is kept only in this browser. **Heroes** stand as cards with a life bar, the six values with bonus, level, XP and gold. The word "leiche" in the list of the tone "from 8" now only matches the start of a word, so "schleichen" or "Federleicht" are no longer a hit.

**AI Dungeon Master: equipment, carrying and merchants (V01.0.962, Dominik 2026-10-23 "ja so umsetzten" = recommendations 1a to 6a of stage 4):** **Nine places as in D&D** (`SLOTS`): head, neck, body, main hand, off hand, hands, feet and two rings. On the own sheet they stand as a figure of three by three fields; an item from the bag goes onto its place with **Equip** or by dragging, and back into the bag with × or by dragging. Every catalog item has its place (`platz`, otherwise the first of its kind: weapons in the main hand, armour on the body, jewellery on a ring); shield and tower shield go into the off hand, helmet and leather cap on the head, gauntlets on the hands, iron boots on the feet. A **two-handed weapon** (`zweihand`, for example greatsword, bow, staff) takes both hands: whoever equips it puts the shield away, and whoever takes a shield puts it away. Protection adds up everything worn. **Weight and carrying:** every item weighs something (`kg`, otherwise the value of its kind). A hero carries strength times 3.75 kg (`TRAG_KG`, like the 15 pounds in D&D); from three quarters of that the hero is **burdened** (`BELASTET`) and rolls dexterity checks with disadvantage, advantage cancels it (the roll shows "burdened"). The panel takes nothing beyond the carry limit or more than 12 places in the bag: loot without room **stays on the ground** ("Lying here", at most 12, `BODEN_MAX`), anyone in the group can **pick it up**, and anyone can **put something down**. A new place in the narration leaves the ground behind. Potions, spells and scrolls of the same kind **stack** up to 3 on one place (`STAPEL`). Trading and giving check places and the carry limit after the swap. **Merchants:** when the group meets a merchant in a settlement, the model only reports its name and one sentence (`haendler`); the host can also call one with **⚖ Open market** and close it again. The panel always sets goods and prices: 6 to 10 goods (`WARE`), rarity as for loot by the group's level, price from `preise` per kind or the item's own `preis` times the rarity's factor (1, 3, 8, 20, 50). **Charisma** gives 5 % off per point of its bonus, at most 20 % (`RABATT`). Every good exists only once. **Selling** fetches half the worth, a quarter while an item is unknown; the merchant adds it to its goods. A new place closes the market. **Unknown magic:** items of the two rarest kinds (epic, legendary) arrive **unknown** ("Longsword (unknown)"): only their base counts, bonus and effect only once known. A **scroll of knowing** from the bag (`erkennt`, used up) names one, or the merchant for a fifth of its worth, at least 5 gold (`ERKENNEN`). **Older heroes** get their weapon, armour and jewellery onto the new places when read (`nine`); what has no place goes into the bag. The catalog now has 137 items. The model only hears that a merchant is there and who bought, sold or named what. Status → Live shows open markets and markets opened, purchases and sales today (numbers only); Logs only fixed words (`dungeon: markt | auf | 8 waren`, `dungeon: handel | kauf | selten`, `dungeon: handel | erkannt | rolle`). API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/held` with `was` `kaufen`, `verkaufen`, `erkennen` (`mit` for the scroll), `aufheben`, `fallen` and `platz` when equipping and unequipping; `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/markt` (host, `{auf}`). **Prologue** (Dominik "nach erstellung eines spiels fehlt der einleitungs text, stimmung und ausgangssituation"): every new round and every new chapter opens with a prologue by the Dungeon Master (head "Prologue"): the place with all senses, the mood and the starting situation, every character of the group is in it, at most one NPC speaks, at the end a question to the group. The model gets world, group, the host's opening, "The story so far" and the story's first act; it is checked like every narration, damage, loot and successes in it do not count. The first turn waits until it stands (at most two tries), then the turn time starts; with reading aloud on it is spoken. When the model does not answer, the host's opening or the first act's content stands there as the prologue. When a round hung while narrating after a restart of the panel, the next request frees it. Log `dungeon: prolog | ok | 4100 ms`. The scroll bars at the table now have the world's colours. **In the car** (V01.0.963, Dominik 2026-10-23 "Alles umsetzten"): in CarPlay the row "Play Dungeon Master" reads the newest narration aloud and takes the spoken action; short fixed questions about the own hero (life, bag, drink a healing potion, who is missing, again; at most 60 characters, today `dungeon_auto.FRAGEN`) are answered by the panel with fixed sentences, everything else is the action of the open turn. For that every narration has its own number (`k`). `POST /api/profile/dungeon/auto` (`{text, nach, lang}`, answer `{sagen, n, warten, aus}`), also opened for the iPhone app, own rate limit `dungeonauto` (60 a minute); Status → Live counts the steps in the car, log `dungeon: auto | leben`.

**AI Dungeon Master stage 3: pictures (V01.0.964, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "Stufe 3", Dominik 2026-10-23 "ok stufe 3 umsetzten" = recommendations 1a to 5a):** pictures come only when Features → Painting pictures is released for the host and the picture model is installed; otherwise the game runs without them as before. **Scene picture:** when the place changes (`szene.ort`, also in the prologue), the model gives the field `bild` in the same JSON: an English description of the scene, at most 300 characters. It is only data and is checked like a picture wish: the word list of "Painting pictures", the tone's words, the list "nie" and the story's taboos, no names of people the Spark knows. The panel puts the world's fixed style in front (`welten[].bildstil` in the rules, the admin may change it, only letters, digits and punctuation), in the 8+ tone always the picture-book style, and "no text, no letters, no watermark" after it. It is painted at 1024×576 through the queue "Heavy jobs" (one model at a time, voice first, maintenance pause "bild"); the picture service's check throws away NSFW above 0.5, without the check there is no picture. **Display:** the newest picture stands large above the narration, without a mark on it (Dominik 2026-10-23, V01.0.968; that it is AI-made is still written into the PNG file itself), in the log it shows small at its turn, while it is painted "The scene picture is being painted …" or the place in the queue shows. Guests get pictures through their seat code (`POST /api/dungeon/bild`), only of their own round. **Limits:** the host's "🖼 Paint the scene now" takes the last description (else place and mood); at most one picture every 3 turns, 12 per round, 40 a day for the whole game on the Spark (`grenzen.bilder_tag`, 0 to 500, counted in the rounds file, so also across a restart). The button is grey and names the reason. **Hero portrait:** a tick when creating or "🖼 Have a portrait painted" on the own sheet paints 512×512 from people, class and the sentence about the character (fixed portrait style), painted again once per level; the portrait shows on the hero cards, stays with the hero (profile or hero code) and goes with it. **Map and cover:** the side column shows the places visited as a chain (without the model, at most 20, carried into the next chapter); when a chapter ends its last scene picture becomes the chapter's cover (at most 20 per campaign), it shows in the campaign list and in the iPhone app under *My things → Dungeon Master*, together with the portraits of the own heroes. Stored in `dungeon-bild/` in the state folder: scene pictures go with the round, portraits with the hero, covers with the campaign, "Delete all game data" takes everything; once an hour the panel clears pictures nobody owns. Status → Live counts pictures painted, discarded and failed and those on their way; Logs: `dungeon: bild | ok | szene | 14000 ms`, `dungeon: bild | abgelehnt | abstand`, `dungeon: bild | ohne_pruefung | szene | 900 ms`, `dungeon: portraet | abgelehnt | stufe`. API: `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/bild/{b}`, `GET …/{rid}/portraet/{pid}`, `POST …/{rid}/bild` (host), `POST …/{rid}/portraet`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/held/{hid}/bild`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/bild/{n}`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/bild|portraet|portraet/malen`; rate limit `dungeonbild`.

**AI Dungeon Master stage 5a: your turn, game date and look back (V01.0.968, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "Stufe 5", Dominik 2026-10-23 "Alle vorgeschlagenen Punkte 1 bis 8 umsetzen"):** so that a round goes on over days, the game reaches out by itself. **Your turn:** when a new turn opens and a profile is not at the table (no request for 15 seconds), a note "It is your turn in "title" (turn n)." comes once per turn through the profile's own ways (Me → Notifications, quiet hours apply). **Reminder:** when the turn is still waiting for you, ""title" is still waiting for your turn n." comes once: after half the turn's time when others acted already (the turn closes soon), else after 6 hours. Whoever acted or sits at the table gets nothing; nothing during a pause or a narration. The texts are fixed, never a sentence of the game. At the table "🔔 Notes on/off" in the side column switches this for the own seat; guests without a profile and profiles the admin blocked get no notes. Once a minute the panel now also closes a turn whose time is over without anybody having the page open. When a turn closes after "Act", the Dungeon Master now starts on the panel's event loop (`dungeon._go`); before, that ended in an error 500. **Game date:** the host sets the next date in the side column under "Game date" (in the future, 60 days at most); 15 minutes before, every profile seat gets ""title" goes on at hh:mm." once. "Add to calendar" downloads a calendar file (.ics, two hours, reminder 15 minutes before), guests too through their seat code. **Look back:** when someone comes to the table after more than 12 hours (kept in the browser), "Last time" stands above the narration: the last two narrations, the latest loot and levels, from the round's log, without the model; "Play on" closes it. Status → Live counts game dates and the notes sent today; Logs: `dungeon: melden | dran`, `dungeon: melden | erinnert`, `dungeon: melden | termin`, `dungeon: melden | an`, `dungeon: termin | gesetzt`, `dungeon: termin | kalender`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/melden` `{an}`, `POST …/{rid}/termin` `{t}` (host, 0 removes it), `GET …/{rid}/termin.ics`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/termin`.

**AI Dungeon Master: better stories (V01.0.971, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "Bessere Geschichten – Bauplan", Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-6"):** Six parts make the narration denser and more varied, all without a switch, because they only concern the own language model. **World truth:** Before the prologue the model writes a hidden truth once per round (3 to 6 people with role, goal and way of speaking, up to 3 groups and 3 secrets); the panel checks it (fixed lengths, no hero names, no doubles, word list) and gives it to every turn as facts. New people from narrations are added (at most 12). Only the host sees it at the table under "Truth of this chapter". **Open threads:** With each narration the model reports new tasks, promises or riddles (at most 2) and finished ones (only open threads can be closed); at most 8 stand in the side column under "Open threads". **Running summary:** Every 5 turns the model sums up the round in at most 900 characters; after that each turn gets only the summary and the last two narrations instead of a long list. **Rewrite:** The panel checks every narration with fixed rules: too short (under 80 characters), a hero with an action is missing from the text, or it repeats the last narrations (more than 35 % equal word groups). Then it asks once more with a fixed hint; if that fails too, the first one stays. **Style and tone:** Each world has fixed style examples in the rules (`welten[].stil`, up to 3 pairs German/English, 20 to 400 characters) that go with the narration; the narration asks with more variety (temperature 0.85, repetition penalty), checks and summary stay sober. The world truth and the threads go on into the next chapter. **Thumbs:** Under each narration there are 👍 and 👎 per seat (guests too); the admin sees the numbers per world and tone under "Adjust rules", never who pressed what. Status → Live counts world truths, summaries, rewrites and thumbs today; logs: `dungeon: wahrheit | ok`, `dungeon: wahrheit | ausfall`, `dungeon: kurz | ok`, `dungeon: kurz | ausfall`, `dungeon: kurz | fehler`, `dungeon: nachbessern | namen`, `dungeon: nachbessern | kurz`, `dungeon: nachbessern | wiederholung`, `dungeon: daumen | hoch`, `dungeon: daumen | runter`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/daumen` `{zug, gut}`, for guests `POST /api/dungeon/daumen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: big windows for hero sheet, merchant and map (V01.0.971, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "5k", Dominik 2026-10-23 "ok so umsetzten" and "clicking on equipment and inventory items should open a window"):** The side column at the table now shows only a short card of the own hero (portrait, level, life, gold, bag, hints on a new level, trade offers and things on the ground) with the buttons "📜 Hero sheet", "⚖ Merchant" (only with an open market) and "🗺 Map". They open big windows in the table's look over the narration: the hero sheet shows values, abilities and voice on the left, the nine equipment places large and the bag as tiles with pictures on the right, drag and drop works as before, below them the ground and trading. The merchant shows the goods as big cards with the picture in the rarity's frame, values, price and "Buy", below them what the own bag can sell or have identified. The map opens the large map view. A tap on a thing in the bag, the equipment, at the merchant or on the ground opens its own window with a big picture, rarity, weight, description, values and the fitting buttons (equip, drink, cast, identify, sell, give, put down, unequip, buy, pick up). ✕, Esc or a tap beside the window closes it (Esc closes the item window first); on a phone it fills the screen. If the Dungeon Master narrates on while a window is open, "New turn …" with "To the table" shows at the top. The window always shows the panel's state; the panel checks every action as before, there are no new endpoints. Guests get the same windows. Self-test `Windows` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py` (browser at 1280, 390 and 320 px).

**AI Dungeon Master: a pleasant wait (V01.0.975, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "5j", Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-7 but many more fixed sentences than variants so it does not get monotonous too quickly"):** While the Dungeon Master narrates, the table shows at the bottom of the story where it stands: the steps reading, dice, narrating with one fixed sentence each (a rewrite has its own), the panel's rolls right away, before the narration comes, three lore cards of the world (peoples, places, rules, seafaring, spaceflight …) and a hint "While you wait". All texts are fixed (`app/panel/dungeon_warten.json`: 122 progress sentences per step and world, 160 lore cards, 30 hints), all fine from age 8 and checked against the word list of the youngest tone, never from the model; the panel picks them and does not show the same ones again soon in a round (a step's sentences after 10 at the earliest, cards after 90). A new narration appears sentence by sentence (without motion when the device asks to reduce motion). When someone else is writing their action, "✎ Name is typing …" shows above the field (memory only, 6 seconds). Below the field lies the "💬 Table talk": short lines only between the seats of the round (up to 200 characters, cleaned like actions, the last 40 stay); the Dungeon Master never reads it, it is in no facts and no task for the model (self-test). Status → Live counts rounds waiting for the narration and lines of table talk today; logs: `dungeon: tisch | gesagt`, `dungeon: tisch | abgelehnt`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/tisch` `{text}` (rate limit `dungeontisch`), `POST …/{rid}/tippt` (rate limit `dungeontippt`); for guests `POST /api/dungeon/tisch` and `POST /api/dungeon/tippt` via the seat code. Self-tests `Waiting` and `Windows.test_waiting_and_table_talk_at_the_table` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: deleting old rounds and narration by the map (V01.0.975, Dominik 2026-10-23 "old rounds should be deletable" and "the story should respect the map and only allow that"):** In the list "Your rounds" an ended round has a button "Delete" next to "Open" (host: the round with its read-aloud sound and pictures is gone) or "Hide" (player: only off the own list); the second tap ("Really?") confirms. A running round is still only ended by the host. If "Open" fails, the page scrolls to the message. Logs: `dungeon: runde | geloescht`, `dungeon: runde | ausgeblendet`. The panel now also checks the narration against the map: when it names a place of the map the group cannot be at (neither the place where it stands, nor a neighbour, nor the chosen way), the panel asks once more with the fixed hint to narrate only the place and the ways from the map (`dungeon: nachbessern | karte`); the scene's place is always the map's anyway.

**AI Dungeon Master: items and values explained, tools that work (V01.0.977, Dominik 2026-10-23 "item descriptions are missing, what do they do, what do they help with", "tapping values etc. should explain what it is and what it is for", tool card "+2 on a fitting check" and "1"):** Each of the 137 items in the catalog has a fixed description in German and English (`info` in `dungeon_regeln.json`, at most 160 characters, all fine for the youngest tone): what it is, what it does and what it helps with. It shows on every merchant card, in the bag and in an item's window. On the hero card at the table and in the hero sheet a tap on a value (STR, DEX, CON, INT, WIS, CHA), on protection, life, level, gold or a worn thing explains right below what it is and what it counts for; a second tap closes it. The six values explain themselves from `werte[].info`, life, level, protection and gold with fixed sentences. The tools (climbing rope, lockpick, compass, puzzle cube, lantern …, 33 with the SRD catalog) are a kind of their own, `werkzeug`, since V01.0.999 (Dominik 2026-10-24 "alles umsetzten", point 4): own sign 🧰, own picture text "a tool or a piece of gear" instead of the scroll's, own filter "Tools" on the rules page, price `preise.werkzeug`; `hilft` is allowed on `werkzeug` and `rolle`, so the scroll, the treasure map and the admin's older own tools stay valid. They have a fixed effect (`hilft`: values and words): when the action names the tool (its name or one of its words) and the check fits one of its values, the panel adds +2 (`dungeon.WERKZEUG`), one tool per check at most. On a rolled 1 the tool used breaks and leaves the bag; compass, spyglass, everburning torch and treasure map (`fest`) never break. The roll shows "🔧 Lockpick helps" or "🔧 Lockpick broke", the narrator learns both in the checks. Log: `dungeon: werkzeug | kaputt`. The admin's own items may have `info` and `hilft`, both are checked (`check_rules`).

**AI Dungeon Master: throwing your own die and the check bar (V01.0.977, Dominik 2026-10-23 "1 and 2 but with sparks on a 20 and an own sound for the dice result"):** When a check for your hero comes during the wait, a big d20 with "?" shows: a tap (or a shake, where the browser gives motion without asking, such as Android) rolls it for about a second, then it lands on the number the panel rolled before (`dungeon.roll`); the tap does not change the result. The other seats see "X is rolling …" without a number until then (the panel does not send it to them), after the throw (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wurf`, guest `POST /api/dungeon/wurf`, rate limit `dungeonwurf`) or after 12 seconds at the latest (`dungeon_warten.WURF_S`) it rolls for them too. Below every roll the check bar shows die, bonus and tool as a bar up to the red line of the difficulty, with "just made it!" or "just missed" one point away. A 20 throws sparks, a 1 wobbles; with own sounds for success, failure, the 20 and the 1, made in the browser like the other sounds and only when sounds are on. A die already thrown does not roll again in the narration. With "reduce motion" only the number shows. Live: "n rolls thrown today". Log: `dungeon: wurf | geworfen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: time to throw and a throw that bounces (V01.1.038, Dominik 2026-10-25 "it disappears so fast if you do not throw in time", "1a 2a 3 4a b c"):** If your own die is not thrown yet when the narration comes, it stays in the feed at its place, with a ring that runs down for 30 seconds. The narration after it is blurred until then ("Throw first, then it goes on"), afterwards it throws itself. Only your own seat sees its die waiting, the others read on. The panel rolled the number before, the tap only shows it (`rd["wurf_offen"]` with seat, check and turn, never the number in the view). The throw flies over the card, hits with its shadow, hops once more, rolls out and always lands on the panel's number, about 1.4 seconds with a click on each impact. Holding before letting go throws further (display only). On landing the die glows green when made, turns grey when missed, gold with sparks on a 20 and cracks on a 1; the phone buzzes briefly where the browser allows it. When an own die appears, a soft chime sounds once. With "reduce motion" the number shows at once. Everything is drawn in the browser, no library and no files.

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice at the table, fanfare, turning over and campfire (V01.1.080, D&D ideas 5, 1, 2 and 4, Dominik 2026-10-25 "1-12 you decide the order"):** **Real dice** (admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Real dice at the table*, `chat.dungeon_echtwuerfel`, off by default): the host chooses in the round which seats use their own dice (`rd["echt"]`). Before the panel rolls a turn's checks, `dungeon_echt.real_ask` asks these seats: the table shows in big letters "Throw a d20 for Dexterity against 14" (two d20 with advantage or disadvantage), below a number pad from 1 to 20, "↶ Back", "Take it" and 🎤 (the game's speech recognition, number words from eins to zwanzig and one to twenty). The others see "Mara is throwing at the table …". The panel takes only whole numbers from 1 to 20, only from the own seat, only for the turn asked, exactly as many as asked and only once (own rate limit `dungeonecht`). Then it goes on as always, only this seat's d20 is the number given (`Hand.d20`); luck and heroic courage are still rolled by the panel. The dice receipt says "real". If no number comes within 30 seconds (`WARTE_S`), the panel rolls itself. **Never in the car:** if a profile is playing from the car right now (`dungeon_auto.im_auto`), its seat is not asked; a guest has no car. Doors: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/echt` `{zug, zahlen}` and `POST /api/dungeon/echt` `{code, zug, zahlen}`. Live: asked, real throws, rolled by the panel after 30 s, refused (numbers only); log `dungeon: echt | frage | n plaetze`, `dungeon: echt | alle da …`, `dungeon: echt | rueckfall …`, `dungeon: echt | eingetragen`, `dungeon: echt | abgelehnt | …`. **Fanfare:** when the narrator reads the honours of the evening (log kind `ehrung`), a short, soft fanfare sounds once (WebAudio, no file). **Turning over:** when a natural 20 or 1 sets off an entry of the critical or fumble table (`krit_tabelle`, `patzer_tabelle` on the throw, a fixed name of the panel), the die card visibly turns over on landing and shows "Critical: …" or "Fumble: …"; with "reduce motion" without turning. **Campfire:** while the group rests (`rd["rast"]`), an own music mood "Campfire" per world plays (calm, 70 beats per minute, in the world's major key, with guitar, flute, soft hand drum and warm bass), and the fire crackles in the background. Only a fight comes first. The store now has eight moods per world; the storage defaults to 2400 MB. The table "Music at the table" in the admin window counts its columns from the moods: first the world, then one column per mood (since V01.1.094, Dominik 2026-10-26 "table shifted"; the browser test `test_dungeon_musik.Grid` checks the alignment).

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice in a fight too, short wait, difficulty as a word (V01.1.165, dungeon review 2026-10-27 T14 and package 12, Dominik "ok"):** Until now "Real dice at the table" asked only for the model's checks, never in a fight. Now in a fight the Spark asks every seat with own dice whose action attacks by the panel's fixed rules (`dungeon_kampf.attackers`, the same choice as the attack itself) "Throw a d20 for the attack on X" instead of its checks; X is the foe named, else the first still standing. The number goes into the attack roll, the attack says "real dice". With advantage or disadvantage the Spark rolls the second d20. A hero a prop of the fight takes rolls the prop's check. If a seat gives no number twice in a row, the turn waits for it only 10 seconds (`KURZ_S`, else 30) until it throws again; the count is kept in the round (`echt_aus`) and forgets a seat the host deselects. The check bar under every roll and the question now give the difficulty as a word after the SRD table ("target 15 (medium)": 5 very easy, 10 easy, 15 medium, 20 hard, 25 very hard, 30 nearly impossible, a number between takes the step below, `dmStufe`). Live: attacks asked and short waits (numbers only); log `dungeon: echt | frage | n plaetze | n angriff | n kurz`. Self-tests `Rules.test_an_attack_is_one_question_instead_of_the_checks`, `Rules.test_two_turns_without_a_number_wait_short` and `Table.test_an_attack_in_a_fight_takes_the_own_die`.

**AI Dungeon Master: sounds for just missed, the foe's announcement, alarm and torch (V01.1.098, D&D ideas round 2, points 1, 2 and 6, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 implement, I leave the order to you."):** Everything sounds in the browser (`dungeon-ton.js`, WebAudio, no files) and follows only fields the panel sets; the model names no sound. **Just missed:** when a throw in the dice receipt carries `knapp` (the check missed by 1 or 2 and the hero paid the price, `dungeon_knapp` then sets `ok`), the wait takes `knapp`, the fixed price id `preis` and the price's text `preis_t` (`dungeon_warten.step`, for other seats only once the die is open); the die card shows "Just made it, at a price: …" with a short shake (without it with "reduce motion"), with two slightly falling tones instead of the success tone (since V01.1.125). **Foe's announcement:** a log entry of kind `ansage` blows a deep warning horn. **Critical and fumble on an attack:** when a log entry `angriff` (or `wurf`) carries `krit_tabelle` or `patzer_tabelle`, a bright run or three falling tones sound. **Vault:** when `karte.alarm` (0 to 6, map thread) rises to 3, a bell strikes once, at 6 the storm bell. While `karte.fackel` is a number (rooms of light left, −1 burns always), the torch crackles and flickers softly in the background; at 0 it goes out audibly and a dark hiss with drops remains. Outside a vault (`fackel` missing) the place counts as before. None of it needs an own switch: the sounds belong to "Sounds" at the table, each device switches them itself, and they sound only once the features of the main and the map thread set the fields.

**AI Dungeon Master: bard's hero song (V01.1.106, D&D ideas round 2, point 8, Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 implement, I leave the order to you.", after the listening probe "1 deutsch"):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Bard's hero song* (`chat.dungeon_heldenlied`, off by default), plus music at the table ("Prepare music" and the music model). When a chapter ends (log kind `kapitel_ende` with `kapitel`, from the main thread), the own music model sings a song of 60 to 90 seconds (60 and 6 per hero) in German, in the sound of the world (`STIL_LIED`: lute in fantasy, cello in horror, guitar and pads in science fiction, accordion with pirates). The text comes only from fixed verses of `dungeon_lied.py`: "Hört, was im zweiten Kapitel geschah:", per hero one fixed deed line after a word list from the chapter's log (own hit or critical hit, spell, new level, loot, else "ging mutig stets voran"), one chorus per world and a closing line. A name is sung only if it is a plain name (letters, space, apostrophe, hyphen, at most 24) and the word list of Make music lets it through, else "ein Held"; brackets like `[chorus]` thus never get into the lyrics. The song is made on the heavy jobs' place after the voice (`dungeon_musik.render`, the same place as the pieces) and before the store's pieces, one try per chapter. At the table the new song plays once (then the mood's music again); under 🎵 in the music bar the round's songs can be heard again, the host deletes one with 🗑 ("Really?"). Only a seat of its round can hear a song (the same doors as the pieces, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{{rid}}/musik/{{id}}` and `POST /api/dungeon/musik`); delete: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{{rid}}/lied/{{id}}/weg`, only the host, rate limit `dungeonmusik`. Each round keeps at most 20 songs, at night the songs of rounds that are gone go. Storage `STATE/dungeon-lieder/` (MP3 with the heroes' names). Live: songs, sung today, did not come (numbers only); log `dungeon: lied | ok | kapitel N | s | ms`, `dungeon: lied | geloescht`, `dungeon: lied | loeschen abgelehnt | …`.

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice wired in (V01.1.091, D&D idea 5):** From this version on the real dice work in the game. The host chooses in the game master console under "At the table" at "🎲 Own dice" which seats throw their own d20 (a tap on a name switches it on or off, a 🚗 shows who is playing in the car right now and is therefore not asked). Only seats of the round with a hero, each once, only with the admin switch, only the host sees the choice (`dungeon_echt.choose`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` `{echt: [seat, …]}`, log `dungeon: echt | plaetze | n` or `… | abgelehnt`). When a turn closes, `run_turn` asks these seats after the checks and before the attacks (`dungeon_echt.real_ask`); `dungeon.roll` and `dungeon_kampf.attacks` take a seat's d20 from its numbers first. The throw and the attack then carry "real" in the dice receipt (W-n · real), and the die does not wait for a second tap at the table. Without a number after 30 seconds the panel rolls as always.

**AI Dungeon Master: own voices for narrator and characters, clear messages (V01.0.977, Dominik 2026-10-23 "narrator voices and the NPCs' … made by mood and character, the narrator's varying from story to story" and "1-4", plus "the 'besetzt' looks lost and says nothing"):** When the speech output can make voices from a description (Settings → Speech output "Also serve VoiceDesign"), the Dungeon Master reads with voices from fixed descriptions (`dungeon_stimmen.json`, `dungeon_stimmen.py`). Every new story gets a narrator voice of its world (six per world and four for all, such as "old sea dog, rough" for pirates), never one of the same host's last three rounds; a campaign keeps it from chapter to chapter. The host picks it under "🎙 Narrator's voice" from the list, rolls another with "🎲 New" or listens with "▶ Sample". The scene's mood colours the narrator: fixed key words (eerie, battle, tense, sad, cheerful, calm) add a fixed addition, such as quieter and slower when eerie. Every NPC speaks with its own voice: on its first appearance the model only names traits from fixed lists (age, gender, sound, nature), the panel builds the description from fixed English words and keeps it for the round and the campaign (40 at most); a missing or unfitting trait comes from the name, always the same. The traits are not in the log. Without VoiceDesign the fixed voices of the rules stay. Live: number of NPC voices. Log: `dungeon: erzaehler | neu` or `gewaehlt`. Every refusal of the panel now says its reason (`dungeon._http` sends the rule's fixed reason word, such as `held spielt`, `runden`, `tasche voll`), and the page shows a whole sentence with what to do (`DMGRUND` in `dungeon.js`) in a notice box with ⚠; "besetzt" when starting a round now reads "This hero is playing in another round right now. End it there or pick another hero." `test_dungeon.Refusals` checks every reason word has its sentence. When the second engine is not running (loading, stopped or failed; log `dungeon: stimmen | voicedesign <reason> | feste stimme`), the fixed voice stands in, for the narrator the one from the rules: reading aloud goes on, a sample adds "Voices from a description are not running right now" and is not kept as that voice; after 10 minutes the panel asks again (V01.0.982). When the speech output is only busy, it stays at "busy right now".

**AI Dungeon Master: right column and class choice tidied up (V01.0.983, Dominik 2026-10-24 "very muddled and long", "hard to take in"):** At the table the right column shows the own hero on top (the turn stands in the head since V01.1.004), below tabs for map, host, open threads and date with only one open at a time; the device remembers the last one. Under "Host" the frequent buttons come first, "End chapter" and "End round" under "Less often". The class cards only count what the class can do ("1 power · 20 spells"); below them the chosen class's starting powers and spells by grade stand as small chips. Class names no longer break in the middle of a word.
