**AI Dungeon Master: a prologue with a hold (V01.0.987, Dominik 2026-10-24 "the prologue is still too short, the players have no clue what to do", "1-5"):** The prologue now has a fixed shape: first place and mood, then a clear quest (who wants what from the group, why, what reward waits), then a first lead, at least 700 characters. If it is shorter, has no quest or fewer than two possibilities, the panel sends it back once; the second try counts as it is. Below the prologue each hero gets one sentence why exactly they are here (only names of the group), and **What you can do** with two to four buttons; a tap writes the possibility into the own field, own ideas go on as before. The **quest** stands in the side column under the turn and stays there until the narration reports a goal reached (then ticked); the model gets it with every turn. Without a story plan the panel picks a kind of quest from a fixed list as the frame (rescue, search, protection, mystery, escape); with a plan its goal counts, also when the model gives no quest. All of it is the model's text, checked like every narration (tone, "never", taboos). **In the same step** (Dominik "the picture should look like in the hero card", "dice and bonuses get lost in the running text … everywhere in the game"): a tap on a worn thing on a hero card of the group opens its big window with picture, rarity, description and values like in the hero sheet; for the own hero with "Unequip", for others only to look at. Dice (1d6+2, d20) stand out everywhere in the game as a violet box, bonuses green, penalties red: in narrations, rolls, values, items and "What I can do". **A calmer side column** (Dominik "the menu is not well arranged, it looks restless", "all"): the second "Map" button goes (the map is the first tab, large via ⤢), the four tabs map, host, threads and date are equally wide with the sign above the word. In the tab *Host* "▶ Go on now" stands wide on top with "waiting for …", below it four tiles of the same size with their state (pause, market, read aloud, scene picture), invite and the narrator's voice as one row each, ending the chapter or round behind "⋯ More".

**AI Dungeon Master: painted pictures instead of signs, the class large, a hero sheet with tabs (V01.0.988, Dominik 2026-10-24 "pictures, not icons. a tap on abilities etc. should open them and show picture and values", "all"):** Peoples, classes and every class's abilities get a painted picture of their own, one each in every world's style (since V01.0.999, points 4 and 8a of 2026-10-24: a people of the science fiction world looks like science fiction, not like fantasy; the fixed texts name no genre any more, the world's picture style says it, for the foes of the bestiary too), from a fixed text of the panel (the English name, for abilities their sentence) and painted like the item pictures (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures for gear and spells, at night or on the button, the same check and day limit). Until a picture is there a calm placeholder stands in its place (since V01.1.002 the sign on parchment, see below). When **making a hero** the people and class cards show the picture, bonuses in colour below, on the class a badge "1 power · 10 spells". The chosen class stands large: picture, sentence, main values, life per level and its powers as picture tiles by grade (starting powers, cantrips, grade 1); a tap on a tile explains it right below with picture, kind, sentence and values. Peoples and classes have a short sentence for it (`satz`, optional, checked by `check_rules`). The **hero sheet** shows the portrait large on the top left (or the colour), beside it life, experience, the six values as tiles with a coloured bonus, and protection, gold and bag; below four tabs: *Equipment* (the nine slots), *What I can do* (powers as picture tiles, coloured on top by kind: red attack, green healing, blue protection, gold other, with a filter and the charges per grade as dots), *Bag* (bag and trading) and *Spend points* (new value points, a new ability, voice, hero code). The last chosen tab stays remembered on the device; "New level: spend it in the hero sheet" opens *Spend points* directly. A tap on a power opens its window with picture, description, values and "✨ Cast" ("Use" for abilities), which writes the sentence into the own action; the panel checks it like every action. A power's kind comes from fixed words of the catalog (`dungeon.power_kind`), never from the model. No new endpoints, the pictures come through the known picture route. Self-tests `Heroes` in `app/tests/test_dungeon_dingbild.py` and `Windows` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`. **In the same step** (Dominik, screenshot "The mail did not go out right now"): if no mailbox is chosen for guests with e-mail, the page now says so ("No mailbox is chosen for guest mails yet …") instead of "try again shortly", and the log names a fixed reason for every guest mail that did not go: `dungeon: gastmail | code nicht gesendet | kein postfach` (or `kein tls`, `nicht erreichbar`, `anmeldung`, `empfaenger`, `abgelehnt`, `abbruch`, `ausgang fehlt`, `notaus`), never the address or the server's text. The mailbox choice now also lists mailboxes of known providers without an own outgoing server (iCloud, Gmail, GMX, WEB.DE: the known server counts); before, it stayed empty with those. Self-tests `GuestMail.test_a_failed_code_mail_says_why` and `test_a_known_provider_mailbox_can_be_picked`. **After "Act"** (Dominik "a click on act does nothing more", "1-6"): instead of an empty field the own action stands as a green card with ✓ ("Your action for turn 3", the text, "Waiting for Gandalf · at the latest in 1:55"), it pops in briefly and a phone vibrates once. "Change" brings the text back into the field; a new "Act" replaces the action while the turn is open. The own name never stands under "Waiting for" after acting. If the panel refuses an action, the text stays in the field and the reason stands right below it. Once everyone has acted, the narration shows the steps reading, dice, narrating as before. Self-test `Windows.test_acting_shows_the_own_action`. **A seat link fills the window** (Dominik "the game guest should see nothing of the assistant in the background and the icon at the bottom right"): a seat link opens the table over the full width and height of the browser, without the assistant behind it and without the assistant icon at the bottom right; Whoever is signed in with a profile gets back with "Close". A guest without sign-in (Dominik "this must be properly secured so nobody gets out somehow to see something else") gets no "Close", everything else of the page (sign-in, emergency access, search) is hidden and not reachable by keyboard either; the server lets a seat code reach only `/api/dungeon/*` of its own seat anyway. Self-test `Windows.test_a_seat_link_fills_the_browser`. **Managing fellow players** (Dominik "can we edit other players, or delete them etc., so it does not fill up, or give the player the code again if they lost it", built as recommended, 1 to 4 and 6): as the host every hero card of a fellow player has a "⋯" with **New link** (guests only: the old link stops at once, the hero stays, the new one shows once with its QR code), **Stop waiting** (the turn no longer waits for them until they act again; "Wait again" undoes it) and **Take off the table** after asking once more (a hero with a profile stays in the profile, with a guest account in the guest account, otherwise it is gone). A free seat whose link nobody used for 72 hours goes by itself. New endpoint `PUT /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/platz/{pid}` (host only, sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit), logs `dungeon: platz | link`, `dungeon: platz | ruhen`, `dungeon: platz weg | 1 frei abgelaufen`. Self-tests `Seats` and `Windows.test_acting_shows_the_own_action`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pictures in the right places (V01.1.002, point 7 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "implement everything"):** The newest scene picture stood twice so far, large above the log and small again inside it, and the large one stayed in place while reading and covered the start of the narration. Now it stands once, large, at its place in the log. It scrolls with the story and never lies over the text. Older scene pictures stay small with their place, and "The scene picture is being painted …" stands as a calm line at the end of the log. Every **action** in the log shows the hero's portrait when there is one, otherwise its initial as before. Where **no picture** is painted yet (peoples, classes, powers, the portrait in the hero sheet), a calm placeholder replaces the colour blob: the sign on parchment (for powers ⚔ attack, ✚ healing, 🛡 protection, ✦ other, otherwise the initial), the large ones with "No picture yet". No new endpoints. Self-test `Windows.test_pictures_once_in_the_story` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: reading aloud reliable and fast, every character with its own voice (V01.1.003, points 16 and 17 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "reading aloud does not work reliably. how can we improve and speed it up", "implement all proposals", "how are the voices made that are in quotes, e.g. they sound the same as the narrator's", "yes, implement it all like that"):** So far a whole turn was made as one file. You waited until all of it was done, and when one part failed everything stayed silent. It played by itself only on the host's device, where the browser often blocked the sound. Now the turn is made **piece by piece** (one or a few sentences, the first very short), each piece its own file. The devices play the pieces in order while the next ones are made, so the first sound comes after a second or two. Every piece gets a **second try**, then the speech output's own voice speaks. If that fails too, only that piece is left out and the rest goes on. Reading aloud runs on **every device** that has it switched on, guests and the iPhone app too: one tap on "🔊 Sound on" unlocks the page's sound (browsers allow sound only after a tap), after that it runs by itself. 🔈/🔇 switches reading aloud off or on for this device only, "⏹ Stop reading" stops it, "🔊 Listen again" reads the last turn once more. Whoever joins mid-turn hears nothing old. While it reads, **the clock waits**: reading time and turn time start only after the last piece, for at most 4 minutes ("being read aloud, the clock starts afterwards"). **Voices:** under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules the part "Voices" has three fixed, different voices, preset narrator "ryan", NPCs (men) "eric" and NPCs (women) "serena". If the speech output lacks one of them, its own voice speaks. What stands in quotes in the narration is now spoken **by the character, never by the narrator**: whoever is named right after it ("…", says Grimma) or last before it, an NPC in its voice (women and girls in the women's voice), a hero in its own, otherwise the NPC voice. If the same line also stands in the NPC field, it is read once. The model is told to put quoted speech with the name into the NPC field. With VoiceDesign the described voices stay as before. When a **hero** sits down at the table, it gets a voice of its own proposed: one of the speech output's that is neither a fixed voice nor another hero's at the table. It can be changed in the hero sheet. New module `dungeon_vorlesen.py`, pieces under `STATE/dungeon-ton/<round>-<turn>-<piece>.wav` (as before only the last three turns), the endpoint is now `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/ton/{n}/{k}` and for guests `POST /api/dungeon/ton` {code, zug, k} (sign-in or seat code, size and rate limit). One log line per piece with milliseconds: `dungeon: vorlesen | teil | ok` (or `nochmal`, `ersatz`, `fehler`) `| 812 ms`, at the end `dungeon: vorlesen | ok | 7 teile | 9400 ms` (or `teils`, `ausfall`, `pause`). The live overview shows "reading aloud running", how many turns were read aloud today, pieces with a stand-in voice and failures. Self-tests `ReadAloud`, `Voice` and `Windows.test_reading_aloud_piece_by_piece_on_this_device` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: look at foes and loot closely, more voices for side characters (V01.1.021, Dominik 2026-10-24 "click auf beute etc. sollte ein fenster aufgehen lassen um es genauer zu betrachten - auch gegener. ist alles zeimlich klein", "alles nach empfehlung umsetzten. 2b mit voice design für varianz"):** **Look closely:** a tap on a foe of the encounter card or the fight box opens a big window like for equipment: big picture, name, count, size, description, in a fight its life as a bar, plus level, life, armour class, attack, damage and XP as chips, the six scores with their bonus and under "Can cause" the conditions its hits can cause (from the bestiary, `dungeon_kampf.card`; the fight box gets the same values via `dungeon_kampf.view`). ‹ › or the arrow keys page through all foes of the same encounter or fight. A tap on a loot line opens the item: in the own bag with "Equip" and the bag's other buttons, on the ground with "Pick up", otherwise to look at (`dungeonfenster.js`, keys `g:`, `kg:`, `b:`). Browser test `Windows.test_a_foe_and_a_loot_line_open_a_big_window` at 1280 and 390 pixels. **Voices of side characters:** without VoiceDesign the fixed male and female voice stay (choice 2b). With VoiceDesign every side character already has its own description; now a speaking pace (slow, even, quick, halting) and four more timbres (hoarse, nasal, velvety, scratchy) join, so many characters sound different (`dungeon_stimmen.json` "npc", `MERKMALE`). Characters kept before the pace get it from their name. Self-test: 60 names give at least 50 different voices, every description at most 80 characters. **For the car:** `set_pause(rid, uid, an)` is drawn out of the pause endpoint so "pause the round" in the car uses the same rule.

**AI Dungeon Master: checks with their purpose, ways of speaking, values as chips (V01.1.019, Dominik 2026-10-24 "bei den Würfelproben sollte noch angezeigt werden, für was die Würfelprobe gerade gemacht wird", "1 umsetzten", "Feste Sprechweisen aus dem Text", "die werte anzeige ist nicht cool gelöst", "1"):** **Checks:** every check carries its purpose (`wofuer`, at most 60 characters): the model's few words, checked like its text (plain text, word filter), else the start of the player's own action. The check card in the story and in the dice view shows "Gandalf rolls Intelligence" on top, the purpose large, below it "14 +2 = 16 vs difficulty 13" and on the right "success" or "failed"; at the own throw the purpose stands large above the die. **Ways of speaking:** with VoiceDesign every character speaks a quote the way the text says: the words right after the quote ("…", flüstert er mit archaischer Stimme) or the clause with a colon before it (Kael zischt: "…") are matched against a fixed list of 20 ways of speaking (`dungeon_stimmen.json` "sprechweise": whispering, shouting, stammering, growling, hissing, laughing, sobbing, murmuring, trembling, angry, ancient, cold, soft, hurried, mocking, commanding, tired, singing, pleading, warning); at most two fixed English additions go onto the character's voice description (`stimmen.weise`). The model's text never reaches the speech output. Without VoiceDesign the fixed voices stay. Log `dungeon: sprechweise | 2` (quotes with a way of speaking in the turn). **Values as chips:** people and class cards when creating a hero show their values as small chips that never break: the people's bonus ("KON +2", "WEI +1"), the class's main values, "❤ life", "✦ powers" and "✨ spells" (instead of the cut-off badge on the picture); the names stand whole, without a hyphen, two cards a row on a phone. No new endpoints. Self-tests `Turns.test_every_check_says_what_it_is_for`, `Voices.test_a_quote_is_spoken_the_way_the_text_says` and the browser tests `Windows.test_waiting_and_table_talk_at_the_table`, `Windows.test_side_tabs_and_class_detail`. **Overview in the middle, full screen** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "nicht wirklich alles zentriert bei größerem bildschirm … ebenso fehlt hier bereits der fullscreen modus"): on wide screens the rounds overview and heroes stand in the middle, at most 1240 pixels wide, the world cards spread over the whole row, input fields stay readably narrow; "⛶ Full screen" is already in the overview's head (one source `dmVollKnopf`, also at the table). Browser test `Windows.test_overview_in_the_middle_and_full_screen` at 1920 and 390 pixels. **Only on the fitting body part** (Dominik "Ausrüstungsgegenstände müssen wir auch darauf achten, dass die an den entsprechenden Körperteilen nur angelegt werden können"): belt and sash stood at "neck" in the catalogue, so a hero wore a belt at the neck. Now there is a tenth place "belt" (`SLOTS`, `PLATZ`, `PLATZ_ART`); belt, sash and belt of giant strength sit there. Dragging an item to a place it does not belong, the panel refuses (`platz falsch`, log line `dungeon: platz | falsch`) and says every item belongs on its body part; while dragging, the fitting places light up and the others fade. What is worn wrongly in running rounds or stored heroes moves on reading to a free fitting place, else into the bag (`right_place`). Self-test `Equipment.test_only_on_the_fitting_body_part` also checks the catalogue: whatever is named -gürtel, -stiefel, -handschuhe, -helm, -amulett, -halskette or -schild sits at the fitting place.

**AI Dungeon Master: longer narrations at the moments, way of telling per round (V01.1.018, Dominik 2026-10-24 "können wir die erzählungen etwas ausweiten … für die stimmung währe es zum teil länger besser", "alles wie vorgeschlagen" = 1 and 2):** **Length by moment:** Before every narration the panel decides by fixed rules whether the turn is a moment (`moment_of`): "ort" when an action asks for a neighbouring place of the map (by its name or direction, `dungeon_karte.named_way`), "kampf" in the first turn of a fight or when a foe falls, "wurf" on a natural 20 or 1. Then the narration may be up to 1,600 characters long (`ERZ_MOMENT`, else 900, the opening of a round, chapter or act 1,500), and the model gets the fixed task to tell the moment in 3 to 4 paragraphs with every sense. **Way of telling per round:** brief, normal or rich (×0.7, ×1, ×1.6 on every limit, `WEISEN`), when creating under "The round" and for the host in the tab "Leiten" under "📜 Way of telling", from the next turn on. Endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` with `{weise}` (host, login, profile, CSRF, size and rate limit; an unknown word is refused). Longer narrations take a few seconds more at the model and when read aloud; the turn's clock waits for the reading. Log `dungeon: laenge | ort | 1600` (or `normal`), `dungeon: erzaehlweise | knapp`. Self-test `Fight.test_longer_at_the_moments_and_the_rounds_way_of_telling`. **The encounter matches the text** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "stimmt das auch so wie du das geschrieben hast und anzeigst?", the card showed dark elves, the text shadow wardens): the model must name every chosen foe by its name from `angebot`; if one is missing it rewrites the narration once with the note "gegner" (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`), log `dungeon: nachbessern | gegner`. The encounter card now wears the colours of the table theme (light and dark), picture, name, count and values are always visible; "Stufe ¼" is the challenge rating from the bestiary, the tooltip explains it. **Tap on the picture:** in the hero sheet a tap on a tile's picture (What I can do, Bag, Equipment) opens its window too; the picture used to carry the attribute of the "change things" button and swallowed the tap. Self-tests `Fight.test_the_narration_names_the_foes_of_the_card`, `Fight.test_a_picture_never_carries_a_buttons_attribute` and the browser test `Windows.test_hero_sheet_merchant_and_item_windows`. **NPC lines once and without names** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "beim vorlesen der npc sagst du die namen mit"): when a side character's line already stands in the text, even split over two quotes ("You do not understand", he stammers, "…"), the panel reads it only there in the character's voice and does not show it again as a block below (`dungeon_vorlesen.in_text`, `npc[].im_text`); a line not in the text the character speaks without its name in front. In the car every line also comes only once. Self-test `ReadAloud.test_an_npc_line_once_and_never_its_name`. **The voice fits "er" and "sie"** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "Wurde von einer Frauenstimme gesagt - er ist aber männlich"): when "…", warnt er leise follows a quote, the panel takes as speaker only someone named before whose gender does not contradict it, and speaks a quote without a named speaker in the voice that fits the pronoun (`dungeon_vorlesen.pronoun`, `stimmen.sex`). Self-test `ReadAloud.test_the_pronoun_after_a_quote_picks_the_voices_gender`. **Scene pictures on their own** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "male selbstständig Szenenbilder"): after a turn the panel paints the scene picture by itself when the place changes, a fight begins, a new act of the story starts or 5 turns passed without a picture (`auto_scene`, `BILD_AUTO`); with the model's picture text, else from place and mood. The limits of Painting pictures hold (one picture every 3 turns, 12 per round, the day's limit, none in an admin's round). The button "🖼 Paint the scene now" stays. Log `dungeon: bild | auto | ort | ok` (or with a reason such as `abstand`). Self-test `Pictures.test_the_panel_paints_on_its_own`. **A find's name fits the thing** (Dominik 2026-10-24, screenshots: a belt was called "Versiegelte Truhe" and hung at the neck, a short sword was called "Verlassenes Versteck"): the model could give a find its own name while the panel picked the thing from the catalogue; when the model named the container or the place, the thing carried that name. Now the panel checks the own name by fixed rules (`name_fits`): a proper name like "Glockenbrecher" for a sword stays; when a word for where a find lies is in it (chest, crate, cache, hiding place, tomb … in `_FUNDORT`), it stays only when it names the thing too ("Truhenbrecher-Dolch"); otherwise the thing is called as in the catalogue and the model's name stands with it as "Found in: Versiegelte Truhe" (`own_name`, field `fund`). Finds in running rounds, in the stored heroes and their loot lines are repaired the same way when read. "rare" is the middle one of the five rarities (common, uncommon, rare, epic, legendary), not an old name. Self-test `Equipment.test_a_find_keeps_its_name_only_when_it_names_the_thing`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the voice only by fixed traits, also when someone goes on talking (V01.1.085, Dominik 2026-10-25 "»Kein Fremder hier«, sagt er heiser … die stimme war weiblich obwohl er genannt ist"):** The gender of a quote's voice comes only from fixed traits: first the character's kept one (`npc_stimmen`), else the pronoun after the quote ("…", says he), else none; then the NPC voice speaks as a person (`offen`), never guessed from the name. A quote that only goes on (»Kein Fremder hier«, sagt er heiser. »Nur Schatten und Stille.«: no name after it, no pronoun, nobody named since the last quote) keeps the speaker and gender of the one before (`dungeon_vorlesen.rede`); before, this second quote spoke in the women's voice. Among several named before, the panel first takes the one whose kept gender fits the pronoun. Self-test `ReadAloud.test_he_speaks_with_a_mans_voice_also_when_he_goes_on`.

**AI Dungeon Master: reading aloud audible again, table at small widths, prompt without the JSON rule (V01.1.016, Dominik 2026-10-24 "Aktuell werden Texte nicht mehr gesprochen", Chrome console "violates … media-src 'self' blob:", "bei kleinerer auflösung sieht die seite schrecklich aus", "1 2"):** **Reading aloud:** The short silence with which "🔊 Sound on" unlocks the player came as a `data:` address. The panel's security rule (CSP `media-src 'self' blob:`) blocks that in every browser, the player stayed locked and no piece played, although the Spark spoke every one. Now the silence is made on the tap as a `blob:` (`dmStill`). Self-test `Fight.test_table_sound_only_from_sources_the_csp_allows` (no `data:audio` in the table's scripts). **Table at small widths:** The head wraps before the title gets narrower than 320 px; music, sound and buttons move into a second row and never lie over the title. The three columns follow the table's own width, not the window's (next to the menu a laptop leaves about 700 px): up to 1060 px two columns with the group as a row on top, up to 720 px one column. Browser test at 1280, 1000 and 390 px. **Prompt:** In the rules (Funktionen → KI-Dungeon-Master → Regeln anpassen) rule 4 "format as JSON" goes, the fixed schema rules that more precisely; new is rule 7 "Invent no values, items or foes outside the facts; the panel drops them." `dungeon.schutz` is gone: armour works through the armour class.

**AI Dungeon Master: scene picture in the prologue, "🔊 Sound on" (V01.1.013, Dominik 2026-10-24 "Male während des Prologes das Szenenbild gleich immer mit", "passiert aber nichts"):** Every prologue (new round, next chapter) paints its scene picture right away, within the limits of "Paint pictures" (`may_paint`): with the model's picture text, without one from the scene's place and mood (`scene_text`, the same source as "Paint scene picture now"). When the check refuses the model's picture text, the prologue paints nothing, not the place either. Log `dungeon: bild | prolog | ok` or with the reason (`aus`, `malt`, `leer`, `person` …). "🔊 Sound on" unlocks the sound with a real 0.1 s silence (Safari did not play the empty file, so the button stayed locked) and wakes the audio context; only a refusal of the browser (NotAllowedError) keeps it locked. Self-test `Pictures.test_every_opening_paints_its_scene`. **Quest done only at the end** (Dominik 2026-10-24 "Wieso ist ein Auftrag direkt nach dem zweiten Zug erledigt?"): A goal the model reports (`erfolg` "ziel") used to tick off the pinned quest at once, even when only a lead was found. Now a fixed rule holds (`quest_may_end`): with a story plan only in its last act or after it, without a plan after 8 turns at the earliest (`AUFTRAG_ZUEGE`). An earlier goal is a step on the way, its 100 XP count, the quest stays open and the model keeps getting it as a fact. A quest of a running round ticked off too early is open again after the next turn. Log `dungeon: auftrag | erfuellt` or `… | zu frueh`. Self-test `Fight.test_the_quest_is_not_done_after_one_goal_early_in_the_story`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fights by fixed rules (V01.1.013, review point 2 with bestiary points 1 and 2 and item point 9 of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Ja, alles nach Empfehlung umsetzen"):** **Encounter by budget:** The panel works out the experience budget of a medium D&D encounter from the level of every living hero (`dungeon_kampf.BUDGET`, half at the children's table) and, without a running fight, offers the model at most 5 foes of the round's world from the bestiary (fact `angebot`, drawn fixed per turn from the 12 nearest to a third of the budget). The model picks only from them (field `begegnung`: id and count); the panel takes only offered ids, at most 4 of a kind and 6 in all, and takes the strongest away until the experience with the D&D multiplier for the count is at most 1.5 times the budget. The card "⚔ Encounter" shows every foe with picture or sign, count and danger (easy, medium, hard); a tap shows text, level, life, armour class, attack, damage and experience. **The panel rolls the attacks:** When a hero attacks, the model gives the check the target (`ziel`, the foe's name). The panel rolls d20 + the bonus of the weapon's value (with equipment and blessings) + the proficiency bonus by level (`prof`: +2, from level 5 +3 …) against the foe's armour class; a 20 always hits and rolls the damage twice, a 1 never hits. The damage is the weapon's die + the value's bonus + the rarity's bonus, with an attack spell its die by grade, without a weapon 1d2. Then every foe still standing attacks: the hero who last hit it, else a standing one; d20 + attack from the bestiary against the hero's **armour class** (10 + twice the body armour + helmet, shield, gloves, boots + Dexterity bonus, at most +2 in medium armour, none in heavy; shown on the hero sheet and the card instead of protection), damage by the bestiary's dice. Only the panel changes the foes' life; damage the model reports in a fight does not count. A foe at a quarter of its life or less flees on a d20 of 9 or less, at the children's table it "gives up". The bar "⚔ Fight" above the story shows every foe with its life, defeated or fled. **End and experience:** When no foe stands, the check "offer peace" or the group's flight succeeds (both only by the panel's check, since V01.1.127 and V01.1.138), the fight ends. The experience comes from the bestiary (fled ones half), divided among the living heroes and rounded down (since V01.1.139); only a won fight brings a find. The model no longer reports a defeated foe or loot "foe" or "boss", only a goal reached (100 XP). Levels follow the D&D curve (level 5 from 6,500 XP). **Rooms of the map:** When the map puts `raum_gegner` (the room's tag and bestiary ids) into the round for a guardian or boss room, the panel offers only these foes, once each and without the budget; when the fight is won (no foe stands), `sieg` with the room's tag is in the round and the way is free. Self-tests `Fight` (seven cases). Live overview: running fights, standing foes, today encounters, fights, hits, misses, defeated, fled. Log: `dungeon: kampf | start | 2 gegner | mittel`, `dungeon: kampf | ende | sieg | 2 besiegt | 0 geflohen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: thing memory (V01.1.010, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1 a und b und c", a rusty iron box that one turn later was a rotten box):** In every narration the model reports at most 3 important things that come up for the first time, with their features (field `dinge`: name, merkmale with material, colour, state). The panel checks them by fixed rules (short, the round's word list, no hero's name, none twice, none against one already kept) and keeps at most 12 per round with place and turn (`dungeon_erzaehler.DINGE_MAX`). Every narration gets the kept things at the group's place or from the last 10 turns as facts with the fixed rule never to change material, colour or state. **Fixed contradiction check:** when a narration names a kept thing with a feature from a fixed word list (material: wood, iron, silver, gold, stone, bronze, copper, glass, leather, bone, crystal; colour) that matches no kept feature of that kind ("the rotten box", "wooden box" instead of "iron box"), the Dungeon Master writes it once more, with a fixed hint naming the thing and its features; as with missing names, the first one stays otherwise. **For the host:** under "Truth of this chapter" stands "Kept things · only you see this" with name, features and place; ✏️ changes a thing in place, 🗑 forgets it. Players never see the list. The kept things go on into the next chapter and count there at their place. A line out of the game (ooc) that speaks for the panel ("the panel confirms …") is dropped. Endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/dinge` (host, login, profile, CSRF, size and rate limit). Live overview: kept things, contradictions rewritten today. Log: `dungeon: dinge | neu | 2`, `… | geaendert`, `… | weg`, `… | wortliste`, `… | nicht da`, `dungeon: nachbessern | widerspruch`. Self-tests `ThingMemory` (six cases) and `Windows.test_kept_things_for_the_host`.

**AI Dungeon Master: life 0 has consequences, powers work by fixed rules, experience only at the end (V01.1.008, points 1 and 3 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alles umsetzten", "1" with permadeath, "nur ohnmächtig" at the children's table):** At 0 life a hero is **down**: it does not act, the turn does not wait for it, and bag, sheet and trading are locked (reason "boden"). A friend gives it one of their own healing potions with "🧪 Give a healing potion" on its card, a healing spell for the group gets it up, or the narration reports healing when someone helps it up in their action. After 3 told turns without healing (`BODEN_ZUEGE`) it has **fallen, for good**: everything it wore and carried and its gold lie under "Lying here" for the others, the experience of this table is gone. A fallen hero stays so in the profile's hero list, in the guest account and in a campaign; it sits at no table again (reason "tot"), gets no hero code and no more consequences. For a new hero the host takes the seat back and invites again. When everyone is down, the Dungeon Master narrates on once the turn time is up. **At the children's table** (the youngest tone) nobody dies: after the 3 turns the hero is only unconscious and wakes at the next table (the next chapter), with its things but without its gold. **Rest:** whoever sits down at a new table (new round, next chapter, seat) has full life and full charges again, only the living. The model gets every character's state ("down", "unconscious", "fallen") with the fixed rule that it does not act, and may never write "the panel confirms". **Powers by fixed rules:** abilities carry a `wirkung` (fixed words, checked by `check_rules`) and `immer` in `dungeon_regeln.json`. Iron skin (`weniger_schaden`) takes 1 off every hit, even below 1. Shield wall (`abfangen`), when the action names it with a word of using it, takes the first hit meant for a friend in that turn, once a scene (place); a second time at the same place a line for that seat only says "used already". Battle cry (`gruppe_probe`) gives the whole group +1 on the checks of that turn, the roll shows "Battle cry +1". Lasting abilities (Iron skin, Steadfast, Weapon master, Master thief and others) show "always works" in their window instead of "Use". **Experience at the end:** XP gather at the table during play ("+X XP still at the table" in the sheet, the hero list and the log) and are credited only at the regular end of the round (the host ends it, time is up, the chapter is closed), at most one level per game evening as before. Whoever leaves before (the host takes the seat back, a hero code mid-round) keeps the experience it came with; things and trades stay so nothing is doubled. A hero still sits at one table only. Live overview: heroes down, today down, back up, potions for friends, fallen, unconscious, powers worked. Log: `dungeon: zustand | boden | noch 3`, `… | auf`, `… | tot | 5 dinge`, `… | ohnmacht`, `dungeon: kraft | abfangen`, `dungeon: ep | ende | 2 helden`, `dungeon: beitreten | tot`. Self-tests `Permadeath` (six cases).

**AI Dungeon Master: every character in its clone voice with a Base model (V01.1.007, points 16.2 and 17.3 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "Beides, je nach Modell"):** when the speech output runs a Base model (Settings → Speech output, model "…-Base") and VoiceDesign is provided as well, a character's first sentence (narrator, NPC or hero with a described voice) is spoken with VoiceDesign as before and then kept as its clone reference if it is 3 to 15 seconds long. Every later sentence of that character is spoken by the Base model from the reference: the same sound in every turn and faster than VoiceDesign. If that fails twice VoiceDesign steps in, then the fixed voice. The references live in the state folder `dungeon-klone` (at most 200, the longest unused go first) and count for all rounds, so a character with the same description sounds the same in the next round too. With the preset CustomVoice model nothing changes. The Live overview counts the clone voices, the log writes `dungeon: vorlesen | klon | neu | 5.0 s`. Self-test `ReadAloud.test_a_character_speaks_as_its_clone_with_a_base_model`.

**AI Dungeon Master: a bar of its own on the phone (V01.1.006, point 10 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alles umsetzten"):** on the phone the game table shows one part at a time, a bar of its own at the bottom picks it: 📖 Story (narration, clock and field), 👥 Group (the other players), 📜 Hero (the own hero with the hero sheet, Threads and Date; the game master console since V01.1.078 under “⋯” in the head, the field to act in every pane) and 🗺 Map (only the map, once there is one). When the Dungeon Master tells on while another part is open, "Story" gets a red dot. The panel's own bar (Assistant, Today, Documents, Me, Manage) is hidden at the table and comes back with "← Rounds". On a wide screen everything stays in three columns as before. Self-test `Windows.test_phone_bar_one_pane_at_a_time`.

**AI Dungeon Master: a calm table without doubles (V01.1.004, point 9 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "alles umsetzten"):** the guide "How to play" is a window in the table's look with one page per part (map, writing, dice, life, merchants, reading aloud and more). "‹ Back", "Next ›", the dots and on a computer the arrow keys page through; Esc, ✕ or a tap beside it closes it. It opens by itself once on every device (since V01.1.078 first only as a small card "How it works" with "More"), then through "❔ How to play" in the head (on the phone under "⋯"). Music, sounds and the volume now sit in the table's head instead of a row of their own. Turn, clock and "have acted" stand only once: the turn in the head, clock and state above the field; the right column no longer repeats them. Once you have acted the clock stands in your own card, your own extra minute there as "+1 minute for you". The own hero stands in the group only as a short row (name, life, state), the large card with the hero sheet stays on the right. At the table the floating assistant face is hidden, on every other page it is back. The tab "Host" uses the table's font, an odd last tile fills its row. Self-test `Windows.test_table_calm_guide_pages_and_no_doubles`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the word list also for what players write, the host hides and mutes (V01.1.001, point 13 of the review of 2026-10-24, Dominik "implement everything"):** Until now a round's word list (its tone's words, the rules' list "never" and its story's taboos) only checked what the model tells. Now it holds just the same for everything players write: the table talk, every action, the new name when renaming and the name of a hero who sits down at the table (also a kept hero or a hero code from a rougher table). At a table "from 8" "There is blood!" does not go through. The page then says "That does not work at this table: a word is on the round's word list … Put it another way.", and the text stays in the field. The list "never" holds for every new hero. As the **host** every line of table talk and every action in the log has a small ⊘ "Hide for everyone": after that no seat sees it any more, but it stays in the round's file. A fellow player's "⋯" has the new **Mute in table talk** and "Let talk again". A muted seat can still act; in the table talk it only sees the note "The host muted you in the table talk". New endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/verbergen` {art: tisch|aktion, n, pid} (host only, sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit), `PUT …/platz/{pid}` also knows `stumm` and `laut`. Logs `dungeon: aktion | wortliste`, `dungeon: tisch | abgelehnt | ValueError`, `dungeon: platz | stumm`, `dungeon: verbergen | tisch` (or `aktion`, `… | nicht da`), `dungeon: beitreten | wortliste`. The live overview counts hidden lines, refused player texts and muted seats, never contents. Self-tests `Moderation` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py` and `Windows.test_acting_shows_the_own_action`.

**AI Dungeon Master: values for items and foes (V01.0.997, Dominik 2026-10-24 "should always show values, how strong, what they do"):** Under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules every row now shows more. Items show kind, rarity, the value the item uses, damage, protection, healing, blessing with its value, tool bonus and price. Foes show level, life, armour, attack, damage and EP. The detail also has a list of values (`dungeon_regelseite.werte`). For items it shows what the item does at the table (the same lines as on the hero sheet), what it uses, whom it is for, spell grade, classes, weight and price. For foes it shows life, armour, attack, damage with its average, EP, the six values, the conditions it can cause, and size. Conditions show effect and duration. Everything comes from fixed words and the catalogue's numbers.

**AI Dungeon Master: the player menu is no longer cut off (V01.0.996, Dominik 2026-10-24 "menu not visible, cut off"):** A fellow player's "⋯" menu in the side column was wider than the column and was cut off on the left. Now it is exactly as wide as the hero's card and opens under its head. A browser test checks this at 1280 and 390 pixels wide. **Painting under the rules without a day limit** ("in the Dungeon Master I cannot make new pictures"): only the admin reaches the "Malen" button under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules. It therefore no longer stops at the day limit of catalogue pictures (300 a day, `dungeon_dingbild.TAG_MAX`), but it still counts. The run over the whole catalogue, at night or by button, still ends at that limit.

**AI Dungeon Master: the die lands on the number that counts (V01.0.994, Dominik 2026-10-24 "the die shows 14, then 9 in the calculation"):** On the own throw, random numbers used to run over the die before it jumped to the panel's number. Now it slows down, and its last face is always the panel's number. With advantage or disadvantage the panel rolls two dice; the throw and the log now show both and say which one counts ("Disadvantage: 14 and 9, counts: 9"). Before the throw it says "two dice, the lower counts" (or the higher). A natural 20 or 1 is named under the bar ("natural 20: always a success", "natural 1: always a miss"). Every wait has its own stamp (`warten.wt`), so the same check next turn is a new throw, not an old number.

**AI Dungeon Master: the map follows when the narrator forgets the id (V01.0.993, Dominik 2026-10-24 "moved into the old library, but no picture, the position is not current"):** When the model narrated the way to a neighbouring place but left `szene.ort_id` empty, the group stayed put on the map and the new place got no picture. Now the panel decides by a fixed rule (`dungeon_karte.named_way`): when the narration names exactly one neighbouring place and a player asked for that place in this turn, by its name (also inflected, "zur alten Bibliothek") or by its direction ("nach Nordosten"), the group moves there. A place only told about, a far place or two named neighbours never move. Log `dungeon: karte | name statt id`. **Only who wants to go goes** (V01.1.077, Dominik 2026-10-25 "laut karte und fahne aber in wolfshafen"): even a valid `ort_id` of the model moves the group only when an action of the turn wants to go: a word of going (gehen, weiter, folgen, betreten, verlassen, reisen, zurück … `GEHEN`, in English go, head, travel …), a neighbouring place or room, or a direction. When a hero only searches a letter pointing to Wolfshafen, the group stays in the Schattengasse, flag and narration agree (log `dungeon: karte | verworfen | ungefragt`). A turn without an action never moves. **The narration names the new place** (V01.1.082): when the `ort_id` points to a neighbouring place the narration does not name, it tells another place than the map. `dungeon_karte.ort_fehlt` then returns that name (for the narrator's "karte" fault, which has the turn rewritten), and the map keeps the group where it was at worst (log `dungeon: karte | verworfen | nicht erzaehlt`).

**AI Dungeon Master: ways in stages and the way to the nearest settlement (V01.1.082, D&D ideas 9 and 4):** The map only counts; the checks and events are rolled by the panel elsewhere. `dungeon_karte.weg(r, rd, "o3")` gives for a way from here its stages by its length on the map (`ETAPPE` = 80 units, the ways are 1 to 3 stages long, at least one) and the land it leads through (the id of `ideen.landschaften` whose place kinds hold the target, else the start, else "weg"; one source with the travel events). `LAND` only draws the ink signs around the places. `dungeon_karte.zur_siedlung(rd)` searches the ways for the nearest settlement (village, town, tavern, station, bazaar, harbour as for trading) and gives stages and steps, the name only when the players already know the place; where the group stands in one, it is 0 stages.

**AI Dungeon Master: props by room and place (V01.1.097, D&D idea 3 of round 2 "use the surroundings"):** `dungeon_karte.requisiten(r, rd, rng)` fixes 2 to 3 props of `ideen.requisiten` for the fight where the group stands: first those whose `arten` hold the room id (e.g. `speisesaal`) or room kind in a vault, or the place kind outside, filled up with those without `arten` (fit anywhere). They are rolled once per room or place (`karte.req`) and stay there; `requisit_benutzt(rd, id)` takes a used one away (each only once). Effect, check and buttons are the fight's (switch `dungeonumgebung`). Logs `dungeon: karte | requisiten | N`, `requisit | benutzt`, `requisit | fehlt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: alarm and torches in a vault (V01.1.126, D&D idea 6 of round 2, switch Features → AI Dungeon Master → Alarm and torches, off by default):** The panel counts an alarm from 0 to 6 per vault (`dungeon_alarm.py`). Noise raises it: a fight, a door, the price "noise" of a near miss, a fumbled sneak (the fight's events via `dungeon_karte.laerm(rd, n, grund)`) and a sprung trap, each by at most 3. At 3 a bell tolls and the next chamber or passage the group enters holds a patrol with guardian foes; at 6 the master of the vault has one more follower. When new dwellers come (the vault fills again) it is calm again. On entering the panel lights a light of the group: first one that never burns out (light spell, Everburning torch with `licht` −1), else the first torch or glow stick in a pack (`licht` 4, used up). Every room entered burns one, then the next follows. Without light, whoever cannot see in the dark (not dwarf, elf, orc, gnome, half-elf or tiefling) rolls every check at a disadvantage; the receipt says "dunkel". Above the vault plan it says "🔔 Alarm 2/6 · 🔥 Torch: 3 rooms" or "🌑 Dark"; the view carries `karte.alarm` and `karte.fackel` (rooms left, −1 always burns, 0 dark, `null` outside) for the bell and the flicker, the model gets alarm and light only as data. Logs `dungeon: alarm | <grund> | <stufe>`, `alarm | streife`, `alarm | herr bereit`, `alarm | licht | <rooms>`, `alarm | verworfen`; the live overview counts noise, patrols, torches and darkness.

**AI Dungeon Master: the map no longer blinks (V01.1.129, Dominik 2026-10-26 "die karte blinkt immer wieder auf der rechten seite"):** The table writes itself anew on every refresh. Until now that drew the map again too, and the pictures of the places came a moment later. Now the map stays the same element while its drawing does not change (`dmkKeep` in `dungeonkarte.js`, called by `dmTable`), and a picture already loaded is in the drawing at once. It is drawn again only when something really changes, such as a way, a new place or the width.

**AI Dungeon Master: coming along only after the check and people on the move, the map's part (V01.1.144 and V01.1.158, LlamaTale ideas 4 and 6, Dominik 2026-10-27 "1-7", ideas only):** When the switch "Persuade to come along" (`dungeonmitkommen`) is on, a word like "come with us" no longer takes a person along at once; the map only remembers which seat asked (`leute[name].fragt`). Whom the open actions ask so, `dungeon_karte.fragen(rd)` tells already before the narration (read only, the same words), so the panel can roll the check first. The panel's check decides, `dungeon_karte.mitnehmen(rd, name, ja)` takes the person along or not and clears the question. Without the switch it stays as before. `dungeon_karte.wandern(r, rd, rng, anlass)` (occasion end of a chapter or a long rest, switch `dungeonwandern`) lets every known person not travelling along whose goal the panel knows (`dungeon_nsc.ziel_ort`) go one way of the map on towards it at 50 %. It returns a list of who went from where to where for rumours; the model only tells the meeting again. Logs `dungeon: karte | mitkommen | gefragt|ja|nein|ungefragt`, `karte | wandern | <anlass> | <anzahl>`.

**No daily limit also without the second step (V01.0.992, Dominik 2026-10-24 "admin still has daily limits"):** The rule from V01.0.989 only held for an admin role that counts, and that exists only with the second login step. A profile without the second step therefore kept its daily limits. Now the admin ticks "frei" under People and devices, at the profile under Rights "No daily limit", for any profile and without the second step (`PUT /api/admin/profiles/{uid}/tageslimit` with `{"frei": true}`, admin only, no manager, rate limit, CSRF). A profile with the admin role has the tick set for good. Stored in `state/ohne-tageslimit.json` (in the backup), changes in the admin log (`profile_daily_limit`) and in the log `coadmin: tageslimit | frei|begrenzt|kein profil`. The admin rights themselves still need the second step. Me → Pictures and Me → Music show "no daily limit".

**No daily limits for admins, rarity under Regeln → Gegenstände (V01.0.989, Dominik 2026-10-24 "daily limits for admins should be off" and "clicking items makes the list jump"):** A profile with the admin role (`coadmin.unbegrenzt`, the same rule as for the quota) has no daily limits any more: painting and changing pictures, songs, own music files and the pictures of an own Dungeon round. Me → Pictures and Me → Music then show "no daily limit". Space, the queue of heavy jobs and everything that leaves the house (mails, calls, reMarkable) keep their limits. Under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules the item list stays put on a tap, only the mark and the detail change. Every item stands in its rarity's frame; most basic items roll it only when found, they carry a dashed frame and a colour gradient, and their detail shows the ladder common to legendary with roll, bonus, price and minimum level. **Renaming a fellow player** (point 5 of the proposals on managing fellow players, Dominik "1-6"): in a fellow player's "⋯" the host can change the hero's name (`PUT /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/platz/{pid}` with `{"was": "name"}`), only the name, checked as when creating (2 to 24 characters, at least one letter); values, level and bag stay. Log `dungeon: platz | name`.

**AI Dungeon Master: More time between turns (V01.0.991, Dominik 2026-10-24 "the interaction goes away too fast for the user, sometimes no chance to read the window and act between turns", "1-5"):** After every narration a reading time runs first (15 s plus 1 s per 60 characters of the narration, at most 90 s, `dungeon.read_s`); only then the turn time counts, until then the clock shows "reading time, the clock starts in … s". New rounds have 300 s instead of 120 s (60, 120, 300 stay selectable; running rounds keep their time). Once the last player acted, the turn closes only 10 s later (`GNADE_S`), the own card shows the seconds and "Change" still works. Below the input field and in the own card there is "+1 minute": once per turn and seat (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/mehrzeit`, guests `POST /api/dungeon/mehrzeit`; sign-in or seat code, CSRF, size and rate limit like the other game endpoints), everyone sees "more time for …". From 30 s left the clock turns red and a phone vibrates once. Logs: `dungeon: mehrzeit | ok`, `dungeon: mehrzeit | schon`, `dungeon: mehrzeit | nicht jetzt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: tab "Rules" with bestiary and balance (V01.0.984, Dominik 2026-10-24 "the rules page should be here for the admin", "see and edit the bestiary, items etc.", "do all points … balance 1-5, too strong only a hint"):** On the **Dungeon Master** page the admin has a third tab *Rules* next to *Rounds* and *Heroes*; *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules* opens it (without a profile as a card as before). On the left are the parts: stories, items, foes (bestiary), classes, peoples, backgrounds, worlds, tones, conditions, then voices, pictures, music, guests with e-mail, how it lands and *Advanced (JSON)*. A part of the catalogue is a list with the picture or sign in front, one line of values, where it comes from (base, SRD, own, changed, hidden) and the balance mark, with search and filters (items by kind, foes by level, both by balance); beside it (below on a phone) the detail with *Edit* (the whole entry, checked like every rule), *Own copy*, *Hide* and *Back to shipped*. **Pictures:** foes get a painted picture in the style of their world like items (a fixed sentence of the panel, at night or by button, the same day limit). *🖌 Paint* paints a missing picture right away, *Paint again* paints a second one and shows the old and the new side by side: *Keep the new one* or *Keep the old one*; one nobody decides goes after a day. **Balance** (`dungeon_balance.py`): foes are compared with a fixed table per level (life, armour, attack, damage per round), items with a budget per rarity (damage, protection, healing, blessing, spell grade, price); the mark says *balanced*, *strong*, *too strong* or *weak*, the reason is in the detail. *Too strong* is only a hint, saving stays possible. A self-test checks the shipped catalogue; the list of entries still too strong may only shrink. Live counts own and hidden entries and pictures waiting.

**AI Dungeon Master: room for the SRD data (V01.0.978, agreed with the data part "DnDAppFiles", Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-6", "1-3"):** Next to `dungeon_regeln.json` the panel reads a second shipped file `dungeon_srd.json` (data from the D&D SRD 5.1/5.2, CC-BY-4.0) as a layer between the base rules and the admin's own: the same lists, the same id replaces the entry, `{"id": …, "dazu": {…}}` adds to the lists of an existing entry (such as a class's weapons, spells per grade, an item's classes; also in the admin's own rules). Without the file everything stays as before; if it does not pass `check_rules`, the panel plays without it (log `dungeon: regeln verworfen | srd`). New in the rules and checked: the lists **gegner** (sign, worlds, level 0 to 30, life, armour, attack, damage, experience, six values, size, conditions), **zustaende** (effect only from fixed words like `nachteil_angriff` or `kein_zug`, duration in rounds) and **hintergruende** (value bonuses 0 to 2, starting gear), the fixed difficulty table **sg** (very easy 5 to nearly impossible 30), **ladungen** per level and spell grade 1 to 5, on classes `start_faehigkeiten` and `zauber` per grade, on spells `grad` 0 to 5. Items may have a fixed `seltenheit` (loot and merchants offer them only when the roll reaches that rarity), jewellery a fixed value (else drawn), the new kind **treasure** (gems, art) is only for selling and stacks. Items with `welten` exist only in those worlds: loot and merchants offer only what fits the round's world. Each merged list holds up to 500 entries; the admin's own file (*Features → AI Dungeon Master* "Adjust rules") stays at 200 per list at most.

**AI Dungeon Master: spells and powers only when the hero has them (V01.0.979, Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-3"):** Until now a character at level 1 could have a fireball narrated, because the model only knew name, people, class, level and life. Now, as in D&D: every class brings its **starting powers** (`start_faehigkeiten`, shown under the class as "can: …" when a hero is made), spell classes have their **spells per grade** (`zauber`), cantrips (grade 0) work any time, higher grades need a **charge** (`ladungen` per level); the charges fill up with every new round and chapter, a level brings the new ones. The hero sheet shows **"What I can do"**: abilities, spells with "1 of 2 charges left" and spells or tools from the bag; a tap writes "I cast …" into the own action. **The panel checks** every action by fixed rules (`check_powers`): if, with a word like "cast", "use" or "wirke", it names a spell or ability of the catalogue the hero neither has nor carries, only that seat sees the line "(OOC: Bela cannot do that yet: Fireball.)", and the model gets `nicht_bekannt` and tells that it fails; without a charge it says "no charge left in this chapter". A known spell with a charge is cast when the turn is told (charge gone, healing and blessing by the catalogue, line "✦ … casts …"). The model gets the list `kann` per hero (names and charges only) and the fixed rule to cast only from it. Status → Live counts spells from charges and refused powers; Logs only `dungeon: kann | nicht | 1 namen` and `dungeon: kraft | grad 1`. *Features → AI Dungeon Master* "Adjust rules" changes starting powers, spells per class and charges like everything else (checked by `check_rules`).

**AI Dungeon Master: guests with e-mail (V01.0.981, plan stage 3, Dominik 2026-10-22 "Ok", 2026-10-23 "hash, address only when mail is wanted"):** Own admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Guests with e-mail* (`chat.dungeon_gast`, off by default; `dungeon_gast.py`). Under "Adjust rules", in the section "Guests with e-mail", the admin picks the mailbox with an outgoing server the mails go from and sees only numbers (guest accounts, with turn mail, heroes, mails today). A guest (only with a seat link, so only by invitation) opens "Guest account with e-mail" in the invitation or at the table, types their address and gets a 6-digit code (valid 10 minutes, at most 5 wrong tries, at most 3 codes an hour per address, 40 game mails a day). With the code they have a guest account on this device (a key in the browser, the Spark knows only its hash, up to 5 devices); the hero at the seat goes in, "Keep this hero" adds one later, up to 8 heroes, written along after every turn and at the end of the round. A new invitation offers "Hero from your guest account". The Spark stores only an HMAC of the address (`dungeon-gaeste.json`, 0600). **"Your turn by mail"** is switched on by the guest with their address (it must match the account): then it lies sealed like a password on the Spark, switching it off deletes it. The mail comes when a turn has been open 10 minutes, the guest has written nothing and is not at the table, once per turn, at most 3 a day. All mails are fixed texts of the panel, never model or player text; the emergency stop holds them. "Delete guest account" removes heroes, devices and address. Live counts guest accounts, turn mails, codes and mails; Logs only `dungeon: gastmail | code gesendet`, `angemeldet`, `dran | gesendet` and the like, never an address. API (guest): `POST /api/dungeon/gast` (`{code, was: code|anmelden|konto|merken|zugmail|held_weg|loeschen, …}`, rate limit `dungeonkonto`), joining with `{code, konto, konto_held}`; admin `PUT /api/admin/dungeon/gastmail`.

**AI Dungeon Master: props in the scene picture (V01.1.124, D&D ideas round 2, idea 3 "use the surroundings", Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir.", plan `plaene/dungeon-dnd-ideen-2.md`):** While a fight goes on and "use the surroundings" is on, a scene picture (`dungeon.queue_scene`) takes the props still there (`dungeon_umgebung.here`, rows of `ideen.requisiten`) into the picture: at most `REQUISITEN_MAX` (3), by their English name from the catalog, never with the model's text (`dungeon_dingbild.props`). A name the picture rules refuse is left out; a used prop is missing from the next picture. No switch of its own: the switch of "use the surroundings" counts. Self-test `test_the_fights_props_are_in_the_scene_picture`.

**AI Dungeon Master: faces for companions (V01.1.124, D&D ideas round 2, idea 9 "hire companions", Dominik 2026-10-26 "1-9 umsetzten reihenfolge überlasse ich dir.", plan `plaene/dungeon-dnd-ideen-2.md`):** A hired companion (`rd["gefaehrten"]`, key `gf:…`) gets its portrait like a person with a card (`dungeon_gesicht`), but at once on hiring instead of after its first word: from its fixed English line of the table (`GEFAEHRTE_EN` + `satz`, at the children's table the little animal as it is), never with its name, with the world's style, light and mood from the fixed lists, checked by `dungeon.picture_text`, within the daily picture limit. The group shows the face instead of 🤝 (`gefaehrten[].bild`, `dmGefaehrten`); when the last narration names the companion, a scene picture takes the face as a reference ("the companion from reference image N with the same face"). It only holds while "Faces for NPCs", "Hire companions" and "Paint pictures" are on for the host; "NPC memory and chronicle" is not needed for it. The face goes on with the companion into the next chapter (`dungeon_gefaehrte.keep`), the sweep keeps it while a round or campaign has the companion, only a seat of that round may load it. Self-test `test_a_hired_companion_gets_its_face_at_once`. The settlement service "hire a companion" gets its own picture (`DIENST_EN["gefaehrte"]`, a hiring corner by an inn's door, no person in front) and three lines of life in `dungeon_siedlung.json`.

**AI Dungeon Master: portraits in the NPC catalog (V01.1.149, Dominik 2026-10-27 "portrait fehlt bei den npc"):** Under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "People (NPC)"* the list and the detail now show each person's face (`dungeon_dingbild.person_row` in `dungeon_personen.view`): first the one the admin kept by hand, then the face from a round, else a catalog picture. A person without a face from a round is a catalog picture like items and foes (`dungeon_dingbild.people`): it is painted at night and with "Paint all pictures", "🖌 Paint", "Paint again" and "Keep the new one" go through the catalog picture door (`/api/admin/dungeon/katalog/personen/bild`). The picture's prompt is made of fixed parts only (`person_text`): its world's style, age and gender of its stored voice, the English column of its card (`dungeon_gesicht.katalog_text`) and light and mood from the fixed lists; never its name. When the person comes back in a round (`dungeon_personen.adopt`), it brings this face, not at the children's table. The page "People" of the monster album shows it too. The sweep keeps a face while a round or the catalog names it. This only holds while "Pictures for gear and spells", "Faces for NPCs" and "People catalog" are on. Self-test `test_a_catalog_person_gets_its_face`.

**AI Dungeon Master: innkeeper or landlady, names only as a whole word (V01.1.162, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 10, Z13 and Z14, Dominik "ok"):** The settlement's lines of life (`dungeon_siedlung.json`) never name the innkeeper of one world once as "Wirtin" and once as "Wirt"; the line for "hire a companion" is neutral for that. A scene picture takes the face of a person or a companion only when the narration names it as a whole word (`dungeon_nsc.named` in `dungeon_gesicht.vorlagen`): "Perle" is not in "Perlen". Self-tests `test_one_innkeeper_per_world` and `test_a_hired_companion_gets_its_face_at_once`.

**AI Dungeon Master: what a foe is, companions with gender and age in the picture (V01.1.173, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 10, Z7 and Z11, Dominik "ok"):** A foe's picture takes what it is from its field "wesen" in the Bestiarium (`dungeon_bestiarium.wesen`), no longer from two lists by hand: humans, humanoids, giants, undead, constructs, fey, fiends, celestials and aberrations each have a fixed phrase (`WESEN_EN`), animals, dragons, elementals, plants, oozes and monsters already say it with their name. Ten humans the old list did not know (such as the sea robber and the villager) now look human; zombie, troll and dryad are no longer "humanoid". About 126 of the 268 foes get a new picture prompt by this: their old picture stays until the new one is painted (at night or with "Paint all pictures"). A companion's face puts gender and age from its data in front ("a middle aged woman, a companion of the heroes, …"), an animal or a machine gets "an animal/a machine companion"; nothing is guessed from the line. Self-tests `test_a_foe_is_painted_from_its_description_size_and_kind` and `test_a_hired_companion_gets_its_face_at_once`.

**AI Dungeon Master: three small night foes for science fiction (V01.1.188, data, Dungeon review 27.10. Paket 11, asked by the main thread, thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master"):** With the newly measured budget (one hero alone has only about 60 XP on level 1) science fiction had only three night foes on levels 1 and 2. New in the bestiary (`app/panel/dungeon_srd.json`, each with `"nacht": true`, the "new" mark and a saying): the glow moth (10 XP, an animal that nibbles at the light of lamps), the cable crawler (25 XP, a maintenance robot from the cable ducts, construct, immune to poison and psychic, weak to lightning) and the night flitter (50 XP, an animal that flits through the cargo holds in the dark). Every world now has at least five night foes for one hero on levels 1 to 3. The picture thread paints the pictures.

**AI Dungeon Master: portrait by the gender on the card (V01.1.182, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 10, Z1 and Z6, Dominik "ok"):** A person's portrait takes the gender written on its card (`rd["nsc"][k]["geschlecht"]`, set once when the card is made since V01.1.167), through the same place as its voice (`dungeon_stimmen._of`); so does the age once the card has a field "alter", else from the fixed voice traits as before. A person with "divers" is painted as an androgynous person ("an old androgynous person", the word from `dungeon.GESCHLECHT`), no longer as a "person" without a word. None of it is guessed by the model; no new switch, no new endpoint.

**AI Dungeon Master: hair, eyes, beard and build in the portrait (V01.1.189, Dungeon review 2026-10-27 package 10, Z1):** A person's portrait adds the fixed looks of its card (`dungeon_nsc.MERKMALE`: hair, eyes, beard, build, rolled once by the panel since V01.1.186), in the tables' English column, such as "grey hair, blue eyes, slender build". So the picture fits what the table reads on the card; the model guesses none of it. New portraits and catalog pictures take it at once, a portrait already painted stays until the host has it painted again.

**AI Dungeon Master: pictures for runes and glowing sockets (V01.1.194, plan "Runes and sockets" point 11a, Dominik "empfehlung" 2026-10-27):** With "Runes and sockets" on, each of the 13 runes gets a catalog picture per style, as its world's word (rune, chip, pearl, seal; `dungeon_runen.word`) and in its fixed colour by rank (`dungeon_dingbild.RUNE_FARBE`, `rune_text`); it is painted at night or with "Paint all pictures" like every catalog picture, none while the switch is off. The rune bag in the hero sheet and the trader's runes show the picture instead of "ᚱ" once it is there. A thing's sockets are no new picture: `dungeonsockel.js` (`dmSockel`) lays them as dots over the picture or sign there is, a free socket dark, a set rune glowing in its colour (`ru_farbe` from `dungeon_dingbild.add`). The picture of a finished runeword comes with stage 2. The model decides none of it; no new switch, no new endpoint.

**AI Dungeon Master: rune pictures on the rules page (V01.1.204, Dominik 2026-10-28 "in den regeln sehe ich diese nicht."):** In the part "Runes" of the rules tab (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules) every rune now has its picture with "Paint", "Paint again" and "Keep new"/"Keep old" like items and foes. "Pictures in: …" above the list chooses the world; the rune shows as that world's word and in its style (rune, chip, pearl, seal; `dungeon_regelseite.WELT_WAHL`, `dungeon_dingbild._current` with `rune_text`). A rune picture stays while "Runes and sockets" is off: the sweep keeps it, only nothing is painted at night then.

**AI Dungeon Master: a picture of its own for an awakened runeword, pictures of the smith's rune services (V01.1.206, plan "Runes and sockets" point 11a, stage 2):** When a runeword wakes in a thing (`it["rw"]`), the panel calls `dungeon_dingbild.runenwort` once: like a named find the thing gets a picture of its own (`fund-<i>`) with the runes in their colours and the runeword's name, painted within a minute through the queue and counted to the day limit of the game's pictures. The picture of an earlier runeword of the same thing goes first; once the runeword is freed again the thing shows the catalog picture. The smith's new services, boring a socket, freeing a rune and fusing runes, have a picture per world without people and lines of life in the settlement (`DIENST_EN`, `dungeon_siedlung.json`). No new switch, no new endpoint; the model decides none of it.

**AI Dungeon Master: one picture a house of the settlement (V01.1.210, settlement reordered point 6a, Dominik 2026-10-28 "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a 6a"):** Every house of the new settlement view (`dungeon_gold.HAEUSER`: inn, smithy, healer, teacher) has a picture of its own per world, seen from the street, no people in the foreground (`dungeon_dingbild.HAUS_EN`, `house_text`). It is painted at night or with "Paint all pictures"; the view shows the house's picture once it is there, until then the first picture of its services or the place's. Each house also gets a line of life from its services. The pictures of the single services (the room inside) stay. No new switch, no new endpoint; the model decides none of it.

**AI Dungeon Master: a house's picture whole instead of cut off (V01.1.234, Dominik 2026-10-28 "das obere bildbanner siehr nicht gut aus - abgeschnitten. bei allen"):** In the settlement window the chosen house's picture no longer stands as a wide band above the list, which showed only a strip of the square picture, but whole as a square on the left beside name and line (up to 200 pixels on a computer, 96 on a phone). The offers below use the full width. Display only (`app.css`), no new pictures.

**AI Dungeon Master: "Paint all pictures · N" counts only what a run paints; a picture for the healer (V01.1.113, Dominik 2026-10-26: page 3984/3990 and "Paint all pictures · 6", every run ended at once with 0 painted):** An entry for which the admin fixed a picture under "Adjust rules" with "Keep new" (`fest`) is never painted again by a run, even when its prompt changes later; but the page counted it as missing. Now one rule decides both (`dungeon_dingbild._stand`: there, fixed, refused, open): fixed counts as done, "Paint all pictures · N" and "left N" name only the open pictures (`status()["offen"]`, the same as `missing()`), refused ones stand beside as their own number as before; without open pictures both buttons are off. Also the settlement's new service "healer" (`dungeon_gold.HEILER`, scars idea 8) has its picture per style (`DIENST_EN["heiler"]`, a calm room with herbs, bandages and small bottles, no blood) and three lines of life in `dungeon_siedlung.json`; until the first run the window shows 🌿. Self-tests `test_the_count_names_only_what_a_run_paints`, `test_every_service_painted_and_lined`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pictures for gear and spells (V01.0.967, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "5i", Dominik 2026-10-23 "2" and "the picture generation, but also startable directly by a button"):** Own admin switch Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures for gear and spells (off by default, `chat.dungeon_bilder`); Features → Painting pictures must be on as well and the picture model installed. **Catalog:** every catalog entry (weapons, armour, jewellery, potions, spells, scrolls, the admin's own entries too) gets one picture per world in the world's fixed style (`welten[].bildstil`), and in the picture book style while a round in the tone from 8 is open. The panel builds the text by a fixed rule from the style, the entry's English name and a fixed phrase per kind (for spells also the spell's English sentence), followed by "no text, no letters, no watermark"; it is checked like a picture wish (word list, known people), a refused entry never goes to the picture service. It is painted at 512×512 one after the other through the queue "Heavy jobs" (the voice comes first, maintenance pause "bild") and kept at half size with the picture service's AI marks (`dungeon-bild/ding-<hash>.png`, the hash of the text is the name, a changed style or name paints anew). When: at night from 01:00 to 05:00 once a night, and at once with "Create pictures now" under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules (section "Pictures for gear and spells" with the count, the last run and "Stop"); at most 300 pictures a day. Next to it **"Paint all pictures"** (since V01.1.000, Dominik 2026-10-24 "alle bilder generieren lassen knopf ohne sich ans limit zu halten") paints every missing picture without the day limit: items, spells, tools, foes, kinds of places and rooms, peoples, classes and abilities; the button shows how many are missing, the run "painting all, no day limit · painted · left", "Cancel" stops after the picture on its way; space, the queue, Notaus and the picture check hold, only the admin reaches it (like `coadmin.unbegrenzt`). Log: `dungeon: dingbild | lauf start | alle`. **Foe pictures** (since V01.1.000, Dominik 2026-10-24 "2": a cultist came out as a horned demon from its name alone) get besides the English name the English description (`info`, only when the word list of Painting pictures lets it through), the size (`groesse`: tiny to huge) and, from fixed lists of the panel, what they are (`MENSCH`: "an ordinary human person", `HUMANOID`: "a humanoid with two arms and two legs"), never from a choice of the model; since V01.1.043 the humans and humanoids among the 62 new foes of the bestiary (V01.1.039) are in these lists too, every foe is painted at night and with "Paint all pictures"; instead of "plain dark background" it says "in a fitting place of its world", matching the background of the world's style. Every picture text goes through the word list of Painting pictures and the game's list "nie", in the picture book style through the youngest tone's words too. The new text paints every foe once more. **Pictures stay** (since V01.1.005, Dominik 2026-10-24: pictures newly painted in the bestiary were gone after every restart): the sweep after a start took every picture whose picture text a new version had changed. Now the panel remembers the last picture painted per entry and style (`zuletzt`) and shows it until the new text's picture is there; a picture painted and kept under Adjust rules is fixed (`fest`), never painted over and never swept. When the rules fall back to the state without the SRD data, it does not sweep at all (log `dungeon: dingbild | aufraeumen ausgelassen | regeln unvollstaendig`). The run ends on Notaus, "Stop", the switch off, in the morning (only the night's run), at the day limit, with a full queue and when the picture model or the image check is missing; a picture the image check holds back twice is not tried again. **Finds:** when the model names a find from "rare" on, the panel paints its own picture within a minute (the round's style, the English name from the catalog, the model's name and sentence, checked like a scene description with tone, "nie" and taboos); unknown magic only once it is known. It counts to the day limit of the game's pictures (`grenzen.bilder_tag`) and needs the host's "Painting pictures". **At the table:** every item in the bag, the equipment, loot, on the ground and at the merchant shows its picture in its rarity's frame (green, blue, purple, orange), until then the kind's sign (⚔️ 🛡️ 💍 🧪 ✨ 📜). Profiles load pictures with `GET /api/profile/dungeon/ding/{name}`, guests with their seat code (`POST /api/dungeon/ding`); rate limit `dungeonding`. The state lives in `dungeon-dingbilder.json`, hourly the panel removes pictures no entry and no hero needs any more. Status → Live counts item pictures, finds, painted, discarded and failed today on the Dungeon Master line; logs: `dungeon: dingbild | lauf start | knopf`, `dungeon: dingbild | ok | katalog | 3100 ms`, `dungeon: dingbild | ok | fund | 3300 ms`, `dungeon: dingbild | lauf ende | tag | 300 gemalt`, `dungeon: dingbild | abgelehnt | fund | tag`. **Peoples, classes, backgrounds and worlds on the rules tab** (since V01.1.009, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1a 2 und 3"): under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules classes, peoples, backgrounds and worlds show their picture too, with "Paint", "Paint again" and "Keep the new one"/"Keep the old one". Above the list "Pictures in: …" chooses the world in whose style peoples, classes and backgrounds are shown and painted (the same picture as on that world's hero form); a kept picture is fixed. **Backgrounds** get one picture per world from the English name and description (only when the word list lets it through) and are painted at night and with "Paint all pictures"; the hero form has no choice of background, so they show only on the rules tab. **Every world** gets a title picture in its own style ("landscape art, a wide view of the world …, no people", not in the picture book style) that the world cards under "New round" show instead of the sign; until it is painted and with the pictures off the sign stays. **Stage pictures per kind of place** (since V01.1.037, Dominik 2026-10-24 "alle umsetzen", idea 10 "the scene as a stage"): every kind of place (`ortsarten`) and every kind of room (`raeume`) gets one wide picture per world style in the format of the scene pictures (16:9, painted at 1024×576, kept at half size), since V01.1.043 three per place: by day, at night and in a fight (fixed text "scene art, a wide view of …" from the English name, for rooms with the sentence, without people, in a fight only distant silhouettes without faces). The panel picks by fixed rules: fight while a fight runs (the round's `kampf`), night for the daytime `abend` or `nacht` (the round's `tageszeit`, unknown values count as day), else day. It is painted at night and with "Paint all pictures", in the picture book style too while a round in the youngest tone is open. At the table `view()` names it as `buehne.ersatz`: outside the picture of the kind of the place the round is at, in a vault the one of the room's kind (a deleted entry takes the first room of the same kind); the stage shows it only while the scene has no picture of its own. Without a map, without a painted picture or with the pictures off it is empty; the stage lays weather and daytime over it. **Pictures with flair** (since V01.1.043, Dominik 2026-10-25 "alles nach empfehlung umsetzten", plan "Bilder mit Flair"): every picture takes from fixed lists of the panel a light (morning light, golden hour, moonlight, candle glow, storm light, light through a window, the glow of magic), an angle (close-up, low angle, from above, three-quarter view, through an archway) and a mood (calm, mysterious, menacing, festive, forgotten and dusty); which one is fixed by the entry's id with the style (sha256, the same choice every time), never by the model. In the picture book style never menacing or storm light, in a dark world style (gothic, horror, grim, dark) never festive; places, worlds, heroes and the stage never close-up. Items, spells and tools lie in a place of their kind (for example a weapon on an anvil in a smithy, a potion on an alchemist's table, a spell above an open spell book), sharp before a soft background instead of a dark ground; a fixed rarity shows (common plain and worn up to legendary with aura, runes and sparks), rolled finds keep their frame. Foes get a pose (lurking, attacking, roaring, stepping out of the dark, waiting for prey, circling; in the picture book only proud, curious or patient) and surroundings by size (large and huge in the open, in a hall, on a slope, the rest in a passage, a ruin, a thicket). Peoples, classes and backgrounds stand in their world with a pose instead of a dark ground, abilities as a moment in the middle of action; worlds and the map vignettes of the kinds of places and rooms get light and mood.
