With the new texts every picture is painted once more (at night 300 a day, "Paint all pictures" without a limit); until then the old picture stays visible, one kept under Adjust rules stays fixed. **Settlement with life** (since V01.1.043, Dominik 2026-10-25 "so umsetzten", draft "Siedlung mit Leben"): in a settlement's window the view names as `siedlung.kopf` the place's stage picture (by day or at night) and per service (`gasthaus`, `schmied`, `lehre`, `geruecht`, `verbessern`) a painted picture in the world's style (`bild`, fixed text from `DIENST_EN` with light, angle and mood of the lists, no people in the foreground, at night and with "Paint all pictures") and a line of life (`zeile`) from `dungeon_siedlung.json`: three lines per world and service in German and English, `alle` for any other world, another one per turn and place by a fixed rule, never from the model. Without a painted picture or with the pictures off `bild` stays empty and the window shows the sign.

**AI Dungeon Master: map and random vaults (V01.0.969, plan `dungeon-master/karte-plan.md`, Dominik 2026-10-23 "karte 2" and "1-5 aber viel mehr varianzen reinbringen", `dungeon_karte.py`):** the map belongs to the panel, not to the model. At the start of a round or a chapter the panel rolls 8 to 15 places in one of five landscapes (scattered, river valley, around a lake, two realms with one crossing, coast), at least 150 points apart, joins them with a spanning tree and a few short extra ways that cross no other (at most 4 ways a place), puts the start in the west, the goal farthest away and a place on the way there for every act of the story. Names come first from the story's places (the first is the start), else from the world's kinds of places (`ortsarten`: 33 kinds, each world its own, with name lists) or from a stem of the world (`silben`) and an ending of the kind, such as "Crow" and "Wood". The next chapter starts at the last place. **The model** gets only the current place, the ways from there with their direction and the direction of the next act's place (`karte` in the facts) and names one of those ways in `szene.ort_id` when the group moves on; anything else, a made-up name or a request in an action as well, leaves the group where it is (`dungeon: karte | verworfen`). The scene's place is always the map's name. **Fog:** the page shows only visited places (solid) and known ones (neighbours of visited ones, dashed, name in italics); a way into the unknown ends as a stub with "?", and neither the page nor the model learns the name behind it. The view grows with what was discovered, "⤢" shows the map large. Text, circles and ways have the same screen size on every width as the text beside them; each name finds a free spot below, above, right or left of its place, long names take two lines, and the place stands as "📍 You are at …" above the map (V01.0.973, Dominik "text too big, overlapping"). **Pictures instead of signs** (V01.0.973, Dominik "should we generate pictures and not use emojis", option 2): with Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures for gear and spells and Features → Painting pictures, the night run (or the button "Create pictures now") paints after the items one picture per kind of place and kind of room in the style of every world it belongs to, from fixed phrases with the English name; it counts to the same day limit. Besides, every visited place of a running round whose host has "Painting pictures" gets its own picture from kind, name and the round's style, checked like a scene picture (tone, "nie", taboos, word list, people) and counted to the game's day limit; a refused name gets none. Until a picture is there, the sign stays. An own place picture is shown only to seats of the same round (profile or guest link); since V01.1.077 the check reads only that one round or the profile's rounds (`dungeon_ablage.runde`, `rids_von`), never all of them; any name outside the fixed pattern is refused; the sweep keeps what a map still names. Live counts the place pictures. Under "From here" a tap on a way writes "We go on to …" into the own field; it stays the player's text. The host may roll the map again before the first step. **Look** (V01.0.986, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1,3,4,6"): the map is a parchment with grain, a dark edge, a double frame, a compass and the round's title plate; trees, mountains, hills, grass and marsh fit the nearest kind of place and stand the same on every repaint (a fixed random from the map), the river has a current, lake and coast have waves, the ways are slightly bent ink lines, a small flag shows where the group stands, the unknown lies under clouds of fog. Every world has its style from its colours (fantasy parchment, horror pale paper, pirates sea chart, sci-fi star chart). The vault shows stone slabs, thick walls, doors between the rooms, a colour per kind of room, torches in the rooms entered, light around the group and darkness towards the edge. Motion: the flag walks along the way, new places fade in, the torches flicker; with "Reduce motion" on the device everything stands still. When the group enters a vault, the map switches to the tab "Vault". **Drawn plan** (V01.1.051, idea 17 of the tabletop ideas, Dominik 2026-10-25): the vault is first a plan in the style of the One-Page Dungeons: black ink walls on paper, the rock around them shaded grey and crosshatched, a dotted grid in the rooms, doors as bars in the corridors (open where the group already went), unknown neighbouring rooms dashed with "?". Every room entered carries its number and its furniture after its entry in the room table (stairs at the entrance, a pool with ripples at the spring, a hatched pit at the trap, a rune circle at the riddle, a chest and coins in the treasure, columns and a plinth at the guardian, a dais with columns and a throne at the master, shelves in the library, cells in the dungeon cells, barrels in the powder room, pods in the cryo room and so on; a room the admin adds gets the drawing of its kind). The numbers of rooms not yet conquered are red, a dashed red frame and a pawn show the group. Below the plan stands the key with number and name of every room entered. The paper takes the colour of the world (sci-fi as a blueprint) and is dimmed at the dark table. The crosshatch follows the method of benjcooley/dungeongen (MIT licence, © 2024 Benjamin Cooley): bundles of three parallel strokes, spread evenly, each bundle turned away from its neighbours and cut where it meets a neighbour's stroke; a fixed chance from the vault's name keeps it the same on every redraw. The button 🔥 next to the tabs switches to the torchlight look with stone slabs and painted room pictures, 📜 back; the device remembers the choice. **The world goes on** (V01.1.067, idea 7 of the tabletop ideas, map part): when the group has left a vault and then gone at least 6 steps on the map (`NEU_NACH`), the vault has new dwellers on the next entry: beaten guardians, the master of the vault, sprung traps and emptied chests come back, their rooms are unknown again, the panel picks the foes anew by the group's level; riddles, springs and altars stay as they were. The table reads "Something new has moved into the vault …", the log `dungeon: gewoelbe | neu belebt | N raeume`. When a faction's clock is full, it holds a place (`dungeon_fraktionen.occupied`): the map shows a dark banner with a golden crest there (the name on pointing), "From here" a ⚑ at the way there, above the map "⚑ Held by …" while the group stands there. When "Arms for group and factions" is on, the banner carries that faction's arms (V01.1.082, as an SVG image that runs nothing) and the line above the map shows them instead of the ⚑; otherwise the golden dot stays. The model hears it as `besetzt_von` at the place and at the ways; arriving, the table reads once "… is held by …" (log `dungeon: karte | besetzt`). **Key doors** (V01.1.070, point 15 of the review of 2026-10-24, on the quest things of V01.1.069): while "Quest things" is allowed for the round, every second vault of 5 rooms and more (`SCHLOSS`) has a locked door on the way to the master of the vault. Its key is a quest thing with a fixed name per world (fantasy Eisenschlüssel/iron key, Bronzeschlüssel, Runensiegel; horror Knochenschlüssel …; pirates Messingschlüssel …; sci-fi Zugangskarte …, each the first the round does not have yet) and lies in a room the group reaches without that door, off the way when possible. To open the door, an action names the key and the group must carry it. Then the panel opens the door, the key is used up and the group may go through in the same turn. The model never opens a door. On the plan the door carries a red lock, in torchlight 🔒. "From here" shows "🔒 locked door". Above the map it says whether the key still lies somewhere in the vault or which name the action should name. With "Quest things" off no door is locked, and one already locked opens on the next turn. Log: `dungeon: gewoelbe | schloss`, `… | schloss | offen`, `… | schloss | ohne schluessel`, `dungeon: karte | verworfen | verschlossen`. **On the way:** a way gone for the first time brings an encounter from `begegnungen` 40 percent of the time (quiet, weather, a hint, a find from the catalog, a foe from the fight's offer, a merchant at the new place); the model tells it in the next turn. **Vaults:** a place of a vault kind (cave, ruin, crypt, tower, wreck, lab, manor, fortress …) or the host's button "🎲 Roll a vault" (which only lays an entrance below the place: ⚔ on the map and "into the vault" under "From here"; the group goes in only by its action) leads into a grid of 5 to 12 rooms (6 to 9 columns, 4 to 6 rows) in one of five forms (branching, long corridor with side rooms, maze with loops, cross around a hall, winding cave), the entrance at the west edge, the master of the vault in the farthest room, a treasure next to it, and about half of the other rooms special. Rooms come per world from `raeume` (42). **Trap:** on entering, the panel rolls d20 plus value for every hero against the trap's difficulty (`fallen`, 17, plus half the group's level) as a saving throw like in D&D: failed it deals the whole damage (at most 6), made half of it, armour does not help against traps (V01.1.014). **Riddle:** the door stays shut until an action holds one of the fixed answer words (`raetsel`, 40, case and umlauts do not count); after 3 turns the page shows the hint, solved it gives half the goal's EP to everyone. The model gets the question, never the answer. **Guardian and master** keep the ways shut until the fight is won (V01.1.014): on entering, the panel picks the room's foes from the world's Bestiarium (a world without foes of its own takes the fantasy ones, V01.1.044) by the group's level L and since V01.1.181 within the fight's budget (guardian: kinds of the challenge nearest L/2; master: one nearest L+1 with helpers near L/4; the panel fills the count by experience, see below) and offers only them for the fight; only the victory in the fight opens the room; **treasure** gives the catalog's loot "truhe" once, a **spring** heals everyone by 2 to 6, an **altar** gives everyone +1 on one value for 3 turns. The way out is only at the entrance. Everything stays in the round (`dungeon-runden.json`, no new file); the admin changes the tables like the rest of the catalog (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*, button "Adjust rules"), the panel checks them. Live counts maps and rounds inside a vault; the journal writes fixed words and numbers only (`dungeon: karte`, `gewoelbe`, `falle`, `raetsel`, `quelle`, `altar`, `unterwegs`). **Places and people stay where they are** (V01.1.023, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1-5 umsetzten"): back to the place the group came from only when an action names it, its direction or says "back"; otherwise the panel drops the way (`dungeon: karte | verworfen | rueckweg`). Whoever speaks in the narration stays at the place where the group met them (up to 40 people); only someone an action takes along with fixed words ("come with", "follow us", "join us" …) travels with the group, and "stay here", "wait here" or a farewell leaves them behind. The model gets here, elsewhere (with the place) and along. When an action and the narration name a neighbouring place but the map does not move, the map shows "Place not changed" (`dungeon: karte | ort bleibt`). For the voice (CarPlay) `dungeon_karte.wege(rd, lang)` gives the same ways as "From here" with direction and goal, never a place in the fog (V01.1.020). Endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/karte` (`{was: neu|gewoelbe}`, host only, login, profile binding, CSRF, size and rate limit).

**AI Dungeon Master: music, sounds, dice (V01.0.970, plan `dungeon-master/plan.md` section "5f Bauplan", Dominik 2026-10-23 "not just a few but quite a few more", "render in the background once you know which act comes next", "1a, 2a, 3a, 4a"):** **Music** only when the admin switches on **Prepare music** under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "Music at the table" (off by default) and the music model is installed; otherwise the table has sounds and dice only. The own music model (Making music, `musik.make`) builds one shared store for the whole Spark, one field per world and mood: exploring, tavern, tension, fight, finale, victory and vault. Each field holds as many pieces as the admin sets (default 12, 4 to 30). A piece runs 3 to 6 minutes, a victory 30 s, all instrumental. Each piece has its own seed and a way of playing from fixed lists of the world (instruments, tempo, key). **Fitting the theme** (V01.1.017, Dominik 2026-10-24 "more to do with the themes … drums and basses welcome", after 2026-10-23 "nothing annoying or too fast"): every world has its own scene and instruments per mood, for example a fantasy tavern with lute, fiddle, frame drum and upright bass, a fantasy fight with war drums, deep bass and horns, a pirate fight with barrel drums, horror tension with a heartbeat bass. Drums and bass are everywhere, but with measure and never hectic: exploring 70 to 80, tavern 90 to 100, tension 65 to 80, fight 95 to 110, finale 85 to 100, vault 60 to 72 beats per minute. **Longer and soft** (V01.1.042, Dominik 2026-10-25 "at least triple the length", "smooth transition … maybe the same chord progression"): a piece now runs 3 to 6 minutes (a victory stays short). Every field has a fixed tempo (exploring 75, tavern 96, tension 72, fight 100, finale 92, victory 96, vault 66 beats per minute) and every world a fixed key (fantasy D minor, horror C minor, sci-fi A minor, pirates E minor; tavern and victory in the relative major), so pieces of the same mood following each other fit together harmonically. Every piece starts softly and fades out long, the crossfade takes 12 s. Because the pieces are longer, a round orders 2 instead of 4 pieces per field ahead, and the storage defaults to 2000 MB; if the set storage is too small for the number of pieces, the card "Music at the table" says so. Older pieces keep playing until their field has new ones. Pieces of an older style keep playing until their field has new ones (the table is never silent); their field orders new ones, the night fills up, and once a field is full the old ones go first. The description for the music model is made only of fixed words of `dungeon_musik.py`; actions, narration, scene, story or the name of an own world never go in. **The panel picks the mood:** fight (a fight at the table or fight words in the scene), then vault (the group stands in a random vault of the map), then a merchant or a settlement (tavern), then the story's last act (finale), then tension words in the scene, else the act's mood (word list over title, content and turning point; act 1 exploring, then tension). **Ahead:** every running round asks for 2 pieces each for the mood now, the act's, the next act's, fight and victory. Every new act orders 2 new pieces for the act after it, which replace the most played ones, so campaigns do not sound alike. At night (1 to 6 o'clock, not during a Notaus) and with "Prepare music now" the Spark fills every field up to the number. Only one piece is made at a time, on the place of the heavy jobs (`schwer.run("musik")`), with speech first, and only while no other heavy job waits. After 3 failures in a row the store rests for an hour. **Full:** per field the most played pieces go first (never the new one), and the store stays within the set storage (default 2400 MB). **At the table** a bar sits above the story: mood, piece x of n, music on/off, sounds on/off, volume and ⏭. Each device remembers its own choice. The music loops quietly (default 20 %) and fades over 12 s into the next piece of the same mood, never one of the last 3. A change of mood fades over at once. A new act or a new level plays a victory piece once, and the music gets quieter while the narration is read aloud. **Sounds** are made in the browser (WebAudio, no files): dice, door (door or gate in the narration), hit, healing, coins (loot, trade), spell, level and, quietly in the background, rain, wind, fire or water (word list over place and mood, else the world's own). **Dice:** on every throw the d20 rolls for 0.8 s and always stops on the panel's number (display only), with a bright sound on 20 and a dull one on 1; with "reduce motion" the spin is left out. Guests fetch pieces with their seat code (`POST /api/dungeon/musik`), profiles with `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/musik/{sid}`, only a seat of a running round, own rate limit `dungeonmusik`. Storage: `dungeon-musik.json` (setting, in the backup) and `dungeon-musik/` (pieces, stays local, can be made again). Live: pieces, MB, full fields, making now, waiting fields, made and failed today. Log: `dungeon: musik | …` with fixed words and numbers only.

**AI Dungeon Master: annoying piece (V01.1.017, Dominik 2026-10-24 "mark pieces as bad or annoying, players too, renewed at night", "everything as proposed"):** Next to ⏭ the music bar has a **👎** button for the piece that is playing, for every seat of a running round, guests too. One mark is enough: the piece leaves every table's list at once, the next one fades in, and its field no longer counts it, so a new one is ordered. At night (with the nightly fill) the Spark throws marked pieces away with their files and makes new ones. Each seat marks a piece only once; only the number of marks is kept, no name and no seat. Under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → Music at the table the list **"N marked"** shows each piece with world, mood and count; **Take back** lets it play again until the night. **▶ Listen** (V01.1.046, Dominik 2026-10-25 "listen to them to decide once more") plays the piece right there, the button turns into "■ Stop"; only one plays at a time, and listening does not count as played and keeps the mark. If a field fills up before that, marked pieces go first. Doors: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/musik/{sid}/nervt` (a profile with a seat) and `POST /api/dungeon/musik/nervt` (a guest with a seat code), own rate limit `dungeonnervt`; `POST /api/admin/dungeon/musik/{sid}/zurueck` and `GET /api/admin/dungeon/musik/{sid}` (listening, marked pieces too) for the admin only. Live: marked pieces and today's marks, log `dungeon: musik | nervt …` with fixed words only.

**AI Dungeon Master: bigger catalog from the SRD (V01.0.980, thread "DnDAppFiles für den Dungeon-Master", Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-6"):** Nothing was taken from the DnDAppFiles repo (no licence, copied word for word from the protected books). The data comes from the **System Reference Document 5.1 (SRD 5.1)** by Wizards of the Coast, licensed under **CC-BY-4.0**. It is shortened, converted to the game's values and translated into German. Own entries for horror, sci-fi and pirates come on top. Everything lives as the fixed file `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` in the repo, nothing is loaded at runtime. The base rules stand on their own: what the five old classes and their items get from the SRD (weapons, spells per level, new classes) is a `dazu` entry in the SRD file. The panel puts it as a layer between `dungeon_regeln.json` and the admin's rules (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules); the admin changes or removes any entry by its id as before (`"aus": true`). Contents: **212 foes** (142 from the SRD, 70 own) with level (challenge rating), life, armour class, attack bonus, damage dice, six values, experience, size, worlds and the conditions they cause; **254 items** (47 weapons, among them magic ones like Flame tongue or Vorpal sword with a fixed rarity, flintlock pistol and musket only for pirates, laser weapons only for sci-fi; 18 armours, 23 jewellery pieces with a fixed value like Gauntlets of ogre power, 19 potions, 85 spells from cantrip to level 5 with classes, 24 tools with keywords for the bonus on fitting checks, 38 gems and art objects to sell); **17 conditions** (blinded, charmed, deafened, frightened, grappled, incapacitated, invisible, paralysed, petrified, poisoned, prone, restrained, stunned, unconscious, exhausted, burning, bleeding) with fixed effects and rounds; the **difficulties** very easy 5, easy 10, medium 15, hard 20, very hard 25, nearly impossible 30; **15 backgrounds** (such as soldier, sage, sailor, smuggler, spacer, investigator) with a bonus and a starting item; **4 peoples** (dragonborn, gnome, half-elf, tiefling) and **7 classes** (barbarian, bard, druid, monk, paladin, sorcerer, warlock) with six abilities each. **Powers only when you have them** (Dominik 2026-10-23 "1-3"): each of the twelve classes has 1 to 2 starting abilities (`start_faehigkeiten`, e.g. rogue: ambush and picking locks, paladin: divine sense and lay on hands); the spellcasting classes (mage, sorcerer, warlock, healer, druid, bard, paladin, ranger) have a spell list per level (`zauber`, level 0 to 5, every spell carries its `grad`), warrior, rogue, barbarian and monk have none. Cantrips (level 0) work any number of times, from level 1 on charges per chapter by hero level count (`ladungen`, level 1: two charges of level 1, level 10: 4/3/3/3/2 as in the D&D table). The data is only data: rolls, damage and loot are still decided by the panel by its rules, the model only narrates. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: attribution in the file and in the docs, no address except the licence, no id shared with the base rules, `check_rules` on the merged rules empty, every weapon belongs to a class, every condition and starting item exists, every world has at least 15 foes from weak to strong. Attribution: *This work includes material taken from the System Reference Document 5.1 ("SRD 5.1") by Wizards of the Coast LLC and available at https://dnd.wizards.com/resources/systems-reference-document. The SRD 5.1 is licensed under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License available at https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode.*

**AI Dungeon Master in the car (points 8 to 13, V01.1.022, Dominik 2026-10-24 "implement everything as recommended. 2b with voice design for variety."):** everything for the car now lives in `dungeon_auto.py` (before in `dungeon.py`), the endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/auto` stays (`{text, nach, lang, rid, ok, neu, wahl}`, answer `{sagen, n, warten, aus, rid}`, plus `danach`, `ton`, `frage`, `weiter` when they fit). **Summary** (point 8): after the narration the panel says with fixed sentences what happened, from the log since the previous narration (rolls, hits, damage, healing, loot, level, down, unconscious, dead, up again, spells, victory; at most `EREIGNIS_MAX` = 8) and whose turn it is. **Asking back** (point 9): every action and every command that changes something (`TUN`) first comes back as "… Is that right?" (`frage`); only the same request with `ok` acts. **Commands** (point 10, `FRAGEN`, only up to 60 characters, else it is the action): fight status, equip, cast (only a spell from the own bag), pick up, potion to a friend, merchant with buy and sell, stat point, ability at a level up, the map's ways, the host's "Go on now". Every command goes through the same rules as at the table (`hero_act`, `act`, `maybe_close`); the panel finds names only among the own things, the wares of the open market and the seats of the round. **Choosing and creating a round** (point 11): `rid` picks an own round (only an own seat counts, else the newest), `neu` offers three stories (`WAHL`), `wahl` takes only stories the panel offered itself; after "yes" `make_round` creates the round with the first free hero, and the prologue starts. **Voices** (point 12, "2b"): with reading aloud on in the round, the car waits up to `TON_WARTEN` = 90 seconds for the round's pieces (narrator and NPC voices from Voice Design) and plays them (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/ton/{n}/{k}`, now also open for the iPhone app), else the Spark voice reads. **Waiting** (point 13): the iPhone app asks again without the 10-minute limit (4 seconds, after 2 minutes 10 seconds, up to 4 hours) and only while nothing else runs. Logs only fixed words: `dungeon: auto | anlegen | ok`, `dungeon: auto | kaufen | abgelehnt`, `dungeon: auto | neue runde | angeboten`, `dungeon: weiter | gastgeber | auto`. Status → Live counts the steps in the car as before. "Where can we go?" says the map's ways as "From here" at the table (`dungeon_karte.wege`, only what a seat may see). Still open: "Pause the round". Self-tests `test_dungeon_auto` (panel) and `test_carplay.DungeonInTheCar` (app).

**AI Dungeon Master: damage types and bosses in the SRD catalog (V01.1.025, combat part 2, data):** Each of the 212 foes carries its damage type (`schaden_art`: slashing, piercing, bludgeoning, fire, cold, lightning, acid, poison, necrotic, radiant, psychic, thunder or force, taken from the strongest attack in the SRD), plus, where the SRD names them, `resistenz` (half damage), `immun` (no damage) and `schwaeche` (double damage), e.g. skeleton: vulnerable to bludgeoning, ghost: immune to cold and poison. The game knows no magic weapons, so immunity to nonmagical slashing, piercing and bludgeoning counts as resistance (stone golem, werewolf), which keeps every foe beatable. `boss` marks foes with legendary actions or from level 10 (aboleth, stone golem, young red dragon, unicorn, archmage) and the own leaders xeno queen, rogue AI, pirate captain and puppeteer; the 70 own foes are set by hand (ghost pirate: resistant to slashing, piercing and bludgeoning, vulnerable to radiant). Each of the 47 weapons from the SRD has its damage type and `eigenschaften` (light, finesse, reach, thrown, heavy, versatile); spell foci deal force, lasers and plasma fire, light weapons radiant. What follows from this in combat is computed by the panel by fixed rules. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: types and properties only from these lists, no type in two lists at once, every SRD weapon with a damage type.

Stage M0: The Spark makes music itself with ACE-Step 1.5 (MIT licence), without the internet. The model only comes with the installation on the model computer: `sudo ./install.sh --musik 2b` (2B turbo with the 1.7B planner, recommended), `2b-klein` (0.6B planner, less memory) or `xl` (XL turbo, sounds better, needs more); the first install asks, the default is none. The engine is acestep.cpp (`ace-server`, pinned to one commit, built for the GB10 with CUDA, no torch and no Docker); the GGUF files are pinned by revision and SHA-256 (`musikmodell.py`). The service `speech-spark-musik` (`musik_server.py`, only `127.0.0.1:31004`, with a split install through the TLS-Tor, API key) loads the engine only for a song (`127.0.0.1:31014`, after checking the free memory) and stops it after 10 minutes without a song; the picture and music services share `bedarf.py` for it. A song takes two steps: the small planner sets structure and audio codes, then the sound model turns them into an MP3 (192 kbit/s). The service sets every field itself, takes only an MP3 back and writes its own ID3 mark into it ("KI-erzeugt mit ACE-Step 1.5"). The admin measures: State → Checks → "Test music" makes two fixed samples for every installed model (30 s of piano, 60 s of pop with German lyrics) and shows done in, planning, sound and memory at the peak, plus both samples to listen to. When the engine stops a sample, it says "engine error"; the details are in the log (area "Music"). State → Live: target "Making music" with the last measurement (numbers); service guard part "Making music"; maintenance pause part "Making music"; logs (area "Musik"): `musik: test gestartet`, `musik: test xl 1 ok 12.5 s`, `musik: gemacht 30 s instrumental 12.5 s spitze 8.1 gib`, `musik: engine gestartet 2.0 s`, `musik: engine beendet leerlauf`, `musik: engine nicht gestartet speicher 6.2 gib frei`. API (admin): `GET|POST /api/admin/musik-test`, `GET /api/admin/musik-test/{2b|2b-klein|xl}/{1|2}.mp3`.

**Heavy jobs: one model at a time (V01.0.776, plan `plaene/schwere-auftraege.md`, Dominik 2026-10-18 "only one model loaded at a time, never both"):** Painting pictures, changing pictures and making music share one place on the model computer: only the picture model or the music model is loaded, never both. A fixed rule without a switch, like Speech first. All jobs stand in one queue in the panel (`schwer.py`), "Bild testen" and "Test music" too. The order is by arrival, but while the loaded kind still waits it goes first, at most 3 times in a row while the other kind waits; so the panel seldom reloads and nobody is left behind. When another kind is next, the panel first ends the loaded model (`/v1/bild/stop` or `/v1/musik/stop`), else it stays loaded until its idle time as before. The services hold the rule themselves: whoever starts its engine first takes the lock `state/bedarf.lock` on the model computer; when the other service holds it, it answers 503 "belegt" (counter `refused_place` in `/health`). Too little memory no longer ends a job at once: it keeps its place, tries again every 30 seconds and only gives up after 10 minutes. Who waits behind others gets a fixed sentence from the panel, e.g. "Many requests are running right now, you are in the queue at place 2. It takes a moment, about a minute."; the panel estimates the time from the measured seconds per picture or song and the load time. The card in the chat and under *Me → Pictures* or *Me → Music* shows "Waiting · place 2 of 3 · about a minute" or "waiting for free memory"; when it is done the notice comes as before. At most 10 heavy jobs wait on the whole Spark, pictures and songs together. State → Live: under "In the background" every picture and song with "waiting for the place (one model at a time)" or "waiting for memory", at the targets "Painting pictures" and "Making music" "hat den Platz" or "wartet auf den Platz", the same in the control centre. Logs (area "Heavy jobs"): `schwer: wartet bild platz 2`, `schwer: dran musik nach 40 s`, `schwer: entladen bild wechsel`, `schwer: wartet musik memory naechster versuch in 30 s`, `schwer: abgesagt musik memory nach 600 s`; on the model computer `bild: engine nicht gestartet belegt`. The memory budget under State → Models counts pictures and music as one place (thread Own model installation).

**Messages in the conversation:** hovering over a message (on a phone: tapping it) shows a bin and, on answers, a speaker. The bin deletes the message, a question together with its answer. The speaker reads the answer out again.

**Timers and reminders:** "remind me about the oven in 10 minutes" or "remind me tomorrow at 8 about the appointment". Pending reminders show as small chips below the toggles (✕ cancels); "which timers are running?" and "cancel the oven timer" work by voice too. When one is due, the page chimes, shows it in the conversation and speaks it; if the tab is in the background, a browser notification appears as well. This works while the page is open. Reminders need a profile; they are kept on the Spark. Guests have none. Since V01.0.176 a profile's reminder rings only once: the first device that plays it (an open page, the iPhone app or push) takes it on the Spark, all others stay silent. While a page of the profile is open, push waits 20 seconds so the reminder does not ring there and arrive as a notification too. A reminder that was more than 10 minutes overdue when the page opens shows quietly in the conversation ("Missed"). The iPhone app without Apple push rings with its own alarms; if it is closed then, it reports that the next time it opens. At the same moment as an open page it can then still ring twice; with Apple push it does not. Can be turned off under Features.

**Reminders as notifications** (profiles only): in the "Me" window under "Conversation", "Reminders on this device → Switch on" turns on push notifications. A reminder then arrives also when the page is closed, and only on the devices of that profile. This needs https with a valid certificate (e.g. behind a reverse proxy); on an iPhone first add the page to the home screen ("Share → Add to Home Screen"). The Spark only sends to the browsers' push services (Apple, Google, Mozilla, Microsoft), encrypted. Since V01.0.181 every notification (reminder, "speaking up" note, morning briefing, agent report) arrives once: on the device the profile used last (an open page, the iPhone app or Telegram, at most 12 hours back), else the iPhone app, else Telegram, else the browser set up last. Private notes go over Telegram only when the profile allowed personal data there.

**Notes to the device in use (V01.0.864, plan `benachrichtigung-geraet`, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ok", stage 1; `zustellung.py`, admin switch Features → Timers and reminders → "Notes to the device in use", `chat.note_device`, off):** "Used last" meant until now: some time in the last 12 hours, also a page left open in the background, never the iPhone app. With the switch every device only tells the Spark how long ago its last input was: an open page and the Android app every minute while in front, at once on the first input after a pause, and "away" when they go to the background (`POST /api/zustellung/da`, only a number and a state); the iPhone app when opened, every minute in front and "away" in the background; the Spark companion with its poll every 30 seconds the computer's idle time (Windows `GetLastInputInfo`, Mac `CGEventSource`). A note then goes to the device with the last input within the last 2 minutes (the device's word no older than 90 seconds), else at once to the phone app (iPhone, else Android), without a phone app as before. A way the profile chose for a kind of note (service watch) still holds. Kept in memory only, nothing is stored. Logs: `push: route aktiv|handy <way>`; Live: the connection "Gerade benutzte Geräte" with the count and the notes routed so today. Self-test `test_zustellung`. Urgent notes, one quiet time for everything and Me → Notifications follow in stages 3 and 4.

**Tapping a note opens exactly that note (V01.0.960, Dominik 2026-10-23 "nach Empfehlung umsetzen"; `mitteilungen.py`, no switch, a fix):** until now tapping a notification only opened the assistant with an empty conversation. Now every note to the iPhone app, the Android app, the companion and the browser stays on the Spark for a day with a random number (in memory only, at most 30 per profile); only that number goes along (through Apple, as before, only "New message from the Spark"). Tapping opens the conversation and shows the note as a card above it: title, the whole text, the time and one button the Spark picks by fixed rules from the kind of note, never the model: a note about something open shows its card ("View and handle"), a chat "Open chat", a new mail "Read mail", a confirmation code "Copy code", a way in the text ("Me → Music") "Open: …" as a jump link; else only the text. The cross closes the card. Only the own profile gets its note back (`GET /api/profile/mitteilung`, the app `GET /api/iphone/note?tapped=1`, with a login and a rate limit); older than a day or after a restart of the Spark, the card says so. Browser: through the service worker (`#meldung=<number>`), Android from 1.4.4, the companion on the Mac on a click on the note (Windows shows it in the Meldungen tab as before). Logs: `push: mitteilung geoeffnet <kind> <way>` or `nicht mehr da`, never the text; Live under events "Mitteilung". Self-test `test_mitteilungen`.

**Following up and "read everywhere" (V01.0.877, stage 2, same switch):** `push.send` remembers every note (title and text in memory only, 6 hours at most). If it went to a device other than the phone app and the Spark chose the way itself, it comes once to the phone after 5 minutes (`zustellung.follow`, every minute), an urgent kind (`vorgaenge.ARTEN` "dringend", the one list; without a Vorgang a reminder counts as kind "erinnerung") after 2 minutes; never a third time. Not when the device was touched afterwards (its signal from stage 1) or its Vorgang is no longer open. Notes about a Vorgang carry its number (`vg`, to Apple only the number): a tapped note says `gesehen` with the signal (5 numbers at most, `vorgaenge.seen`), the answer `zu` names the Vorgänge no longer open; pages close their browser notifications, the iPhone app takes them out of the notification centre, the companion and the Android app no longer fetch them. Logs: `push: nachgefasst <way> → <phone>`, `push: nachfassen nicht noetig, gesehen`, `push: nachfassen ohne handy`, `push: gesehen <n>`; Live: followed up and waiting as numbers.

**Daily briefing as a notification** (profiles only): below that, each profile sets a time under "Tagesbriefing als Mitteilung" (daily briefing as notification). Every morning at that time a short briefing (appointments, reminders, unread mail, your topics) then goes to all devices of the profile with notifications on, and it is also kept in the history. "Aus" (off) turns it off.

**Daily briefing and calendar:** say "good morning" or "what's on today?" and the assistant reads out today's and tomorrow's appointments, today's reminders and a few lines on your own topics (e.g. "weather Berlin", via your SearXNG). "What do I have on Friday?" works too. Each profile connects one or more calendars (up to 8) on the page Calendar, tab Settings; an appointment found in two calendars is read out once. Each one is a CalDAV address with user and app password (iCloud: https://caldav.icloud.com with the Apple ID and an app-specific password from appleid.apple.com; Nextcloud: …/remote.php/dav; Radicale …), or a subscription link with webcal://, webcals:// or https:// (Google: "secret address in iCal format", a public iCloud calendar). New appointments: "put dentist on Tuesday 10 am in my calendar" makes the assistant read out a proposal; only when you answer with a plain yes in the same conversation does the panel itself write it to the first CalDAV calendar (optionally with an alarm). Subscription links stay read only. The password stays on the Spark and is never shown again. Guests get no calendar. Can be turned off under Features.

**Changing appointments on the page (V01.0.812, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` stage 2):** on the Calendar page "+ Appointment" adds one (title, all day or from/to, place, with several calendars the choice), the details of an appointment hold "Change" and "Delete" (delete asks first). "Save" is the person's own yes; the panel checks as for the assistant: the Notaus, only own CalDAV calendars (never a subscription link), never a repeating appointment, and changing or deleting with the server's etag (If-Match), so a change made elsewhere is never overwritten. It finds exactly the appointment the page shows (calendar, title and start); if it is no longer there like that, the answer is "changed or repeating" (409). Into a calendar shared with "See and add" the page adds only when it is chosen; changing and deleting stay with the owner. Endpoints `POST` and `PUT /api/profile/calendar/event` (own browser login only, rate limit `kalenderaendern`); Live shows at the calendar only how many appointments were changed on the page today; log `calendar: appointment saved|changed|deleted` or `page change refused, not found`.

**Sharing calendars (V01.0.811, Dominik 2026-10-18 "1a 2a"):** Features → Calendar and daily briefing → "Kalender teilen" (off by default; once released it applies to every profile at once, only the admin blocks single profiles; never guests). Under Calendar → Settings every connected calendar has "Share": for each other profile "Not shared", "See only" or "See and add", with the own code (level Confirm, `PUT /api/profile/calendar/{id}/share`, rate limit `kalenderteilen`); "Not shared" takes it back at once. The other profile sees the calendar on its Calendar page in its own colour as "Name · Calendar" and under Settings in "Shared with you"; its assistant reads it with `calendar_events` ("Was hat Sabine morgen?", "Hat Tom am Freitag Zeit?", the Weiche sends such questions to the calendar). It is read with the owner's access, address and password stay in the owner's file. A private appointment (CLASS PRIVATE or CONFIDENTIAL) shows at the other profile only as "belegt" with its time, without title and place. With "See and add" the other profile's assistant enters an appointment there only when the calendar is named ("put … in Sabine's calendar") and only after that profile's yes; changing, deleting and undo stay with the owner. The daily briefing, Proactive and Today show only the own calendars. Live shows at the calendar only how many shares there are from how many profiles. Log: `calendar: share set see|add`, `share taken back`, `share refused`.

**Page Calendar (V01.0.805, Dominik 2026-10-18, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` 1a 4a):** the calendar is a page of its own in the menu (on the left below "Assistant", on phones through the card "Appointments" under Today), no longer a page under Me. Tab **Appointments**: day, week, month or list, paging with ‹ Today ›; all-day appointments on top, overlapping ones side by side; each calendar has its colour; a tap shows time, place and calendar. A calendar that does not answer is named on top as "Not reached"; one still loading after 8 seconds is named "Still loading" and appears by itself once it is there (see the cache below). Tab **Settings**: everything that was on the Me page "Calendar" before (connect and remove calendars, topics for the briefing, "Show next appointments"). Every way "Calendar → Settings → …" in help, hints and apps leads there; old links to `#me=calbox` open the page. Read only through `GET /api/profile/calendar/view` (own profile, at most 42 days, rate limit); titles and places reach the page only as text. Changing appointments on the page comes in stage 2; until then through the assistant ("Move the appointment …").

**Calendar fast: cache and background refresh (V01.0.817, Dominik 2026-10-19 "calendar slow, caching for entries and background refresh"):** The Spark keeps the appointments of each calendar month by month in the panel's memory (`calendars.py`), never on disk. Page, assistant, briefing and "On its own" read the same months; a month is fetched once even when several ask at the same time. Where an address's calendars are (CalDAV discovery, several requests at iCloud) is kept for 6 hours; after that every fetch goes straight to the calendars. The page waits at most 8 seconds: whatever is still loading then keeps loading in the background and lands in the cache, and the page asks again by itself after 3 seconds (at most 5 times). Before, the fetch was cancelled after 8 seconds and nothing was kept, hence "3 of 3 calendars slow" on every opening. A month older than 5 minutes is shown on the page at once and fetched anew behind it (up to 24 hours old); the assistant waits for a fresh one. Every 5 minutes the panel fetches this and the next month anew for every profile that read its appointments in the last 24 hours. When an appointment or a setting changes, the cache is dropped at once, and a fetch begun before stores nothing. A big calendar is parsed outside the event loop so the panel does not stall. No switch: it is the existing calendar, only faster. Logs: `calendar: view loading | n of m calendars`, `calendar: kept fresh | n calendars of m profiles`, `calendar: refresh failed (error)`. The uConsole (`spark-tui kalender`) shows "Lädt noch: …".

**Calendar without panel stalls (V01.0.838, Dominik 2026-10-19, the panel stood still for 6-8 seconds):** working out the appointments from the calendar texts (expanding repetitions) is pure Python and ran anew for every look, every minute check and every refresh, for every calendar at once; two such runs held the panel so often that pages hung for seconds. Now the Spark works out each appointment once per calendar month and keeps the result in memory (at most 30,000 entries, never on disk); only a changed appointment or a new month is computed again. All of it runs in one background thread at low priority, never two at once, and lets the panel go after every appointment.

**Calendar renewed: full width, floating card, quick add, drag, share window (V01.0.821, Dominik 2026-10-19 "kalender: 1a 2a 3a … 7a", "ok"):** the Calendar page uses the whole width, the column on the right is gone. A tap on an appointment opens a card floating beside it over the view (on a phone a sheet from below): calendar, title, when, where and, for own CalDAV appointments, "Change", "+1 day", "+1 week" and "Delete". Esc closes it, E changes, Del deletes (after asking). Above the view sits "Quick add": the panel reads a line like "Zahnarzt morgen 10 Uhr bei Dr. Weber" by fixed rules, without the language model (`calendars.quick`, `GET /api/profile/calendar/quick`, reads only): day (heute, morgen, übermorgen, today, tomorrow, a weekday, "nächsten Montag", "in 3 Tagen", 24.12. or 24.12.2026), time (10 Uhr, 10:30, 14-15 Uhr, 3pm), place after "bei", "in" or "im", the rest is the title. Without a time the appointment is all day, without a day it is today or, when the time has passed, tomorrow; short weekdays (Mo, So) only count as "am So" or "So.". What was understood shows as chips below; it is saved only with "Add" or Enter, into the first own calendar, the same way as the form. "More …" opens the form already filled in. In week and day own appointments are dragged with the mouse to another time (quarter hours) or day; on a phone "+1 day" and "+1 week" move them, since the finger scrolls the view. After every move "Undo" stays above the view for a minute. Under Calendar → Settings every calendar is a card with its kind (CalDAV, iCloud, subscription) and "Shared with …"; the address and "Remove" are under "⋯". "Share" opens a window with one row per profile and Not · See · See + add; the search filters the list while typing, those with access are on top, "Save" sends only the changes (each with the code as before). "Shared with you" is a section of its own. The briefing topics are chips with ✕, a new one is added with Enter and saved at once (at most three). No new switch.

**Sharing only the chosen calendar (V01.0.823, code review C2):** an address can hold several calendars (an iCloud or Nextcloud account). Until now "Share" released every calendar of the account. Now a share holds exactly one of them: with several, the share window asks "Which calendar of this account" (`GET /api/profile/calendar/{id}/parts`, reads only), the server asks for the calendars anew when saving and refuses a share without the choice. Per person `share_parts` is stored (profile → the calendar's address); the other person reads and adds only there (`calendars._only`). An older share of such an account without that choice shows nothing until it is shared again; addresses with a single calendar stay as they are.

**Page E-mail (V01.0.809, Dominik 2026-10-18, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` 2a 4a):** mail is a page of its own in the menu (on the left below "Calendar", only with reading e-mail; on phones through Today), no longer under Me. Tab **Inbox**: the newest 50 messages of the last 14 days from every mailbox, unread ones bold with a dot, search by sender or subject, "Unread only"; a tap shows the message as plain text (HTML becomes text, pictures and links from outside stay off, long links become "[Link]"), attachments only by name. It is read with PEEK, so the message stays unread. Tab **For you** (only when "For you from mail" is released): what was found in the mails. Tab **Settings**: everything that was on the Me pages "E-mail" and "Parcels" (mailboxes, tidying, mail style, parcels). Every way "E-mail → Settings → …" jumps there; old links to `#me=mailbox` open the page. Reading goes only through `GET /api/profile/mail/inbox` and `GET /api/profile/mail/message` (own profile, rate limit "post", log without contents). Replying, moving and sending come in stages 2 and 3; until then through the assistant.

**E-mail fast and replying below the mail (V01.0.819, Dominik 2026-10-19 "mail must be cached too", "the windows should react at once and refresh in the background", "reply right below the selected mail"):** All mailboxes of a profile are read at the same time instead of one after the other. The list of the E-mail page lives in the panel's memory (`mail.py`, never on disk): the page shows the last state at once (up to 24 hours old) and fetches anew behind it when it is older than 2 minutes. Without a kept state it waits at most 8 seconds, then shows "The mailboxes are still loading …" and asks again by itself after 3 seconds (at most 5 times); the fetch goes on in the background. Several requests for the same list at once start one fetch; the assistant's shorter list ("unread mail of the week") comes from the page's longer one when that holds all of it. Opened messages stay in memory for 30 minutes (at most 40 per profile), and the panel fetches the 10 newest ahead in the background (with PEEK, they stay unread). Every 5 minutes the panel fetches the list anew for every profile that read its mail on the page or through the assistant in the last 24 hours (tidying and "For you" do not count). Read/unread, archive and move change the kept list at once without a new fetch; a fetch begun before stores nothing. No switch. "Reply" opens the reply form right below the mail, as in a mail program; the mail stays above it, preview and send as before. The tab "For you" reads all mailboxes at the same time, shows the last reading at once (up to 24 hours old) and reads one older than 10 minutes anew behind it; the first reading waits at most 8 seconds, then "Reading your mails …" and the page asks again by itself (log `fuerdich: laedt noch`). Fixed: after a visit to the tab "Settings" the pages E-mail and Calendar stayed empty on the next opening, because drawing them anew took the settings out of the page. Logs: `mail: inbox loading | n mailboxes`, `mail: kept fresh | n profiles, m messages ahead`, `mail: refresh failed (error)`.

**E-mail like a mail client (V01.0.830, Dominik 2026-10-19 "Email 4a 5a"):** the E-mail page uses the whole width: the list on the left, grouped by day (today, yesterday, weekday with date), each row with the sender's initials in a colour of their own; a wide reading pane on the right, on a phone the mail instead of the list as before. When "For you" found something in a mail (bill, contract, suspicious …), a small chip says so in the row (from its cached reading, only with "For you from mails" on). Keys on a computer: J and K go through the mails, Esc closes, R replies (with "Send mail"), E archives and U toggles read/unread (with "Order mail", like the buttons, with "Undo"). On a phone a row is swiped left to archive, right for read/unread (with "Order mail"). "As appointment" below a mail opens the calendar with the subject in "Quick add"; the panel reads it there by its fixed rules, it is added only with "Add". No new switch, no new endpoint.

**Always a way back (V01.0.833, Dominik 2026-10-19):** an open mail, an answer and a new mail have a visible button at every width: on the phone "‹ Back" or "‹ Discard", on the computer beside the list "✕ Close" or "✕ Discard". Esc closes the answer even from inside the text field. When text was typed, the Spark first asks "Discard the draft?".

**Suggested answers (V01.0.837, Dominik 2026-10-19 "1a 2a 3a 4a 5b"):** whoever answers a mail on the E-mail page or in the iPhone app finds three short answers in different directions (e.g. accept, decline, ask back), in the mail's language and form of address, signed with the profile's name. The Spark prepares them while the mail is open and keeps them 15 minutes in memory (never on the disk), so "Reply" mostly finds them at once. A tap puts a suggestion into the text, where it stays free to change; "shorter", "friendlier", "more formal" and "in English" rework the text with the Schreibhilfe's tasks. It is still sent only with "Send" in the preview. The suggestions belong to "Send mail" (no own switch). Only the own language model reads the mail, behind speech; the mail is data, never a command (between fixed marks, the answer is only taken as text, at most three, cut). The log writes "mail: suggestions | 3 in 2.1 s" or "mail: rewrite kuerzer", Status → Live shows today's number at the E-mail box.

**Warming up (V01.0.830, Dominik 2026-10-19 "1a 3a 4a 5a 7a 9"):** when a profile signs in or opens the panel or the app, the Spark reads ahead in the background what its pages will need: this and the next month from all its calendars (shared ones too), the e-mail listing with the newest messages, "For you" and the address book. The pages Calendar, E-mail and Today then show at once what is there; Today takes the calendar from the same memory as the page Calendar. From then on the known refreshes every 5 minutes keep it fresh. Rules: only functions the profile may use now, never for guests; a profile at most every 10 minutes; at most two profiles at the same time; every step waits while somebody speaks (voice first). Everything stays in memory, nothing new on the disk. Status → Live shows for Calendar and Mail only how many profiles are warm and how long ago; the log (area Priority) writes one line "vorwaermen: done" with numbers.

**Per-profile store, stage A (V01.0.879, Dominik 2026-10-20 "build all points", plan `vorrat-je-profil/plan.md`; `vorrat.py`, no switch):** When the person asks the assistant about appointments, weather or parcels, the tool takes the data from its service's memory while it is fresh enough (calendar 10 minutes, weather 30, parcels 15; `vorrat.DIENSTE`). When the reading is older (up to 24 hours, weather and parcels up to 6), the service asks the server anew and the answer waits at most 2 seconds for it; when nothing comes by then or the server does not answer, the assistant names the older reading with its time ("as of 14:05"), and the new one lands in memory for the next question. "Look again", "refresh" or "reload" always ask the server. Before, the assistant only took calendar blocks younger than 2 minutes although the refresh runs every 2 to 5 minutes, and waited for weather and parcels on every first question. Mail follows the same rule with the encrypted mail store. Pages and background work keep their rules. It applies only to functions the profile may use anyway, never to guests, and only changes when data is read, not what. Measuring: every spoken answer keeps how long the tools waited and, per service, whether the data came from the store, as an older reading or after waiting (fixed words only). Status → Checks shows it under "Per service: store, last 7 days" and adds the column "Tools wait" per kind of question; Status → Live shows for Calendar, E-mail, Weather and Parcels "today n questions, x % from the store". The log (area Priority) writes "vorrat: older reading" with the service and the age in minutes, never a profile or content.

**Per-profile store, stages B and C (V01.0.879; `vorrat.py`, `halive.py`, no switch):** One round every minute (`vorrat.loop`) replaces the calendar's own loop: it compares the calendars every 2 minutes as before and, for every profile active in the last 24 hours, refreshes what is older than its fresh age: the weather at home, parcels and "For you", the latter as soon as a mail is newer than its last reading. At most two profiles at the same time, each step waits while somebody speaks, only released functions, never guests; mail keeps its own round with the mail store; its times for the assistant (10 minutes fresh, 2 seconds wait) come from `vorrat.DIENSTE`. Weather (only at the own place) and parcels also keep their last reading sealed in the profile folder (`wetter-vorrat.bin`, `pakete-vorrat.bin`, through `vorrat.Vorrat` like the mail store, written at most every 30 or 5 minutes): after a restart the assistant answers the first question from it at once, with the time it was read when older. Parcels only from mailboxes read completely; deleted with the last mailbox, for the weather with the place, for both after 14 days without a reading and with the profile. Another place asked for once stays in memory only. Warming up now also reads weather and parcels at sign-in, and a device in use right now (signal of "Note to the device in use") warms the profile too (at most every 10 minutes). The daily briefing is written 10 minutes before its time and sent on the minute; calendar, mail, weather and parcels are then already in memory for the morning's questions (a text written ahead counts for 20 minutes). Home Assistant live: for active profiles with Home Assistant released the Spark keeps one WebSocket connection to their own Home Assistant (at most 4 on the whole Spark). It asks for all states once and then only listens for changes (state_changed); "Is the window open?", states, history and finding a device to switch use this copy instead of loading all states over HTTP every time. The connection only reads, goes through the guard (home network rules, checked address), uses TLS as set, ends with My kill switch and a block; a lost connection is tried again after 1 minute (up to 15 minutes). At most 5000 devices and 8 MB per message, nothing on disk. Status → Live: at Calendar "Vorrat: today n rounds, m services refreshed", at Home Assistant "live: n connections, today m changes", at Weather and Parcels "versiegelt bei n Profilen" (sealed for n profiles); the log writes "vorrat: kept fresh" and "homeassistant: live connected | n entities" with numbers and the profile as a short name only.

**Up to date (V01.0.891, Dominik 2026-10-21 "always check that all information is current", plan `tagesaktuell/plan.md`; `frisch.py`, no new switch):** Fixed rules of the panel hold every source against today, never the model. Found: the weather took the first day of the kept forecast as "today", which can be yesterday after midnight; the morning briefing named unread mails of the last two days without a date; "as of 18:00" never said the day; the web search asked without a period and passed on no date of its hits. Now: a stand always names its day ("as of heute 06:40", "as of gestern 18:00", `frisch.stand`), for calendar, weather, parcels and mail. The weather works out "today" and "tomorrow" from the clock at the place; when today is not in the forecast it says so, and "Jetzt …" (now) comes only from a reading of the last 2 hours. The morning briefing names every mail with its day and time ("heute 06:12", "gestern 21:40") and only what was still unread when it was read; a mail listing from the Spark is at most 24 hours old. When the person asks for today's or the latest (fixed words like heute, aktuell, neueste, Nachrichten, today, latest, news), SearXNG searches only the last day (for "gestern", "diese Woche" the last week), for news also in its news category; every hit carries its date ("Datum: gestern 21:00", "Datum: unbekannt"), older ones are dropped for such questions. The briefing's topics always search the day's news. When the SearXNG knows no period, it searches once without and the dates decide. When nothing of today is found, the result says so with the date of the newest hit. When a service came only with an older reading or a search for today found nothing of today, and the answer does not say so itself, the panel adds "Nicht ganz aktuell: Wetter: Stand gestern 23:10 Uhr." (not quite current; a rule of the answer check, off with it); the daily briefing's push gets the same sentence. A mail opened in the Spark counts as read at once like in Apple Mail (computer and iPhone, only when "Order mail" is released; it sets the "read" flag on the mail server). Status → Live shows at Web search "tagesaktuell: today n searches with a period, m without a hit of today, k old hits dropped, j stand notes"; the log (area Chat) writes "frisch: search dropped", "frisch: search without a current hit", "frisch: hint said", "frisch: weather …" with fixed words and numbers only.

**New mail at once (V01.0.852, warming up stage C, Dominik 2026-10-19 "6a"; `mailidle.py`, admin switch Features → Reading e-mail → "Neue Mails sofort", `chat.mail_idle`, off):** for profiles that read their mail in the last two hours the Spark keeps a connection to the inbox open (IMAP IDLE). When the server reports a new mail (EXISTS) or one another program moved (EXPUNGE), the profile's mail rules run at once (with "Mails ordnen"), the E-mail page's list is fetched anew with the newest mails ahead, and "Für dich" hears of it (`mailidle.on_new`); at most every 20 seconds per profile, a change in between comes right after, and never while someone speaks. At most 4 connections on the whole Spark, the newest readers first; a server without IDLE stays with the fetch every five minutes (`mail.keep_fresh`, asked again after a day). The connection only reads (EXAMINE), each waiting one sits in `select()` on its socket and holds neither the panel's loop nor a lock; IDLE is renewed every 24 minutes, a lost connection is tried again after 1 minute (up to 15 minutes). From the server only the words EXISTS, EXPUNGE, BYE and the answer to DONE count; over-long lines end the connection. My emergency stop, the admin's block and the switch close it. Live (node E-mail): open connections and new mails today, numbers only; the log names only fixed words and the profile as a short name.

**Mail supply (V01.0.872, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ja, so umsetzen"; `mailvorrat.py` on `vorrat.py`, part of "Read mail", no switch of its own):** the Spark keeps the newest headers of each mailbox (sender, subject, time, read and flag marks; at most 300 per mailbox from the last 30 days) and up to 40 mail texts per profile (the ones fetched ahead and the opened ones, each at most 4000 characters of plain text, never attachments, HTML or pictures) in the profile folder (`mail-vorrat.bin`). The file is sealed with AES-GCM, bound to profile and name, and written at most every 5 minutes; without a key everything stays in memory only. It is filled only from what the Spark reads anyway (the page's listing, a longer listing every 30 minutes, the texts fetched ahead), never with a connection of its own. When a mailbox does not answer, the assistant, the briefing and the page answer from the supply ("For you" and the deadlines use their last reading) with its time ("as read on 20.10. at 14:05") instead of "not reachable", and a mail opens from the kept text. After a restart the Mail page shows the listing at once from the supply and fetches it anew behind. For the assistant: a listing up to 10 minutes old is taken as it is, an older one is fetched anew with at most 2 seconds of waiting, otherwise it answers with the older state and says the time. Ordering on the page follows in the supply (read, flagged, archived, deleted). The supply goes with the mailbox, with all mailboxes, with the profile and after 14 days in which nobody read the mail. `vorrat.py` is the one way to build such supplies for other services. Live (node Mail): profiles with a supply and how often it answered today, numbers only. **Folders at once and folder menu (V01.0.893, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1" and "better integrated"):** the page's folder list (names, what for, unread) is in the mail supply too; after a restart "Mailboxes" is there at once and read anew behind (at most 7 days old), a mailbox that does not answer keeps its older list; a new, renamed or removed folder drops it. The "⋯" at an own folder (or a right click) opens a small menu right beside it, like Apple Mail: Rename, New folder inside, Remove folder (empty ones only); on a phone it comes as a sheet from below, Esc or a click beside it closes it, nothing below moves. Renaming and new folders are typed right in the row (Enter keeps, Esc drops). On the iPhone the same with a long press or a swipe, each with its symbol. **Moving without reloading (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "always reloads the whole folder list"):** a moved, archived or deleted mail leaves the list at once, the folder list stays and only counts its unread numbers itself; the Spark reads the folders anew quietly behind (a list already shown is never replaced by an older one). When the Spark says no, the mail comes back with the reason. On the iPhone the list stays the same way, older pages loaded too.

**Load older mail (V01.0.887, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1a 2a 5a 6a"; `mail.more`, part of "Read mail", no switch of its own):** at the end of the list on the Mail page the Spark fetches the next 50 older mails, in the inbox and in every folder, with no limit of days down to the mailbox's first mail. A placeholder of fixed height at the end of the list keeps anything from jumping; at the end it says "No older mail". A search goes over the whole mailbox: the mail server searches sender, subject and text (umlauts too, `CHARSET UTF-8`), page by page like the list; `has:attachment` is checked by the Spark on the header. Where it goes on is the mark `vor` (per mailbox the UID below which the next page starts); it names only the profile's own mailboxes, anything else is refused. Each mailbox goes on in its own order and the mailboxes are merged by date, so no mail falls out; mails with a new number but an old date (moved back from a folder, the sender's clock off) come with the second page. It is fetched straight from the mailbox (read only, headers), nothing of it goes onto the disk or into the mail supply; the pages stay 10 minutes in memory, at most 10 per profile, and go as soon as a mail is ordered on the page. Own rate limit (30 pages a minute). `spark-tui mails --anzahl N` shows up to 200 mails and loads them for it, `spark-tui mail N` opens any of them. Live (node Mail): older pages today, the number only.

**Spark calendar, stage 1: a copy on the Spark (V01.0.830, Dominik 2026-10-19 "1a 2a"):** every CalDAV calendar (iCloud, Nextcloud, …) now has a copy on the Spark. The first time the Spark fetches it whole, after that it asks the server only what changed (WebDAV sync with a sync token; a server without it is compared by the tags of its appointments, one that can do neither is read month by month as before) and fetches just those. The Calendar page, Today, the assistant and the briefing read from the copy in milliseconds instead of 1 to 8 seconds. For profiles active in the last 24 hours the Spark compares every 2 minutes in the background; after an own change (adding, moving, deleting) right at the next look. The copy is on the disk only encrypted (`kalender-spiegel/`, the same key as for the passwords), at most 5000 appointments per calendar, and is deleted with the calendar or the profile. Subscription links (`.ics`, `webcal://`) stay as before. Status → Live shows for Calendar how many calendars have a copy, how many appointments and when they were last compared; the log writes "calendar: mirror full" or "changed" with numbers.

**Spark calendar, stage 2: changes at once (V01.0.832, Dominik 2026-10-19 "3a"):** what you add, change or delete on the Calendar page or in the iPhone app is in the copy on the Spark at once and shows without waiting; it goes to the calendar server (iCloud, Nextcloud, …) behind it. To change an appointment the Spark takes it and its tag from the copy instead of the server first. If the server refuses (changed elsewhere meanwhile, not reachable), the Spark takes the change back in the copy, the server's version stands, and the page shows "Not saved, the calendar refused" with the title for 10 minutes. What the assistant changes by voice still waits for the server, because a spoken confirmation cannot be taken back. The log writes "calendar: saved behind" or "not saved behind" with the status.

**Spark calendar, stage 3: a calendar without an account (V01.0.839, Dominik 2026-10-19 "5a"):** under Calendar → Settings → "Add calendar" → provider "Spark calendar" (in the iPhone: Connect accounts → Calendar → "Create a Spark calendar") you create a calendar that lives only on the Spark, without iCloud, Google or Nextcloud. You add appointments on the Calendar page, in the iPhone app or by voice ("Put dentist Tuesday 10 am into Zuhause"); Today, the briefing, changing, deleting, "undo that" and calendar sharing work as with any other calendar. Technically it is a calendar entry with the address `https://spark.invalid/<id>/`, which never reaches the network: `sparkkalender.py` answers the panel's CalDAV requests itself, bound to exactly this entry of this profile. It is stored only encrypted in the profile folder (`users/<id>/spark-kalender.json`); without the Spark's key none is created. It moves with the profile and is part of its backup; removing it (after a question) deletes its appointments. What goes in is checked: iCalendar with an appointment, at most 256 KB per appointment and 5000 appointments per calendar. `spark.invalid` cannot be entered as an address. Logs: `calendar: spark calendar made`, `calendar: spark calendar deleted | count`; Live (Calendar) counts Spark calendars and appointments. Stage 4 makes it reachable as a CalDAV server for the iPhone's calendar app.

**Spark calendar, stage 4: the Spark as a calendar server (V01.0.840, Dominik 2026-10-19 "1a-5a"):** a profile's Spark calendars show in the iPhone's calendar (or in Thunderbird, DAVx5) and can be changed there; the Spark is a CalDAV server for them under `/caldav/` (`kalenderserver.py`). Admin switch Features → Calendar and daily briefing → "Calendar server for calendar apps" (`chat.calendar_server`, off). The profile makes an app password under Calendar → Settings → "For calendar apps" (or on the iPhone under Connect accounts), with its code; it is shown once, the Spark keeps only a check value (`users/<id>/kalender-server.json`). On the iPhone under Settings, Apps, Calendar, Calendar accounts add a CalDAV account: server `https://<address>/caldav/`, user = the profile id shown, password = the app password; the calendar app finds the rest itself (`/.well-known/caldav`). It works at home and through the proxy. Protection: only the own Spark calendars under the own path (other paths and calendars are 404), wrong passwords lock like a PIN, every request counts against the rate limit, a request from a foreign web page (Origin) is refused, Mein Notaus and the admin's block close the door, every appointment is checked as in stage 3 (iCalendar, size, count, etags). Name and colour stay on the Spark (PROPPATCH and MKCALENDAR are refused). "Delete access" ends it at once, a new app password replaces the old one. Logs: `calendar: caldav saved|deleted|not saved (status)`, `calendar: caldav login refused`, `calendar: caldav password made|removed`; Live (Calendar) shows profiles with an app password and the day's requests, changes and refusals.

**Appointments: changeable or read only, with their text (V01.0.853, Dominik 2026-10-20):** on the Calendar page an appointment the page cannot change carries a 🔒 in the week, the day and the list; its card says why, in fixed words from the panel (`calendars._why`): a subscription link (iCal), a calendar someone shared with you, a repeating appointment (change the series in the calendar app) or a calendar that cannot be changed here. The card now also shows the appointment's text (DESCRIPTION, "Note") and its link (URL), both as text only: at most 2000 characters, no control characters, a link only when it starts with http or https, and it never opens by itself; long notes show 8 lines and "Read on". A private appointment from a shared calendar stays "belegt" (busy), without text or link. The same in the iPhone app (lock in the list, section "Note", the reason in the card).

**Order mail (V01.0.816, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` stage 2; Features → Reading e-mail → "Mails ordnen", `chat.mail_manage`, off):** an opened mail shows "Read"/"Unread", "Archive" and a folder choice; after archiving or moving, "Undo" brings the mail straight back into the inbox (found by its Message-ID). The button is the person's own yes. `postfach.py` knows only these four things: the `\Seen` flag of one inbox mail, one mail into the archive (the folder flagged `\Archive`, else "Archive"/"Archiv") or into an ordinary own folder, and back. Never into trash, junk, sent or drafts, never deleted, never a folder created or renamed; without IMAP MOVE only COPY and then UID EXPUNGE of exactly the confirmed original (as with tidying). Notaus holds every action off. Endpoints `GET /api/profile/mail/folders` and `POST /api/profile/mail/act` (own browser login only, own mailboxes only, rate limit `postordnen`); Live shows at mail only how many mails were ordered on the page today; log `mail: page <action> done|refused, not found|refused, notaus|failed`, never sender, subject or folder.

**E-mail as a full mail client, stage A: folders (V01.0.843, Dominik 2026-10-19 "1a 4a 5a", plan `kalender-mail/mailclient/plan.md`; part of "Mails ordnen", off):** above the list stands "📁 Inbox ▾". It opens, in the list's place (no scrollbar of its own), every folder of every mailbox: inbox, drafts, sent, archive, own folders indented, junk and trash, each with its number of unread mails. A tap opens the folder like the inbox (reading only: `EXAMINE`, `BODY.PEEK`, the newest 50; search and "Unread only" work on this list). "+ New folder" makes a folder at the top of the mailbox, "⋯" on an own folder offers "Rename", "Folder inside" and "Remove (empty only)". Folders of the mail program (inbox, sent, drafts, trash, junk, archive) and the folders of "Tidy mails" are never renamed or removed; a folder holding mails or folders stays, a mail is never deleted. Rename and remove ask for the code (`stufen.LIST`, confirm). New on every mail: "★ Flag" (flag `\Flagged`, ★ in the list), from a folder also "Move to … Inbox"; mails are moved only out of the inbox, own folders and the archive, never out of sent, drafts, trash or junk. Multiple selection: a tick before each row, above it "N selected" with Read, Unread, Flag, Archive, Move (into a folder of one mailbox) and "Clear selection", at most 50 at a time, "Undo" brings moved ones back. Endpoints `GET /api/profile/mail/tree` (kept 60 s), `GET /api/profile/mail/folder`, `POST /api/profile/mail/acts`, `POST /api/profile/mail/acts/back`, `POST /api/profile/mail/folder/new|rename|remove`; `GET /api/profile/mail/message` and `POST /api/profile/mail/act` take the folder (`folder`, `src`). All with the own login, own mailboxes only, size limit, rate limit `postordnen`/`post`, Notaus holds every change off. Live counts folder actions with the ordered mails (a number). Log `mail: tree shown | <folders>`, `mail: page many <action> done | <number>`, `mail: folder new|rename|remove done|refused|failed`, never a folder name, sender or subject. On the iPhone: folder button top left (pick, make, swipe to rename or remove), "Select" for the multiple selection, flag by swipe and in the mail.

**E-mail as a full mail client, stage B: rules (V01.0.843, Dominik 2026-10-19 "2a 3a"; `mailregeln.py`, part of "Mails ordnen", off):** every mail has "⚙ Rule from this …": a form in which the mail's sender and subject already stand (subject without Re:/AW:/Fwd:, since V01.0.846, Dominik 2026-10-20; both free to change), with "When" (sender as address or @domain, plus optionally a word in the subject, a recipient, only with attachment) and "Then" (into a folder of this mailbox, mark as read, ★ flag, not in "For you"). Folders of the mail program such as trash, junk, sent and drafts are never a target, a mail is never deleted. "Dry run" counts how many mails in the inbox of the last 90 days match and changes nothing. After saving, the panel asks whether to sort the existing mails too; that asks for the code (`stufen.LIST`, confirm), takes at most 50 per mailbox, and "Undo" brings them back. Otherwise a rule takes only mails arriving after it was saved; the panel looks every 5 minutes (at most 200 mails per run). The first matching rule decides. The new tab E-mail → Rules shows all rules in one place with "This month N", Pause, Move up, Edit and Delete, below them the sender rules of "Tidy mails" (only a pointer there now). At most 100 rules per profile, file `users/ID/mail-regeln.json`. A rule is fixed data, never a command; the language model never sees it. Endpoints `GET|POST /api/profile/mail/rules`, `POST /api/profile/mail/rules/test`, `POST /api/profile/mail/rules/order`, `POST /api/profile/mail/rules/{id}/apply`, `DELETE /api/profile/mail/rules/{id}`; own login, own rules and mailboxes only, size limit, rate limit `postordnen`, Notaus holds rules off. Live: "N rules at P profiles, today X mails sorted". Log `mail: rules run | N taken, E errors`, `mail: rule apply done|refused|failed`, never a sender, subject, folder or word of a rule. On the iPhone: "Rules" in E-mail (⋯) and "Rule from this …" in every mail, with dry run, sorting and "Undo".

**E-mail as a full mail client, stage C: search, forward, attachments, drag (V01.0.843, Dominik 2026-10-19 "4a 5a"):** the search above the list knows, besides words, `von:anna`, `betreff:rechnung`, `hat:anhang`, `ist:markiert` and `ist:ungelesen` (English `from:`, `subject:`, `has:attachment`, `is:flagged`, `is:unread`), plus the buttons "★ Flagged" and "📎 Attachment". The panel compares these words as text with sender, subject and the header lines (`mail._words`, `mail.fits`), nothing of them is ever run; in the inbox the last 14 days as before, in a folder the newest 300 mails. Mails with an attachment show 📎 in the list. **Fetch an attachment** (with "Mails ordnen"): on a mail each attachment stands as "⬇ name (KB)"; a tap downloads it (`GET /api/profile/mail/file`, at most 25 MB, own mailboxes only). It always comes as a file (`application/octet-stream`, `nosniff`, `sandbox`), never shown in the page. **Forward** (with "Mails senden"): "Forward" on a mail opens the form with "Fwd: …"; below your lines (which may stay empty) stands the mail with From, Date, Subject and To, its attachments only with "Send the attachments along" and together at most 20 MB. Like every mail it goes out only from the preview. From a folder only with "Mails ordnen". **Drag into a folder** (on a computer, with "Mails ordnen"): drag a row, or all ticked ones, onto the "Drop here" bar above the list; the same move as with the button, with "Undo". Live counts fetched attachments (a number). Log `mail: file shown | <KB>`, `mail: file refused|failed`, never a file name. On the iPhone: a search field above the list with the same words as suggestions, attachments in the mail open by a tap in the preview (Quick Look), "Forward" in every mail; there is no dragging on the iPhone, there "Move to …".
