**The bar of a mail (V01.0.853, Dominik 2026-10-20 "1 und 3"):** the buttons of an open mail sit in one bar that stays on top while the mail scrolls: "Reply" large with its word, beside it symbols in three groups, writing (forward, as appointment), ordering (read or unread, ★ flag, archive, move to … as a short menu of the folders) and rule from this. Hovering a symbol shows its name with its key: R reply, F forward, T as appointment, U read, S flag, E archive, V move, G rule, J and K browse, Esc closes (the menu first, then the mail). On a phone the five most used sit at the thumb below (reply, forward, archive, folder, appointment), on top back, ✎ new mail, ★ and ⋯ (read, rule from this); the iPhone app does the same in its bottom bar and top right. "✎ New mail" stays above the list as always. A function that is off shows no button.

**Folders on the right, dragging, deleting and the rule hint (V01.0.861, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ja so umsetzen"):** the Mail page shows the list, the mail and the folders as three equal boxes with a head line (the list with its name, count and new mails). The folders sit on the right (since Apple style stage 1 on the left, see below); the "Folders" button or the key O shows and hides them, the browser remembers it. The folders of the mail program carry their own names and symbols (Inbox, Sent, Drafts, Archive, Trash, Junk). A mail (or several picked ones) can be dragged onto a folder; while dragging the folders open by themselves, and the archive and trash symbols of the open mail take it too; afterwards "Undo" is there. On a phone the folders come in from the right as a sheet, moving goes through the menu.

**Deleting** has its own admin switch under Functions → Read mail → Order mail → "Delete mail" (`chat.mail_delete`, off). Only the person deletes, on the page (trash symbol, the Delete key, dragging onto the trash, "Delete" for several): the mail goes into the mailbox's trash (the folder with `\Trash`), "Undo" brings it back. "Delete for good" exists only inside the trash, after its own question, and hits exactly that mail: the mailbox must know UIDPLUS, the Message-ID must still match, then `UID STORE \Deleted` and `UID EXPUNGE` of that UID only, never a bare EXPUNGE. The model, rules, "For you" and the voice never delete; an ordinary move still never goes into the trash. Live (node Mail): "of them N deleted", numbers only.

**Conversation (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1"):** under an opened mail stand the other mails of the same conversation, oldest first, one line each (who, when, the first words; own ones as "You"), opened with their own text without quotes. The Spark finds them by Message-ID (References, In-Reply-To and the replies to this mail) in the inbox, the Sent folder and the mail's own folder, read only (EXAMINE, PEEK), at most 10, and checks every id exactly, one that only looks alike does not count (`mail.thread`, `GET /api/profile/mail/thread`, loaded after the mail so opening never waits). No switch of its own, part of "Read e-mail". Live: "N conversations shown today". iPhone: the same section "Conversation" under the mail.

**Only the own text (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "2"):** `mail.plain` also cuts quote heads the mail program breaks over two lines ("On Mon, Oct 20, 2026 at 10:00 AM Anna <" / "anna@x.de> wrote:"), the Outlook divider and lines like "Sent from my iPhone" or "Get Outlook for iOS"; summaries, "For you" and reply suggestions so get only the sender's words (`test_mail_text`).

**Rule suggestions from your filing (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "ok" and "only the folders below your KI folder", plan `plaene/mail-regeln-lernen.md`):** at night between 2 and 5 the Spark looks once per profile into the folders below the main folder of "Tidy mails" (the KI folder, `tidy` `root`), and only when something there changed since the last night (`STATUS` MESSAGES and UIDNEXT; read only, headers only, per folder the newest 300 of the last 30 days). Only what the person filed there counts: mails "Tidy mails" moved (its log by Message-ID) do not, senders with a rule here or in "Tidy mails" and pairs on the never list are left out. Fixed rules, never the model: at least 5 mails of a sender in one folder and at least 80 % of its mails there (the inbox counted too); a domain (`@company.de`) only when several addresses all lie in the same folder and none in the inbox, never freemail (gmail, gmx, web.de, iCloud …); at most 3 new suggestions a night, the strongest first, open for 7 days at most. They stand on top of E-mail → Rules as "Suggestions from your filing" with "Make the rule and file" (the rule and the sender's mails in the inbox, with "Undo"), "Rule only" and "Do not ask again"; a rule comes only after the click. No switch of its own, part of "Order mails". Live: "N suggestions from filing"; the log has numbers only. iPhone: the same list on top of "Rules". **As a card:** each suggestion also stands in the morning as a card "Rule suggestion" in the assistant and under Open (`vorgaenge.QUELLEN["regel"]`), with the page's buttons: "Make the rule and file them" (only while some of its mails lie in the inbox), "Make the rule", "Don't ask again"; a button runs the same checks as the page (no buttons without "Mails ordnen"), and when the suggestion is taken on the page or expires after 7 days the card closes.

**Rule hint:** per profile the Spark counts how often mails of the same sender are moved into the same folder (`mailregeln.learn`, only address, folder, count, 30 days). The third time a hint stands below "Undo" with three buttons: "Make the rule and apply it now" (the rule and the fitting mails in the inbox, with "Undo"), "Only the rule" and "Don't ask again" (remembered for that pair). If the sender already has a rule, it does not ask. Live: "today N rule hints". In the iPhone app: delete on swipe, in the mail and for several picked ones, "Undo" and the rule hint above the list, in the trash "Delete for good" with a question, the mail program's folders with their own names.

**Mail like Apple Mail, stage 1 (V01.0.874, plan `apple-stil/plan.md` 1a 2a 3a, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ok"):** the Mail page follows the layout of Apple Mail, without a new switch (part of "Read mail").

**Calendar like Apple Calendar, stage 2 (V01.0.880, plan `apple-stil/plan.md` 4a 5a, Dominik 2026-10-20 "ok"):** no new switch (part of "Calendar"). A sidebar on the left like in Apple Calendar: a small month (a tap jumps to the day), every calendar with its colour to show and hide (the browser remembers), shared ones and subscriptions marked; on a phone ☰ opens it as a sheet. New view **Year** (twelve months, a tap opens the day). **Creating like Apple:** in week and day drag in free time (quarter hours) or double-click, double-click the all-day row for an all-day appointment, long-press on a phone; the appointment shows at once as a draft and the **inspector** opens beside it with the title to type. A tap on an own appointment opens the same inspector: title, place, all day, start and end, repeat (Never, Daily, Weekly, Every 2 weeks, Monthly, Yearly, with "Ends on"), calendar (only when creating), alert (5 min to 2 days before), people, note and link. Closing (Esc, a click beside it, "Done") saves; then "Undo" stays at the bottom for two minutes. The lower edge of an appointment is dragged for another length. On a phone the day shows a week strip on top; a swipe changes the day. **Repeating appointments** (5a) can now be changed, moved and deleted: the panel asks "Only this appointment" or "All future ones" (with a changed rule only "All future ones"). "Only this" writes an exception (`RECURRENCE-ID`) or removes the one day (`EXDATE`); "All future ones" changes the series itself from the first appointment, from a later one the old series ends before it (`UNTIL`, a `COUNT` is converted) and a new one begins. **People are only a list in the appointment** (`ATTENDEE` with `SCHEDULE-AGENT=CLIENT`): neither the calendar server nor the Spark sends an invitation; invitations come later with an admin switch of their own. Technology: `kalserie.py` (pure iCal functions: reading and checking fields, changing a series), `calendars.page_series`, `change_series`, `keep_undo`, `page_undo`. Every change goes into the Spark's copy first and to the server behind as before, in order per profile, so "Undo" never arrives before the save. Endpoints: `PUT /api/profile/calendar/event` takes `uid`, `rid` (its original start) and `scope` `one`|`future` for an appointment of a series; repeat, alert, people, note and link only when the form names them (a move or an older app leaves them as they are). New `POST /api/profile/calendar/undo` `{"undo"}`: the token from the answer of every save, bound to the profile, valid 120 seconds, at most five, usable once (then 410); login, CSRF, size limit, rate limit "kalenderaendern". Logs: `calendar: series changed|deleted, this one|from this on`, `calendar: series not changed (…)`, `calendar: page undo done|gone|failed`, each with `… refused, notaus`. Live: "heute N auf der Seite geändert, N davon Serien, N zurückgenommen". Left for later: invitations, moving an appointment to another calendar, dots in the year view.
- **Mailboxes on the left:** with "Order mail" a column "Mailboxes" stands on the left: all inboxes, the smart mailboxes Unread, Flagged and With attachment, below them the folders per account. It is open until you hide it (sidebar button or key O, the browser remembers it); dragging a mail opens it by itself. On top there is only one quiet bar: search, "Select", reload, "New mail" (key N). Ticks only after "Select" or with ⌘/Ctrl-click.
- **List:** the sender with the time on the right (today the time, yesterday "Yesterday", this week the weekday, else the date), the subject below, 📎 and ★ as small marks.
- **Reading:** a head like Apple Mail (circle with the initials, name, address, to, time on the right, the subject big), attachments as tiles. A mail with HTML is shown formatted as it was sent (paragraphs, bold, lists, tables, quotes): `mailansicht.py` cleans it on the Spark with a fixed list of allowed tags and attributes (no script, no style from outside, no `position`, no `on…` attributes, no SVG, no forms, no frames); the page shows it in a `sandbox` frame without script with its own CSP (`default-src 'none'; img-src 'self' data:`). Pictures from outside stay off so the sender does not learn that you read the mail; "Load pictures" fetches them for this one mail through the Spark (`netguard`, public addresses only, only addresses named in exactly this mail, only PNG, JPEG, GIF and WebP by their first bytes, never SVG). Embedded pictures (`cid:`) come from the attachment. A link opens only after the question "Open the link? It leads to …" with its real address; when the link text names another address than the target, a warning stands with it (a common trick of phishing mails). `mailto:` opens a new mail. Mails without HTML stay text.
- **Phone:** the bar with reply, forward, archive, folder and delete stands fixed at the bottom above the tab bar (before, it lay over the subject).
- **Writing:** reply, forward and "New mail" open a window of their own over the page (at the computer bottom right, movable by its head and to be made small, the mail stays readable beside it; on a phone a sheet over everything) with compact lines To, Copy, Subject, the text and the Spark's suggestions as ✨ chips. "Send" (or ⌘/Ctrl+Enter) has the Spark check the mail (the same check as the preview sheet, `send/preview`), then it waits 10 seconds with "Undo" and "Send now"; "Undo" brings the window back with everything typed, nothing went out. When the mail holds text of the Spark (a ✨ suggestion, ✨ shorter/friendlier/more formal/in English, a draft from "For you"), the preview sheet still comes before sending and only it sends. Mails by voice keep their approval card with "yes".
- **iPhone app:** the same mail formatted in a web view without script that loads nothing from the network (a content rule blocks every http/https load, the page carries its own CSP, embedded pictures come along as `data:`), a head like Apple Mail, links only after the question with the address (then Safari), pictures from outside stay off in the app. "Send" with 10 seconds "Undo", text of the Spark with the preview.
- **Live** (node Mail): "today N shown formatted, M pictures from outside loaded", numbers only. Logs: `mail: view html|text | N pictures outside`, `mail: picture loaded|shown|refused …`, never an address, subject or text.
- Not yet in stage 1: preview lines in the list, drafts in the folder "Drafts" and address suggestions from contacts (they come after the mail cache).

**Contacts like Apple Contacts, stage 3a (V01.0.888, plan `apple-stil/plan.md` 6a, Dominik 2026-10-21 "Weiter"):** Contacts is a page of its own in the menu (`kontakte.js`), shown with "Contacts". On the left a search, the address books as groups (chips with counts), A to Z with a letter bar at the edge; on the right the card with initials, Message (`sms:`), Call (`tel:`), Mail (opens "New mail" on the page E-mail, without "Send mail" the mail program), Event (Quick add on the page Calendar) and Share (the card as `.vcf`), below phone, e-mail, address, website, birthday, note and "With Anna": the next appointment within 60 days (by mail address or name) and the latest mail from the mail supply, without asking a mailbox for it. Tapping the sender of a mail opens their card. The address books themselves are under Contacts → Settings (before: on the Me page "Contacts"). Changing: admin switch Features → Contacts → "Edit contacts" (`chat.contacts_edit`, off). Then "Edit" like Apple (a minus per field, "+ add" per kind, "Done"), "New contact" (into the chosen address book or "On the Spark") and "Delete contact" after asking. `kontaktkarte.py` checks every field (numbers only digits and signs, mail form, websites only http/https, birthday as a date, maximum counts) and replaces only the page's lines in the vCard; photo, UID and everything else stay. `contacts.save` fetches the card fresh, compares the ETag, writes with `If-Match` (new ones with `If-None-Match: *`); if it was changed meanwhile, the Spark answers 409 and overwrites nothing. Cards "On the Spark" are kept sealed in the profile. Endpoints `/api/profile/contacts/page|with|vcf|card` with login, profile, CSRF, 64 KB, rate limit `kontakte`; Notaus blocks changing (423). The assistant and the voice never change contacts. Live: address books, cards on the Spark, changed on the page today (numbers only). Coming in stage 3b: the Spark as a CardDAV server for the iPhone.


**Contacts like Apple Contacts, stage 3b: the Spark as a CardDAV server (V01.0.897, `kontaktserver.py`):** admin switch Features → Contacts → "Contacts for contacts apps" (`chat.contacts_server`, off; needs "Contacts" and "Calendar server for calendar apps"). Then the address book "On the Spark" shows in the iPhone's Contacts app (on the iPhone: Settings → Apps → Contacts → Accounts → Other → CardDAV account: server = the Spark's address with `/carddav/`, user = profile id, password = the app password from Calendar → Settings → "For calendar apps"). The door is the calendar server's (Basic login, wrong guesses lock like a PIN, rate limit `caldav`, other sites' web pages refused, Mein Notaus). Only the own address book "On the Spark", never iCloud address books (the iPhone has those itself) and never another profile. PROPFIND, REPORT (addressbook-query, addressbook-multiget), GET, PUT, DELETE with etags; per card exactly one vCard with a name or company, at most 256 KB, at most 2000 cards. Writing only with "Edit contacts", otherwise read only (403). A change on the page keeps the app's object name. Live: requests, changes, refusals today.

**Invitations from mail (V01.0.897, `einladung.py`, Dominik 2026-10-21 "ja alles umsetzen 1-6", point 3):** admin switch Features → Reading e-mail → "Invitations from mail" (`chat.mail_invites`, off; needs Reading e-mail and Calendar). If a mail carries an invitation (a `text/calendar` part or an `.ics` file; `mail.show` only reports `invite`), the E-mail page shows a bar under the subject (`einladung.js`): title, time, place, who invites, how many people, the own answer so far and whether the appointment is in a calendar already. "Accept" and "Maybe" put it in (into the chosen calendar, else the first one the Spark may write into, CalDAV or Spark calendar), "Decline" takes it out; an invitation with `METHOD:PUBLISH` only has "Add to calendar", a cancellation by the organiser (`CANCEL`) only "Remove from calendar" after asking. The invitation is someone else's text: at most 256 KB, one appointment (VEVENTs of one UID, at most 50), only REQUEST, PUBLISH and CANCEL. Into the calendar goes a new calendar of the Spark's own making with only the fields needed (time, repetition, title, place, description up to 4000 characters, organiser, at most 100 people, time zones); the sender's alerts, attachments and links stay out. The place in the calendar follows from the UID: the same invitation again or its update replaces the appointment instead of adding it twice. With "Send mail" the answer (iMIP `METHOD:REPLY` with the own `PARTSTAT`) goes from the mailbox the invitation came to, to the organiser (`senden.send_now`: day limit, Notaus, journal); it can be left out with the tick "Answer to … send". Endpoints `GET|POST /api/profile/mail/invite` with login, profile, CSRF, 4 KB, rate limit `einladung`; Notaus 423. The assistant does not answer invitations. Live (Calendar): shown, entered, answered today (numbers only). iPhone: the same section above the mail (`Einladung.swift`).

**Cards in the assistant (V01.0.816, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` stage 2):** when the assistant reads appointments (`calendar_events`), "Show in calendar" under the answer opens the Calendar page on that day; when it reads a mail (`mail_read`), "Open mail" opens exactly that mail on the E-mail page (event `goto`, at most three buttons, only a valid day or mail id, only with the page, never for another person's voice). The card is only a way there: the server decides what the page shows; adding, moving and deleting by voice keep their approval card with yes/no.

**Send mail (V01.0.814, plan `kalender-mail/plan.md` stage 3; Features → Reading e-mail → "Mails senden", `chat.mail_send`, off):** on the E-mail page "New mail", or "Reply" under a mail; "Preview" sends the form to `POST /api/profile/mail/send/preview`, the server checks every field (no line break in a field, so no header from the form; real addresses only; at most 10 recipients; subject at most 200, text at most 20,000 characters; no attachment, no HTML) and keeps the finished mail for 10 minutes under a random token bound to the profile. The sheet shows exactly that mail; only "Send" there (`POST /api/profile/mail/send` with the token) sends it, once. An answer takes subject ("Re: …"), In-Reply-To and References from the mail in the own inbox. `senden.py` sends through the mailbox's outgoing server (`SMTP_FOR`: iCloud smtp.mail.me.com 587, Gmail 465, GMX 465, web.de 587; with another IMAP server only the outgoing server typed in when connecting, never guessed since V01.0.844, without one the mailbox only reads, and the preview shows the server under "Via") with the same login, TLS only (465) or required STARTTLS (587), through `netguard.SMTP`/`SMTP_SSL` (checked address, USER rules). Except for iCloud and Gmail it puts a read copy into "Sent". At most 30 mails a day per profile, the Notaus holds sending off, a wrong password keeps the sheet for another try; if the server breaks off midway the sheet counts as used (never twice). Rate limit `mailsenden`; Live shows at mail only how many mails were sent today; log `mail: send preview|sent|send refused|send failed|sent copy …` with numbers, never address, subject or text. **Sending by voice** (`chat.mail_send_voice`: off, answers only, all; default off): no tool for the model, fixed rules of the panel on the person's own words (`senden.answer`): "Antworte ihr per Mail: …" up to 15 minutes after the assistant read a mail (to its sender, `From` only, never `Reply-To`; the name only from the own contacts, never from the mail), with "all" also "Schreib eine Mail an Anna: …" (Anna only from the own contacts with exactly one address, or an address said in full; several fit → asked back). The Spark reads recipient and text back, approval card (kind "senden"), it is sent only after the plain "Ja" in the next message on the same device, through the same checks as the sheet. Never for guests, another voice or Telegram without the private release. Log `mail: voice proposed|sent|declined|refused, <reason>`.

**Send later (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "4"; part of "Mails senden", no switch of its own):** the preview sheet and the "New mail" window offer "Later …" (iPhone: "Send later …" with date and time). `POST /api/profile/mail/send` with the token and `at` plans exactly the sheet's finished mail, at least 1 minute and at most 30 days ahead, at most 20 at once; until then it lies sealed in the profile (`mail-spaeter.bin`, `vorrat.seal`, bound to profile and file name). Above the list stand the planned mails (when, to whom, subject, never the text) with "Withdraw" (`DELETE /api/profile/mail/later/{id}`, also when "Mails senden" was switched off meanwhile). Each minute `senden.later_due_once` sends what is due, each mail exactly once, through the same checks as "Send" (30 a day, TLS, copy in "Sent"): the Notaus holds it back until it is over; with "Mails senden" off it does not go out and shows "not sent"; if the outgoing server breaks off it shows "unclear, check Sent" and is never tried a second time. With the sending the text goes, the line stays a day. Live shows at mail only the number of planned mails; log `mail: send later planned|refused|dropped|unclear …` without address, subject or text.

**Confirmation codes (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "5", idea from email-mcp, own code; Features → Reading e-mail → New mail at once → "Bestätigungscodes", `chat.mail_code`, off):** when a mail with a code arrives ("482913 ist dein Bestätigungscode"), `mailcode.py` sends a note "Bestätigungscode · 482913 · GitHub" to the device in use, with "Copy code" (iPhone: the button or a tap puts it on the clipboard for 3 minutes, local only; Begleiter on Mac and Windows likewise). Fixed rules, never the model: unread, at most 10 minutes old, a word like code, Bestätigungscode, TAN, verification code close (80 characters) to exactly one code of 4 to 8 digits or capitals with digits; voucher, discount, order, tracking and customer numbers do not count, a year never. At most 10 notes an hour per profile, one per mail. Needs "New mail at once"; a profile with this function that used any device in the last two hours gets its IDLE connection for it. **Never through Telegram:** if Telegram is the device in use, it goes to the next other way (iPhone app, Android, Begleiter, browser); with none there is no note. Apple carries only "New message from the Spark", the app fetches the text from the Spark; the code lives only in memory with its note, never on disk, never in the log (`mail: code looked|no device|limit|too slow …` with numbers only); Live shows at mail only the number of notes today. My emergency stop holds them.

**Mail statistics (V01.0.906, Dominik 2026-10-21 "6"; part of "Mails ordnen", no switch of its own):** at the top of the tab Rules (panel and iPhone) "Who writes most" opens: the mails a week of the last four weeks and the 15 most frequent senders of the last 30 days with count and unread ones, and "has rule" or "Rule …" (the form with that sender). `mailregeln.stats` counts in the panel from the listing the Mail-Vorrat keeps anyway (300 headers a mailbox) and reads nothing again while it is fresh; nothing goes to the model. `GET /api/profile/mail/stats` (rate limit `postordnen`), log `mail: stats shown|too slow …` with numbers only, Live at mail: how often looked at today.

**Sort every mail here (V01.0.985, Dominik 2026-10-24 "1-3"; part of "Mails ordnen", no switch of its own):** in the "⋯" (or right click, a long press on the iPhone) at the inbox, the archive and every own folder of the page E-mail, "Sort every mail here" sorts every mail of that folder once, without a limit of days down to the oldest (the newest 50,000), not only the last 90 days like "old mail". Per mail the first rule that fits decides: first your mail rules (only their folder), then, where inbox tidying is on for the mailbox, its stages 1 to 3 (protected senders, your tidying rules, fixed header signs). The language model never decides here: a mail no rule knows stays, so does one whose target is the folder itself. First a proposal comes in the background with progress ("1,240 to Rechnungen, 830 to Spark/Newsletter; 2,100 stay"); mails move only after "Move" (with the code like any bulk change), in packages of 100; "Stop" halts after the step running. "Undo" brings every mail of the last run back into the folder by its Message-ID; after "Close" the bar above the list goes, "Undo the sorting" then stays in the "⋯" of the folder that was sorted (V01.0.995). Never out of sent, drafts, trash or junk, never deleted, never flagged; if the mail server numbered the folder anew meanwhile, nothing moves. One run per profile, at most two at once on the Spark, speech goes first between the steps. `einordnen.py`, `GET/POST /api/profile/mail/einordnen`, `…/go`, `…/back` (both with the code), `…/stop` (rate limit `postordnen`); the proposal lives in memory only, the last run for "Undo" sealed in `mail-einordnen.bin` (30 days). Log `mail: sort folder …` with numbers only, Live at mail: runs and mails moved today.

**Reading e-mail:** "Any new mail?", "What does Anna write?", "Read me the mail from the bank". Each profile connects up to 4 mailboxes over IMAP in the "Me" window, tab E-mail: iCloud (imap.mail.me.com, your iCloud mail address and a new app-specific password from appleid.apple.com), Gmail (imap.gmail.com, app password, needs 2-step verification), GMX and web.de (allow IMAP in their settings first) or any other IMAP server with TLS. Outlook/Microsoft 365 is not supported yet (needs OAuth). The assistant only reads the inbox of the last 30 days and changes nothing: the mailbox is opened read only, no mail is sent, deleted, moved or marked as read. It summarizes mails and reads them out word for word only when asked; the daily briefing names the unread ones. Mail text is treated as someone else's words: once the assistant read a mail in an answer, in that answer it cannot switch smart home devices, change memory, cancel reminders, search the web or enter appointments, so a crafted mail cannot trigger anything; answers made from mails are not learned into memory. Guests and another person's voice at a different device get no access. The password is stored encrypted on the Spark and never shown again. Mail times are given in your time zone (else the Spark's), not with the sender's offset (V01.0.466). Off until turned on under Features.

**Inbox tidying** (Features → Mail and contacts → "Inbox tidying", off by default; needs reading e-mail; profiles only): the Spark sorts advertising, newsletters, invoices, notifications and social media mail into folders under a main folder (`Spark/` unless renamed, e.g. `Spark/Werbung`; renamed in the "Me" window under E-mail, empty puts the folders at the top. Renaming also renames them in the mailbox with IMAP RENAME, the mail moves along; if the new name exists already, the old folder stays) so the inbox keeps only what matters. It only moves; it never deletes, never sends and never marks mail as read. When the server has no IMAP MOVE (iCloud), it copies the mail into the target folder, checks that the copy arrived, and only then removes exactly that original from the inbox (UIDPLUS); no other mail is touched. Each profile picks per mailbox in the "Me" window, tab E-mail, part "Inbox tidying": off, preview (only shows what it would move; a week is recommended to start), safe automatic (moves only what a rule or clear headers such as List-Unsubscribe prove) or fully automatic (also when the model is at least 85 % sure). Below the switch Off / Preview / Safe / Full one line says what the chosen mode does; pointing at another shows its line (since V01.0.542, on phones too, without a tooltip). When the profile has the second login step, switching asks for the code like every setting (before, the switch seemed stuck). Only new mail is checked, every 5, 15 (default) or 60 minutes. Who decides, in this order: people you wrote to (sent folder), sign-in and security mails and your "always keep" list stay put; then your rules (sender or @domain); then fixed headers; only then the language model, which may only return a category from the fixed list and has no tools. It asks first for invoices and notifications and for unclear mail; your answer can become a rule. When no folder fits, "＋ New folder" in the question adds an own one (since V01.0.515): type a name, "Create and file"; only then is the folder made under the main folder in the mailbox, the mail moves there and "Always" turns it into a rule. The name follows a fixed rule (1 to 40 letters or digits, plus space and . , & ( ) + ' -, no `/`, no control characters, never a folder of the mail program such as inbox or trash); if a folder already has that name, the Spark takes that one. Own folders (at most 20) then show in every choice and under "Folders and exceptions", where they can also be added, renamed and removed. Only your answers and rules fill them, never the language model; "Remove" only takes them off the list and deletes their rules, folder and mail stay in the mailbox. Move a mail back into the inbox and it is not moved again, and the Spark asks. Brakes: at most 200 mails per run, and a run that would move more than half of at least 10 new mails stops and puts everything into the preview. Every move stays in the history for 30 days and can be undone. "Old mail" proposes once where the last 90 days belong; nothing moves until you press "Move". By voice: "Tidy my inbox", "Move everything from Lidl to advertising", "Undo that"; the assistant reads out a proposal and the panel carries it out only after your plain "yes". **Reply drafts** (per mailbox): "Write Anna that I'm coming" saves a draft in the drafts folder after your "yes"; you send it yourself in your mail app. Guests and other people's voices get no access.

**Learn mail style** (`mailstil.py`, Features → Mail and contacts → "Mailstil lernen", `chat.mail_style`, off by default; needs reading e-mail; once released for every profile, never guests): under *E-mail → Settings → My mail style*, "Learn style" reads the "Sent" folder of the chosen mailboxes read-only (EXAMINE and BODY.PEEK, nothing is marked, moved or deleted), at most the newest 200 mails of the last 12 months. The panel cuts quotes, "On … wrote", forwarded parts, signatures and "Sent from my iPhone" with fixed patterns and puts every mail into a group by fixed rules: "Sehr geehrte", "Guten Tag" or "Sie" → Work, "du" or "Hallo/Hi/Liebe" → Private, the first mail to someone also → Unknown. The panel counts the form of address (du, Sie, mixed) and the length (median of words) itself. The language model gets at most 40 own texts per group between `<<<MAIL` and `MAIL>>>` as data, without tools, in the background with speech first, and only fills a fixed form: greeting (names become `{Name}`), sign-off, tone (casual, friendly, matter-of-fact, formal), up to 8 typical phrases, up to 4 habits. The panel checks every field; addresses, numbers, links and marks are dropped, nothing is repaired. Only this summary is kept in `users/<p>/mailstil.json` (stays on the Spark), recipients only as a salted hash, never a mail. Every field can be adjusted with "Change" and stays when it learns again; "⋯ → Delete mail style" and "Delete data" remove everything. It learns only when you press the button. It works in mail drafts: by voice and in the chat `mail_draft` reshapes the draft in the style of the recipient's group (on an error the draft stays as it was), and "Draft a reply" in the Begleiter (Outlook classic, Mac Mail, the Outlook button "Antwort") gets the style as `<<<STIL … STIL>>>` (form and tone only); the panel finds the group from the "Von:" line, unknown senders get "Unknown" and the form of address their mail uses. `"stil": false` in `/api/write/assist` writes once without it. The writing help page, Word, Excel and the browser stay without it. Logs only with fixed words: `mailstil: gelernt … postfaecher=1 mails=180 gruppen=3`, `mailstil: abgebrochen grund=kein_gesendet_ordner`, `mailstil: benutzt weg=begleiter gruppe=beruflich`. API (profile): `GET|PUT|DELETE /api/profile/mailstil`, `POST /api/profile/mailstil/learn`. Plan: `plaene/mailstil.md`.

**For you from mail** (`fuerdich.py`, plan `persoenlicher-assistent/plan.md`, stage 1; Features → Mail and contacts → "Für dich aus Mails", `chat.mail_insight`, off by default; needs reading e-mail; once released for every profile, never guests): under *E-mail → For you* (a tab of its own on the mail page since V01.0.809) the Spark shows what it found in the mails of the last 30 days. Everything is read read-only (EXAMINE and BODY.PEEK): the inbox, the tidying folders Werbung, Newsletter and Rechnungen when they exist, and the headers of the "Sent" folder. Fixed rules, no language model: **bill** (amount, deadline from "fällig am", "zahlbar bis" …, or "wird abgebucht"), **contract** (renews on …, cancel by …), **order** (order confirmation with amount), **offer** (advertising or newsletter with coupon/discount ending within 14 days, at most 5), **reply missing** (someone you wrote to before asks something and has had no answer for 2 to 14 days; told by In-Reply-To and later mails to the address), **suspicious** (sender or subject names a known brand like Sparkasse, PayPal, DHL but does not come from its domain and wants something confirmed, or the header says DMARC failed; since V01.0.857 a brand only in the subject does not count when the mail server confirms that the sender's own domain sent the mail (DMARC, DKIM or SPF passed) and the subject itself does not urge to confirm or log in: so an o2 offer with Netflix is no longer suspicious; a brand in the sender's name or in a lookalike domain stays suspicious). Amounts and days count only when they parse; every sentence on a card is a fixed sentence with the checked values. Sender name and subject are outside text and appear only as text; links from mails are never opened or linked. Per run at most 60 mail texts per mailbox, in the background with speech first (`vorrang.in_thread("Für dich")`), the result kept 10 minutes in memory. ✓ (done) and ✕ (not interesting / is genuine) keep only a checksum of the Message-ID in `users/<p>/fuerdich.json` (stays on the Spark); "⋯ → Forget the marks" and "Delete data" remove them. Under *Me → Overview* the card "For you" only counts how many cards need you (waits at most 3 seconds, else "being read"). Logs only with fixed words: `fuerdich: gelesen funde=3 fehler=0 sekunden=1.2`, `fuerdich: markiert als=done Profil …`. API (profile): `GET /api/profile/fuerdich` (`?fresh=1` reads again), `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/mark` `{"id", "as": "done"|"no"|"open"}`, `DELETE /api/profile/fuerdich`. 

**For you, stage 2: buttons, voice, by itself (Dominik 2026-10-18 "5a", 2026-10-20 "build all further stages"):** every card gets the buttons the panel allows by fixed rules for its kind and the released functions (`fuerdich.acts`): **Remind me** (bill, contract, offer, reply missing; with "Timers and reminders") sets a reminder with a fixed text, e.g. "Rechnung Stadtwerke bezahlen (84,20 €)", at 9 am the day before the deadline, without a deadline tomorrow at 9 am, never sooner than in an hour. **Add to calendar** (cards with a deadline; with "Calendar") opens the calendar with a fixed line such as "31.10.2026 Kündigungsfrist congstar" in "Quick add"; it is saved only with its "Add". **Draft a reply** (reply missing from the inbox; with "Send mail") opens the mail on the E-mail page with the reply form and the reply suggestions in the own mail style. **Draft a cancellation** (contract; with "Send mail") opens a new mail with a fixed text ("hiermit kündige ich meinen Vertrag bei … fristgerecht zum …"), to the sender unless it is a no-reply address. Both send only through the preview sheet of the E-mail page. By voice: "What do I have to do this week?" reads the cards as numbered fixed sentences (tool `fuer_dich`, at most 8, mail data like any reading from mail); "number two is done", "… not interesting" or "remind me of number one" acts through `fuer_dich_tun`, only with a number of the list read last (valid 30 minutes) and never in the same answer that read mail text (the tool changes something). The daily briefing names at most three cards that need you. **Speak up by itself → Deadlines and suspicion** (`pro_fuerdich`, under E-mail → Settings, on once "Speak up by itself" is on): a bill or a cancelling deadline due today or tomorrow and a suspicious mail, once per card, 8 am to 8 pm, at most 3 a day, fixed sentences only. It checks every 30 minutes from the last reading, right after a new mail (`fuerdich.new_mail`, IMAP IDLE of the E-mail page). Endpoint `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/act` `{"id", "do": "remind"|"cancel"}` (own profile, only cards of the last reading, only allowed buttons, size limit, rate limit "fuerdich"). Logs: `fuerdich: erinnerung gesetzt`, `fuerdich: kuendigung entworfen`, `fuerdich: knopf abgelehnt grund=…`, `fuerdich: werkzeug liste funde=N`, `fuerdich: briefing teile=N`. On the iPhone: Today → For you with the same cards and buttons (swipe for Done and Not interesting, ⋯ for Read again and Forget the marks). Next stages: interests and "What I know about you", web tip, weekly review, spending. Since V01.0.857 For you reads anew at once when a new mail comes, if "New mail at once" (IMAP IDLE, `mailidle.py`, `chat.mail_idle`) is on (`mailidle.on_new(fuerdich.new_mail)`); otherwise it stays with the check every 30 minutes. Since V01.0.870 the spoken list names the mail of every entry (sender, day, subject, folder outside the inbox), "reply missing" reads "… sent you a question 4 days ago and waits for your answer", and when reading takes longer than 20 seconds the tool uses the last reading or says "still reading" instead of "not readable" (log `fuerdich: werkzeug laedt noch` or `… liste aus letzter lesung`). Since V01.0.877 the last reading per mailbox stays sealed in the profile (`fuerdich-vorrat.bin`, built with `vorrat.Vorrat` like the mail supply, only the found entries with sender, subject and time, never a mail text): after a restart the tab shows it at once, and when a mailbox does not answer its entries come with "Stand hh:mm" (up to 24 hours old), in the tab, when read aloud and in the card under Today. Without a kept reading the assistant says that this mailbox cannot be read right now. The file goes with "forget marks", with the profile and after 14 days without a reading; Status → Live counts under E-Mail how often it answered from the last reading today.

**What I know about you, stages 3 and 4 (Dominik 2026-10-18 "3a 4a 6a 7a", 2026-10-20 "build all further stages"):** a page of its own *Me → What I know about you* (`ichweiss.py`, part of "For you from mail", `chat.mail_insight`, no switch of its own; never guests). **Interests (3a):** proposals with evidence, never from a mail text: a shop with at least two orders, offers or newsletters in 30 days (only from the headers of the last reading of For you, `fuerdich.shops`) and the keywords of Reddit and feeds. An interest counts only after "Right"; "Not really" is remembered and does not come back; own interests can be added. On an order or offer under *E-mail → For you*, "More of this" makes the shop an interest; an offer that fits an interest comes first and shows "fits …". Only name, state and time are kept, in `users/<p>/interessen.json` (at most 60). **From the web (4a):** for an interest with "Tips from the web" a separate run searches at most once a day between 10 am and 7 pm in the own web search (SearXNG) for exactly the word of the interest, never with mail text or sender; the first hit with an https address becomes the tip (title as text, link only in a new tab without referrer), visible for three days. Needs the web search released. **Recurring (6a):** bills of the same sender in three or more months around the same day (at most 6 days apart), from the headers of the last 200 days (read-only, once a day); "Remind me monthly" sets a monthly reminder "Rechnung … kommt um den 15." at 9 am. **This week and this month (stage 4, 7a):** "Deine Woche: 3 erledigt, 2 offen. Nächste Woche: …" from the marks of the last 7 days and the deadlines of the next 7 days; the sum of this month's bills and orders only from checked amounts (also under E-mail → For you). On Sundays from 6 pm the weekly review comes by itself (By itself: "Weekly review", `pro_rueckblick`, once a week, under E-mail → Settings). **Kept:** the newest facts of the memory and the mail style with the ways to their pages. "⋯ → Forget every interest" and "Delete data" remove the file. iPhone: *For you → What I know about you* with the same sections and buttons, "More of this" on the card, CarPlay row "Read deadlines aloud". State → Live: event "Was ich über dich weiß" (only "Netz-Tipp gefunden"). Logs (area "mail"): `ichweiss: angesehen vorschlaege=2 interessen=1 wiederkehrend=1 …`, `ichweiss: interesse als=yes …`, `ichweiss: erinnerung monatlich gesetzt …`, `ichweiss: netztipp gefunden|ohne treffer|fehler=…`, `ichweiss: kopfzeilen gelesen ordner=2 absender=5`, `fuerdich: mehr davon …`. API (profile): `GET /api/profile/ichweiss`, `POST /api/profile/ichweiss/interest` `{"name", "state": "yes"|"no"|"forget", "src", "web"}`, `POST /api/profile/ichweiss/recurring` `{"id"}`, `DELETE /api/profile/ichweiss`; plus `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/act` `{"id", "do": "more"}`. **Topics instead of sender names (since V01.0.922, Dominik 2026-10-22 "ok", plan `interessen-themen/plan.md`):** a proposal is a topic from a fixed list (`ichweiss.TOPICS`, about 25 topics such as "Music production & samples", "Smart home & IoT", "Food delivery"; per topic fixed words and sender domains, whole words, no model). A sender gets the topic whose words stand in at least two and at least a third of its mails (subject and sender name) or whose domain it has. Under the topic its senders show as chips (✕ leaves one out) and up to three subjects as examples; the page shows subjects as text only, they stay in memory and never go into a file, a log or the search. A sender with offers or newsletters but no topic shows "Topic unclear": open its subjects and pick a topic (since V01.0.923 from a list of the confirmed interests and the fixed topics, the confirmed ones as chips, with suggestions while typing; a name of the fixed list, German or English, becomes that topic) or type one of your own that it then belongs to. Never proposed: your own addresses and the profile name, services and accounts (for example `notifications@github.com`, addresses like `security@`, `account@`) and machine mail (`Auto-Submitted`); a sender with orders only just when it has a topic. "Right" confirms the topic with its senders: offers of new senders with the same words fit too, and the web tip searches for the topic word, never for a sender. "More of this" on a card takes the shop's topic when there is one. Senders confirmed earlier are asked once "Which topic is this?" with "Take it" or "Leave it". `interessen.json` gains topic and senders. iPhone: the same proposals with a senders menu, "Topic unclear" and the topic question. API: `POST /api/profile/ichweiss/interest` also takes `topic` and `senders` (at most 20) and the states `adopt` `{"name", "topic"}` and `keep` `{"name"}`. **Tidy and editable (since V01.0.928, Dominik 2026-10-22 "cannot be deleted or edited" and "untidy", plan `interessen-themen/seite-aufraeumen.md` "ok"):** one title, one line of explanation, then tabs: "To sort out" (proposals and "Which topic is this?" with word buttons "Right/Not really" and "Take it/Leave it"; opens first while something waits, its count then orange), "Interests" (list on the left, details on the right, on the phone a page of its own with "‹ Interests"), "Recurring" and "Week & kept" (week, month's sum, tip of the day, memory, mail style). Below the list stand "＋ Add an interest" and, folded, "Not really (n)". An interest's details show its senders (✕ takes one out, it does not come back as "Not really"), "✎ Rename or give a topic" (pick a topic there is or type a word), the switch "Tips from the web" with the last tip, and "Forget". A sender name confirmed earlier without a topic shows in orange as "old sender, no topic yet"; once it gets a topic it becomes a sender there. Such old senders are no topic to pick; the chips under "own topic" are called "Quick pick". iPhone: the same tabs on top as a segment, an interest opens its own page (swipe a sender left to take it out). API: states `rename` `{"name", "to"}` and `drop` `{"name", "senders": [one sender]}`.

**Writing help (V01.0.332, `schreib.py`):** improve mails and other texts (also from Word, Excel, Pages). Switches: Features → Mail and contacts → "Schreibhilfe" (admin, off); once released it works for every profile, Me → Writing help shows what it can do; never guests. On the page Me → Writing help: paste a text, pick what to do (Correct: only spelling, grammar, punctuation; Smooth; Shorter; Friendlier; More formal; Translate into German, English, French, Spanish, Italian or Dutch; Draft a reply from the mail you received and your own notes, missing details come as [square brackets]), optionally a free wish about the form ("informal", sign-off). When correcting, smoothing, shortening and rephrasing the panel shows every change in colour; the panel works out the changes itself (word comparison), not the model. "Copy", "Use as my text" (for a second round). With "Picture recognition", "Read from picture" or a screenshot pasted with Ctrl+V reads the text from a picture (the picture is dropped right after). With "Reading e-mail", "Inbox tidying" and the mailbox switch "Drafts", "Put into the mailbox as a draft" saves the text in the drafts folder; nothing is ever sent. For other programs there is the MCP tool `write_text` ("Improve text") under Me → Spark as MCP server. The text is data only: a single call of the language model without tools, between fixed marks, told never to follow requests in the text. Nothing is stored (no history, no memory); the journal, Logs → Requests and Status → Live see only the kind, the number of characters and the time. Limits: 20,000 characters of text and mail, 96 KB per request, one request per profile at a time, two on the whole Spark, 20 per minute. Notaus stage 3 stops the writing help, from stage 2 there are no drafts. Plan: `plaene/schreibhilfe.md`.

**Writing help in other programs (V01.0.339):** sub-switches Features → Mail and contacts → "Writing help in other programs" (admin, off; then for every profile, pairing is under Me → Writing help). Then Me → Writing help shows the programs to download and "Pair a program": one click (with confirmation code) gives a setup line like `https://spark.example.de#schreib=ABCD-EFGH`, valid 10 minutes, usable once. The program swaps it for its own device key of kind "write" that can only improve texts (no chat, no mail, no drafts, no history). Paired programs are listed below and can be removed one by one; at most 10 per profile.
- **Mac** (download "Mac quick action"): Services menu "Mit Spark verbessern" in Apple Mail, Pages, Word, Excel and any other program, optionally with a shortcut. Pick what to do, look at the suggestion, "Übernehmen" replaces the selection. Key in the keychain.
- **Windows** (x64 and ARM): since V01.0.342 part of the Spark companion (below), paired under Me → Companion.
- **Outlook, Word, Excel** (Office add-in, only with the Spark's https address): download the manifest and load it in Outlook under "Get Add-ins" → "My add-ins" → "Custom add-in", or in Word/Excel as a shared add-in. Side pane with kind, suggestion and "Übernehmen" into the mail, document or cell. Works in Outlook for Windows, Mac and the web.
- **Browser** (extension for Chrome, Edge, Firefox): right-click selected text in webmailers (Outlook on the web, Gmail, GMX, web.de and others) → "Mit Spark verbessern". Load as unpacked extension; in Firefox only until restart.
- **iPhone and iPad** (Spark app): Share → Spark → "Improve text" and the shortcut "Improve text with Spark"; uses the app key.
All programs send only the selected text, the Spark does not store it; http only in the home network. Live and logs show program kind, kind and number of characters.

**Spark companion on PC and Mac (V01.0.342, `begleiter.py`, `tools/begleiter`):** a small program in the Windows taskbar (x64 and ARM, GitHub Action "Spark-Begleiter Windows", release `begleiter-windows`) and the Mac menu bar (macOS 13 and later, Action "Spark-Begleiter Mac", release `begleiter-mac`). Switches: Features → On the go and devices → "Spark companion on PC and Mac" (admin, off); once released it works for every profile, pairing is under Me → Companion; never guests. Pairing: Me → Companion → "Pair a device" gives a setup line `…#begleiter=ABCD-EFGH` (10 minutes, once, with confirmation code); the program gets its own key of kind "desk" that knows only its own paths. Parts, on or off per device under Me → Companion (new devices: writing help, Ask Spark, notes and state on; dictation and the kill-switch button off):
- **Writing help** (needs the writing help itself): select text, Ctrl+Alt+S (Mac ⌥⌘S or the Services menu). Defaults per device: kind, language for translating, fixed wish, which kinds show in the menu, show the suggestion first or replace at once.
- **Ask Spark**: Ctrl+Alt+A (⌥⌘A) opens a window for a question, the answer comes as text; follow-ups within 10 minutes keep the context, a day's questions are a conversation "Begleiter" in the history. Since V01.0.860 with cards as in the panel (`chat.desk_cards`): follow-up cards (Yes/No, "A or B?"), the approval card ("Appointment waits for your yes"), "Show in calendar"/"Open mail" (open only `#me=calbox` or `#me=mailbox`) and the line of tools used; a click only sends the fixed word as if typed.
- **Dictation**: Ctrl+Alt+D (⌥⌘D) starts and ends the recording (at most 60 seconds, visible indicator), the recognised text lands at the cursor. Language per device.
- **Notes**: the Spark's notes as system notifications when the companion is the device used last (like the Android app, the program fetches them every 30 seconds).
- **State dot**: icon normal, grey (Spark not reachable) or red (Notaus or my kill switch).
- **My kill switch**: menu item with a confirmation, stops the own profile (as Me → Security). Lifting only in the browser with the PIN.
Hotkeys are set in the program under Settings. Changes in the panel reach the program with its next check. Removing a device under Me → Companion stops its key at once. Windows: not signed, SmartScreen on first start ("More info" → "Run anyway"), key encrypted with DPAPI. Mac: not notarized yet, first start right-click → Open; for the writing-help hotkey and pasting macOS asks once for "Accessibility", for dictation for the microphone; key in the keychain. Status → Live shows pairing, questions, dictations (character count only) and notes to "Spark-Begleiter".

**Companion: updates, look, coffee (V01.0.347):** The programs now look like the panel: the Spark face as icon in taskbar and menu bar (dot bottom right: green reachable, grey not reachable, red kill switch, accent colour when a new version is there), a window on clicking the icon with all parts that are on and their hotkeys, light and dark look following the system. Updates: the Spark fetches new versions itself from the GitHub releases `begleiter-windows` and `begleiter-mac` (once a day and only when a device of that kind is paired; Features → Spark companion → "Fetch new version now" at once) and checks size and SHA-256 against the version's `begleiter.json`. The program learns from its check that a new version is there, downloads it only from the paired Spark, checks size and SHA-256 again and installs only after your click on "Update"; a damaged file is deleted and the old version keeps running. In the Companion app: Settings → About shows the version, "Check for updates" and the switch "Download updates automatically, ask before installing". The first version with updates has to be installed by hand once. The program and Me → Companion carry the link "☕ Support Spark" (buymeacoffee.com).

**Companion deeper in the computer (V01.0.353):** eight more parts, all off, turned on per device under Me → Companion:
- **Right-click on files:** "With Spark" in Explorer (Windows 11: "Show more options") and under Services in Finder: summarize, translate, explain (PDF, Office, text, mail, pictures; at most 20 MB, the first 40,000 characters are read) or add to knowledge (needs own documents).
- **Ask about the screen:** Ctrl+Alt+B (⌥⌘B), drag a region, optionally a question, then explain, read out the text or translate. The language model reads the picture; it is kept nowhere.
- **Know the app:** the writing help learns the kind of program (mail, Word, Excel, chat, browser …) and fits tone and form; program names and window titles do not go to the Spark.
- **Draft a reply:** Ctrl+Alt+M (⌥⌘M) for the open mail in Outlook (classic, Windows) or Apple Mail: the answer is a draft in the reply window; nothing is ever sent. In the new Outlook it is on the clipboard.
- **Spark-Wissen folder:** what lands in the folder (default: Spark-Wissen in the user folder) goes into the own documents; a changed file replaces its older version, at most 50 a day. Sub folders you leave out under *Me → Documents → Left-out folders* (since V01.0.777) are not sent; what was sent from them leaves the search at once.
- **Buttons in notes:** Reply, Later, Open panel. A reply goes to the Spark with the note as context; the note counts as outside text (nothing switches). Since V01.0.860 the buttons depend on the kind of note (fixed in the panel, `begleiter.NOTE_ACTS`, never from its text): a deadline or suspicion from For you has **Done**, **Remind** and **Show** (`POST /api/desk/note/act`; the panel looks up the note and the card itself, the program only names the note's number and the button). "Open panel" goes to the note's way (`weg`: E-mail → For you, or the first way in its text).
- **Mail in the companion (since V01.0.860, part `post`, off; follows Features → Reading e-mail):** a section "Post" in the tray window: unread mail and For you as numbers, the up to 3 urgent For-you cards with **Done**, **Remind** and **Show** (`GET /api/desk/post`, `POST /api/desk/post/act`; the panel looks up the card itself) and "Open e-mail". An **important new mail** comes as a note as soon as "New mail at once" (IMAP IDLE) reports it (`mailidle.on_new`): only unread, at most 15 minutes old, from a contact or from someone you wrote to in the last year (Sent folder, read only), never from lists or no-reply addresses; at most 3 at once, each only once. Sender and subject are only text in it. Buttons: **Draft a reply** (opens Ask Spark with the note and the request in the field, nothing is sent), **Read** (only with "Mails ordnen") and **Show** (E-mail → Inbox). Only devices with this part get such notes; on the Mac the lock screen shows only "Neue Mail" when previews are hidden.
- **Quick capture (since V01.0.860, part `capture`, off; follows Features → Calendar or Features → Timers and reminders):** Ctrl+Alt+N (⌃⌥N), the tile "Erfassen" or the menu open a small window for one line like "Dentist Fri 10 am at Dr. Weber"; marked text comes along as the line. The panel reads it by fixed rules (`calendars.quick`, no language model) and shows what it found: appointment or reminder, day and time, place (`POST /api/desk/capture`). It is saved only with **Eintragen**; the panel reads the same line once more for that, so exactly what was shown lands in the calendar or the reminders. A reminder without a time is for 9:00 (if that has passed, in one hour); an appointment needs an own calendar account. Then **Rückgängig** for 10 minutes (`POST /api/desk/capture/undo`, only the entry this profile just made). At most 200 characters, 60 calls a minute. Logs only with fixed words: `begleiter: erfassen termin|erinnerung ok`, `begleiter: erfassen rueckgaengig … ok`, `begleiter: erfassen abgelehnt grund=eingabe|notaus`.
- **Reply suggestions in the companion and the Outlook add-in (since V01.0.860, follows reply suggestions and the writing help):** in the window "Draft a reply" (Windows, Mac) and in the Outlook add-in's side pane **3 Vorschläge** fetches three short replies to the mail that was read (`POST /api/write/suggest`, like the reply suggestions in the panel: the mail is only data, one call without tools, nothing is stored). A click takes a suggestion as the reply; then **Kürzer**, **Freundlicher**, **Förmlicher** and **Englisch** reshape it (`/api/write/assist`). Nothing is ever sent, the draft opens in the reply window as before.
- **Shared calendars in "Als Nächstes" (since V01.0.860, part `agenda`):** "Als Nächstes" in the companion window also shows the appointments of calendars other profiles share with you (Features → Share calendars), with the profile's name as a chip; a private appointment there is only "belegt". A click on an appointment opens the calendar in the panel on that day (`#kal=YYYY-MM-DD`; the program takes only a plain date).
- **Lists in the companion (since V01.0.860, part `lists`, off; follows Features → Lists for shopping and tasks):** a section "Listen" in the companion window: shopping, tasks and own lists with open entries, each with a count and the newest entry as a fixed sentence ("Sabine hat „Milch“ ergänzt", else "zuletzt „Milch“"; `GET /api/desk/lists`, at most 6). "Öffnen" goes to the page Lists. **Quick capture** also knows the kind **Liste**: "Milch, 2 Eier und Butter auf die Einkaufsliste" puts three entries onto that list (only lists of the profile, else shopping), only with "Eintragen", with you as "who" and "Begleiter" as "from where"; "Rückgängig" takes only the new entries off again.
- **Act on the PC** (own admin switch "May act on the PC", off): for a request like "Open tagesschau.de on the PC" the computer asks in a window whether it may (60 s, else no). Allowed only: open a page (http/https), a program released under Me → Companion, music (play, pause, next, previous), volume, lock the screen, show a text. Locked after mail, web pages or other outside text.
- **Wake word on the PC:** "Hey Spark" through the computer's microphone, as in the browser (short pieces to speech recognition, a hit starts the question). Listens only while the part is on; the icon shows it, "Pause wake word" in the window.

**Companion: seven more parts (V01.0.375):** all off, turned on per device under Me → Companion. **Read aloud** (Ctrl+Alt+L, ⌥⌘L): marked text in your Spark voice, at most 5000 characters. **From the phone to the PC:** text (into the clipboard), a link (opened only after asking, only http/https) or a file (at most 20 MB, into "Downloads/Vom Spark") from the panel ("Send to the PC" at the device) or by voice ("Send the link to my PC"); waits up to 24 hours. **Appointments in the companion:** the next 24 hours with a note 10 minutes before, "Remind me in 20 min" on notes. **Recognise do not disturb:** while another program uses the microphone or camera or a presentation runs, notes and announcements on your own speakers wait; a summary "Während du beschäftigt warst" follows. Notaus, security notes and reminders always come. **Write along in meetings** (own admin switch "Write along in meetings", off): only on a click, with a red bar; the microphone and the computer's sound go to speech recognition, the transcript stays in memory only (at most 3 hours). Afterwards a summary and tasks; with "Ausgewählte auf die Liste" chosen tasks go onto a list ("Tasks" by default, selectable; with the date from "due" and the source "from a meeting", since V01.0.866), with "Als Erinnerung" into the reminders, the summary on request a document on the reMarkable (folder "Meetings"), and it is in the history. Everybody taking part must agree. **Smart home buttons:** up to 12 buttons (lights, switches, fans, climate, scenes) of your Home Assistant, set up under Me → Companion; with a code word the companion asks for it. **Start and end of the day:** in the morning the daily overview, in the evening tomorrow's appointments and what is open. For "Act on the PC" the companion sends the list of installed programs; under Me → Companion you pick from it instead of typing name and path.

**Companion update looks right away (V01.0.377):** "Check for updates" in the program and every program start make the Spark look at GitHub at once (at most every 5 minutes per kind of computer); before, it looked once a day. The Mac names the failing step when it refuses an update.

**Companion with the chosen face (V01.0.395):** the companion shows the face picked under Settings → Defaults (robot, comic or comic woman) in every window and the icon; a change arrives with the next check. In the companion the comic face is a still picture.

**Acting on the PC right at the companion (V01.0.397):** asked in Ask Spark on the computer itself ("open Safari with Google"), the computer need not be named; a verb of doing (open, start, play, louder, quieter, lock, show) is enough. The question at the computer stays.

**Acting on the PC from a speaker or the phone (V01.0.419):** a clear order naming the computer ("Open heise.de on my Mac") is now reliably sent to the computer from there too; the question still comes at the computer. When it cannot go (no computer paired, "Act on the PC" off at the device or for the profile), the Spark says exactly that instead of trying other tools. Speech recognition may write "Mac" as "Mäc".

**Remote control of the computer, stage 1: more fixed orders (V01.0.739):** "Act on the PC" can now also bring a released program to the front ("Switch to Outlook"), close, minimize, maximize or full-screen the front window, press fixed keys (next, previous, space, enter, Esc, Tab, undo, redo, copy, paste, save, reload, find), type your own words ("Type on the PC: be right there"), open a folder (Documents, Downloads, Desktop, Pictures, Music, Videos, Recent), put the computer to sleep or turn the screen off and run a shortcut. Shortcuts (Mac: from the Shortcuts app; Windows: scripts, links, programs) are released only at the computer itself (companion → "Am PC handeln einstellen"); the Spark knows only their names. There are no free key combinations. Only what you said yourself in this sentence is typed; if the model adds words, the panel refuses. The computer asks as before; in the window you can pick "Without asking from now on" for a kind (not for pages, typing and showing a text). You set and undo that only at the computer, Me → Companion only shows it. Requests now arrive at once: the companion waits for them with an open request instead of looking every 10 seconds.

**Remote control of the computer, stage 2: show and operate the screen (V01.0.739):** Under Me → Companion tap "View screen" or "Operate" for the device and you see the main screen of your own PC or Mac in the panel or in the iPhone or Android app. The computer asks every time: Decline (preselected), View only or Allow (only when operating was asked for). After "Allow" a tap or click on the picture is a click on the computer, dragging moves the mouse, the wheel scrolls, "Right click" makes the next tap a right click; text and fixed keys (Esc, Tab, Enter, Backspace, arrows, Copy, Paste, Undo) come through the bar below the picture. There is no Windows key, Cmd-Space, Alt+Tab or Ctrl+Alt+Del. While it runs the computer shows a red frame and a bar on top with "Beenden" (End); ending also works in the companion menu (Windows), with My emergency stop and in the panel. Up to four pictures a second, at most 1600 pixels wide; the Spark keeps only the newest picture in memory and forgets it at the end. No recording, no history. At most 30 minutes; when nobody watches any more or the computer stops sending, the session ends after 30 seconds. Own admin switch (Features → Show and operate the screen) and own part per device, both off until switched on; only own computers, never guests. On the Mac the companion needs the permissions "Screen Recording" and, for operating, "Accessibility".

**Remote control of the computer, stage 3: Spark operates the computer (V01.0.739):** "Spark, write to Anna in Outlook that I'll be there at three" – the Spark does the task on your own PC or Mac step by step: it sees the screen, proposes exactly one step (click, double click, right click, scroll, a fixed key or typing text), and the computer shows it with the target marked in red and asks: Stop (preselected), Skip or Allow. Only after "Allow" does the step happen; 60 seconds without an answer count as Stop. Protection against injected orders: the task is fixed from the first sentence and must be in your own words; text on the screen is only content for the Spark. Only text from your task is typed, anything else is shown as a warning above the step. Only a program released under "Act on the PC" may be in front; terminal, settings, login windows, password managers and the Companion itself are always blocked. If something on the screen looks like an order to the Spark, it pauses and asks (No / Continue). At most 30 steps and 5 minutes; Stop, "End" in the bar and My emergency stop end it at once. The result comes as a notice in fixed words ("On the computer: done (4 steps)"); pictures and screen text never go to memory, history or chat. Own admin switch (Features → Spark operates the computer), off until switched on; on the device both "Act on the PC" and "Show and operate the screen" must be on. Needs a language model that sees pictures.

**Describe the screen (V01.0.741):** "What do you see on my Mac right now?" or "What is open on my PC?" – the computer asks (Decline preselected, 60 seconds without an answer count as Decline), takes one single picture after "Allow", and the Spark describes it in a few sentences. Text on the screen stays content, never an order; it never names passwords, codes or account numbers, and in the same answer it changes nothing afterwards. If a protected program is in front (login, password manager, settings, terminal), it describes nothing. You only see and hear the description: in the history, on other devices and in memory there is only "Here was a description of your screen" instead. Switched on with Features → Spark operates the computer; on the device "Show and operate the screen" must be on. If something is off, the Spark says exactly what is missing.

**Follow-up requests to the computer (V01.0.752):** After "What is running on my Mac?", "Bring the Terminal to the front." or "Again" is enough without naming the computer again: if one of your two messages before named it, the request goes to it (a fixed rule, only with an order word or "again"). "… in den Vordergrund" or "… nach vorne" brings a released program to the front, and the Spark must really ask at the computer for it. After a screen description the next request is no longer locked.

**Office add-ins from the companion (V01.0.433):** new part "Office add-ins" (per device, off; needs the writing help). Then "Office-Add-ins einrichten …" in the companion; it asks first. **Outlook classic (Windows):** a "Spark" button in the ribbon (main window and reading: draft a reply, translate; composing: improve text, translate), also with iCloud or IMAP accounts. The button only sends one fixed word to the running companion, which works as with its hotkeys; nothing is ever sent. **Word and Excel (Windows and Mac):** the companion registers the Office add-in itself. For that open the panel once at its https address, save under Me → Companion; the admin must have released "In other programs" of the writing help. **New Outlook, Outlook for Mac and on the web:** the companion shows the file and opens the page "Add from file"; adding it is done by the person (Microsoft allows nothing else, only for Microsoft 365, Exchange or Outlook.com). Part off: everything is removed again.

**Outlook classic hung at start (V01.0.537):** with the Spark add-in, Outlook classic stopped at "Loading add-ins". The cause was the self-declared Office interface: the `custom` parameters were not passed as a SAFEARRAY like in Office. The interface is now declared exactly as Microsoft does, and nothing in the add-in can disturb Outlook with an error. If affected: switch the part "Office add-ins" off in the companion and on again after the update. The setup dialog now names this way out too.

**Blocked Spark add-in (V01.0.545):** after a hang Outlook moves an add-in to "Disabled items", and its tick under COM add-ins stays empty. "Office-Add-ins einrichten …" now lifts that block, for Spark only (Resiliency\DisabledItems and CrashingAddinList); other add-ins stay as Outlook set them.

**Companion window in order (V01.0.913):** the window on a click on the icon (Windows) or on the face in the menu bar (Mac) grew higher than the screen with all parts on, so parts and the Notaus were cut off at the bottom. Now the head, "Frag Spark …", the tiles and the foot with "Panel öffnen" and Notaus always stay visible; below are three tabs: **Heute** (Als Nächstes, Post, Listen), **Meldungen** (with a count) and **Rechner** (Zuhause, Dieser Rechner or Dieser Mac). Tabs without content are left out, with only one there is no bar. The window is at most as high as the free screen and only the tab scrolls (one bar); all tabs have the same height so nothing jumps when switching. The tiles are small, four in a row, the hotkey is in the tooltip. Als Nächstes and Post show the first three, "Alle n zeigen" opens the rest; lists are one line each with count and newest entry, a click opens the page Lists. When a new note comes, the window opens on "Meldungen" (newest on top, "OK" and "Erledigt" take it away), else on the tab chosen last; it is kept only on the device. On the Mac "Meldungen" now also shows the Spark's notes since the start (at most 10, only in memory) with the same buttons as the notification; "OK" also removes it from the Notification Center. No new switch, no change in the panel. Needs the new companion build. Since V01.0.998 the Mac window always sits exactly as high as its content right under the menu bar; before it did not get smaller again after a longer tab, and the content stood in the middle with empty room above and below.

**Notes with "OK" (V01.0.663):** in the Windows companion's tray window every note has the button "OK" first. It takes the note away without opening anything, and the window stays open so further notes can be confirmed right away. On the Mac notes are macOS notifications and go away as usual with "Close". Needs the new companion build.

**Microphone open/closed (V01.0.549):** the companion (Windows: right-click menu and tray window, Mac: menu bar window) has the switch "Mikrofon schließen" or "Mikrofon öffnen" as soon as a part with the microphone is on (dictation, Ask Spark, wake word, meeting). Closed really means closed: the wake word stops, a running recording is discarded and not sent, a meeting ends, and no button opens the microphone until you open it again. The state stays after a restart. The icon in the taskbar or menu bar then shows a struck-through microphone. Under Me → Companion every connected device shows "Mikrofon offen" or "Mikrofon zu"; Status → Live only counts the devices with the microphone closed. Only this state goes to the Spark, never sound. Needs the new companion build.

**"Draft a reply" no longer hangs (V01.0.545):** when Outlook asks something while creating the reply (e.g. "Download external content?"), the companion waits for it. That question used to sit behind the companion window, so everything looked frozen. Now the companion brings Outlook to the front, no longer stays on top of it and shows "Outlook öffnet den Entwurf … bitte dort antworten". While the Spark writes, the seconds count up, and "Abbrechen" stops it.

**Speaking into the companion windows (V01.0.466):** with the part "Dictation" on, every free text field in the companion windows has a mic button: "Draft a reply" (notes and mail text), "Improve text" (wish, source text, result), "Ask about the screen" (question) and, as before, "Ask Spark". First click starts, the second (■) stops the recording (at most 60 seconds); the Spark recognizes the text, it goes to the cursor or the end and is never run. So the Outlook classic buttons "Draft a reply" and "Improve text" can be used by voice too. Never in code word or password fields. The Office add-ins in Word, Excel and new Outlook have no mic button. Button labels never break inside a word; if the Mac panel is too narrow, "Spark unterstützen" moves to its own line.

**Companion tidied up (V01.0.729, Dominik 2026-10-17):** the window on clicking the face is reordered: at the top a greeting, device and state as a dot, on the right a gear (settings) and ✕ or power; below the field "Frag Spark …" (Enter asks, the microphone asks by voice), the commands as tiles with their hotkey and sections like in the panel: next up, notes (Windows), home and "this computer" or "this Mac" with switches for microphone and wake word and, when "act on the PC" is on, the row "act on the PC … set up" (a running screen session can also be ended there on the PC). At the bottom "Open panel" and "Notaus …". The settings have a side menu like the panel (overview with the state and the parts switched on in the panel, Ask Spark, hotkeys, start of the day, knowledge folder, below "device" with pairing and About and update; on the Mac also permissions); pages of parts that are off are left out. The coffee button is only in About and update now, quietly. Parts, rights and data are unchanged.

**Room mode** (Features → Home → "Room mode", off by default; profiles only): the switch "Raum" below the face lets the assistant listen to the conversation in the room at this device for a chosen time (15 minutes to 4 hours, it ends by itself); an orange dot shows it. In a pause it helps when a fixed cue came up: an open question (with web search, nothing when in doubt), an appointment ("Shall I enter it?"), "cold", "too warm" or "too dark" (the room's real temperature from Home Assistant, offering to change the heating by one degree or switch on the light) and things to buy (Home Assistant shopping list). It changes something only after a "yes", for the smart home with the code word when one is set. In the "Me" window under "Room mode" each device sets the duration, how much it may say (questions only, also hints, also comments), each kind on its own, the device's room in Home Assistant and "text only" (silently in the conversation instead of spoken). What it hears stays in the panel's memory for five minutes and is never stored; room mode uses neither memory, mail nor earlier conversations. Everyone in the room should know it is listening. Since V01.0.95 it also offers a timer ("The pizza needs 12 more minutes") and a reminder ("I must not forget to call grandma"), both only after a "yes", and calculates unit questions ("How much is 180 degrees in Fahrenheit?"). A question that points back ("When did he die?") is completed from the last sentences, only with words that were said. With a taught voice and speaker identification on, only the profile's own voice can say "yes". A soft tone comes before it speaks; "Stopp" ends the sentence, "Nicht jetzt" keeps it quiet for 15 minutes, "Raummodus aus" (also "Hör auf zuzuhören") switches it off, from any voice. Since V01.0.126 the profile's quiet hours apply (Me → By itself, 22 to 7 by default): then everything appears as text only, never spoken. When the page is in the background or the phone locked for more than two minutes (the microphone hears nothing there anyway), room mode ends with its summary; closing the tab ends it at once. Since V01.0.128 it also runs on the own ESP32 speakers, all board variants: "Raummodus an" after the wake word or the switch "Room" under Me → Speakers (when the speaker sleeps, it starts at its next wake word), where duration, how much it may say and the room in Home Assistant are set per speaker. The Spark keeps the connection open, cuts out every sentence, runs it through speech recognition and speaks in a pause after the soft tone; boards with a display show the sentence. It ends after its time, with "Raummodus aus", the switch, when the connection drops or when the admin or the profile switch it off; then the board waits for its wake word again. In quiet hours the speaker stays silent. Since V01.0.140 the speaker cuts long stretches of talk after at most 8 seconds and checks every sentence on its own, speaks after 1.5 seconds of quiet (counted from the last loud sound, not from the end of a piece), streams the speech, and each speaker has "detect more". Questions are now recognized without a question mark from the speech recognition ("Wie hoch ist die Zugspitze"), after "Sag mal" or "Weißt du, wann …" and with "Stimmt es, dass …"; questions about the people in the room ("Wo ist meine Brille") stay out. The answer is looked up while people still talk and is ready in the pause (in the browser too). "Also comments" is chattier: at most every two minutes, after three seconds of quiet, one casual sentence about the conversation. Since V01.0.142 Me → Speakers has "Stimme hier anlernen" (speaker identification on, browser login, with the second step when set up): the speaker asks for three sentences and keeps its own voiceprint for its microphone; a sentence counts for a profile when it fits any of its voiceprints (browser or speaker). Until somebody taught a voice through a speaker, it listens to everybody despite "only known voices" (log: "listens to everybody (no voice taught through it yet)"), because a voice taught in the browser is rarely recognized through the small board microphone. At most two speakers at once, because speech recognition runs all the time. While it speaks, the wake word interrupts it. Option "Genauer erkennen": the model looks for missed questions, appointments and shopping, counted only with a word-for-word quote. When room mode ends, the page shows what it said and did (not what it heard), not stored. Every decision writes a "room:" line to the log (why it spoke or stayed silent, never the heard text).

**Ignoring TV voices** (since V01.0.139; Features → Room mode → "Fernsehstimmen überhören", off by default): every device picks under Me → Room mode "Who it listens to" (speakers under Me → Speakers): *everybody* (as before, default), *only known voices while the TV is on* (recommended) or *always only known voices*. A voice is known when a profile taught it under Me → Speaker identification; speaker identification must be on. It then runs for every sentence in room mode, on the CPU next to speech recognition (about 0.1 s per sentence), with no extra GPU load.
