Whether the TV is on comes from Home Assistant at most every 30 seconds: a media player on, playing or buffering in the device's room, without a room set anywhere in the home; when Home Assistant does not answer, it listens to everybody as before. A sentence in a foreign voice triggers no question or suggestion and is dropped at once; the model never sees it, not with "Genauer erkennen" or comments either. Always counted: a sentence starting with "Spark" (also from visitors), "Stopp", "Nicht jetzt" and "Raummodus aus". A "yes" still counts only in the profile's voice. Very short sentences (under about one second of speech) are often not recognized and count as foreign. With *Trial* (on at first) nothing is ignored, only counted: at the end the summary and the log say "Probelauf: Von 40 Sätzen hätte ich 12 als fremde Stimme überhört.", for speakers also under *Check*. Only this number is kept, never the text or whose voice it was; the log says "room: ignored (unknown voice)" or "(unknown voice, tv on)".

**Where it listens right now** (since V01.0.208; part of room mode, no switch of its own): the Spark keeps a list of every device in room mode, browser pages and speakers, in memory only and only with name, kind, since/until, "who it listens to" and trial, never anything heard. A page signs in when switched on and gives a sign of life every minute; without one for three minutes (tab closed, phone locked) it drops out. After a restart it signs in again with its next sign of life. It shows (1) as a green strip on top of every page ("Kitchen listens until 21:30", View opens Me → Room mode), (2) at the face as "Also the kitchen is listening", (3) under Me → Room mode → *Active now* with *+30 min* and *Stop* (speakers waiting for their next wake word with *Do not start*), (4) for the admin under Status → *Listening right now* with profile, device and time, *Stop* and *Stop all*, and as a mark "listening" and the filter "listening right now" under People and devices. A page in room mode shows "● Room" in the tab title and a red dot on its icon. Everybody who sees the list may end a room (it only means less listening); extending only in the profile's browser login, never beyond 4 hours from now. A browser's name is set under Me → Room mode → *Name of this device* (else e.g. "iPad · Safari", with a device key its name). *Reminder tone* (per device, speakers under Me → Speakers, off): the soft tone every 15 minutes, never in quiet hours. *Note when a speaker starts listening* (profile, off): when somebody says "Raummodus an" at your speaker, a note goes to the device used last, at most once an hour per speaker, never in quiet hours. *Recently*: the last 20 times within 14 days with device, time, how it ended and how many sentences were heard, said and ignored, never text (`users/<profile>/room-history.json`, in the backup). **Home Assistant** (admin: Features → Room mode → *Room mode for Home Assistant*, off): under Me → Room mode → Home Assistant the profile makes a key of its own (with the second step when set up; a new one replaces the old). It may only `GET /api/room/active` (count, first device, until when) and `POST /api/room/end` (`{"all": true}`), nothing else; the page shows the ready text for `configuration.yaml` once (REST sensor "Spark Raum-Modus", command `spark_raummodus_aus`). The log only says "room: listed …", "room: … ended (panel|iphone|ha|admin)" and "room: page … gave no sign of life".

**Starting from afar** (since V01.0.213; admin: Features → Room mode → *Start room mode from afar*, off; then for every profile): "Raummodus im Wohnzimmer an" (also "Kannst du den Raummodus für den Lautsprecher im Wohnzimmer aktivieren?", "… für 60 Minuten") in the panel, the iPhone app or at another speaker. The panel recognizes the sentence by fixed rules (`roomfar.py`); the language model gets no tools in that answer, Home Assistant is not asked and no code word is wanted. Only the profile's own speakers, found by name or by the room entered under Me → Speakers; when several fit, the answer names them. The speaker must be connected right now (it does not start later by itself; the switch "Room" is for that). Only a plain "Ja" in the next message of the same conversation (15 minutes) starts it; the speaker says "Raum-Modus an, gestartet von der iPhone-App" (or the panel, the kitchen speaker). Duration from the sentence (rounded to 15/30/60/120/240 minutes), else the speaker's own. Not via Telegram or Siri, never for guests or a voice recognized as someone else. "Raummodus im Wohnzimmer aus" / "Raummodus überall aus" ends without a question (history: "ended from another device"). With the switch off there is a fixed answer naming the switch. "Starte den Raummodus" without a place stays the device's own room mode. Log: "room: start at speaker … asked from …", "room: on at speaker … (remote from …)".

**Speaking up by itself** (Features → My day → "Speaking up by itself", off by default; profiles only): once released it is on from the start for every profile; in the "Me" window under "By itself" each profile sets why the assistant speaks unasked, each kind on its own, or switches it off entirely: appointments ahead ("In 20 minutes: dentist. Shall I remind you again at 9:55?", a "yes" sets the reminder), its own smart home rules ("Whirlpool above 37.5", "bathroom window is open and rain is on", "washing machine power below 5 for 3 minutes"; read only, never switching), a short greeting with the next appointment or reminder when the conversation is opened after three hours or more, at most one follow-up a day on a plan mentioned yesterday, new mail from chosen senders (sender and subject only) and a weather note for tomorrow via web search. Plus quiet hours, a daily limit and learning from feedback: "nicht jetzt" means two hours of quiet, "das interessiert mich nicht" halves how often that kind may speak (can be reset). The panel decides when, by fixed rules from real data; where the model words something (follow-up, weather), its answer only counts when it quotes the data. With the conversation open the note is spoken, otherwise it arrives as a push notification. Since V01.0.179 only the first device of the profile says it (page or iPhone app), the others stay silent, just like reminders (V01.0.176). Every note is in the log with its reason. Since V01.0.385 the follow-up is a fixed sentence with the person's own words ("Gestern hast du zu mir gesagt: „…“. Hat das geklappt?"); the model only points at a sentence, and the panel takes it only when it is an own plan in the first person (no question, no request to the assistant, no pasted text, no question about a mail or web page, nothing from briefing, jobs or meetings). Asked about a note ("what do you mean?", "where is that from?"), the model gets its basis from the panel and must not make up another source; for mail notes only "sender and subject read", never mail text. Logs → Requests show it as "von selbst: Grundlage", Live shows a refused pick as an event. Since V01.0.810 the mail rows (important mail, mailbox tidying, parcel comes today) sit under E-mail → Settings and appointments ahead under Calendar → Settings; Me → By itself keeps the main switch, quiet hours, the daily limit and the other kinds.

**Home Assistant tells the Spark** (since V01.0.314, plan `plaene/home-assistant-meldungen.md`): the smart home rules under "By itself" can do more. Each rule can speak *at most every N minutes* (e.g. a motion sensor at most every 10 minutes), for "changes" *on*, *off* or both, and have an extra condition: *only when I am away* (the person in Home Assistant with your profile name or the one chosen under "Me in Home Assistant"), *only when nobody is home*, *only at night* or *only during the day* (by `sun.sun`, else 9 pm to 6 am). Plus five parts, each with its own admin switch under Features → Speaking up by itself, all off at first, never for guests. Once released they work for every profile; what speaks up by itself is on from the start and can be switched off one by one under Me → By itself: *Instant notes from Home Assistant*: the Spark keeps a WebSocket connection to your Home Assistant (the same address and token, only the devices of your rules, `subscribe_entities`); a change arrives in about a second, also a motion sensor that triggers only briefly. If the connection drops, the Spark checks every minute again. *House notes on speakers*: a rule also says its own sentence on chosen speakers that take announcements from you, e.g. "Someone rang the bell."; only its own sentence without names, never for person devices and never with a camera picture, not in the speaker's quiet hours, at most 20 an hour. *Home Assistant tells the Spark*: a list of event names with a fixed sentence (e.g. `klingel` → "Es hat geklingelt.") and a key of its own; Home Assistant calls `rest_command.spark_melden` with `event: klingel` (`POST /api/ha/event`). Only names from the list count, nothing else from the request is read or said; at most 30 a minute, a pause per event (default 1 minute). *Smart home rule by voice*: "Sag mir Bescheid, wenn jemand an der Haustür ist", "Melde dich, wenn Anna heimkommt": the panel recognizes the sentence by fixed words, finds the device and asks "Soll ich mich melden, wenn …?"; only a "yes" in the same conversation adds the rule. *Describe a camera picture*: a rule can name a camera from Home Assistant; when it applies, the local language model describes the still in a few words. The picture is never stored, the description is outside text and goes to your devices only, at most 30 an hour. Log lines start with "hamelden:". Status → Live shows the live connection (under channels) and each note, event and described camera picture in fixed words, without device names, sentences or camera words. You don't have to type devices for a rule (since V01.0.317): the field suggests all devices from Home Assistant, "Zuletzt geändert" shows the last changed ones, and with "Gerät erkennen" you trigger the sensor within 40 seconds (motion, door, bell); whatever changes shows up to tap. The panel only compares the change time Home Assistant reports itself. Rules can be edited (since V01.0.319): "Edit" brings a rule back into the form, "Check and save" checks it like a new one and replaces it in the same place; it then starts fresh (speaks only after it was false once). Since V01.0.334 each fired rule and event shows on Status → Live as a request of "Home Assistant" (rule or event) with camera picture, note (Web Push, Apple Push, Android app, Telegram or the open page) and speaker announcement; everything else the Spark says by itself as a request of "Von selbst" (e.g. greeting, reminder, task). Never the sentence.

**Natural turn-taking** (on by default, conversation settings): during a short pause the recording is transcribed already. A finished sentence ends the turn at once and its transcript becomes the question, so the answer starts noticeably sooner; after "and …", "because …", "um" or a comma the assistant waits up to 2 s for you to go on.

**New conversation every day** (on by default, conversation settings → Display): the first question of a new day starts a new conversation; earlier ones stay in the history and can be picked and continued.

**Speaker identification** (off by default, Features): each profile reads three short sentences in the "Me" window. When the assistant then clearly recognizes a voice, it answers for that profile, with its memory, documents, reminders and voice, even on a guest device. The conversation shows "🎙 name" below the question. When someone other than the logged-in profile speaks at a device, the assistant gets only that one question (not the device's conversation), and the exchange stays out of the logged-in profile's history and memory. When the voice is unclear, nothing changes. Recognition runs on the Spark's CPU (Resemblyzer model, Apache 2.0) and needs no GPU memory. Strictness is adjustable. A voice is not a password: someone who sounds very similar or plays a recording can get into a profile.

**Profiles and memory:** under People and devices the admin creates profiles with a name and PIN. In the chat you log in with the "Guest" button at the top of the conversation by typing name and PIN. Logged in, the assistant remembers things for good when you tell it to ("remember that I'm vegetarian") or when they will be useful later, and forgets them on request; the Me lists what it knows, for deleting. Memory and conversations belong to the profile and live on the Spark, so the same history shows on every device you are logged in on: which profile is asking is decided only by the login (cookie) or the device key, never by the model, and guests get no memory. Speakers and your own programs get a device key under People and devices that belongs to one profile, and send it as the header `X-Speech-Device` to `/api/chat`. A new PIN logs out every browser of that profile; deleting a profile removes its memory and devices. The data lives on the Spark under `/var/lib/speech-spark/users`, one folder per profile.

**Home Assistant** (Funktionen → "Home Assistant", off by default): each profile connects its own Home Assistant in the "Me" window under "Smart home" with the address (e.g. `http://homeassistant.local:8123`) and a long-lived access token (Home Assistant: your profile → Security; best for a separate non-admin user). Then "turn on the living room light" or "how warm is it in the bathroom?" work in the conversation. Commands go to Home Assistant's own Assist (`/api/conversation/process`), so only entities exposed to Assist (in Home Assistant: Settings → Voice assistants → Expose) can be switched. Devices Assist does not know are switched directly through their service (e.g. `switch.turn_on`), except locks, alarm systems and updates. With a **code word** ("Me" window → Smart home) the assistant switches only when the code word is in the same message ("lights off, code word sunflower"); without it, it asks. The panel checks this itself, not the language model; the model and the stored conversations see only "[Codewort]", it is stored encrypted and compared with some tolerance for speech recognition (a letter or two off, "Apollo 13" = "Apollo dreizehn"). Questions need no code word. Also (ideas from ha-mcp): history ("how warm was it in the bathroom yesterday?"), several devices at once ("all lights in the living room off", each checked; an unknown room never switches the whole home), typo-tolerant device search and to-do lists such as the shopping list (read, add, tick off; changes need the code word). Questions about values, states, zones and where someone is are read directly (`/api/states`) instead: the assistant sees every device, sensor, zone and person the token's user may see, exposed or not. Home Assistant tells rooms (areas) only to an admin token; without admin rights devices are found by their names only. Only that profile can use its Home Assistant: guests never, and a voice recognized by speaker identification at someone else's device neither. The token is checked before saving, stays on the Spark (`users/<id>/homeassistant.json`, mode 0600) and is never sent back to the browser. When several devices fit a switching command, the device whose name, room or device class fits wins over one that only fits by its kind ("TV in the living room" is the TV there, not the Apple TV box next to it). If it stays unclear, the Spark switches nothing and names the fitting devices; the model then gets no switching tool (since V01.0.461).

**Ask instead of guessing** (Funktionen → "Rückfrage statt Raten", off by default; released for every profile): when several devices fit a switching command, the Spark switches nothing at random but asks: "Several fit: Samsung, Samsung The Frame or TV. Which one do you mean?". In the chat the devices are buttons below it, plus "None of these". When it cannot find the device exactly it asks "I can't find that exactly. Do you mean …?"; "None of these" or "show all" brings the whole device list with a search field, grouped by room (never locks, alarm panels or remotes). The answer may be typed, spoken or tapped: name, room or number ("the second"). It only counts in the same conversation, on the same device and for two minutes; the code word of the command covers the choice but is never stored or spoken. When the Spark itself picked one of several devices, "No, the Apple TV" corrects it: the wrongly switched device goes back to its old state, the right one is switched. Fixed rules in the panel decide, never the language model. (since V01.0.470)

**Remember the choice** (sub-switch, off by default): after a choice or a correction the Spark asks "Shall I remember that "TV in the living room" is Samsung?" (buttons "Yes, remember" and "No"). Only a yes stores it. From then on the same sentence switches that device at once; the code word is still needed. All mappings are under Me → Memory → "Mappings": change words, other target, delete, or assign your own from the device list with "Give own names". They live in the profile's `zuordnungen.json` and go with the memory into your own storage and the backup. The live page shows numbers only (open questions, asked today, chosen, corrected, remembered, used), the log fixed words only ("klaeren: frage ha 3 kandidaten"), never devices or sentences. (since V01.0.470)

**Asking back for bus and train, weather and messages too** (same switch): "to the station" fits several stops and names none exactly, so the Spark asks with buttons instead of taking the first; the same for place names in different regions ("Karlsbad" in Germany and Czechia) in the weather and for lists in Home Assistant ("Einkauf" fits "Einkaufsliste" and "Einkauf Baumarkt"; for a change the code word of the request covers the choice). The choice runs the same request with the chosen target; after a yes the word stands for it from then on (Me → Memory → Mappings, areas "Bus and train", "Places (weather)" and "Lists"). With "Do you mean Quirin Meier or Quirin Müller?" the names are buttons below. In Telegram the choices come as a keyboard under the answer; a tap sends the name like a typed answer. In the apps and at a speaker the spoken or typed answer does it (name, room or number). A calendar named for an appointment that does not exist is never silently replaced by the first one any more: the Spark enters nothing and names the calendars there are. (since V01.0.470)

**Searching conversations**: the magnifier next to the conversation list (on phones in the menu) searches all earlier conversations, a profile's with a profile, the browser's as a guest. A click opens the conversation at that spot.

**Earlier conversations** (Funktionen → "Frühere Gespräche", on by default; profiles only): the assistant can look up what you talked about before ("what did you tell me about the grill last week?", "what did we talk about yesterday?"), searching only your own profile's saved conversations. Conversations that have been quiet for ten minutes are also read once in the background, while nobody is talking, and up to three lasting facts per conversation go into the profile's memory, marked "automatic" in the "Me" window, where they can be deleted. Each profile can switch this learning off in the conversation settings ("Learn from conversations"). Once a week (or with "Propose cleanup") the assistant proposes merging duplicate entries and removing outdated ones; nothing changes until you accept the proposal under Me → Memory. With notifications on, a note is sent.

**Pronunciation:** Settings → Speech output → Pronunciation takes one rule per line, e.g. `DGX = De Ge Ix`. It applies to whole words and to all speech output, Open WebUI included.

**Barge-in:** while the assistant speaks, the microphone keeps listening. Talking for about a quarter of a second interrupts the answer, and what you said becomes the next question. This relies on the browser's echo cancellation; if the assistant interrupts itself on loud speakers, use headphones or untick "barge in" below the chat. With "Barge-in: pause first" (see below) the answer only pauses, and a "mhm" lets it play on.

**Read answers aloud (since V01.0.409):** if you do not want speech output, switch it off. For one device, the "Vorlesen" switch below the face (on a phone the speaker icon at the top right of the chat) is enough; it applies to this browser or the Android app only. For all devices of the profile: conversation settings → answer → "Read answers aloud". The Spark then answers as text only, does not call the speech output at all (saves the GPU) and plays no instant acknowledgement either; notes, messages and reminders come with a chime and text but are not read aloud. Speakers without a screen and CarPlay keep speaking, there is nothing to read there. "Read again" on an answer still works. The Spark-Begleiter does not read "Frag Spark" answers aloud either while "Read answers aloud" is off.

**Your own voice:** under Voices you can record a reference straight from the microphone (read the suggested text, about 10 seconds); the panel fills in the transcript with the speech recognition. Cloning needs a Base model (Settings → Speech output → model `…-Base`), then pick the new voice as the default voice. The default voice is a list that fits the chosen model: CustomVoice shows the built-in speakers, Base your own voices, with VoiceDesign it is locked (the voice comes from the speaking style); a saved name missing from the list stays as "(not in the list)". A German reference gives much steadier German than the built-in speakers, which are not native German speakers. Under Voices, one or all of your own voices can be exported as a ZIP (reference audio and transcript) and imported again on this or another Spark; on a name clash the import renames, replaces or skips.

**Required tools and answer check (V01.0.117):** questions about appointments, mail, reminders, timers, weather, parcels, transit, lists, match results and news have to go through the matching tool (one table `NEED_TOOLS` in `chat.py`, also used by the quality test). While the model chooses the tool, the "tool choice" temperature (0.1) applies, the normal one afterwards. The answer check (Settings → Language model, on by default) lets no sentence through for such questions that names a time, date, score or amount that is in no tool result, the question or the conversation so far; instead the assistant says once "Genauere Angaben dazu habe ich nicht." If the model answers without looking anything up, the answer is dropped and the model is asked once more. Both show up in the tool log as "Antwort-Prüfung". General questions without a tool are not checked. Optional: the admin allows "think while choosing tools" (off); then it works for every profile, never for guests. Since V01.0.404 a round with thinking holds its text until the thinking is over: when the server does not hand the thinking over apart (reasoning_content) but as text up to a lone `</think>` (Qwen gets the opening `<think>` in the prompt), everything before it is never shown, spoken or kept; a lone `</think>` is never shown (before, the answer came twice with `</think>` between). Since V01.0.455 no sentence of an answer comes twice: when the model writes the answer before a tool call (also one it only writes as text) and again after the result, the next round shows each sentence only once it is complete and leaves out sentences already said, in the chat and in the voice; Logs → Requests then show "Repetition left out" with the count.

**Check deeds and stop error loops (V01.0.477):** two protection rules, on by default like the answer check (Settings → Language model, `chat.action_check`, `chat.loop_stop`, reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`), without a switch per profile (`taten.py`, fixed rules, no model). **Check deeds:** every tool that changes something has a kind (`taten.KIND`: geraet, erinnerung, termin, mail_entwurf, dokument, remarkable, nachricht, merken, aufgabe, musik and more). Per answer the panel keeps which kind worked (the same failure words as Logs → Requests; "NOT saved yet" counts as a proposal, not as a deed). When a finished sentence claims a deed ("is on now", "I have entered it", "has been sent", "I'll remember", a bare "Done." while something that changes things was offered) and none of the kinds it names worked, it is neither shown nor spoken. At the end comes one fixed sentence: "That did not work: TV." (the tool ran and failed) or "I did not do that." (it did not run); nothing while a proposal is open. No second try. When a tool of that kind works later in the same answer ("I'm making the song." before `musik_machen`), the held sentence is left out silently (since V01.0.700). In doubt the sentence is said (it names several deeds and one of them worked). **Stop error loops:** when the same tool fails twice with the same details (or three times with any), it is not offered again for the rest of the answer, the model hears why, a further call is not run. Visible in Logs → Requests ("Taten-Prüfung", "Schleife gestoppt"), in the journal (`chat: action check: held back kind=… reason=…`, `chat: loop stop: tool=… fails=…`) and on the Live page ("Taten-Prüfung": held back and stopped today, numbers only). The settings assistant's rule set will use the same interface `taten.check(text, acts, offered)` (a fixed "built-in" rule).

**Three red questions in the quality test (V01.0.574):** (1) **Announced deed:** a sentence that only announces a tool call or a deed ("I'm calling the timer tool …", "I'll set a timer …", `taten.announces`) is neither shown nor spoken while no tool has worked in this answer. When a tool call follows, it is left out silently; when none follows, the panel says "I did not do that." at the end. Check deeds now also applies when no tool at all was offered (then every claimed deed is false). Journal: `chat: action check: held back kind=ankuendigung …`, `chat: action check: announcement left out, a tool ran`. (2) **Remember only what there is to remember:** a message that only asks (one sentence with a question mark, no "remember", nothing the person says about themselves like "I am …"; `chat.just_asks`) is not offered `memory_save` (`chat.offer_memory`, in the quality test as well), so "Do you know my brother's name?" never saves a made-up fact; journal `chat: memory_save not offered - the message only asks`. (3) **Account balance:** there is no bank tool, so every honest answer is right ("no access", "no tool", "check your banking app"). Since the fix after V01.0.597 the quality test checks no wording here, only facts: an amount in figures or words (`quality.MONEY`) or an unsuitable tool fails. The two cases without calendar and mail access still accept every "no access" answer (`quality.NO_ACCESS`).

**Appointments only fresh from the calendar (V01.0.348):** "Guten Morgen" on its own (also with "Spark", "wie geht's") counts as asking for the day: the switch files it under the calendar and the first round has to call `daily_briefing` (without the briefing: `calendar_events`). Before another request ("Guten Morgen, turn on the light") it does not. The same for the briefing by name, also as one word (V01.0.847): "Morgenbriefing", "Gib mir mein Tagesbriefing", "Was ist mein Morgenbriefing?" go to the calendar and call `daily_briefing`; only "Was ist das Morgenbriefing?" or "Wie stelle ich es ein?" asks about the function (Spark help). Every calendar result marks appointments that have already ended with "(already over)"; briefing and daily overview do not name them as coming up. Once an answer has read the calendar, the answer check takes times, dates and quoted appointment titles only from the question, the instructions and this answer's results, no longer from earlier answers in the conversation, so yesterday's appointments never come back as today's (tool log: "Termin aus früherer Antwort").

**Learning from corrections (V01.0.118):** admin switch Features → Conversation → "Learning from corrections", off; released for every profile, never guests or another voice. When a message right after an answer starts like a correction ("Nein, …", "Das stimmt nicht", "Du sollst …", at least three words), the model words a short sentence on the side (`fixes.py`) and the assistant ends with "Soll ich mir merken: …?". It is stored only after a plain yes from the same conversation within 15 minutes. Never proposed: sentences about code words, PINs, passwords, rules, security, confirmations, locks or alarms, world knowledge (scores, amounts) and links; after mail or outside text there is no proposal, and the earlier answer is then not sent along. The tool log shows a "Korrektur" entry; "Send the question to the admin as a test case" puts the corrected question into `quality-cases.json` (at most 30) and it runs with the quality test: all tools offered, each finds nothing, made-up figures count as a failure. The admin deletes them under Status → Checks → Quality test.

**Memory check (V01.0.467):** admin switch Features → Memory check, on by default since V01.1.247 (protection: what may be out of date is never stated as a fact; reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`, plan *nutzerwissen*, question 1); released for every profile, never guests (`gedaechtnis.py`, plan `gedaechtnis-pruefen/plan.md`). Every entry keeps its evidence, also while the switch is off: where it came from (`said` remembered in a conversation, `fix` from a confirmed correction, `auto` learned from a quiet conversation, `hand` changed under Me, `tidy` from an accepted cleanup proposal), which conversation (own ones only, never to the model) and when it was last confirmed. Entries of older versions count as `auto` when they were learned, else as `said`. Fixed rules of the panel, no model, mark: **R1** a date it names is more than a day past (never birthdays, "seit", "ab", "jeden"; "Still right" after the date keeps it), **R2** "tomorrow", "next week" … older than 14 days or "currently", "right now" … older than 90 days, **R3** learned automatically, never confirmed and older than "Learned counts as old after" (setting under Features, 1–60 months, default 6), **R4** contradiction: two entries say the same thing with another value (fixed patterns "X is called", "lives in", "works at/as", "drives a", "is married to", "favourite … is", kept apart by whom it is about); the one said or confirmed last wins. Nothing is deleted. The model gets the entries in three blocks: sure, learned (with the month), "maybe outdated, never say it as a fact". Under Me → Memory there are marks (confirmed, learned, outdated?, contradiction), the filter "Outdated", a note card and, on a tap, where from (with "Open the conversation"), remembered, confirmed, the reason and "Still right" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/confirm`, rate limit "memory"). Live: node "Memory" with counts per mark; Logs: area "Memory" (`gedaechtnis: pruefung …`, only when the numbers change); Logs → Requests: step "Memory (panel)" with numbers.

**Versions and check-back (V01.0.467):** with Memory check on, a confirmed correction about the same thing ("lives in …", "drives a …", "X is called …") becomes a new version of that entry instead of a second entry; ✎ Change keeps the old wording too. Up to 5 earlier versions show in the detail under Me → Memory with "Bring back" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/restore`, login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit). Sub-switch Features → Memory check → "Check back in conversations" (`chat.memory_ask`, off; released for every profile): when an entry that may be out of date fits the question (a shared word, fixed rule), the panel adds "Quick check: is this still right: …?" after the answer. At most once per conversation, three times a day, the same entry once in 30 days; never with someone else's voice, after mail or web pages, during an emergency stop or while another question is open. The answer is open for 15 minutes (`users/<id>/memory-ask.json`): "yes" confirms, "forget it" deletes, "no" changes nothing, a correction goes through "Learn from corrections" and becomes a new version after a yes. Live shows open questions as a number, Logs → Requests the step "Memory check-back (panel)", the journal `gedaechtnis: nachfrage …` and `gedaechtnis: fassung … grund=…`.

**Memory topics (V01.0.467):** sub-switch Features → Memory check → "Memory topics" (`chat.memory_topics`, off; released for every profile). In the background (never while someone is talking) the language model in the home network sorts entries without a topic once into a fixed topic: person, home, on the move, work, health, right now, other; any other answer becomes "other", after a failure it tries again only after an hour. Me → Memory then shows the entries in groups; in the detail the person picks another topic (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/topic`), which the model never changes again. The topic is only for display, does not go to the model when answering and changes no rights. Live shows "N ohne Thema", the journal `gedaechtnis: themen … gesetzt=N`. The quality test has the cases "gedaechtnis-veraltet" (an outdated entry is never stated as a fact) and "gedaechtnis-bestaetigt" (a confirmed one is used).

**Cap without silent throwing away (V01.1.247, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 1):** a profile has at most 200 entries in its memory (`profiles.MAX_FACTS`; earlier versions do not count). Until now the 201st entry silently pushed out the oldest one, even one the person had said themselves. Now a fixed rule of the panel applies (`profiles._cap`, when remembering and when tidying up): above the cap the oldest entries learned by itself and never confirmed make room first (`auto` without "Still right"). Entries said, corrected, changed by hand, tidied or confirmed never go. When only such entries are left, the new entry is refused and nothing is lost: `memory_save` answers "Not done: the memory is full …", the assistant says so and names the way Me → Memory ("Suggest tidy-up" or delete entries); learning by itself stores nothing, "Learn from corrections" says "not remembered, memory full". A correction about a fixed thing still becomes a new version and needs no room. Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: platz frei … | entfernt=N grund=alt-gelernt` and once a day per profile `gedaechtnis: voll … | abgelehnt=said|auto max=200 | next log in 24 h`, never the text of an entry. Live: the node "Memory" shows "N Profile voll". Self-test `test_gedaechtnis_grenze`.

**Fixed topics (V01.1.248, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 2, Dominik 2026-10-29 "a"):** with Memory check the panel knows fixed topic places (`gedaechtnis.SLOTS`, one list, it replaces the former `KEYS`; R4 reads the same): home, employer, job, partner, children, birthday, diet (vegetarian, vegan …), car, pet (one place per kind of animal), names ("brother is called …") and favourites, each apart by whom it is about (`WHO`: "brother lives in …" is the place `wohnort:bruder`). Only fixed patterns of the panel recognize them (`gedaechtnis.slot_of`), never the model; a sentence about another person by name ("Anna lives in Ulm") or without a value ("lives near …") fills no place. Allergies add up (nuts and pollen) and are therefore no place. Per place one entry holds the value: what the person says (`memory_save`), corrects or changes by hand gives it the new wording, the old one stays as an earlier version (at most 5, reason "new value for the same topic", `grund=platz` in the journal). **A value learned by itself never replaces one that was said or confirmed:** it waits on the entry as an offer and comes as a card "Learned" under Open (`vorgaenge`, kind `gedplatz`, not urgent, only under Open) with "Take it" and "Discard"; the same buttons are under Me → Memory at "Fixed topics" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/offer` with `{"do": "take"|"drop"}`, login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit "memory"; also the iPhone app). "Take it" makes the learned value the wording (the old one becomes an earlier version, reason "learned value taken"), "Discard" keeps the value so the same one never asks again; if the person says something about the topic first, the offer goes with its card. Learned over learned: the newer wins, the old one stays as a version. The model gets per place only the value that holds it; older contradicting entries, earlier versions and offers never. Me → Memory shows the box "Fixed topics" on top: every own place (empty as "–") and every other place held, with value, source, "since" and "n earlier"; tapping opens the versions with "Bring back". On top also "N of 200", yellow from 180 (`profiles.WARN_FACTS`), red at 200; when the memory is full, a card "Gedächtnis voll – aufräumen" comes once under Open (with "Show" to Me → Memory, kind `gedvoll`; at most once a day again), it goes by itself as soon as there is room again. iPhone: My things → Memory with "Fixed topics", Take it/Discard, "Bring back" (press and hold) and "N of 200"; Android shows the panel. Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: angebot … | platz=wohnort`, `gedaechtnis: angebot uebernommen|verworfen …`, `gedaechtnis: fassung … | grund=platz|gelernt-uebernommen`; never a value. Live: the node "Memory" also shows "N gelernt, wartet auf Übernehmen". Self-test `test_gedaechtnis` (Places, FullCard).

**Remembered note (V01.1.249, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 3, Dominik 2026-10-29 "a"):** when the assistant remembers something in a conversation (`memory_save`), "✓ Remembered: …" stands under the answer with "Undo" and "Show" (opens Me → Memory). The event `memory` carries the entry's number, its state (`rev`, a short fingerprint of wording, source, confirmation and time, `gemerkt.rev_of`) and whether there is something to take back; the conversation keeps only number and state (`mem`, `profiles._clean_msg`), the page takes the text from the memory when it draws it again, so the note stays after reloading and on the other devices. "Undo" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/undo` with `{"rev"}`, `gemerkt.py`; login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit "memory"; also the iPhone app) takes back exactly what just happened: a new entry goes, a fixed place gets its earlier version back as it was (source, confirmation; the wording taken back is not kept as a version). This works only while the entry is unchanged since, otherwise 409 "Changed since"; only own entries, never guests. "Undo that" right after (`aendern.undo`) takes the same way. **What was learned by itself** (learning from quiet conversations) does not go into the old conversation but comes as a card "Remembered by itself" (`vorgaenge`, kind `gemerkt`, key `gemerkt:<conversation>`, up to 3 entries, not urgent, never said into the conversation): next time on top under "Still open", under Open and as a number on the card Memory of Me → Overview, with "That's right" (confirms), "Undo" and "Show …"; after 7 days the card goes, the entry stays as learned, not confirmed. Its title ("Spark hat sich 2 Dinge gemerkt") is all a notification after "Later" or Telegram gets, never the text of an entry. Begleiter: Frag Spark shows the card "Gemerkt: …" with "Rückgängig" (sends "Mach das rückgängig", which asks for a yes like a typed one) and "Gedächtnis ansehen". iPhone: chip under the answer in the conversation, the card like every one under Open; Android shows the panel. Switch Features → Remembered note (`chat.memory_note`, on by default, reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`: protection and transparency). Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: rueckgaengig geloescht|zurueckgeholt | weg=chip|karte|sprache`, `gedaechtnis: rueckgaengig abgelehnt grund=geaendert|fehlt|gleich`, `gedaechtnis: gemerkt-karte neu|ergaenzt fakten=N`, `gedaechtnis: gemerkt-karte stimmt fakten=N`; never a value. Live: the node "Memory" with "heute N gemerkt · N Karten · N rückgängig · N bestätigt". Self-test `test_gemerkt`.

**One way to the own data (V01.1.250, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 4a):** memory, conversations, tool log, room mode history, the read marks of learning, remembered matches, interests, the Spark diary and the open question about the memory are read and written by the panel only through `profiles.read_own`, `write_own` and `drop_own`, never by a module itself; so they can be sealed in one place (stage 4b). Nothing changes for the person. Self-test `test_siegel` (OnlyThroughTheHelpers).

**Memory and conversations sealed (V01.1.251, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 4b):** memory, conversations, tool log and room mode history now lie on the disk only sealed; whoever copies the files or puts them into another profile sees only noise. At the first start the Spark seals the older open files once, after a backup "vor-siegel". When a backup comes to another Spark without the move backup, the profile starts empty and nothing is overwritten: the old files lie aside, the admins see a hint (Settings → Backup), and after the move backup "Read in again" under Me → Memory brings them back, together with everything that came since. Live shows at the box Memory how many files are sealed and how many lie aside. Self-test `test_siegel`.

**What was learned sealed too (V01.1.252, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 4c):** what the Spark learned about the person now lies sealed as well: the read marks of learning, remembered matches, the open question about the memory, "What I know about you" (interests) and the Spark diary. The start seals the older open files the same way once, after a new backup "vor-siegel"; "Read in again" brings them back too. Every file of memory and conversations in the file register must be sealed (`test_siegel`).

**Download all my data (V01.1.255, plan *nutzerwissen*, stage 5, no switch: right of access):** under Me → Security → My data "Choose …" shows the areas with count and size (each can be left out) and, folded, what stays out and why. "Pack and download" asks a fresh code or passkey every time (without a second step the PIN) and downloads a ZIP with everything that belongs to the own profile: memory, conversations and what was learned opened, documents, lists, calendars, settings without secrets and from chats with others only the own messages; with `LIESMICH.txt` (de+en) and `uebersicht.json`. Login and keys, the voiceprint, queues, read mails and the supplies of foreign servers stay out. At most 3 per hour and 2 GB; the pickup link holds 10 minutes and exactly once. In the iPhone app under Me → Security → My data (with a second step) to share or save, in the Android app through the download. Live shows only counts at the Memory box. Self-test `test_meinedaten`.

**Language model settings (V01.0.121):** Under Settings → Language model → Advanced the admin sets the history length (4000–64000 characters, default 24000), web searches per answer (1–3, default 2), the time limit for an answer (60–600 s), top_p and presence_penalty (0 = the server's default) and own keywords that require a tool (one line per tool, e.g. `web_search: elfmeter, aufstellung`; whole words only, at most 20 per tool, the built-in words always stay). "Restore default" fills in the shipped system prompt, "Show the whole prompt" lists all parts in order and marks the fixed ones. With Features → "Own conversation style" (off) each profile can say under Me → Conversation → Answer, in at most 500 characters, how the assistant should talk to it; the text sits below the system prompt and above the fixed rules, never applies to guests or another voice and cannot be changed with a device key. Fixed: tool rules, handling of outside text, code word, locks and the values of background jobs (learning, briefing, mail sorting, room).

**Roles by voice (V01.0.249):** With the admin switch Features → Own conversation style → Roles by voice (off) every profile adds up to 8 own roles under Me → Conversation → Answer, one per line like "Butler: polite, dry, with British understatement"; nothing needs to be switched on for it. "Sei jetzt der Butler", "Wechsle zur Rolle Erklärbär" or "Rolle Butler" switch, "Sei wieder normal" ends the role, "Welche Rollen hast du?" lists them. The panel recognizes these sentences with fixed rules only in the person's own latest message and only for a saved name ("Sei leise" stays a normal question); that answer has no tools, the model only says the result. The active role applies on all devices of the profile, sits in the prompt after the own conversation style and before the fixed tool rules, and changes only the tone, never the rules. Never guests or another voice; roles are changed only in the own browser, not with a device key, and the admin's defaults never hand out roles. Log line: "rolle: set - Butler".

**Wikipedia straight away (V01.0.249):** With the admin switch Features → Web search → Wikipedia straight away (off, works without a SearXNG address too) every profile the admin does not block gets in the assistant the tool `wikipedia`. For knowledge questions ("What is a quasar?", "Who was Ada Lovelace?") the panel fetches the intro of the matching article with one request to the MediaWiki API (at most 1,500 characters, answer at most 1 MB) instead of SearXNG and whole pages; the model reads far less and starts sooner. With no hit on de.wikipedia.org it asks en.wikipedia.org; the admin can enter an own address (then without a second try). Answers are kept in memory for 24 hours (at most 300). The article is outside text: wrapped as data, no switching or saving in the same answer afterwards. "Wikipedia" or "Lexikon" in the question requires the tool; with "only web search and memory" for unclear questions Wikipedia is included. Only the search words leave the Spark. Never guests.

**Own Kiwix archive (V01.0.258):** With the admin switch Features → Web search → "Own Kiwix archive" (off) and an address (best in the home network, e.g. `http://192.168.178.20:8080`; changing it needs the second sign-in step) the assistant looks for every profile things up in your kiwix-serve. "Choose books" opens the catalog as a list (V01.0.260): search, language (default German and English, "All languages" or one), kind (Wikipedia, TED, PhET, Docs …), grouped, each book with language, variant (maxi/nopic), article count, size and date; chosen books sit on top as chips with ×. At most 10; without a choice the German and English Wikipedia. The book name is saved without its date (V01.0.261): when the Kiwix replaces a file with a newer one (e.g. `…_2025-01` → `…_2025-03`) the Spark takes the newest automatically; when a book answers 404 it reads the catalog again at once, and "Tell me more" finds the article in the new file. "Look in my archive …" always means the Kiwix, never the uploaded documents; when the archive is not available for the profile the assistant says why (admin switch, address, profile blocked) instead of searching the documents (log line `kiwix: archive asked, not offered`). Since V01.0.266 the router also recognises "dein/unser Archiv", "im Archiv" and "Archiv durchsuchen"; when the archive is offered the first round must call exactly `archive_search` (not the document search). The "not available" note comes only when the admin switched the Kiwix on or "Kiwix" is in the question. The tool `wikipedia` asks the Kiwix's Wikipedia books first (title suggestion, else full text) and the online Wikipedia only without a hit or when the Kiwix fails; a profile with only the Kiwix switch gets `wikipedia` without internet. `archive_search` searches the full text of the chosen books (at most 5 at once) and names the best article with further hits; `article_more` reads on in one of them (at most 3 parts of 1,500 characters, only by the id a tool named before). If the web search fails (SearXNG unreachable or no search engine answers), the assistant answers from the Kiwix and says it comes from the offline archive (with its date) and holds nothing current. Security: addresses only from the admin's address plus fixed endpoints (`/catalog/v2/entries`, `/suggest`, `/search`, `/content/<book>/…`), redirects only to the same host and inside the book, netguard HOME (home network allowed, own ports and link-local never), answers at most 1 MB (articles 4 MB), 10 s, XML with a DOCTYPE is refused, HTML only turned into text (no scripts, tables, footnotes). Everything is outside text. Answers are kept 24 hours in memory (at most 300), the catalog an hour. Status → Checks → "Check Kiwix" shows reachability, books and a test search; Logs → area "Knowledge" shows `kiwix:` lines with book, hits and time, never the question. Never guests.

**Look locally first (V01.0.264):** With the admin switch Features → Web search → "Look locally first" (off; time limit 200 to 1500 ms, default 400) the Spark itself, for every profile, first looks in the own sources for knowledge questions before anything goes out. A fixed rule (`lokal.py`, never the model) sorts the person's message: **outside** (web search asked for, weather, trains, parcels, news, prices, rates, "today", "right now", years from 2020 …) goes straight out as before, without delay; **local** (an own source named: "in my documents", "in the archive", "we talked about", "last time") searches only there, the web search is left out of this answer (a new message can ask for it); a **knowledge question** (anything else with search words) gets the quick look. It asks the own and shared documents (full text; meaning search only when that model already runs and is free), the own Kiwix (title suggestion, else full text, only articles whose title fits the question) and the earlier conversations (without answers from mail, without the current conversation) at the same time. What is not back by the time limit is not waited for. A passage counts only when it holds at least 60 % of the search words (question words like "who", "how high" do not count). At most 3 hits of 500 characters each (since V01.0.972 the part with the search words instead of the start, from a table the head row and the rows with the rarest search words, `lokal.excerpt`) go to the model in the first round as the tool result `local_search`, wrapped as outside text: data, never an instruction, and the answer is locked like one from the web (no switching, saving, reminders). The model names the source briefly ("In your documents …", "According to your archive …"); when nothing fits it answers timeless questions from its own knowledge or searches the web. It only looks where the answer may look anyway (the tools `document_search`, `archive_search`, `history_search` of this turn): guests, a voice not recognised at a shared speaker and Telegram without personal data get no quick look. Web search after documents: without the switch it is locked after a document; with it, it stays possible, but the panel sends only the words of the question (without code word, at most 120 characters), never the model's query. After mail it stays locked. Status → Checks → "Test looking locally first" shows class, search words, hits per source and times for one sentence (admin, 20 per minute); Logs → area "Knowledge" shows one `lokal:` line per question with class, hits per source, time and what happens next, never the question.

**Kiwix in the quick look (V01.0.898, Dominik 2026-10-21):** The Kiwix has its own time limit in the quick look (`chat.local_first_kiwix_ms`, Features → Web search → "Look locally first" → "Time limit for the Kiwix archive", 200 to 2000 ms, default 900), because a big archive often needs longer than the own documents; documents and history still wait only up to their limit, no source borrows time from another. Which books a question asks is a fixed rule (`kiwix.pick`, never the model): at most 5 (`kiwix.MAX_SEARCH_BOOKS`), the biggest German Wikipedia always, then the books whose title, description or kind holds a word of the question (most shared words first; a word found in half of the chosen books or more, like "Wikipedia" or "Docs", does not count), free places in the order of the choice. "How does git rebase work?" so asks the Git Docs, "sort a list in Python" the Python Tutorials. The same holds for the tool `archive_search`. The order of the chosen books can be changed under Features → Web search → "Own Kiwix archive" by dragging or ◀. An archive article from the quick look stands under the answer as a source with its book and the book's date ("Albert Einstein (Wikipedia, Stand 2025-01)"), linked into the own Kiwix; with "Address for links" (`chat.kiwix_link_url`, empty = the Kiwix address, changing it needs the second sign-in step) the links point to another name, such as the one behind the own proxy, while the Kiwix is still asked at the home address (V01.0.912). When the book is older than a year (`frisch.ARCHIV_TAGE`) and the answer does not name its year, the answer check adds the fixed sentence "Not quite current: archive: as of 2024-11." (`frisch.note_archive`, `frisch.nachsatz`, like old mail or weather readings); this also holds for `archive_search` and `wikipedia` from the Kiwix. A chosen book the Kiwix catalog no longer knows (replaced or removed by an update) is skipped while the catalog is readable; "Check Kiwix" names it ("No longer in the Kiwix and skipped"), the journal `kiwix: chosen books missing in catalog n=1`. A mistyped long word still fits (V01.0.912, Dominik 2026-10-21 "Dresdner Fraunkirche" found nothing): `lokal.same` allows one wrong letter for 6 to 9 letters and two from 10 letters (edit distance, `common.edit_distance`), shorter words must match exactly; this holds for titles and hits of all sources of the quick look. When the whole question finds no title, the panel asks up to 3 further title suggestions per book (`lokal.variants`: a long word cut to its first 4 letters at the end, "Dresdner Frau…", or a long word alone), and of all fitting titles the one naming most words of the question wins (`lokal.fit`: "Dresdner Frauenkirche" before "Dresdner Bank"). Self-tests `test_lokal.Turn.test_archive_article_despite_a_typo`, `test_lokal.Turn.test_the_archive_has_its_own_limit`, `test_kiwix.Pick`, `test_kiwix.ArchiveAge`.

**reMarkable notes (V01.0.273):** A profile connects its my.remarkable account (reMarkable 2, Paper Pro, Paper Pro Move); the assistant then finds the picked notebooks in the document search, also with meaning search, document card and "Look locally first". Switches: Features → Own documents → Read pictures and scans → "reMarkable notes" (admin, off); released for every profile, connecting is under Me → reMarkable; never guests. Pairing: get a one-time code for a desktop app on my.remarkable.com (eight letters) and enter it under Me → reMarkable (own browser only, never with a device key; with the second sign-in step a fresh code). Like the desktop app the Spark signs in as device "desktop-linux"; the long-lived device token is sealed in the vault (`users/<p>/remarkable.json`), the short-lived user token lives in memory only, neither is ever logged. reMarkable has no official interface: this uses the same sync protocol ("sync15") as rmapi, the Obsidian plugin and remarkable-mcp (MIT, used as a model); if reMarkable changes it, the error shows under Me → reMarkable, exporting a PDF and uploading it stays as a way. Without a Connect subscription reMarkable syncs to the cloud only in part. Only what is ticked is read (notebooks, or folders with everything in them, or "All notebooks" without PDFs/books; nothing by default; never the trash). Per page (format v6, `rmscene` 0.8.0 pinned): typed text (Type Folio, keyboard, "Convert to text") and highlighted text come directly; handwriting is drawn from the strokes as a picture (Pillow, cut to what is written, at most 1600 pixels) and read like a scan by the language model in quiet minutes (same daily limit, night window). For PDFs and e-books only highlights and own notes. Each notebook becomes one document with source "reMarkable" (`Folder/Name`, cited as "reMarkable: Folder/Name, Seite 4"). Compared every 30 minutes, never while someone speaks (`vorrang.py`), "Compare now" at once: pages with the same hash are neither fetched nor read again, changed ones are; notebooks deleted in the cloud or no longer ticked disappear; one deleted under Me → Documents does not come back until ticked again. "Disconnect" deletes the token and all notebooks on the Spark (then remove the device on my.remarkable.com). Limits: 5,000 entries, 500 pages per notebook, 300 pages per comparison (the rest follows two minutes later), page file 20 MB, index 2 MB, 2 million stroke points per page, the documents' space quota. Connections only through netguard to `*.remarkable.com`, `*.remarkable.engineering` (and `storage.googleapis.com` without login), https only, redirects only there. The content is outside text. Logs: `remarkable: 2 notebook(s), 5 page(s) new, 0 removed`.

**Sending to the reMarkable (V01.0.273):** Below "reMarkable notes" the admin switch "Sending to the reMarkable" (off; released for every profile). Then every answer has "To the reMarkable" (on a click only, own browser), and "Write on the reMarkable: …" makes a dictated note. The tool `remarkable_note` is only offered when the person's own message names "reMarkable" and is locked after outside text (web, mail, documents) in the same answer. It creates a new EPUB in the folder "Spark" (made when missing); existing documents are never changed, moved or deleted; the root index only gets lines added (generation checked). At most 20 a day and 60,000 characters.

**Put into (V01.0.291):** Under Me → reMarkable the profile picks which of its own folders new documents go to (default "Spark", created when needed). The Spark only adds new documents there and never changes anything that exists. If the folder is deleted or in the trash later, it takes "Spark" again, says so once and resets the choice.

**Date of the last change and last comparison (V01.0.929, Dominik 2026-10-22 "1a"):** Me → Documents shows a reMarkable notebook with the day it last changed on the device, not the day it first came in; "Neueste zuerst" and the groups (today, last 7 days, months) follow it, in the app as well. A document's page names both days ("Added" and "Changed"). With the source reMarkable picked at the top, the count is followed by "Letzter Abgleich 10:25 · 3 Seiten neu" (time and number only, with "mit Fehler" when the last comparison failed); without a connected account the line is left out. The comparison itself stays as it was: every 30 minutes, in between a short look at the account every 3 minutes, and after a break it goes on with the pages still missing; pages already read are never fetched again.

**Newest first (V01.0.299):** sub-switch under "reMarkable notes" (Functions, admin), off; released for every profile. The Spark keeps when each page last changed (newer reMarkable software names the time per page, else the notebook's; uploaded documents count with their upload). With similar hits the newer passage moves up at most two places, the gain halves every two weeks, a clearly better old hit stays first; this applies to the document search and to "Search locally first". Each hit then carries "changed on …", and the model is told to follow the newer passage where they disagree and say its date. "What did I note last / today / yesterday / this week / last month?" is a fixed rule on the person's own words: the first round must search the documents and gets the pages changed last, newest first; if nothing is in that span, the assistant says so. New notes arrive sooner: between comparisons the Spark looks every 3 minutes with one small request whether anything changed in the account, and only then compares at once (journal "remarkable: account changed, comparing now"). Since V01.0.369 the Spark's own writes (answers, guide, inbox) do not count as a change; when the reMarkable cloud briefly answers 502/503/504, the Spark tries once more after 2 seconds. The page reader's hints about newer page parts are counted into one journal line per comparison.

**More with the reMarkable (V01.0.344):** admin sub-switch "More with the reMarkable" below "Sending to the reMarkable", off; released for every profile. Morning sheet, week in review and daily inbox come by themselves; they are on from the start and can be switched off under Me → reMarkable (code: `app/panel/rmplus.py`). *Morning sheet:* at the chosen time (default 06:30, two hours' window) the daily briefing as a PDF with lines for notes. *@Spark:* "@Spark" and a question on a page that is read; after the comparison (for handwriting after reading) "Antwort: …" lies next to it, from the own documents and, if there is one, the web search; each question only once. *Tasks:* lines with ☐, [ ], "TODO" or "Aufgabe:" show up under Me → reMarkable, a reminder only after clicking "Remind". *Meetings:* notebook or first line with "Besprechung", "Meeting", "Protokoll" …: 30 minutes after the last change a summary with decisions and tasks as a new document plus a note. *Week in review:* on Fridays from 5 pm a PDF with the week's main points. *Web pages and attachments:* tools `remarkable_page` (a public page as EPUB) and `remarkable_attachment` (the newest PDF or EPUB attached to a fitting mail of the last 30 days, whole files up to 30 MB only), only when the person's own words name an address or an attachment. *Daily inbox:* dictated notes gather in one document "Eingang DD.MM.YYYY". *Spark guide:* an e-book only about the reMarkable functions and the service (comparison, privacy, limits, disconnecting), each function marked on/off; the Spark compares the text every 10 minutes and renews the same document as soon as it changes (a new function, a switch, the admin's permission). A self-test demands a section for every function below "reMarkable notes", so the guide cannot go stale. Only the guide and the day's inbox belong to the Spark and are renewed (your marks in them stay); everything else is always a new document. At most 30 documents of its own a day; never while someone is talking. Notes only go to the language model as data, without tools.

**Paper for the reMarkable (V01.0.485, Dominik 1a 2b 3a 4a 5):** admin sub-switch "Paper for the reMarkable" below "Sending to the reMarkable" (`chat.remarkable_paper`, off), released for every profile, never for guests; only "Tidy up by itself" is on from the start and can be switched off under Me → reMarkable → Paper (code: `app/panel/rmpapier.py`). **Folders by kind** (`rm_folders`): what the Spark puts on the reMarkable goes below "Spark" into Morgenblatt, Wochenrückblick, Besprechungen, Antworten, Web und Anhänge, Eingang or Archiv; the folders are kept by id (renaming or moving them is fine, a missing one is made again), the guide is pinned on top; "Sort in now" moves its old loose PDFs and e-books (only its own, known from the list `made` or the old titles, never notebooks, at most 30 at once). **Tidy up** (`rm_tidy`, needs the folders): once a day morning sheets and week reviews without handwriting go to the archive after 7 days, archived ones without handwriting to the reMarkable's trash after 30 more days (still recoverable there). **Designed sheets** (`rm_paper`): morning sheet, week review, summaries, answers, inbox and the guide come as PDF (fpdf2, DejaVu) exactly in the device's screen format: reMarkable 1/2 157.8 × 210.4 mm, Paper Pro 179.7 × 239.6 mm, Paper Pro Move 91.8 × 163.2 mm (detected from the page size of notebook pages or chosen under Paper), with a header band, numbered sections, cards and lines; web pages stay EPUB without boxes (3a). The box "Kann weg" (can go) sits top right; on the morning sheet every reminder of the day has a box. **Reading:** only sheets the Spark made itself and whose boxes it stored (`papers`) are read; a copy on the device never triggers anything. On each look into the cloud (at most 12 changed sheets) the strokes (rmscene, without highlighter and eraser) are converted to mm with the page size and each box is checked geometrically: at least 3 mm of ink in the box (2 mm margin) = ticked, 70 % filled = taken back, ink only beside it = unclear (nothing happens, Paper shows "unclear"), else empty. Fixed rules, no model: "Kann weg" puts the sheet into the trash, into the archive when there is handwriting on it (without folders it stays); a tick at a reminder completes it, filling the box in brings it back; each box acts once per state. Both can be taken back under Paper (`POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/zurueck`). A sheet with handwriting is never filled with new text: a new version is made and the old one goes to the archive. **Calibration:** the calibration sheet has four circles; the crosses give a mapping per model (least squares, kept only when every circle fits within 2.5 mm). Under Paper a control picture per sheet shows the boxes in the colour of what was read and the strokes on top. Endpoints (profile browser login, CSRF, rate limits `rmsend`, `rmpic`, `rm`, body 1 KB): `GET /api/profile/remarkable/papier`, `GET …/papier/{id}/{page}.png`, `POST …/papier/abgleich`, `POST …/papier/einsortieren` (409 while a sync runs), `POST …/papier/zurueck`. One cloud job per profile at a time; the sync writes back only its own fields so a sheet placed meanwhile is not lost. Journal with numbers only ("remarkable: sheet read | 2 ticked, 0 unclear, 1 done"), live overview "Blatt gelesen: n angekreuzt, n unklar". Next (after the test with the Move): the task sheet for admin points (2b, executed directly) and the field test on paper.

**reMarkable documents well formatted (V01.0.892, Dominik 2026-10-21 "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a", smaller head):** every document the Spark puts on the reMarkable reads its text through one shared reader (`rmpapier.blocks`): headings ("## …" and lines that are only "**part**"), paragraphs with their single lines, bullets, numbered steps, tables, quotes, rules and code blocks; "**bold**", "*italic*", "`code`" and "[link](url)" lose their marks, and a left-out emoji no longer leaves a gap. The designed sheet (`Sheet.text`) draws sections, bullets, numbers (1. 2. 3. in the accent colour) and tables of up to 3 columns with a head row; wider tables become "column: value" lines. The head is smaller (title 13 instead of 17 pt, sections 10 pt) so the first page keeps more room. Without "Designed sheets" the morning sheet and the weekly review come as the same sheet in black and white without boxes (`rmpapier.plain_pdf`, device format, replaces the old Helvetica PDF); answers, meetings, inbox, guide and web pages as EPUB with real headings, lists and tables (`remarkable.epub_body`). Answers for the reMarkable are no longer flattened first (`textnorm.plain_markdown` is gone there). Documents from "Create documents" come in the device's page format instead of A4 (`docrender.render(…, page=…)`, narrow pages with small margins and a smaller title, `docrender.SMALL_PAGE`); letters and own templates stay A4. Guard test `test_rmformat`: builds a sample sheet for every kind (`rmpapier.ARTEN`, guide, calibration sheet, document) on every model and turns red on leftover Markdown, text past the right margin, lost numbers or a new kind without a sample. Pictures before/after: `/mnt/project-files/remarkable-format/`.

**Crosses in other units (V01.0.500, Dominik 2026-10-13 "current calibration does not fit"):** the Move delivered strokes on some sheets in units as for 1620 points across while the page said 954; every cross landed 0.59 times too small. The calibration now tries every possible width (`rmpapier.widths`, `fit`), and when reading, `best_map` takes another width instead of the calibration only when the ink then lies clearly better in the boxes (twice as much and at least `INK_MIN`); handwriting alone moves nothing. The journal names the page size, the units used and where the ink lies ("page 954x1696 … units 1620 | ink x … y …"). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_ticks_in_larger_units_than_the_page_says_still_hit_their_boxes` and `test_handwriting_alone_never_moves_the_mapping`.

**"Gelesene Blätter" tidied (V01.0.500, Dominik 2026-10-13 "it does not tidy up"):** of calibration and task sheets the list shows only the newest (`rmpapier.ONE_SHOWN`), sheets deleted on the device not at all, ones put away by "Kann weg" one more day (`GONE_SHOWN`); unread ones show when they were sent. Self-test `test_rmpapier.Reading.test_the_list_keeps_one_sheet_per_replaced_kind`.

**Crosses from another device (V01.0.500, Dominik 2026-10-13 "the crosses on the task sheet are wrong"):** a sheet whose page names another model than the one it was built for (one account, two devices) no longer takes the other device's calibration: `rmpapier.calib_key` then gives "move@pro", without a calibration of its own the page size counts (before, every cross landed 0.59 times too small and off to the side). The journal says "remarkable: sheet for move written on pro". With answers side by side the control picture shows the point once above and only the answer at each box, in the sheets' own font (arrows, umlauts). Self-test `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_a_page_from_another_model_never_takes_this_models_calibration`.

**Reasons in the log when the reMarkable cloud stalls (V01.0.500, log excerpt Dominik 2026-10-13):** instead of "comparison failed: CloudError" the journal names the reasons in fixed words and numbers, e.g. "remarkable: 15 of 75 entries not read | file HTTP 429 ×12, ReadTimeout ×3" (`remarkable.why`, `why_count`, `why_long`; the cloud's own text never reaches the journal). When the cloud answers 429 or 500/502/503/504 the Spark waits once (Retry-After honoured, at most 10 s) and tries again. Self-tests in `test_remarkable`.

**Task sheet to tick (V01.0.491, package D, Dominik 2b):** admin sub-switch "Task sheet to tick" below "Paper for the reMarkable" (`chat.remarkable_tasks`, off, never guests; code: `app/panel/rmaufgaben.py`); following Dominik's release rule there is no profile switch of its own: the first sheet comes by itself, after that there is always one (since V01.0.784, see "Always a task sheet" below); "Send task sheet" under Me → reMarkable → Paper or by voice fetches a new version at once. A living PDF in the folder "Spark/Aufgaben" with what is open, each point with boxes for fixed answers: tasks from the handwriting (remind tomorrow 9:00 / remove / later, `rm_todo`), memory entries "maybe outdated" or "contradiction" (true / not true / later, "Check memory"), proposals for the profile's own settings (take / no). Only for an admin profile (Admin role): open proposals of the settings assistant except stages C, U, I and P (take / reject / later, ticked = done directly, Dominik 2b; role and switches are checked again when acting) and the field test after an update (the new lines from CHANGELOG.md, works / does not work; "does not work" becomes a case under Status → Improve). The panel fixes what each answer does when it builds the sheet (`rmaufgaben.OPTS`, `_do`); the paper only picks. It is evaluated at once when "Fertig – bitte auswerten" is ticked, else after 15 minutes without a change on the sheet; two ticks in one point = unclear, nothing happens, the point comes again; each point acts once. The emergency stop holds everything. Then a new version with "Erledigt seit Version N" (at the earliest after 10 minutes, else every morning from the morning sheet's time); the old one with the ticks goes to the archive. "Take back" under Paper for a reminder, a deleted memory entry, a setting and an assistant proposal (`POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/aufgaben/zurueck`, body 1 KB, rate limit `rm`); "Send task sheet" `POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/aufgaben` (rate limit `rmsend`), both with the profile's browser login, CSRF, `guard.log`. Also by voice: "Leg mir das Aufgabenblatt aufs reMarkable" (`remarkable_sheet`). Journal numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet read | 1 done, 0 unclear"), live "Aufgabenblatt" as background work.

**Note on the task sheet (V01.0.512, Dominik 2026-10-13 "1"):** below the points the task sheet has four lines "Anmerkung" (`Sheet.note_area`, a field of kind `notiz`, never judged as a box). When at least 12 mm of handwriting lie there (`rmaufgaben.NOTE_MIN`) and it changed, the Spark renders only these strokes as a picture and lets the language model read them (`wissen.read_page`, only with "Read pictures and scans"; otherwise the note waits, journal "note not read | reading pictures is off"). One line remains of the result, without the picture description and "[unleserlich]", at most 200 characters. The text is data: it stands in the receipt ("gelesen: …") and as a point "Aus deiner Anmerkung" on the next version with the fixed answers remind (tomorrow 9:00) / remove / later; only the tick makes it a reminder, "Take back" under Paper brings it back. When the fixed rule of "Own tasks" (`beobachten.parse`, never the model) finds a watch sentence in it ("Sag mir Bescheid, wenn es eine neue Firmware gibt"), watches are allowed (`agent.watch_on`) and there is room under `beobachten.MAX_WATCH`, the answer "Als Auftrag" is added: the tick makes the watch under Me → Jobs (`created_by` "remarkable"), where it can be paused, changed and deleted; "Take back" removes it again. Everything is checked again when acting; if it cannot be done, the note stays open. One note per sheet (writing on replaces it), at most 6 wait. When reading, only strokes lying almost wholly in a box count for choosing the units; lines of handwriting that another scale would drag across the boxes move nothing any more. Journal numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet note read | 42 characters"), live "Anmerkung gelesen". Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_the_anmerkung_is_read_and_comes_back_as_a_point_never_a_command`, `test_the_anmerkung_waits_while_reading_pictures_is_off`, `test_note_text_keeps_only_what_was_written`, `test_a_note_asking_to_watch_offers_a_task_only_by_the_panels_rule`, `test_a_task_ticked_after_watches_were_switched_off_does_nothing`, `test_a_tick_and_long_lines_of_writing_never_move_the_mapping`.

**Morning sheet in sections (Dominik 2026-10-15 "not nicely laid out, currently only prose"):** the morning sheet now takes the sorted daily overview (`chat.morning_briefing(..., markdown=True)`, `BRIEF_MD_SYSTEM`): per area with content a line **Termine**, **Erinnerungen**, **Mails**, **Wetter**, **Themen** with short bullets; on the sheet each becomes a numbered section (`rmpapier.heads`) instead of one prose block "01 Überblick". Self-test `test_rmplus.SheetOnRequest.test_the_morning_sheet_is_sorted_into_sections`.

**Task sheet with five parts of one's choice (Dominik 2026-10-16 "1-5 sollte aber der user steuern können was er alles bekommen möchte"):** each profile sets under Me → reMarkable → Paper → "What goes on the sheet" which parts come on its task sheet (`rmaufgaben.PARTS`, profile settings `rm_tasks_cal`, `rm_tasks_lists`, `rm_tasks_mail`, `rm_tasks_new`, `rm_tasks_note`, all on until the profile turns them off; no new function switch). A part only comes while its function is released for the profile; otherwise the page says what is missing. **Today in the calendar:** today's appointments still to come (at most 6, `calendars.events`) with Passt / 30 min vorher erinnern / Auf morgen; all-day ones without "remind". Only a reminder is made (30 minutes before, or "Von gestern verschoben: …" tomorrow at 9:00), the calendar itself stays unchanged; when the appointment is over by the time the sheet is read, the receipt says "schon vorbei". **Tasks and shopping:** up to 6 open entries per list (`tasks.items`) with one box "Erledigt" that ticks the entry off there (`tasks.change`, also in the iCloud calendar); "Take back" puts it on the list again. **Waiting mail:** up to 5 unread mails of the last two days (`mail.find`) with Gelesen / Antwort entwerfen / Später. "Gelesen" only takes the mail off the sheet, the mailbox stays as it is. "Antwort entwerfen" is only offered when the profile may put drafts (`schreib.draft_ok`): the writing help writes the answer (the mail is data for it, never an order), the draft then lies in the mailbox's drafts folder (`tidy.make_draft`) and is never sent. **Quick note:** three lines and one tick for what it is: reminder today (18:00, after 17:00 in an hour), tomorrow (9:00), task or shopping (the last two only with released lists). It is read like the note (`read_new`, only with "Read pictures and scans"); each line becomes one entry, at most 6. The tick is the yes: without a tick nothing happens, two ticks = unclear; "Take back" removes the reminders or list entries again. **Note:** as before. When a service does not answer, only its part is missing from this version (journal "remarkable: task sheet mails not read: …"). Since appointments, lists and mails change daily, with one of these parts a new version comes every morning from the morning sheet's time (since V01.0.784 always, see below). The receipt shows "gelesen" instead of the sign ✎ the font does not have. `mail.message` is now the one place that fetches a mail by its id (also for `mail.read`). Journal fixed words and numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet quick note read | 2 line(s)"). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.TaskParts`.

**Always a task sheet (V01.0.784, Dominik 2026-10-18 "1a 2a 3a 4a"):** there is always a sheet in the folder "Aufgaben", also when nothing is open. The first one comes by itself once the task sheet is released for the profile and a reMarkable is paired (`rmaufgaben.due` "erst"). "Kann weg" or deleting it on the device puts only this version away (trash, with handwriting the archive); at the next check, at the earliest 10 minutes after the last version, a new one lies in the folder "Aufgaben" ("fehlt"). Every morning from the morning sheet's time a new version comes, also when nothing changed ("morgen"), so date and time are right; after the ticks were acted on as before ("neu"). Whoever wants no sheet at all switches the function off. With nothing open the sheet shows (`rmaufgaben.calm`): "Alles erledigt" with the cards "0 offen · Stand DD.MM. HH:MM" and "n erledigt · seit Version N", what the Spark keeps an eye on (the parts switched on: appointments, lists, mail, and the sources notes, memory, proposals), "Zuletzt erledigt" (the receipt), "Als Nächstes" (tomorrow's first appointment, only with the part appointments; "Neue Version spätestens morgen um …") and still Schnell notieren and Anmerkung. The calendar text is data and is cut and cleaned like every point. Journal only fixed words ("remarkable: task sheet | version 5, 0 point(s), why fehlt", reason from `rmaufgaben.WHY`). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_kann_weg_puts_only_this_version_away_a_new_one_comes`, `test_a_new_version_every_morning_also_when_nothing_changed`, `test_nothing_open_says_so_with_the_time_and_what_comes_next`.

**For new profiles (V01.0.492):** when `chat.new_profile_on` names a function that no longer exists or no longer has its own profile switch (such as the old "Nachrichten" from V01.0.488), `admin.get_config` drops it; before, every save of an admin switch failed with "new_profile_on: functions with an own switch, each once". Self-test `test_features`. Since V01.0.494 `admin._tidy` drops whatever this version no longer knows when the configuration is read: keys `config.default.json` no longer has, presets in `chat.defaults` that `profiles.clean_settings` refuses (before: "chat defaults: invalid value" on every save) and Melder in `berater_off` that are gone. What the admin sends is still checked strictly by `validate`. Since V01.0.509 `chat.new_profile_on` is gone; `admin._tidy` removes the old entry and tidies old entries in `chat.deny` (deleted profiles, removed functions) the same way.
