**Answer onto the reMarkable (V01.0.279):** "Summarize the political situation and put it on my reMarkable" puts the whole answer as a new document into the folder "Spark". A fixed rule on the question's own words decides this (put, send, save … plus "on/to my reMarkable", no colon), not the language model: the model only writes the answer, then the panel sends it and says "The answer is now on your reMarkable …" (or why not). This also works after a web search, after which the tool `remarkable_note` is locked; the destination is fixed, outside text can only shape the content of the answer, never where it goes. Same switches and daily limit as above, only in the own browser or at a speaker with the recognised voice, never guests. Logs → Requests shows "remarkable (Panel)".

**"Yes" to "Shall I put it on the reMarkable?" (V01.0.804, Dominik 2026-10-18):** when an answer offers to put itself on the reMarkable (fixed rule `remarkable.offered` on its own sentence: "Soll ich/Möchtest du/Willst du … reMarkable …?", "shall I … reMarkable?"), the panel keeps the text before that question as the profile's one waiting proposal (`remarkable.propose`, `profiles.pending_set`, 15 minutes, same device and conversation). A plain "yes" (`calendars.confirms`) then puts it there like "… and put it on my reMarkable" (`send_answer`, folder "Antworten"); the model only says the result. Any other answer drops the proposal, so does the Notaus. Never after mail or other outside text, never for guests, only with "Send to the reMarkable" and a paired device. With "Show steps and approvals" the yes/no card ("reMarkable") stands below. Also "erstellen", "anlegen" and "machen" now count as asking for the answer there ("Create a note on the reMarkable with the recipe for cherry cake"), also spelled "Remarkabel". Tool rule 5 (`chat.TOOL_RULES`): when the user already said what they want and the tool is there, the model does it at once instead of asking, and never offers anything it has no tool for. Journal only fixed words ("remarkable: answer proposed | waiting for yes", "remarkable: answer sent | after yes", "remarkable: answer not sent | no yes"). Self-test `test_remarkable.AnswerToRemarkable.test_an_offered_answer_waits_for_a_plain_yes`. And "Look locally first": a plain "yes" or "no (thanks)" is never a knowledge question (`lokal.classify` → "Antwort auf Rückfrage"), and when the panel already carried out something confirmed in this turn (appointment, mail, reMarkable …) it does not look locally. Before, a "yes" to a proposed appointment found old conversations; the appointment was saved, but the answer spoke of those ("no function for notes on the reMarkable"). Self-test `test_lokal.Rule.test_a_short_answer_to_a_question_before_looks_nothing_up`.

**My status (V01.0.281):** Under Me → My status every profile sees what the Spark does for it in the background and whether its connected services answer. Switches: Features → My day → "My status" (admin, off); released for every profile, shown under Me → My status; never guests. Section **In the background**: reading documents (page x of y, n of 100 pages today), preparing meaning search, document cards, comparing the reMarkable, tidying the inbox (per mailbox), fetching contacts, jobs, morning briefing, tidying memory, each with its state (running, waiting, fine, needs you), the reason it waits ("paused while someone speaks", "waits for a quiet minute", "waits for the night", "daily limit reached"), last and next time, a progress bar and the history of the last 7 days (at most 50 entries per job, `users/<p>/hintergrund.json`, only for profiles with the switch on). Section **Connected**: mailboxes, calendars (task lists too), address books, Home Assistant, Telegram, iPhone app, Pebble, speakers. A service's state is the outcome of its last real request (`netguard.client(seen=…)`, IMAP login); the page never asks a service itself; after a restart it says "not asked yet" until the service was needed (`state/dienste-zustand.json`). "Compare now" (reMarkable) and "Fetch now" (contacts) use the existing buttons. Only fixed words, numbers and the person's own names (document, mailbox, calendar, device names); never mail subjects, senders, events, document text or a server's error text (reduced to a fixed reason). When something needs the profile (sign-in refused, address not found, storage full, tidying stopped, a memory proposal waiting), a red dot shows at "Me" (asked once a minute, `?brief=1`). The page refreshes every 10 seconds while open. The iPhone app reads the same source (`GET /api/profile/hintergrund`, key "app", read only). Status → Summary "My status of all profiles" shows the admin counts only (`GET /api/admin/hintergrund`). Plan: `plaene/hintergrund-status.md`.

**Targeted tool choice (V01.0.202):** a "switch" in the panel (`intent.py`) sorts every question by fixed rules into a group: smart home, calendar, mail, web search, reminder, list, weather, transit, parcel, contact, memory, document, message, agent, iPhone, small talk or "unclear". It reads only the person's own latest message, never outside text or model output, and writes one `weiche:` line per question to the journal (intent, the word that matched, number of tools, required tool, lock); the diagnosis filter "Chat" shows it. With the admin switch Features → Speed and accuracy → "Targeted tool choice" (off, then for every profile, never guests) the model sees only the tools of a clear question's group (plus "remember"); a group with one tool (weather, web search, transit) makes the first round call exactly that one. The switch only takes away what the rights check before it allowed, never more. After an answer from the web, calendar, a document or a picture, a new request in the person's own words lifts the lock and the old answer is no longer sent along; if the request points at it ("Setz das auf die Liste", "Erinner mich daran", "Und …"), everything stays locked. Mail keeps the rule from V01.0.194. When something is locked the model always gets the real reason in one sentence (also with the switch off). Optionally, under the admin switch, the language model sorts questions no rule recognizes into a group (only the person's own message, answer only from the fixed list, default off). Below the admin switch (V01.0.203): "Show the rules" lists every group with its built-in words and tools, "Try it" sends one sentence through the switch (no model, nothing stored; admin only, rate limit, at most 500 characters), and "Own words per group" (`chat.route_words`, one line per group like `smarthome: sauna, kamin`; whole words only, at most 20 per group, checked on save and restore) adds to the built-in words, never replaces them. Test bench with more than 90 example sentences: `app/tests/test_intent.py`.

**The switch shown and set up (Features → Routing, plan `plaene/weiche-sichtbar.md`):** a page of its own beside "Features" shows the switch as a picture. "Try" sends a sentence through the same rules as a real question and shows its way in six boxes: question (points back or not), checks first (small talk, about the Spark, message to someone), group (with the word that matched, built in or own), tools (from the group, extra, always kept), locks (the protection rules with a lock) and result (read directly, required tool, instant sentence). No model, nothing stored. Below, the groups as a list with details: built-in words, own words as chips (`chat.route_words`), new **"Not with"** (`chat.route_not`: a word takes the group out again), **extra tools** (`chat.route_extra`, at most 6, only the switch's own tools and only when the question is offered them anyway) and **narrowing per group** (`chat.route_off`: the group is recognized but gets every allowed tool). All of it only takes away or stays within the rights; the protection rules (nothing switched or remembered after mail or outside text, emergency stop, at most 2 lookups) are no setting. **Test sentences:** the built-in ones (`app/panel/intent_bench.py`, also used by `test_intent.py`) and own ones (`chat.route_tests`, at most 200, a sentence up to 200 characters with the expected group). "Check and save" runs every sentence through the new rules first; when one lands elsewhere the page lists it and saves only after "Save anyway". "Undo" puts the version before back (the last 20 in `STATE/weiche-history.json`). **"Wrongly sorted …"** comes after a try, at every switch step in Status → Logs → Requests (the question itself is never recorded, it is typed again) and as "Check the switch" in the hint under an answer in the admin's own chat: pick the right group, the switch suggests words of the sentence by a fixed rule (at least 4 letters, not a common word, not in any group yet) with the number of test sentences each would change; "Apply" changes the rules on the page and keeps the sentence as a test. **Statistics:** per day and group only counts, tools and direct reads (8 days, `STATE/weiche-stats.json`), never a word of the question; the live overview shows today's numbers at the switch node. Admin only (not the manager role), rate limit, body at most 40 KB, checked on save and restore. The settings form no longer puts older switch rules back.

**A smarter switch (Features → Routing, plan `plaene/weiche-intelligenter.md`):** the switch is now measured on **everyday sentences** (`app/panel/intent_alltag.py`) that are never used to set the rules up; the test sentences in `intent_bench.py` always land right because every reported mistake became one. The page shows the **everyday score** (right, kept by a hint, open, wrong) and the sentences that do not land right; `test_intent_alltag.py` holds the numbers (good may not go down, wrong not up). New are **hint words** per group (`intent.HINTS`, built in, shown at the group): when no rule recognizes a question for sure ("Am I free on Friday?" before, "We need milk"), they keep their group's tools with "lean" (`chat.route_model: lean`) and beside the model's or the classification's pick. A hint never narrows and never gives more than the rights and locks. **Follow-ups** ("And tomorrow?", "Turn it off again") get the group of the person's own question just before in the same conversation: only when short (up to 80 characters), pointing back and recognized by no rule; an answer or outside text never counts. "Try" has a "question before" field for it. The rules also recognize more everyday questions (meetings, letters from an office, the DAX, opening hours, an umbrella, "write a notice of termination" as a file, "send me … every morning" as a job), and language and nature questions ("What is Fenster in English?") no longer go to devices or contacts. **One place for the switch:** "Questions no rule recognizes" (`chat.route_model`), the classification's threshold (`chat.systemone_min`) with "Measure classification" and the must words (`chat.tool_words`, before Settings → Language model) are now only under Features → Routing and are saved there with "Check and save" (with undo); the switches "Targeted tool choice" and "Classification with probabilities" stay under Features. The live overview shows at the switch node how many questions had a hint today and how many were follow-ups.

**Tune the switch** (since V01.0.757, plan `plaene/opencode-werkstatt.md` point 1, Dominik 2026-10-17; `chat.route_tune` below "Targeted tool choice", off; admin only; `panel/weiche_abstimmen.py`): on *Features → Routing* "Tune" starts a run. The everyday sentences are split by a fixed rule: even places are the **practice half** (plus the admin's own test sentences), odd ones the **check half**. For each group with at least 2 missed sentences of the practice half (at most 8 groups) the language model proposes up to 6 words (JSON; the sentences stand as data in `<<< >>>`, the question goes through `vorrang` behind every conversation). Each word is measured in memory and stays only when no test sentence flips (`weiche.flips`: `intent_bench.BENCH` and the own ones), the practice half gets at least one more right and none more wrong and the check half none fewer right and none more wrong. The result is a proposal with the numbers of both halves; "Apply" saves the words through the switch's normal save (`chat.route_words`, the same checks, undo) and only when the words did not change since the run. It changes only the admin's own words, never the built-in rules (`test_intent_alltag` still measures without them). The check half is the honest number; the everyday score on top rises afterwards also because of the practice half. The model decides nothing, it only proposes words. Log only `weiche: abstimmen …` with numbers. Endpoints `GET|POST|DELETE /api/admin/weiche/abstimmen`, `POST /api/admin/weiche/abstimmen/uebernehmen`; self-test `test_weiche_abstimmen`.

**Unclear questions, anonymous** (since V01.0.927, plan `unklare-fragen/entwurf.png`, Dominik 2026-10-22; `chat.route_unklar` below "Targeted tool choice", off; preference `unklar_anon` per profile, on, under *Me → Conversation → Privacy*; `panel/weiche_unklar.py`): When no rule of the switch recognizes a question of a profile, it shows on *Features → Routing* at "unclear" in the list "Unclear questions, anonymous". Redaction is fixed rules only: first the redactor of "Report a problem" (names of profiles, devices and accounts, mail addresses, links, host names, IP and MAC addresses, phone numbers, coordinates, long codes, every secret from the settings), then the names from the profile's own address book, a name after "Herr/Frau/Dr." and every number (`[PERSON]`, `[ZAHL]` …). Then the leak check of "Report a problem" runs once more; if it finds anything, the question is dropped and only counted. A sentence with the code word is never kept, guests and an unknown voice at a speaker never. Only the redacted sentence is kept with its count and day, never profile, device or time of day; next to it the group whose tool the model took afterwards (from `weiche.learn`). The file `state/weiche-unklar.json` is sealed, at most 100 sentences, gone after 7 days. "Belongs to …" opens "Wrongly sorted" with the sentence and the model's group: words checked against all test sentences, "Apply", "As test sentence", Undo as always; a placeholder is never offered as a word. Afterwards the sentence is gone from the list. One by one or "Delete all" (`DELETE /api/admin/weiche/unklar[/{id}]`, admin log), only admin and co-admin. Live shows only the number at the switch node. Log only `weiche: unklare Frage anonym gemerkt|verworfen (Prüfung)` and `… gelöscht <number>`. Self-test `test_weiche_unklar`.

**Switch check (October 2026):** „Terminal“ no longer counts as an appointment. A price or a strike goes to the web search, „Call me“ to the Spark calling back, „a list of …“ in a file stays with „Create documents“, „difference between …“ is knowledge. The everyday sentences now also measure the telephone, chats, pictures, the state and the tools a module offers only by their own word (volume, undo, pause notes, NAS, reMarkable): such a sentence counts as right when the module's fixed word rule matches. A photo with a wish to change it („Remove the beard“) shows under Logs → Requests as the group pictures („Foto ändern“), no longer as „unclear“; the model does not have to pick. **Learning:** when no rule recognizes a question and the model then calls a tool of one group, the switch counts that group and the fixed hint word that named it (never a word of the question, 7 days). A hint word that led to the same group at least three times is offered under Features → Routing, „Statistics“, as a word of that group; when the admin takes it over, „Check and save“ with all test sentences and Undo applies as always. The switch never changes its rules by itself.

**Help with the Spark (V01.0.326, off by default):** questions about the Spark itself ("How do I get faster answers?", "What does this switch do?", "What should I still set here?") the assistant answers with the tool `spark_hilfe` from what ships with the panel: the guides, the switches with their sentences, the own settings under Me, fixed recommendations and the paragraphs of these docs (`app/panel/hilfe_docs.json`, `python3 app/panel/hilfe.py --docs` rewrites it; a self-test keeps it equal). The search runs by fixed rules on the Spark, nothing leaves the house. Each hit comes with its path in the panel and how it stands for you (on, off and why). Every login only gets what its rights allow: Spark settings, the state of the Spark switches, "Security at a glance" and the docs about running the Spark only for admins (a manager only the feature switches); normal profiles get the guides without the admin's steps and only their own settings; guests nothing. The hits go to the model as data, never as instructions; keys, PINs and names of other profiles never appear. With "Suggesting settings" (`spark_vorschlag`) the assistant shows a card below the answer with switches, before and after. The model never switches: the panel checks every wish by fixed rules (only switches that change how the assistant works for you, never security, nothing to the outside; a profile only its own settings, Spark switches only as admin, switching off never for everybody), and only the tap on "Apply" changes something, through `POST /api/hilfe/karte/{id}` (own browser login, CSRF, rate limit, for Spark switches the admin login and code, admin log with undo). A card lasts ten minutes, once, only for this profile; "Undo" takes it back for ten minutes. After a mail, web page or other outside text in the same answer there is no card, the Notaus from stage 2 holds applying off, outside the browser (Telegram, voice at a speaker) the assistant only names the path. "Ask Spark on every page" lives in the face at the bottom right (no button of its own since V01.0.335): it then also shows over Me and for profiles, names the page you are on, takes a typed question too, and every question through it, spoken or typed, sends that page (only an id from a fixed list), whose guides come first. It is the same conversation as the chat. Switches: Features → "Help with the Spark" (below it "Suggesting settings" and "Ask Spark on every page"); once released it works for every profile. Log: lines `hilfe:`; Status → Live shows the service "Spark-Hilfe" and events (shown, applied, declined), never contents. Plan: `plaene/panel-hilfe.md`; tests: `app/tests/test_hilfe.py`.

**Better help answers (V01.0.343, plan `plaene/hilfe-besser.md`, package A):** the switch recognises questions about the Spark by fixed rules before any model is asked ("How do I set up …?", "What is room mode?", "Why does … not work?", also in English) and sends them to `spark_hilfe`; questions about one's own data ("Where is my appointment tomorrow?", "Turn on the light") stay with their tools. When only the help is asked for, the panel fetches it directly. The search understands split German verbs, everyday words (backup, password, phone, wake word) and knows every page with a fixed sentence and its switches, plus fixed answers to frequent questions (kill switch, my kill switch, inviting, backup, logs, PIN, passkeys). The answer to the model has fixed blocks: what, where, how it stands for you, next step; for admin settings that are a plain switch whether it is on; a profile only gets ways it can see itself. **Origin:** the panel notes where a question came from (panel chat, face on a page, iPhone or Android app, Telegram, speaker, watch, Siri, MCP), only from what it knows itself (login, device key, app marker, page), never from the question, and it never grants more rights. Only the form follows from it: short and spoken at a speaker, "Manage → …" in the phone view, "in the panel in the browser" in the iPhone app and on Telegram; over MCP there is no help. On a settings page every question through the face goes to the help unless it asks for own data; on Status only "What does that mean?" and help questions. **"?" at every switch:** with "Ask Spark on every page" each switch (Features, Settings, Me) gets a small "?"; a tap asks about exactly that switch (only an id of a fixed form goes along). Logs → Requests shows "Origin" at the switch step. Quality test: `app/tests/test_hilfe_quality.py` (61 questions with expected answer and route).

**Help finds e-mail and Live again (V01.0.818):** "Wie bekomme ich Mails vorgelesen?" shows "E-Mail lesen" first again (before, "Für dich aus Mails" came first), "Wo sehe ich die Live-Übersicht?" shows "Status → Live" again (before, "Share calendars" with its "See and add"). The help search knows two more words for it: "vorgelesen" means "mail lesen" like "vorlesen", "Live" also searches "Zustand" (`hilfe.SYN`). Since V01.0.808 the GitHub run "Tests" was red for this, and the update offered only V01.0.807, because it installs only versions with a green run.

**The face at the bottom right everywhere (V01.0.359):** the face at the bottom right is there for every login (not for guests) on every page, over Me and in the phone view, also without the Spark help. One tap starts listening and opens its window with "Open conversation", "Stop" and a field to type in; a second tap stops listening, the second tap of a double tap does not count and marks no text. With the keyboard: tab to the face, then Enter or Space. The page you are on only goes along with "Ask Spark on every page". The wall monitor (`/live`) stays a pure display without a face.

**Folding in the face's window (V01.0.489):** an × at the top right of the face's window, and Escape, fold the window in, also while "Set up" is in it (its steps stay and come back the next time the window opens, "Done" ends them). Listening and speaking go on, the face shows them. On narrow screens (below 760 pixels wide or 600 pixels high, also in the iPhone app) the window takes at most half the height and scrolls inside; the × stays visible at the top.

**Suggesting functions (V01.0.380, off by default):** the assistant points out a function that is still off when it would help, and helps setting it up. Whether a hint comes is decided by fixed rules in the panel (`tipps.py`), never by the model: (1) **a wish without its tool**: the switch recognises a group (calendar, weather, shopping list …) whose function is off for you or on the Spark; (2) **half set up**: e.g. the iPhone app paired but no notifications, a mailbox connected but "Important mail" off, the second sign-in step on but no passkey, an own speaker but no voice taught; (3) **repetition**: e.g. five web searches in seven days without "Look locally first" (only the kind of question per day is counted, never the words); (4) **new for you** (since V01.0.538): the admin released a function that you still have to set up yourself, that is one with its own item under Me → Let's go (calendar, e-mail, weather place, stop, smart home, iPhone app, speaker, voice, second sign-in step …). What works without you doing anything (e.g. "Ask instead of guessing", Chats, Service watch) does not come as a suggestion. The hint goes as soon as the panel sees it is set up, as soon as "Check" reports "Set up" (the Spark remembers that like "Do not show again"), and at the latest 14 days after the release. Setting up (5): if it is only a setting of your profile, the face's window shows the "Apply" card (the same rules and checks as "Suggesting settings", ten minutes "Undo"); if it needs details, it shows the steps from the guide (for a profile without the admin's steps) and jumps to the place where you set it: "Open the page" takes the way from the guide (e.g. Calendar → Settings → "Add calendar"), else the function's row on its Me page, unfolds it and marks it (since V01.0.538); passwords only into the form; "Check" asks the panel whether it works now. If it is off on the Spark, "Ask the admin" sends the admins a notification with the function's name (at most once a day); an admin gets the way to Features. Me → Overview (on a phone above the list) shows at most three suggestions with a reason (6); under Proactive at most one a week arrives as a notification (7, 10 am to 7 pm, never in quiet hours; on from the start, can be switched off). Sparing: at most one hint per conversation and three a week, "Do not show again" is for good, none after text from mail or the web, under the emergency stop, at a speaker, watch or Siri, on Telegram and over MCP, never for guests or a device key. Per profile only which hints came and were declined is stored, and the number of questions per kind (`tipps.json`, 14 days). Switches: Features → below "Help with the Spark" "Suggesting functions" (below it "… also by itself"), released for every profile; the note by itself can be switched off under Me → By itself → "Suggest a function". Log: lines `tipps:`; Logs → Requests shows `hinweis` at the switch; Status → Live events "Suggesting functions" (shown, applied, do not show again, admin asked), never contents. Plan: `plaene/funktionen-entdecken.md`; tests: `app/tests/test_tipps.py`.

**Show steps and approvals (V01.0.743, off by default):** Below every answer a folded line shows which tools the assistant used ("3 tools used · 2.1 s"), unfolded each with ✓ or ⚠ and its time; only the readable name (`werkzeuge.NAMES`), whether it worked (`taten.failed`) and the milliseconds are shown, never what was handed to the tool or what it found. When the answer left a proposal waiting for a plain "Ja" (appointment, mailbox, list, memory, job, message, announcement, own service, smart home alert, undo, NAS filing), a card "Waiting for your yes: Appointment" with "Yes" and "No" stands below it. A tap sends "Ja" or "Nein" like a typed answer from the same device and conversation; the module that made the proposal decides with its fixed rules (`calendars.confirms` …) and only then carries it out. The card itself carries nothing out and names only the kind of the proposal (fixed words in `ablauf.KINDS`), never its text; it comes only for a proposal of this answer, device and conversation, at most one per answer, never under the emergency stop (all proposals are dropped there). Only for a signed-in profile on its own device, never for guests or another voice. Works in the panel's assistant, in the Android app (which shows the panel) and in the iPhone app (the same events `step` and `confirm`). Switch: Features → "Show steps and approvals". Log: `chat: freigabe-karte gezeigt | n`. Plan: `plaene/redde-ideen/plan.md` (package A); tests: `app/tests/test_ablauf.py`.

**Question cards (V01.0.806, Dominik 2026-10-18 "1a 2a 3a 4a 5a"):** part of "Show steps and approvals", no switch of its own. When no proposal waits and the answer ends with a question to the person, its answers stand below as buttons (`ablauf.asks`, fixed rules on the last sentence only, never made by the model): "Ja" and "Nein" (or "Yes"/"No") for a fixed sentence start ("Soll ich", "Möchtest du", "Willst du", "Darf ich", "Passt das", "…, oder?", "Shall I" …); for "A or B?" or "A, B or C?" the short parts from the question's own words (at most 3, each at most 30 characters and 4 words, no links, signs or long numbers; a trailing verb like "ablegen" is dropped) and "Something else …", which only puts the cursor in the text field. After mail or other outside text never parts of the question on a button (then only yes/no, for "A or B?" nothing). A tap sends the word like a typed answer; only then is anything done, with all rules. The Weiche (3a): a plain "yes"/"no" (`ablauf.short_reply`) or a button for this question keeps the group of the person's own question before (`intent.follow_up(…, answered=True)`, `ablauf.was_asked`: same device and conversation, ten minutes, once); before, a "yes" got no group and often not the tools it needed. Everywhere: panel and Android (`choiceCard`), iPhone (`AnswersCard`, event `choice`), Telegram as a one-time reply keyboard, Pebble as follow-ups to tap (then the model makes none); the speaker stays with speech. Only `rueckfrage-pending.json` is kept (the button words, ten minutes). Log: `chat: rueckfrage-karte gezeigt | n`, `weiche: Antwort auf Rückfrage - group`. Plan `plaene/rueckfrage-karten.md`; tests `test_ablauf.Questions`, `test_ablauf.Chat`, `test_pebble`, `test_klaeren`.

**You can also say (V01.0.684, basic set):** functions that are already on for you but that you never use because you do not know the word ("Draw me …", "Call me tomorrow at seven") are shown with an example sentence to tap. A tap only puts the sentence into the input field; you send it yourself, and it then goes through the Weiche and every rule like a typed question. Which sentences come is decided by fixed rules in the panel (`saetze.py`), never by the model; the sentences are fixed in the code, German and English, in the same list `test_werkzeug_abdeckung` checks (`werkzeug_abdeckung.SENTENCES`, every sentence really reaches its tool). Only what `features.allowed` permits for your profile, what is set up (calendar, mailbox) and what works on the device is suggested. Where it shows: (1) **under the answer** up to three matching follow-up sentences (a fixed table, e.g. after the weather "Remind me of the umbrella tomorrow morning", after a picture "Make the background blue"), only tools you never used; (2) **"Try this"** in an empty chat: three sentences, different ones each week; (3) **"Did you mean …?"** for words the Weiche does not know but that are close (e.g. "ring me" → call, "think of …" → reminder); this is only a suggestion, the Weiche switches nothing because of it; (4) **"What can you do?"** names example sentences that work for you; (5) **Me → What I can do**: all sentences by area, with a search field, "new" and "never used", in the apps too; (6) **newly released** without setup: once "New for you: …" under the next answer; (7) **while talking** (speaker, phone, watch, Siri, CarPlay) at most one short sentence at the end of an answer per day, only functions that work by voice (own switch "Tips while talking", each profile can switch it off under Me → Conversation); (8) **Telegram** as reply buttons, **TUI** as a line "Tip: …" and `/tipps`; (9) the **weekly note** under On its own brings a "sentence of the week" when there is nothing to set up; (10) **for the admin** Features → Routing shows "Nobody finds it": released functions nobody used for 30 days, and how often suggestions were shown and tapped. Sparing: one row per answer, one sentence per tool at most every 14 days, six a day, never after text from mail or web, never in the emergency stop, never for guests. Stored per profile is only which tool was used when and which sentence was shown (`tipps.json`, names and numbers, never questions); Me → What I can do → "Delete my count" deletes it. Switches: Features → below "Suggesting functions" "You can also say" and below it "Tips while talking". Log: lines `tipps:`; Status → Live events "Funktionen vorschlagen" (shown, tapped), never contents. Plan: `plaene/funktionen-zeigen.md`; tests: `app/tests/test_saetze.py`.

**Cheat sheet to print (V01.0.849):** under Me → What I can do, "Deutsch" or "English" opens a page of two A4 sheets: per area the routing's cards with sentences to say, in each sentence the word the routing really reacts to marked (from `intent.classify`, with the admin's own words under Features → Routing), plus key words, the name of the function, short commands, "Easily mixed up" and what helps when an answer goes wrong. Only what is released and set up for your profile is on it. Print it or pick "Save as PDF" in the print window (on the iPhone Share → Print); "Download as file" keeps the page. The sheet is built from the code each time it opens (`spickzettel.py`: sentences from `werkzeug_abdeckung.SENTENCES`, areas from `saetze.AREAS`, names from `features.py`, version from `app/VERSION`), so it never goes out of date; `test_spickzettel` turns red when a tool has no card or a sentence, key word or mix-up does not land where the sheet says. Part of "You can also say", no switch of its own; never for guests; the page has no scripts and a strict content security policy. Log: `tipps: Spickzettel de|en <n> Werkzeuge`; Live: event "Spickzettel". Plan: `plaene/spickzettel.md`.

**Basic set for newcomers (V01.0.721, Dominik 2026-10-17):** so a new profile chats well from the first question, a **new installation** has a fixed list on (`features.GRUNDAUSSTATTUNG`, each entry with its reason): targeted tool choice, fetch clear questions directly, instant acknowledgement, fetch ahead on a question back, ask instead of guessing with remember the choice, follow-up without wake word, undo by voice, learning from corrections, face shows what it does, help with the Spark with "Ask Spark on every page", suggesting functions, you can also say, lists for shopping and tasks and instant history sync. Only what needs no setup of its own, sends nothing new out of the house, loads no extra model and wants no admin code may go in; guests still only ask and get answers. An **existing installation** never changes by itself: at the top of Features the card "Basic set for a good conversation" is one line with "x of n on" and "Take them all"; "What is off?" opens what is still off below it, with "Look at each" (since V01.0.788, so Features stays short) (filter "Recommended"; every row of the list carries "Recommended"). Taking it back works under Status → Logs → Admin-Protokoll with "Undo". Below, the card asks once **"May the Spark use the internet?"**: yes switches on the weather, Wikipedia straight away, the service watch (checks two fixed public addresses) and the web search, the web search only when a SearXNG address is stored; no keeps the answer (`chat.grund_internet`) and switches nothing on. Once everything is on and the question answered, the card goes away. The same is in the iPhone app under Manage Spark → Funktionen. In an empty chat the first "Try this" sentence is "Where do I set the voice?" until the profile has asked the Spark about itself once. Endpoint `GET`/`POST /api/admin/grundausstattung` (admin only, never the Verwalter, rate limited, 256 bytes); every take-over is in the admin log ("Schalter geändert", also under Status → Live with the events). Plan: `grundeinstellungen/plan.md`; tests: `app/tests/test_grundausstattung.py`.

**Tool coverage (plan `plaene/werkzeug-abdeckung.md`, Dominik 13 Oct):** every function stands in `werkzeug_abdeckung.py` with its tools, with the panel's fixed rule that answers it, or with the reason there is nothing to ask for; a self-test keeps the list complete. The switch no longer drops tools a module offers only because the person names them ("… onto the reMarkable", the name of an own MCP service): they stay whatever group the rest of the sentence is ("Put my shopping list onto the reMarkable"). Newly recognized: doors, windows, washing machine, dryer, dishwasher, power, battery and camera (smart home), "Call Mum" and "Navigate home" (iPhone), "tick off milk" (list), "Was hab ich heute vor?" and compound appointment words (calendar), "Postfach" (mail), "Bestellung" (parcels). "Turn on web search" goes to the help with its card as a question about the Spark, never to the web search. Fixed: "Delete the reminder …" never deleted anything; "Is room mode on in the living room?" became a start proposal and now says which devices are listening.

**More to read (tool coverage, package 7):** what was only on a page can now be asked. "Which documents do I have?" and "Which invoices do I have?" (`document_list`, offered with the documents) names the document cards, filtered by kind or tags. "What is on my reMarkable?" and "Which tasks did you find on the reMarkable?" (`remarkable_library`, `rmliste.py`) reads the list of the last comparison and the tasks found, never asks the cloud itself and stays when the words name the reMarkable. "What does the front door camera see?" (`camera_describe`) describes the still picture of a Home Assistant camera in a few words, with the same switches as the camera picture in rules (Features → Describe a camera picture and the own "Camera picture" under Me → By itself, both off) and at most 30 pictures per hour; the picture is never kept. "Which smart home rules do I have?" (`home_rules_list`) names the rules of "Speaking up by itself". All four results are outside text (names, handwriting, device names, the model's description): nothing is switched or changed after them, and none goes over MCP.

**Changing by voice (tool coverage, package 6, `aendern.py`):** what could only be created so far can now be changed too. Everything except switching waits for the yes in the next message, goes through the emergency stop and is locked after outside text; none of it goes over MCP.
- **Appointments** (`calendar_change`): "Move the dentist to Friday 15:30", "Delete tomorrow's appointment with Anna". It searches the writable CalDAV calendars by title and day; a repeating appointment is never changed (that only works in the calendar app), and when several fit the Spark asks. The server's etag guards against a change made meanwhile elsewhere. To move one the model no longer uses `calendar_add` (no second appointment).
- **Scheduled jobs** (`agent_change`): "Pause the weekly overview", "Resume the weather plan", "Delete the scheduled job …".
- **Smart home rules** (`home_rule_delete`): "Delete the rule for the front door".
- **Notes by itself** (`proactive_pause`): "Don't speak up any more today" (until midnight or some hours, at most 24), "You may speak up again"; at once, like "not now" after a note.
- **Volume of own speakers** (`speaker_volume`): "Make the kitchen speaker quieter", "Volume to 40"; own devices only, at once like switching.
- **Repeating reminders** (`reminder_set` with `repeat`): "Remind me every Monday at 7 about the bins": daily, weekdays, weekly or monthly (same day, at most the 28th), always at the same clock time, also across the clock change.
- **A message into an own group** (`message_send`): "Write into the family group that I'll be late"; only groups the profile is in, never private chats.
- **Undo** (`undo_last`, Features → "Undo by voice", off; once on it holds for every profile): the Spark keeps its last five deeds for 30 minutes in memory only (reminder, list entry, note, appointment entered, moved or deleted, device on or off, volume, pause, job, rule) and proposes to take back the last one; only the yes does it. For devices behind the code word, say "yes" with the code word.

**Status by voice (`zustand_wz.py`, tool `spark_zustand`):** "Is everything running?", "Is Home Assistant reachable?", "When was the last backup?", "Is there an update?", "Is the emergency stop on?", "Is my mailbox connected?". The Spark reads its own state instead of guessing: every profile its part of Me → My status and its own emergency stop, an admin (as its own recognized profile) also the service watch, the last backup, the installed version and what the last update check found (the tool never asks GitHub itself). Fixed words and numbers only, no error texts or addresses; nothing is switched. Offered only when the person's own words ask about the state (switch group "zustand"). Switch: Features → Status by voice (`chat.status_voice`), off; once on it holds for every profile; never for guests, never over MCP.

**Exact calculations (`rechnen.py`, tool `rechnen`, Dominik 2026-10-22):** A language model is bad at arithmetic, so the panel computes. The model only writes down the calculation or the dates, the panel computes with its own small calculator (split into tokens, then an own parser; never `eval`, `exec`, sympy or a foreign calculator) and returns its result as "Ergebnis vom Panel: …"; the model only says that figure. It handles numbers with German or English decimal marks, + − × ÷, powers, brackets (also "Klammer auf"), percent ("19 % of 249", "249 plus 19 %"), square roots and pi, with `Decimal` instead of floats (0.1 + 0.2 is 0.3). Date arithmetic by fixed rules: days until a day, days between two days, the weekday of a day, the date in n days (also "Christmas", "New Year", "24.12."). Hard limits: at most `rechnen.MAX_CHARS` (200) characters and `rechnen.MAX_TOKENS` (80) tokens, `rechnen.MAX_DEPTH` (12) nested brackets, powers up to `rechnen.MAX_EXP` (100), results up to 10¹⁵, dates up to 100 years; else "Fehler beim Rechnen" with a fixed reason. Offered only when the person's own words ask for a calculation (`rechnen.WORDS`), next to any Weiche group (`werkzeug_abdeckung.BY_WORD`). The answer check also computes every calculation in a sentence of the answer again ("17 times 23 is 381" becomes "… is 391", `rechnen.fix`) before it is shown or said; while the switch is on, every sentence comes only when it is complete. The panel's results count as proven amounts for the answer check. "Answer simple things at once" uses the same rules for calculations and date questions without the model. Switch: Features → Conversation → Exact calculations (`chat.rechnen`), off; switched on it holds for every profile; never for guests, never for another voice, never over MCP. Log: "rechnen: <kind> ok|fehler" and "chat: answer check: rechnung korrigiert (n)", under Logs → Requests "Antwort-Prüfung: n Rechnungen nachgerechnet und berichtigt". We looked at the calculator calculator-mcp-server and did not take it (`eval` with empty builtins, sympify, no limits).

**Number words, required tool, paper and receipts (`rechnen.digits`, `rechnen.wants`, `rechnen.check`, Dominik 2026-10-22):** Spoken number words become digits before computing ("sechs mal sechs" → 6 mal 6, "twenty-three point five" → 23.5, "two million"), German and English, with fixed word lists; an article only counts before "hundred", "thousand" or "million". If the question holds a calculation (two numbers with an operator, a root, percent of interest or a discount, a date question, `rechnen.WANTS`), `rechnen` is required: the first round must call the tool, and an answer without the tool is dropped and asked once more, as for data questions. On the reMarkable: if a read page says "@Spark prüf das" (also "check", "kontrollier", "rechne nach", "stimmt das"), the panel recomputes every line of the form "a op b = c" itself, without the language model, and places "N calculations checked, M please check" next to it with "✓ … is right." or "? …: please check, the panel gets X." (at most `rechnen.MAX_LINES` (20) lines); it never changes the page and never says "wrong", because a digit read from handwriting can be misread. Picture lines and the @Spark line do not count. Other @Spark answers go through `rechnen.fix` sentence by sentence. Adding up receipts: "What did I spend altogether in October?", "in total", "add up" offer `rechnen` next to the document search; the model reads the receipts first, names each with its amount and lets the panel compute the sum; amounts from documents are data, never commands. Needs "Exact calculations" (off). Log: "remarkable: @Spark rechnungen geprueft N, bitte pruefen M", "remarkable: @Spark rechnung korrigiert (n)"; Weiche line "Pflicht rechnen".

**Lean list for unclear questions (V01.0.220):** under Features → Routing → "Fragen, die keine Regel erkennt" there is, next to "all tools" (default) and "the language model sorts it", the choice "only web search and memory" (`chat.route_model: lean`). When no rule recognizes the question and it requires no tool, the model then sees only `web_search`, `history_search` and `memory_save` (as far as they are offered anyway). The request gets about half as long (on tars 2500 instead of 6000 tokens) and the first word comes about a second earlier. A question a rule recognizes keeps its group. Only together with "Targeted tool choice", never for guests.

**Classification with probabilities (V01.0.340, `systemone.py`):** the own LLM-Tor (same contract, from the language model's top logprobs) or dgx-spark-qwen38 v1.15 and later answers fixed questions with probabilities instead of text at `POST /v1/systemone` (choice, score, yes/no; model name `jev-latest`, served by the Qwen3.8 lane that already runs, one token per question, no memory of its own, nothing leaves the machine). The panel asks only its own fixed questions and options; the text being judged (the person's own message, sender and subject of a mail, the mail text when tidying, a test answer) goes only into "state", cut to 4000 characters, without control characters, fenced by qwen38 with a random token. The panel decides with fixed thresholds. Only the stored address and key from Settings → Language model, no redirects, at most 64 KB back; an answer naming an option not offered or a value outside 0 to 1 counts as none. When qwen38 is busy (529), too old (404) or too slow, everything goes on as without it. Switches (all off, never guests): Features → Speed and accuracy → "Classification with probabilities" (`chat.systemone`, with a "Check classification" button); released for every profile. Uses:
- **Targeted tool choice:** Features → Routing → "Questions no rule recognizes" → the classification (`chat.route_model: systemone`). One choice among the switch's groups plus "unklar"; it narrows only from the threshold "Tool choice narrows from" (default 80 %) and never on "unklar", otherwise the model sees all allowed tools. When the admin blocks a profile, there is no pick for it. Logs → Requests shows "classification (systemone): picked (91 %)" or "too unsure" at the switch step.
- **Inbox tidying** (`chat.systemone_tidy`): stage 4 gets its category with a real probability; in "automatic" mode the Spark moves only from 90 % (else it asks), reason "systemone, 93 % sicher". Without systemone, stage 4 asks the language model for JSON as before.
- **Recognize important mail** (`chat.systemone_mail` below speaking up by itself, profile E-mail → Settings → Important mail → `pro_mail_smart`): also mail from senders not on the list when a yes/no on sender and subject (never the mail text) gives at least 80 %. Only mail arriving after switching on, at most 5 questions per check, waits while someone speaks; busy: the same mails next time. Since V01.1.107 only after sorting (Dominik 2026-10-26): only what is still in the inbox after your mail rules and "Tidy the inbox" is reported; while "Tidy the inbox" has not looked at a new mail yet, it waits (at most 2 hours). Fixed rules decide before the model (the same stages as tidying): automatic senders (no-reply, notifications), lists and bulk mail, social networks, bills, ads, login codes, mails one of your mail rules moves and senders on your "Not important" list are never important, the model is not even asked. Senders on your own list still always count. The card's "Not important" button adds its sender to that list (`pro_mail_not`, E-mail → Settings and the iPhone under By itself: view and delete). Open cards that these rules do not count as important closed by themselves once. Live (E-mail): "Wichtige Mail: heute N gemeldet, M aussortiert"; logs `proactive: mail sorted | … new, … not important, … asked`, `proactive: mail cards tidied | n`, `proactive: mail sender not important | added|kept`.
- **Quality test** (`chat.systemone_quality`): a second opinion per question, "answers" 0 to 2 and "made up" in percent. It never counts for passing; a passed question the classification doubts (below 1 or 50 % made up and more) stays open to look at.
- **Classification in the settings assistant** (`chat.systemone_berater`, admin only): picking the fitting catalog proposal for a case (`berater.pick`) as a choice with a probability (options `o0`, `o1` … plus "none"); it proposes only from 60 % (`systemone.BERATER_MIN`), otherwise the catalog order holds. Reason on the proposal "Einordnung, 74 % sicher". The person's words go only into "state". qwen38 busy or missing: the previous JSON question. `_cand` and the check stay the bolt.
- **Check answers in the background** (`chat.systemone_check`, needs the settings assistant): after a finished answer for which tools ran, the panel asks the two questions of the quality test in the background (question, up to four tool results, answer). From 90 % "made up" (`systemone.CHECK_MIN`) a case "Made-up suspected" is made (`faelle.KINDS["erfunden"]`, finding "The answer names details that are in no result", proposals answer check, temperature, quality test). Runs through `vorrang` ("Antwort prüfen", waits while somebody speaks), at most 50 times a day, never before speaking; sentences with the code word never.
- **Recognize complaints** (`chat.systemone_klage`, needs "Stop error loops"): the deeds of a profile's last answer stay in memory for 10 minutes (`taten.note_last`, only kind, worked, a short name). When the next message says "still does not work", "it is still on" (fixed list `taten.COMPLAINT`) or, when the list finds nothing, the classification from 80 % (`systemone.KLAGE_MIN`, 1.5 s limit), the model gets a fixed note to look first and say the state honestly, and the tools that change things of that kind (`taten.locked`) are not offered in this answer. When the message itself asks for it ("… turn it off again", `taten.RETRY`), everything stays offered. Logs → Requests shows "Beschwerde (Panel)" with "Liste" or "Einordnung"; Live shows "n Beschwerden erkannt" at the Taten node.
- **Narrator bench** (`chat.dungeon_erzaehlstand`, admin only, on the button): for every fixed scene the model's own view, "vivid" and "follows the actions" in per cent (use `erzaehler`); never gives points, the fixed rules do.
- **Label mass and two orders** (qwen38 v1.15 and later): `x-systemone-label-mass` says how much probability fell on the options at all. Below 50 % (`systemone.MIN_MASS`) the answer counts as unsure and the caller goes on without it (fixed rule, Live counts "unsure"). `x-systemone-branches` shows whether qwen38 asks every question in two orders (`SYSTEMONE_PERMUTATIONS=2`, more accurate and better calibrated by qwen38's measurement); "Check classification" and Live show it. Switching it on at the Spark: `sudo systemctl edit qwen38-keepalive.service`, in it `[Service]` and `Environment=SYSTEMONE_PERMUTATIONS=2`, then `sudo systemctl restart qwen38-keepalive.service`.
- **Measure classification** (button under Features → Routing at "Questions no rule recognizes", `POST`/`GET /api/admin/systemone/messen`, admin only): the switch's test sentences (`intent_bench.BENCH` without sentences of two groups, plus the own ones from Features → Routing, at most 200) go one by one as background work ("Einordnung messen") through the tool-choice question. Result per threshold 60/70/80/90 %: how many sentences would be narrowed and how many of them wrongly, label mass, median time, mix-ups per group. Suggestion: the lowest threshold with at least 10 narrowings and at most 3 % wrong; "Take the suggestion" sets "Tool choice narrows from" (`chat.systemone_min`, only 0.6/0.7/0.8/0.9, default 0.8), saved like any setting. Memory only, one journal line per measurement.
Journal: `systemone:` lines with use and milliseconds, `weiche: systemone wetter 0.91`; never text. Status → Live shows "⚖ n" (classifications today) at the language model and per use, average milliseconds, busy and no answer in its tooltip; failures show under events ("Einordnung"). Self-tests: `app/tests/test_systemone.py`.

**First sentence sooner (V01.0.220):** a long first sentence goes to speech output at its last comma from 50 characters on (before: 120), if that comma comes after at least 20 characters, so the first sound comes earlier.

**Formatted answers (V01.0.451):** The panel's chat (browser, phone view, Android app) shows Markdown in answers formatted: headings, **bold**, *italic*, numbered and plain lists, quotes, `code`, code blocks, tables and links, already while the answer comes in. Built safely (`md.js`): model text never becomes HTML, every element is made with `createElement`/`textContent`, only a fixed list of elements, pictures are not loaded (only linked), links only for http(s) to public addresses (no localhost, no home network, no `name:password@` addresses, otherwise the address stays text); they open in a new tab without referrer and name the host when the link text says something else; very long answers (over 60,000 characters) stay plain text. The signs are never spoken: both speech services take the Markdown signs out before every sentence (`textnorm.speech_markdown`, whatever the switch "do not read out emojis, code and Markdown" says), `code` is spoken as a word in quotes, links only by their text, code blocks not at all. This runs before the rule "never say passwords", so "**Password** is `…`" is not said either. Where nothing can be formatted, clean text without asterisks arrives (`textnorm.plain_markdown`): Telegram, Siri, companion, reMarkable, watch and the speakers' display; the stored history keeps the original. The iPhone app shows bold, italic, code and links (http(s) only) right in the bubble; copy and share give the text without signs. So that the model structures longer answers, only the panel's chat gets the fixed hint `chat.WEB_HINT` (for spoken answers without tables and code blocks, with "read answers aloud" off with both); short answers stay whole sentences. Checked answers (answer check) now keep their line breaks. No switch: display only, nothing leaves the house. Test: `test_markdown`. The automatic daily briefing (Calendar → Settings, push "Your day") is kept in the history sorted by appointments, reminders, mails and topics (`chat.BRIEF_MD_SYSTEM`); the push gets the same text without signs. The reMarkable morning sheet keeps plain prose. Asked for the briefing in the chat, the model may also sort it by areas with lists there. **Companion formatted:** "Ask Spark" in the companion (Windows and Mac) now shows answers formatted too: headings and "**Appointments:**" lines in bold, lists with marks and indent, `code` in monospace (Windows), or bold, italic, code and links (Mac, public http(s) addresses only). For that `/api/desk/ask` sends, next to `answer` (plain text, for older companions, reading aloud and copying), the field `md` with the Markdown; passwords are hidden in both, and `md` is left out when the plain text hides more than the Markdown. The model gets the same hint for the companion as for the chat (`chat.WEB_HINT`, without tables and code blocks because the companion may read aloud). Nothing is shown as HTML or run (`MdText.cs`, `MdAnswer` in `AskView.swift`). Visible only with a new companion build. The companion's morning start (`/api/desk/daily?kind=morning`) now also comes sorted like the chat's daily briefing (`chat.BRIEF_MD_SYSTEM`): `md` for the window, `text` without signs for the notification and older companions; the evening stays a fixed list without the model. Test: `test_begleiter`.

### More precise and faster (since V01.0.755)

Plan `plaene/genauer-und-schneller.md`, package A (Dominik 2026-10-17).

- **Measuring per kind of question:** every spoken answer keeps, besides the time to the first sound, the switch group, the number of tools offered, the language model's rounds, the share it read from its cache, the seconds to its first character and to the end, and the version (`latency.MEASURE`, fixed words and numbers only, never text, two weeks in `state/latency.json`). *Status → Checks* shows below, folded, "Per kind of question, last 7 days" (slowest first, median and 9 of 10) and "Per version" (the last four). This shows which kind of question is slow and whether a new version helped. The language model now always reports how much it read (`stream_options.include_usage`).
- **Short answers without a screen:** speakers, the phone, the watch, Siri, the Android assistant and CarPlay get at most `chat.SPOKEN_MAX_TOKENS` (700) tokens; their hints ask for one to three sentences anyway. A longer answer ends at its last full sentence.
- **Names for listening** (*Features → Names for listening*, `chat.hoer_namen`, off): the speech recognition gets, with every recording of a profile, the names of all profiles, its contacts and the rooms and devices from its Home Assistant as context (`hoerwoerter.py`, at most 100 names, 700 characters, before the own context under *Settings → Speech recognition*). Contacts and Home Assistant only when they are released for the profile. Shared speakers and the phone get only the profile names, never contacts; guests nothing. Home Assistant is read in the background (at most every 30 minutes) and kept in memory only; the log line `hoeren:` has numbers only. The speakers now also take the default language from *Settings → Speech recognition* instead of always "auto".
- **Complete spoken text:** units after a number (km/h, km, kWh, kW, kg, g, l, GB, MB, min, h, W …) and German abbreviations ("z. B.", "ca.", "bzw.", "usw.", "Nr.", "Str." …) are spoken in full, "30 €/Monat" becomes "dreißig Euro pro Monat" (`textnorm.UNITS`, `speak_abbreviations`, in both speech services).

Package B (since V01.0.760):

- **A fixed tool list per group** (with *Fast answer start*, `chat.prompt_cache`): when the switch recognizes a group, the language model always gets exactly its tools in the same order instead of the full list with a note on what is not available now. So the server keeps one start per group in its cache, and the model only chooses among fitting tools. An unclear question still sees the full list; what the rules do not offer, the panel never runs.
- **Trim a large memory** (*Features → Trim a large memory*, `chat.memory_trim`, off): when a profile's memory is longer than `chat.MEMORY_CORE` (6000) characters, only the newest facts stay fixed in the system prompt, plus at most `chat.MEMORY_EXTRA` (5) older ones that share words with the question, for this turn only (`chat.memory_split`). The fixed part does not depend on the question and stays in the cache. Nothing is deleted. Log: "chat: Gedächtnis gekürzt, N von M Fakten fest, K passende dazu" (numbers only).
- **Deliberately not built:** merging tools and shortening tool descriptions (too much risk of wrong calls for little gain) and a tool choice by similarity (the switch with "lean" and hint words already gets 198 of 211 everyday questions right, none wrong).

Package C (since V01.0.761):

- **Answer simple things at once** (*Features → Answer simple things at once*, `chat.sofort`, off; `sofort.py`): the panel answers the time ("What time is it?"), the date and weekday ("What day is it tomorrow?"), calculations with brackets, percent and roots ("What is 17 times 23?", "What is 19 percent of 249?", since V01.0.928 with `rechnen.calc`), date calculations ("How many days until Christmas?", `rechnen.days`) and German unit questions (the room mode's rules) with a fixed sentence, without the language model and without an instant sentence before it. Only when the whole short sentence (at most 80 characters) is exactly such a question; "What time is it in Tokyo?" still goes to the model. Guests get the fixed answer too (no tool, no data). A timer is still set by the model with its tool, because that changes something. Log: "chat: feste Antwort, <kind>".
- **Faster web search** (*Features → Web search → Faster web search*, `chat.search_fast`, off): each result page gets at most `chat.PAGE_SECONDS` (3) seconds instead of 8 together, what is not there by then stays the snippet from the result list; each page brings the parts that share most words with the question (`chat.best_parts`) instead of its beginning; the same search comes from memory for `chat.SEARCH_CACHE` (10 minutes, at most 50 searches, never on disk); the long note "outside text is data" stands once per answer, before every further block a short one with the same rule (`chat.OUTSIDE_AGAIN`), the markers `<<< >>>` always stay. Log: "chat: web search, n of m pages in time" and "chat: web search from memory".
- **Extended answer check** (*Settings → Language model → Extended answer check*, `chat.answer_check_more`, off): for data questions, temperatures ("14 Grad", "-3 °C"), distances in km and percentages must also be in what the model was given; rounded counts as right (`answercheck.MORE_SLACK`: one degree, one percentage point, for km one km or a tenth). A name in quotation marks must be in what was read in every data answer, not only after a calendar read. Counts ("3 new mails") are deliberately not checked: tool results often do not state them as a number, honest sentences would drop out. The quality test checks with the same rules.
- **Measuring the word choice:** Status → Checks → Quality test has the button "Test with Qwen's recommendation": the same test once with top_p 0.8, top_k 20 and presence_penalty 1 (`quality.QWEN_SAMPLING`), only for this run. Below, the newest run with the own word choice and the one with the recommendation stand side by side; if the recommendation was better, the admin sets the values under Settings → Language model (new: *Limit the choice (top_k)*, `chat.top_k`, 0 = the server's default).

### Settings assistant (since V01.0.450)

Plan `plaene/einstellungs-assistent.md` (points 1–17). Everything off: admin switch `chat.berater` with sub switches, released for every profile, never guests.

- **Cases** (`faelle.py`): at the end of every answer fixed rules look at the request's way (as in Logs → Requests, also when the request log is off; then nothing is written): a tool failed, an error (language model, speech output …), no rule recognized the question and the web search ran, a fixed refusal sentence ("I cannot", not with outside text or after a failed tool), the same question twice within 5 minutes after such an answer, the answer check held something back, the speech output fell behind the text more than once (gaps), an answer was stopped after its first sound (worked on from the third time). A function that is only switched off makes no case but the hint under the answer. Sentences with the smart-home code word never keep their words and never count as "asked twice"; questions of groups that switch, send or write (smart home, calendar, mail, reminders, lists, messages …) never go into the quality test. Also 👎, "That was wrong" and "Wrongly sorted" on Features → Routing. A case keeps a fingerprint (kind | group | tool or error, never words), counts and at most three examples with the request number; the question itself only with consent (👎 "Send the question along" or the profile switch "My name in the request log"), never from guests, never from outside text. 30 days, 1000 cases (`STATE/faelle.json`).
- **👍/👎** (`chat.bewerten`): buttons under the answer, with 👎 five reasons (wrong tool, wrong answer, too slow, not understood, mispronounced) and 200 characters of text; changeable for 24 hours, at most 10 👎 a day. Spoken: "That was wrong", "That was good" (fixed sentence, no tools). 👍 is only counted (per day, group and tool, `STATE/bewertungen.json`); with consent the question becomes a **confirmed sentence** (`STATE/gut-saetze.json`, 200) no proposal may sort differently.
- **Detectors** (every 30 minutes, each can be switched off under Status → Improve → Catalog): many unclear questions (over 30 % of at least 30 in 7 days), often slow (over 30 % of at least 10 in 24 hours), errors pile up (5 in 24 hours), quality test newly red, a service was gone (service watch), memory below the reserve, few 👍 in a group (under 75 % of at least 20 in 7 days).
- **Catalog** (`berater_katalog.py`): stage A Weiche (own word, "not with", test sentence), must-word, pronunciation, word for the speech recognition, question into the quality test; stage B fine values in narrow ranges, one step (web search results and pages, lookups, wait for own sources, history, temperatures, first sound); stage C switches with a second step (a code on the page, 2 minutes); P a profile's own settings. Stage D never: passwords and keys, the language model's address, qwen38, Notaus and Wächter, second sign-in step, roles, ports, guest access, sending mail, MCP to the outside, reporting problems, updates, backups.
- **Proposal**: fixed rules build the fitting candidates from the catalog. Only when the language model runs at home (loopback or private address) it picks one of them (JSON schema, a choice only among the candidates' numbers, one sentence of reason shown as data); at most `chat.berater_calls` (20) a day, through `vorrang` (speech first) and only after a minute without speech. Otherwise the rule takes the first.
- **Test bench**: the same check as saving (`admin.validate`), every test sentence of the Weiche (built in and own), the confirmed sentences, the questions of the last 7 days (how many land differently), ranges and step. Red cannot be taken; "Other" shows the remaining candidates.
- **Take** writes through the panel's own ways (the Weiche with its history, else `admin._save` with undo and service restart, the quality test through its list) and keeps the value before; **Undo** puts exactly that back. **Re-check** after `chat.berater_check_h` (24) hours: the case did not come back "helps", once "unchanged", twice or a test sentence red "worse".
- **Routes of a case**: local (catalog, test bench green), **report** (`chat.berater_issue`, needs Reporting problems): a draft in fixed words (kind, group, tool, numbers, never a question), sent only after its preview; **environment** (`chat.berater_env`): for services outside steps to copy (e.g. `speech-spark status`, `sudo speech-spark logs tts -n 100`), nothing is run; "Done" starts the re-check.
- **Ideas outside the catalog** (`chat.berater_ideas`, admins only, off): when only a report or the environment remains for a case, the language model in the LAN writes an idea (at most 400 characters), also on "Get idea". It is marked "Idea, not checked" and is never applied. When it names a setting, that is only a key of the configuration without secrets, addresses and access (never from stage D); the page shows its name with the way as a jump link, never the key, and the admin changes it alone. The model gets the case in plain words instead of raw codes, and keys or tool names in its text become names before they are shown (`klartext.readable`). Steps for the environment (only with `chat.berater_env`) are to copy; chains, redirection, deleting, downloading and rights are filtered out. "Good idea" counts per setting or wording; from two on "Report as catalog wish" is offered (needs `chat.berater_issue`, a draft under Reporting problems with a preview). Counts against the model calls of the day.
- **Finding before every proposal** (`befund.py`): fixed rules first work out the cause of a case from tool names and yes/no marks of the request (offered, must, called, failed, found nothing, answer begins with "no"), never from words of an answer: wrong group, right tool not called, tool missing, failed, a service reported an error (with the error code in words), found nothing, answer contradicts, no lookup, no rule of the Weiche, refusal, asked again right away, answer check held back, stopped, gaps, detector, answer probably right, cause unclear. Cases noticed automatically always have a cause of their own; "cause not clear yet" stays for a 👎 without an expectation. Proposals only fit the cause; temperature and "Check answers" only for "answer contradicts". A 👎 with an unclear cause gets no proposal but the question "What would have been right?" in the case (expected tool from the tool map, then "Re-assess").
- **Plain words** (since V01.0.555, plan "Verbessern verständlicher" 1–6, `klartext.py`): every case and every proposal shows a card in fixed sentences: *What happened* (the error code or kind of case as a sentence), *Where from* (noticed by the Spark, reported by a person, a detector or taken over from Status → Logs → Diagnosis; device, tool, group, how often, when, "View request" jumps to the request under Status → Logs → Requests), *Why* (the finding with its typical cause) and *What helps* (steps with jump links). Never words of a question. The sentences of the error codes exist once (`klartext.CODES`); Logs → Requests gets them with the list. The same change for several cases is one proposal with "N cases" (also "Prepare as a report": one report for all cases); taking, rejecting, undoing and the re-check apply to all its cases. Words replace the letters (rule, fine value, switch, check yourself, report, profile) with a legend; every proposal says under "Why this proposal" which cause it fits, what it risks and how it is undone. "★ best pick" is set by the panel by fixed rules (test bench green, replay not red, more cases, rule before fine value before switch), never by the model.
- **What needs you, whole way, check yourself** (since V01.0.555, plan 7–9): the top says in words what waits for the admin (proposals to take, checks to do yourself, reports with their cases), the best pick is one tap away, and below it the 👍 share of the last 7 days as a light (green from 85 %, yellow from 75 %, else red; a colour only from 20 ratings). "Whole way" in the card shows the request's way in fixed sentences (with Explain) and its log lines without leaving the page. A "check yourself" proposal shows the service as the service watch sees it now, with "Check now"; the steps are a checklist that stays on the page only (nothing is stored or run).
- **"Should have looked in …"** with 👎 (panel and iPhone app, optional): documents, documents the Spark made, calendar, mails, devices, reminders, web, earlier conversations. Only the choice is kept.
- **Tool map:** every tool belongs to a group of the Weiche or to the general ones; a self-test keeps it so.
- **Gaps** (Status → Improve → Gaps): cases for which no fitting tool was offered, counted per tool (cases, reports, people); the Live overview shows the number.
- **"Not helpful"** on a proposal: like Reject (30 days), but the case gets the next proposal; after three times for the same cause and change that change comes last.
- **Must rules for every tool** (`chat.tool_words`, Features → Routing → must words): a line `tool: word, word` now works for every tool of the Weiche; when exactly one such tool is a must, the first round has to call exactly that one.
- **Tool hints** (`chat.tool_hints`, Settings → Language model → Advanced): one line `tool: sentence` (4 to 200 characters, no angle or curly brackets, at most 20); the model gets the sentence only in answers that offer the tool, also in the quality test. The settings assistant only proposes fixed sentences (look first, look up own doing, no "no" without looking, only the result).
- **Extra tool for a group** as a proposal: when the expected tool is missing because the Weiche left it out, the assistant proposes `chat.route_extra` (Features → Routing, with its history and undo); it is only added when the answer may offer it anyway.
- **Quality test with an expected tool:** a question from a case can go into the quality test with the tool it has to call; the test only counts it as passed when exactly that one was called.
- **Replay:** every proposal for the Weiche, a must rule, a hint, an extra tool or a test sentence runs the case's last question through the fixed rules before and after (group, offered tools, must, called first) and shows "fits" or "does not fit" without naming the question; when the answer is already right without the change, it says so. With "Analyse case" the local model then calls the offered tools once, without running them.
- **Analyse case** (admin switch below the settings assistant, off): the language model in the LAN picks a rule from templates by JSON schema (kind, tool from the tool map, group, template, one word of the question). The panel turns it into a catalog proposal by fixed rules, nothing else; the word has to be a whole word of the question. Test bench and replay run as always, only the admin can take it; every analysis counts against the calls of the day.
- **Rules** (Status → Improve → Rules): every own rule (must words, tool hints, words and "not with" of the Weiche, extra tools, test sentences) with its source (proposal, autopilot, analysis or by hand) and "Remove" for a single entry; the Live overview counts the own rules. On top, **Built in** lists the panel's fixed answer rules: "Check deeds" (a sentence claiming a deed no tool managed in this answer is not said) and "Stop error loops", each with on or off and how often they stepped in today; they cannot be removed there, only switched off with their switches. "Never say no … without" proposals exist only for the tool that knows what the Spark did earlier (document status, put on the reMarkable, reminders, list, appointments); the Spark diary knows the same kinds.
- **Rule versions, export and import:** before every change to the own rules (take, undo, remove, import) the Spark keeps the six rule settings (the last 30, `STATE/regel-versionen.json`, in the backup); "Earlier versions" restores one. "Export" saves the rules as JSON, "Import" checks them like a save and first shows which test sentences of the Weiche would come out differently.
- **Cleanup:** the Rules tab marks rules that are built in already, contradict "not with", are in the group already or were not used for 30 days. Only which rule applied is counted (kind and name), never a word of the question. Removing stays with the admin.
- **Re-check after an update or a model change:** when the version or the language model's name changes, the Spark replays every taken rule and writes "Still works" or "No longer works" on the proposal.
- **Undo automatically** (admin switch, off): the re-check now also counts the 👍 share of the group (7 days before against the time after, from 10 ratings); when it falls by 15 points, the result is "worse". With the switch the Spark then undoes the admin's takes too, otherwise only the autopilot's, and tells the admin profiles.
- **Guard against manipulation:** the autopilot only takes cases from at least two profiles or from an admin; one person alone cannot change anything by itself with many 👎, and the proposal shows that. Rule texts only come from fixed templates.- **English form** (with "Analyse case"): for a must word or a Weiche word the language model in the LAN fetches the English form (one word, schema with a pattern, checked again by the panel); it is the first choice under "Other", with its own test bench.
- **Monthly report** (Status → Improve → Report): 👍 share per group against the 30 days before, rules taken, helpful, worse and undone, open proposals, gaps and own rules, numbers only. On the 1st of a month from 8 am it goes once to the admin profiles with "Note on a new proposal".
- **"Why?" for every profile** (admin switch, off; released for every profile, never guests): the Spark answers "Why did you just search the web?" for every profile in fixed plain sentences: how it understood the question and where it looked, without tool names, rules or settings. Admin profiles with "Ask for proposals in the chat" still get the detailed version.

**Spark diary** (admin switch Features → "Spark diary", off; released for every profile, never guests): the Spark notes what it did for a profile itself: reminder set, mail draft saved, document made, file put on the reMarkable, message sent, device switched (smart home: only the device name and "eingeschaltet"/"ausgeschaltet", never the sentence with the code word; since V01.0.483). 7 days, at most 200 entries, in `users/<id>/tagebuch.json` (stays on the Spark). Questions like "What did you do for me today?" go to the group "create documents", and the first round has to call `spark_tagebuch`. Titles of web pages and mails are stored but never named to the model, only the kind. The journal only gets "tagebuch: <kind> notiert".
- **Questions about the Spark's own doing** ("Was a document made for the reMarkable?", "Is the letter ready?"): a fixed rule (`chat.DONE_WORDS`) makes the first round call `document_status` (with time, where it was sent, and what went to the reMarkable in the last 7 days), with the reMarkable and without "Making documents" `remarkable_sent`; never the uploaded documents. Such a question never sends the answer to the reMarkable.
- **Explain** (`chat.berater_explain`): Status → Logs → Requests → "Explain" describes a request in fixed sentences and names what could help; "Take as a case". Likewise under Status → Logs → Diagnosis at the newest error: what the area is, steps for the service, what could help (only the area, never the log line).
- **In the chat** (`chat.berater_chat`, admin profiles only): "What does the settings assistant propose?" names open proposals; "Why did you just search the web?" (Warum … eben/gerade …) explains the way of the last answer (switch, tools, first sound, errors; from the memory of the last 24 hours); "How do the answers get faster?" names the catalog's steps. A "yes" never takes anything; for everybody else these sentences stay normal questions. **Note** (`chat.berater_notify`, profile `berater_note`): a new proposal to admin profiles, at most 3 a day.
- **Feedback "fixed"** (`chat.bewerten_back`, profile `rate_back`): when a change after a 👎 helps, the person gets one note.
- **Proposals for a profile** (`chat.berater_me`): from the own 👎 of the last two weeks (e.g. twice "too slow": shorter answers, instant reply), at the top of Me → Conversation; only the profile takes them, "No thanks" hides one.
- **Autopilot** (`chat.berater_auto`): stages A and B with a green test bench and at least two reports (two people for 👎, two findings for detectors), at most one change a day, never more than three steps from the admin's value; the re-check undoes it itself on "worse". Never at night (22 to 7 o'clock) and never during a Notaus; every change and every undo goes as a note to the admin profiles with "Note on a new proposal". A rejected proposal does not come back for the same case for 30 days; during a Notaus no proposals are worked out. When a case becomes a report it counts as done once the issue is closed on GitHub; the reporter hears it once.
- **Display**: Status → Improve (inbox, proposals, re-check, done, catalog with detectors; above them the 👍 share per group of the last 7 days), on top of the admin pages and in the iPhone app under "Needs you" "N proposals to improve wait for you" (without changing the status colour), Live node "Improve" with numbers (👍, 👎, cases, open proposals), events "Verbessern". Journal: `faelle:` (kind, group, tool, number) and `berater:`.

## The panel

On the first admin login a **setup wizard** walks through password, language model (with a connection test), voice (to listen to), profiles, access and features, and ends with the live check. Start it again: *Status → Checks → Run setup again*.

**Code check, ease of use** (V01.0.827, plan `code-pruefung/plan.md`, Dominik "4c"): the search also finds words from the descriptions (ranked after the names) and everyday words such as alarm clock (Wecker), light, backup, appointments, PIN or delete; without a hit it offers "Ask Spark" and puts the search text into the assistant. When the microphone fails, a fixed sentence with the way out stands there (blocked, none found, used by another app) instead of the browser's English message. The head under Status → Summary is red only when something really is not running; security advice and services without an answer stand below as "Open" with a button. The groups under Features count function rows like the filters on top. Unsaved changes under Features show their number at "Features", not at "Settings". Pills and marks have at least 4.5:1 contrast; on a phone the small "?" buttons, jump chips, filters and way links are hit through a larger invisible area.
